--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
imu_manager.c - IMU管理实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "imu_manager.h"

#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
#include "fusion/FusionAhrs.h"
#endif

#if ENABLE_BESSEL_LPF
#include "imu_filter.h"
static Bessel_Filter_t g_gyro_lpf[3];       
static float g_gyro_deadzone_z = 0.0f;      
#endif

typedef struct
{
    imu_raw_data_t raw;
    imu_physical_data_t physical;
    imu_attitude_t attitude;
    imu_fusion_t fusion;

    bool initialized;
    volatile bool data_ready;  
    uint32 update_count;
    uint32 last_update_time_us;

    float gyro_offset_x;
    float gyro_offset_y;
    float gyro_offset_z;

} imu_data_t;

static imu_data_t g_imu_data = {0};

#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
static FusionAhrs g_fusion_ahrs;           
static bool g_fusion_calibrated = false;   
static uint16 g_calibration_count = 0;     
static float g_gyro_bias_x = 0.0f;         
static float g_gyro_bias_y = 0.0f;         
static float g_gyro_bias_z = 0.0f;         

#if ENABLE_ONLINE_BIAS_COMP

static uint32 g_stationary_count = 0;     
static const uint32 STATIONARY_SAMPLES = BIAS_UPDATE_TIME / IMU_UPDATE_PERIOD_MS;
#endif
#endif

static void imu_convert_raw_to_physical(void);
static void imu_update_attitude(void);
static void imu_normalize_angle(float *angle);
static void imu_calibrate_gyro(void);

#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
static void imu_fusion_init(void);                    
static void imu_fusion_update_attitude(void);         
#else
static void imu_simple_update_attitude(void);         
#endif

bool imu_init(void)
{
    printf("\r\n========================================\r\n");
    printf("IMU660RA Initialization Start\r\n");
    printf("========================================\r\n");

    printf("SPI Configuration:\r\n");
    printf("  SPI:  SPI_2\r\n");
    printf("  CLK:  P15_2\r\n");
    printf("  MOSI: P15_1\r\n");
    printf("  MISO: P15_0\r\n");
    printf("  CS:   P15_3\r\n");
    printf("  INT:  P14_5\r\n");
    printf("\r\n");

    printf("Step 1: Testing SPI communication...\r\n");
    printf("Manually initializing SPI...\r\n");

    spi_init(SPI_2, SPI_MODE0, 10*1000*1000, SPI2_CLK_P15_2, SPI2_MOSI_P15_1, SPI2_MISO_P15_0, SPI_CS_NULL);
    gpio_init(P15_3, GPO, GPIO_HIGH, GPO_PUSH_PULL);
    system_delay_ms(50);

    printf("Reading Chip ID (register 0x00)...\r\n");

    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
    {

        uint8 chip_id = spi_read_8bit_register(SPI_2, 0x00);

        system_delay_ms(10);

        printf("  Attempt %d: Chip ID = 0x%02X", i+1, chip_id);
        if (chip_id == 0x24)
            printf(" [OK - Expected!]\r\n");
        else if (chip_id == 0xFF)
            printf(" [FAIL - No response (0xFF)]\r\n");
        else if (chip_id == 0x00)
            printf(" [FAIL - No response (0x00)]\r\n");
        else
            printf(" [FAIL - Wrong ID (expected 0x24)]\r\n");
    }
    printf("\r\n");

    printf("Step 2: Initializing IMU660RA using library...\r\n");
    printf("Using retry mechanism (like official example)...\r\n");
    tft180_show_string(0, 64, "Checking IMU...");

    uint8 init_result;
    uint32 retry_count = 0;
    const uint32 MAX_RETRIES = 10;  

    while(1)
    {
        retry_count++;
        printf("  Attempt %d: Calling imu660ra_init()...\r\n", retry_count);

        init_result = imu660ra_init();

        if (init_result == 0)
        {
            
            printf("SUCCESS: IMU660RA initialized on attempt %d!\r\n", retry_count);
            break;
        }
        else
        {
            
            printf("  Failed (error code: %d), retrying...\r\n", init_result);

            if (retry_count >= MAX_RETRIES)
            {
                
                printf("\r\n");
                printf("ERROR: IMU660RA initialization failed after %d attempts!\r\n", MAX_RETRIES);
                printf("Error Code: %d\r\n", init_result);
                printf("\r\n");
                printf("Possible causes:\r\n");
                printf("  1. Wiring error (check SPI connections)\r\n");
                printf("  2. Chip ID mismatch (expected 0x24)\r\n");
                printf("  3. Power supply issue (check 3.3V)\r\n");
                printf("  4. SPI communication failure\r\n");
                printf("\r\n");
                printf("Please check hardware connections!\r\n");
                printf("========================================\r\n");

                tft180_show_string(0, 80, "Error Code: 1");
                tft180_show_string(0, 96, "Chip ID fail");
                tft180_show_string(0, 112, "Check wiring!");
                return false;
            }

            system_delay_ms(100);  
        }
    }

    printf("\r\n");

    printf("Step 3: Calibrating gyroscope...\r\n");
    printf("Please keep IMU stationary!\r\n");
    tft180_show_string(0, 64, "Calibrating...");

    imu_calibrate_gyro();

    printf("SUCCESS: Gyroscope calibrated!\r\n");
    printf("Gyro offset: X=%.2f, Y=%.2f, Z=%.2f\r\n",
           g_imu_data.gyro_offset_x,
           g_imu_data.gyro_offset_y,
           g_imu_data.gyro_offset_z);
    printf("\r\n");

    printf("IMU read mode: PIT timer polling (5ms, 200Hz)\r\n");
    printf("\r\n");

    g_imu_data.initialized = true;
    g_imu_data.data_ready = false;
    g_imu_data.update_count = 0;
    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.attitude.pitch = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.attitude.roll = 0.0f;

    g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_alpha = IMU_FUSION_ALPHA;
    g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_enabled = false;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_encoder = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused = 0.0f;

#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
    
    printf("Step 4: Initializing Fusion AHRS...\r\n");
    imu_fusion_init();
    printf("SUCCESS: Fusion AHRS initialized!\r\n");
    printf("Note: Gyro bias calibration will be performed on first update.\r\n");
    printf("\r\n");
#endif

    printf("IMU660RA initialization completed!\r\n");
    printf("Update rate: 200 Hz (5ms period)\r\n");
    printf("Gyroscope ODR: 200 Hz (matches sampling rate)\r\n");
    printf("Accelerometer ODR: 50 Hz\r\n");
#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
    printf("Attitude algorithm: Fusion AHRS (Madgwick filter)\r\n");
#else
    printf("Attitude algorithm: Simple gyro integration\r\n");
#endif
    printf("========================================\r\n");
    printf("\r\n");

    return true;
}

void imu_update(void)
{
    if (!g_imu_data.initialized)
        return;

    imu660ra_get_acc();
    imu660ra_get_gyro();

    g_imu_data.raw.acc_x = imu660ra_acc_x;
    g_imu_data.raw.acc_y = imu660ra_acc_y;
    g_imu_data.raw.acc_z = imu660ra_acc_z;
    g_imu_data.raw.gyro_x = imu660ra_gyro_x;
    g_imu_data.raw.gyro_y = imu660ra_gyro_y;
    g_imu_data.raw.gyro_z = imu660ra_gyro_z;

    imu_convert_raw_to_physical();

    imu_update_attitude();

    g_imu_data.update_count++;
}

float imu_get_yaw(void)
{
    
    return g_imu_data.attitude.yaw;
}

float imu_get_pitch(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.attitude.pitch;
}

float imu_get_roll(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.attitude.roll;
}

float imu_get_gyro_x(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x;
}

float imu_get_gyro_y(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y;
}

float imu_get_gyro_z(void)
{
    
    return IMU_MOUNT_Z_SIGN * g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z;
}

float imu_get_acc_x(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.physical.acc_x;
}

float imu_get_acc_y(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.physical.acc_y;
}

float imu_get_acc_z(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.physical.acc_z;
}

void imu_reset_yaw(void)
{
    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_encoder = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused = 0.0f;
}

void imu_reset_all_angles(void)
{
    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.attitude.pitch = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.attitude.roll = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_encoder = 0.0f;
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused = 0.0f;
}

void imu_set_yaw(float yaw)
{
    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw = yaw;
    imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.attitude.yaw);
}

bool imu_is_ready(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.initialized;
}

uint32 imu_get_update_count(void)
{
    return g_imu_data.update_count;
}

uint32 imu_get_update_rate(void)
{
    
    return 200;
}

void imu_set_encoder_yaw(float yaw)
{
    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_encoder = yaw;
}

void imu_enable_fusion(bool enable)
{
    g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_enabled = enable;
}

void imu_set_fusion_alpha(float alpha)
{
    if (alpha >= 0.0f && alpha <= 1.0f)
    {
        g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_alpha = alpha;
    }
}

static void imu_convert_raw_to_physical(void)
{
    
    g_imu_data.physical.acc_x = imu660ra_acc_transition(g_imu_data.raw.acc_x);
    g_imu_data.physical.acc_y = imu660ra_acc_transition(g_imu_data.raw.acc_y);
    g_imu_data.physical.acc_z = imu660ra_acc_transition(g_imu_data.raw.acc_z);

    g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x = imu660ra_gyro_transition(g_imu_data.raw.gyro_x);
    g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y = imu660ra_gyro_transition(g_imu_data.raw.gyro_y);
    g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z = imu660ra_gyro_transition(g_imu_data.raw.gyro_z);
}

static void imu_update_attitude(void)
{
#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
    
    imu_fusion_update_attitude();
#else
    
    imu_simple_update_attitude();
#endif
}

static void imu_normalize_angle(float *angle)
{
    while (*angle > 180.0f)
        *angle -= 360.0f;
    while (*angle < -180.0f)
        *angle += 360.0f;
}

static void imu_calibrate_gyro(void)
{
    const uint32 CALIBRATION_SAMPLES = 500;  
    float sum_gyro_x = 0.0f;
    float sum_gyro_y = 0.0f;
    float sum_gyro_z = 0.0f;

#if ENABLE_BESSEL_LPF
    
    float gz_min =  1e6f;
    float gz_max = -1e6f;
#endif

    printf("Calibrating gyroscope (sampling %d times, ~5 seconds)...\r\n", CALIBRATION_SAMPLES);

    for (uint32 i = 0; i < CALIBRATION_SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        
        imu660ra_get_gyro();

        float gyro_x = imu660ra_gyro_transition(imu660ra_gyro_x);
        float gyro_y = imu660ra_gyro_transition(imu660ra_gyro_y);
        float gyro_z = imu660ra_gyro_transition(imu660ra_gyro_z);

        sum_gyro_x += gyro_x;
        sum_gyro_y += gyro_y;
        sum_gyro_z += gyro_z;

#if ENABLE_BESSEL_LPF
        
        if (gyro_z > gz_max) gz_max = gyro_z;
        if (gyro_z < gz_min) gz_min = gyro_z;
#endif

        system_delay_ms(10);

        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0)
        {
            printf("  Progress: %d/%d\r\n", i + 1, CALIBRATION_SAMPLES);
        }
    }

    g_imu_data.gyro_offset_x = sum_gyro_x / CALIBRATION_SAMPLES;
    g_imu_data.gyro_offset_y = sum_gyro_y / CALIBRATION_SAMPLES;
    g_imu_data.gyro_offset_z = sum_gyro_z / CALIBRATION_SAMPLES;

#if ENABLE_BESSEL_LPF
    
    float noise_amp_z = gz_max - gz_min;
    g_gyro_deadzone_z = (noise_amp_z / 2.0f) * 3.0f;
    if (g_gyro_deadzone_z > 0.5f)
        g_gyro_deadzone_z = 0.5f;
    printf("Gyro Z deadzone: %.3f deg/s (noise peak-peak: %.3f)\r\n",
           g_gyro_deadzone_z, noise_amp_z);
#endif

    printf("Calibration completed!\r\n");
}

void imu_int_callback(void)
{
    
    static uint32 int_count = 0;
    int_count++;

    if (int_count % 100 == 0)
    {
        printf("[INT] Interrupt triggered: %d times\r\n", int_count);
    }

    imu660ra_get_acc();
    imu660ra_get_gyro();

    g_imu_data.raw.acc_x = imu660ra_acc_x;
    g_imu_data.raw.acc_y = imu660ra_acc_y;
    g_imu_data.raw.acc_z = imu660ra_acc_z;
    g_imu_data.raw.gyro_x = imu660ra_gyro_x;
    g_imu_data.raw.gyro_y = imu660ra_gyro_y;
    g_imu_data.raw.gyro_z = imu660ra_gyro_z;

    g_imu_data.data_ready = true;
}

#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS

static void imu_fusion_init(void)
{
    
    FusionAhrsInitialise(&g_fusion_ahrs);

    FusionAhrsSettings settings = {
        .gain = FUSION_GAIN,
        .accelerationRejection = FUSION_ACC_REJECTION,
        .magneticRejection = FUSION_MAG_REJECTION,
        .rejectionTimeout = FUSION_REJECTION_TIMEOUT
    };
    FusionAhrsSetSettings(&g_fusion_ahrs, &settings);

    g_fusion_calibrated = false;
    g_calibration_count = 0;
    g_gyro_bias_x = 0.0f;
    g_gyro_bias_y = 0.0f;
    g_gyro_bias_z = 0.0f;

#if ENABLE_BESSEL_LPF
    
    float f_sample = 1.0f / IMU_DT;    
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
    {
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&g_gyro_lpf[i], f_sample, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);
    }
#endif
}

void imu_fusion_calibrate(void)
{
    if (g_fusion_calibrated)
        return;

    g_gyro_bias_x += g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x;
    g_gyro_bias_y += g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y;
    g_gyro_bias_z += g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z;
    g_calibration_count++;

    if (g_calibration_count >= GYRO_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES)
    {
        g_gyro_bias_x /= GYRO_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES;
        g_gyro_bias_y /= GYRO_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES;
        g_gyro_bias_z /= GYRO_CALIBRATION_SAMPLES;
        g_fusion_calibrated = true;

        printf("[Fusion AHRS] Gyro bias calibration completed!\r\n");
        printf("  Bias X: %.3f deg/s\r\n", g_gyro_bias_x);
        printf("  Bias Y: %.3f deg/s\r\n", g_gyro_bias_y);
        printf("  Bias Z: %.3f deg/s\r\n", g_gyro_bias_z);
    }
}

bool imu_is_fusion_ready(void)
{
    return g_fusion_calibrated;
}

float imu_get_gyro_bias_z(void)
{
    return g_gyro_bias_z;
}

static void imu_fusion_update_attitude(void)
{
    
    if (!g_fusion_calibrated)
    {
        imu_fusion_calibrate();
        return;
    }

#if ENABLE_ONLINE_BIAS_COMP
    
    float gyro_mag = sqrtf(g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x * g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x +
                           g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y * g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y +
                           g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z * g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z);

    if (gyro_mag < BIAS_UPDATE_THRESHOLD)
    {
        
        g_stationary_count++;

        if (g_stationary_count >= STATIONARY_SAMPLES)
        {
            
            g_gyro_bias_x += BIAS_UPDATE_ALPHA * (g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x - g_gyro_bias_x);
            g_gyro_bias_y += BIAS_UPDATE_ALPHA * (g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y - g_gyro_bias_y);
            g_gyro_bias_z += BIAS_UPDATE_ALPHA * (g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z - g_gyro_bias_z);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        
        g_stationary_count = 0;
    }
#endif

    float gyro_x = g_imu_data.physical.gyro_x - g_gyro_bias_x;
    float gyro_y = g_imu_data.physical.gyro_y - g_gyro_bias_y;
    float gyro_z = g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z - g_gyro_bias_z;

#if ENABLE_BESSEL_LPF
    
    gyro_x = Filter_Bessel_Apply(&g_gyro_lpf[0], gyro_x);
    gyro_y = Filter_Bessel_Apply(&g_gyro_lpf[1], gyro_y);
    gyro_z = Filter_Bessel_Apply(&g_gyro_lpf[2], gyro_z);

    if (fabsf(gyro_z) <= g_gyro_deadzone_z)
    {
        gyro_z = 0.0f;
    }
    else
    {
        gyro_z -= (gyro_z > 0.0f ? g_gyro_deadzone_z : -g_gyro_deadzone_z);
    }
#endif

    FusionVector gyroscope = {
        .axis.x = gyro_x,
        .axis.y = -gyro_y,
        .axis.z = IMU_MOUNT_Z_SIGN * (-gyro_z)
    };

    FusionVector accelerometer = { .axis.x = 0.0f, .axis.y = 0.0f, .axis.z = 1.0f };

    FusionAhrsUpdateNoMagnetometer(&g_fusion_ahrs, gyroscope, accelerometer, IMU_DT);

    FusionEuler euler = FusionQuaternionToEuler(FusionAhrsGetQuaternion(&g_fusion_ahrs));

    g_imu_data.attitude.roll = euler.angle.roll;
    g_imu_data.attitude.pitch = euler.angle.pitch;
    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw = euler.angle.yaw;

    imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.attitude.yaw);
    imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.attitude.pitch);
    imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.attitude.roll);

    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro = g_imu_data.attitude.yaw;
}

static void imu_simple_update_attitude(void)
{
    
    float gyro_z_calibrated = g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z - g_imu_data.gyro_offset_z;

    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw -= IMU_MOUNT_Z_SIGN * gyro_z_calibrated * IMU_DT;
    imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.attitude.yaw);

    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro = g_imu_data.attitude.yaw;

    if (g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_enabled)
    {
        float alpha = g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_alpha;
        float gyro_yaw = g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro;
        float enc_yaw  = g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_encoder;

        float diff = gyro_yaw - enc_yaw;
        while (diff >  180.0f) diff -= 360.0f;
        while (diff < -180.0f) diff += 360.0f;

        g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused = enc_yaw + alpha * diff;
        imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused);
    }
}

#else

static void imu_simple_update_attitude(void)
{
    
    float gyro_z_calibrated = g_imu_data.physical.gyro_z - g_imu_data.gyro_offset_z;

    g_imu_data.attitude.yaw -= IMU_MOUNT_Z_SIGN * gyro_z_calibrated * IMU_DT;
    imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.attitude.yaw);

    g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro = g_imu_data.attitude.yaw;

    if (g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_enabled)
    {
        float alpha = g_imu_data.fusion.fusion_alpha;
        float gyro_yaw = g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_gyro;
        float enc_yaw  = g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_encoder;

        float diff = gyro_yaw - enc_yaw;
        while (diff >  180.0f) diff -= 360.0f;
        while (diff < -180.0f) diff += 360.0f;

        g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused = enc_yaw + alpha * diff;
        imu_normalize_angle(&g_imu_data.fusion.yaw_fused);
    }
}

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
imu_filter.h - IMU滤波头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef IMU_FILTER_H_
#define IMU_FILTER_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include <math.h>

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif

#define IMU_FILTER_DTOR   (0.01745329f)     
#define IMU_FILTER_RTOD   (57.2957795f)     

typedef struct
{
    float b0, b1, b2, a1, a2;  
    float x1, x2;              
    float y1, y2;              
    float out;                 
} Bessel_Filter_t;

void Filter_Bessel_Init(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float f_sample, float f_cutoff);

float Filter_Bessel_Apply(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float input);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
imu_filter.c - IMU滤波实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "imu_filter.h"

void Filter_Bessel_Init(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float f_sample, float f_cutoff)
{
    
    float Q = 0.57735027f;

    float omega_0 = 2.0f * M_PI * f_cutoff / f_sample;

    float cos_omega = cosf(omega_0);
    float alpha     = sinf(omega_0) / (2.0f * Q);

    float a0 = 1.0f + alpha;

    filter->b0 = ((1.0f - cos_omega) / 2.0f) / a0;
    filter->b1 =  (1.0f - cos_omega)         / a0;
    filter->b2 = ((1.0f - cos_omega) / 2.0f) / a0;
    filter->a1 = (-2.0f * cos_omega)         / a0;
    filter->a2 =  (1.0f - alpha)             / a0;

    filter->x1  = 0.0f;
    filter->x2  = 0.0f;
    filter->y1  = 0.0f;
    filter->y2  = 0.0f;
    filter->out = 0.0f;
}

float Filter_Bessel_Apply(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float input)
{
    
    filter->out = filter->b0 * input
                + filter->b1 * filter->x1
                + filter->b2 * filter->x2
                - filter->a1 * filter->y1
                - filter->a2 * filter->y2;

    filter->x2 = filter->x1;
    filter->x1 = input;
    filter->y2 = filter->y1;
    filter->y1 = filter->out;

    return filter->out;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
imu_display.h - IMU显示头文件
