#ifndef _IMU_DISPLAY_H_
#define _IMU_DISPLAY_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define IMU_DISPLAY_UPDATE_PERIOD_MS (100) 

typedef enum
{
    IMU_DISPLAY_MODE_FULL,   
    IMU_DISPLAY_MODE_SIMPLE, 
    IMU_DISPLAY_MODE_WAVE,   
} imu_display_mode_enum;

void imu_display_init(void);

void imu_display_update(void);

void imu_display_set_mode(imu_display_mode_enum mode);

void imu_display_clear(void);
void imu_display_pause(void);
void imu_display_resume(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
imu_display.c - IMU显示实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "imu_manager.h"
#include "imu_display.h"
#include "encoder_manager.h"

static bool g_initialized = false;
static bool g_paused = false;
static bool g_title_shown = false;
static imu_display_mode_enum g_mode = IMU_DISPLAY_MODE_FULL;

void imu_display_init(void)
{
    tft180_set_dir(TFT180_CROSSWISE);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_init();
    tft180_clear();
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, "System Init...");

    g_initialized = true;
    g_paused = false;
    g_title_shown = false;
}

void imu_display_update(void)
{
    if (!g_initialized || g_paused) return;

    char buf[24];

    sprintf(buf, "Yaw:%7.2f", imu_get_yaw());
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Gz:%7.1f d/s", imu_get_gyro_z());
    tft180_show_string(0, 16, buf);

    if (!g_title_shown)
    {
        tft180_show_string(0, 32, "---- Speed ----");
        g_title_shown = true;
    }

    sprintf(buf, "LF:%4d  RF:%4d",
            encoder_motor_get_delta(ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_FRONT),
            encoder_motor_get_delta(ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_FRONT));
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "LB:%4d  RB:%4d",
            encoder_motor_get_delta(ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_BACK),
            encoder_motor_get_delta(ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_BACK));
    tft180_show_string(0, 64, buf);
}

void imu_display_set_mode(imu_display_mode_enum mode)
{
    g_mode = mode;
    (void)g_mode;   
    g_title_shown = false;
    tft180_clear();
}

void imu_display_clear(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    g_title_shown = false;
}

void imu_display_pause(void)
{
    g_paused = true;
}

void imu_display_resume(void)
{
    g_paused = false;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid_controller.h - PID控制器头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _PID_CONTROLLER_H_
#define _PID_CONTROLLER_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

typedef struct {
    
    float kp;               
    float ki;               
    float kd;               
    float integral_limit;   
    float output_limit;     

    float integral;         
    float last_error;       
    float last_feedback;    
    float output;           
} pid_t;

void pid_init(pid_t *pid, float kp, float ki, float kd,
              float integral_limit, float output_limit);

float pid_calculate(pid_t *pid, float target, float feedback);

void pid_reset(pid_t *pid);

void pid_set_params(pid_t *pid, float kp, float ki, float kd);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid_controller.c - PID控制器实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "pid_controller.h"

static float clampf(float value, float min_val, float max_val)
{
    if (value > max_val) return max_val;
    if (value < min_val) return min_val;
    return value;
}

void pid_init(pid_t *pid, float kp, float ki, float kd,
              float integral_limit, float output_limit)
{
    pid->kp             = kp;
    pid->ki             = ki;
    pid->kd             = kd;
    pid->integral_limit = integral_limit;
    pid->output_limit   = output_limit;

    pid->integral      = 0.0f;
    pid->last_error    = 0.0f;
    pid->last_feedback = 0.0f;
    pid->output        = 0.0f;
}

float pid_calculate(pid_t *pid, float target, float feedback)
{
    float error = target - feedback;

    pid->integral += error;
    pid->integral = clampf(pid->integral, -pid->integral_limit, pid->integral_limit);

    float derivative = -(feedback - pid->last_feedback);

    pid->output = pid->kp * error
                + pid->ki * pid->integral
                + pid->kd * derivative;

    pid->output = clampf(pid->output, -pid->output_limit, pid->output_limit);

    pid->last_error    = error;
    pid->last_feedback = feedback;

    return pid->output;
}

void pid_reset(pid_t *pid)
{
    pid->integral      = 0.0f;
    pid->last_error    = 0.0f;
    pid->last_feedback = 0.0f;
    pid->output        = 0.0f;
}

void pid_set_params(pid_t *pid, float kp, float ki, float kd)
{
    pid->kp = kp;
    pid->ki = ki;
    pid->kd = kd;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid_link.h - PID串口调参头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _PID_LINK_H_
#define _PID_LINK_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define PID_LINK_UART           (UART_0)    
#define PID_LINK_PROTO_VER      (1)

#define PID_LINK_WAVE_CH        (4)         
#define PID_LINK_WAVE_BYTES     (PID_LINK_WAVE_CH * 4 + 4)  

#define PID_LINK_TX_RING        (1024)      
#define PID_LINK_RX_RING        (256)       
#define PID_LINK_LINE_MAX       (48)        
#define PID_LINK_OUT_MAX        (96)        

#define PID_LINK_WAVE_DIV_DEF   (2)         
#define PID_LINK_WDG_MS         (3000)      

typedef struct {
    const char *name;       
    float      *pval;       
    float       lo;         
    float       hi;         
    float       step;       
    uint8       decimals;   
    const char *macro;      
} tune_param_t;

typedef struct {
    const char         *name;                       
    const char         *ch_name[PID_LINK_WAVE_CH];  
    const tune_param_t *params;                     
    uint8               param_num;

    const char *test_name;      
    float      *test_val;
    float       test_lo;
    float       test_hi;
    float       test_step;
    uint8       test_decimals;

    void (*on_select)(void);                            
    void (*on_run)(void);                               
    void (*on_stop)(void);                              
    void (*on_test_change)(void);                       
    void (*sample)(float out[PID_LINK_WAVE_CH]);        
} tune_group_t;

void pid_link_init(const tune_group_t *groups, uint8 group_num,
                   uint8 (*get_enabled)(void), const char *banner);

void pid_link_rx_byte(void);

void pid_link_tick(void);

void pid_link_poll(void);

uint8 pid_link_is_active(void);

uint32 pid_link_get_drop(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid_link.c - PID串口调参实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "pid_link.h"

#define TX_MASK     (PID_LINK_TX_RING - 1)
#define RX_MASK     (PID_LINK_RX_RING - 1)

static volatile uint8  s_tx[PID_LINK_TX_RING];
static volatile uint16 s_tx_head = 0;    
static volatile uint16 s_tx_tail = 0;    

static volatile uint8  s_rx[PID_LINK_RX_RING];
static volatile uint16 s_rx_head = 0;    
static volatile uint16 s_rx_tail = 0;    

static char  s_line[PID_LINK_LINE_MAX];  
static uint8 s_line_len = 0;
static uint8 s_line_ovf = 0;

static const tune_group_t *s_grp   = 0;  
static uint8 s_grp_num             = 0;
static uint8 s_cur                 = 0;  
static uint8 s_param_total         = 0;  
static uint8 s_grp_base[16];             

static uint8  s_wave_on   = 0;
static uint8  s_wave_div  = PID_LINK_WAVE_DIV_DEF;
static uint8  s_wave_cnt  = 0;
static uint32 s_wave_drop = 0;
static uint32 s_rx_err    = 0;

static uint8  s_active    = 0;           
static uint8  s_run_by_me = 0;           
static uint8 (*s_get_enabled)(void) = 0; 
static uint32 s_silent_ms = 0;           
static uint8  s_wdg_fire  = 0;           

typedef enum {
    DMP_IDLE = 0, DMP_INFO, DMP_GROUP, DMP_TEST, DMP_PARAM, DMP_CUR, DMP_END, DMP_MACRO
} dump_state_e;
static dump_state_e s_dump = DMP_IDLE;
static uint8 s_dump_g = 0;               
static uint8 s_dump_p = 0;               

static uint16 tx_free(void)
{
    uint16 used = (uint16)((s_tx_head - s_tx_tail) & TX_MASK);
    return (uint16)(PID_LINK_TX_RING - 1 - used);
}

static void tx_push(uint8 b)
{
    s_tx[s_tx_head] = b;
    s_tx_head = (uint16)((s_tx_head + 1) & TX_MASK);
}

static void tx_drain(void)
{
    volatile stc_SCB_t *scb = get_scb_module(PID_LINK_UART);
    for (;;)
    {
        uint16 fifo_free;
        if (s_tx_tail == s_tx_head) return;
        fifo_free = (uint16)(Cy_SCB_GetFifoSize(scb) - Cy_SCB_GetNumInTxFifo(scb));
        if (fifo_free == 0) return;
        Cy_SCB_WriteTxFifo(scb, s_tx[s_tx_tail]);
        s_tx_tail = (uint16)((s_tx_tail + 1) & TX_MASK);
    }
}

static void link_puts(const char *s)
{
    uint16 n = 0;
    while (s[n]) n++;

    __disable_irq();
    if (tx_free() >= n)
    {
        uint16 i;
        for (i = 0; i < n; i++) tx_push((uint8)s[i]);
    }
    __enable_irq();
}

static void push_f32(float v)
{
    union { float f; uint32 u; } c;
    c.f = v;
    tx_push((uint8)(c.u));
    tx_push((uint8)(c.u >> 8));
    tx_push((uint8)(c.u >> 16));
    tx_push((uint8)(c.u >> 24));
}

static char to_lower(char c)
{
    return (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ? (char)(c - 'A' + 'a') : c;
}

static uint8 str_eq(const char *a, const char *b)
{
    while (*a && *b)
    {
        if (to_lower(*a) != to_lower(*b)) return 0;
        a++; b++;
    }
    return (uint8)(*a == 0 && *b == 0);
}

static const char* skip_ws(const char *s)
{
    while (*s == ' ' || *s == '\t') s++;
    return s;
}

static uint8 parse_float(const char *s, float *out, const char **end)
{
    float val = 0.0f, frac = 0.1f;
    uint8 neg = 0, digits = 0;

    s = skip_ws(s);
    if      (*s == '+') s++;
    else if (*s == '-') { neg = 1; s++; }

    while (*s >= '0' && *s <= '9') { val = val * 10.0f + (float)(*s - '0'); s++; digits++; }
    if (*s == '.')
    {
        s++;
        while (*s >= '0' && *s <= '9') { val += (float)(*s - '0') * frac; frac *= 0.1f; s++; digits++; }
    }
    if (!digits) return 0;

    *out = neg ? -val : val;
    if (end) *end = s;
    return 1;
}

static uint8 parse_uint(const char *s, uint32 *out, const char **end)
{
    uint32 v = 0;
    uint8  digits = 0;

    s = skip_ws(s);
    while (*s >= '0' && *s <= '9') { v = v * 10u + (uint32)(*s - '0'); s++; digits++; }
    if (!digits) return 0;

    *out = v;
    if (end) *end = s;
    return 1;
}

static float clamp_f(float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if (v < lo) return lo;
    if (v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static uint8 idx_locate(uint8 idx, uint8 *gid, uint8 *local)
{
    uint8 g;
    for (g = 0; g < s_grp_num; g++)
    {
        uint8 base = s_grp_base[g];
        if (idx >= base && idx < (uint8)(base + s_grp[g].param_num))
        {
            *gid   = g;
            *local = (uint8)(idx - base);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static void reply_status(void)
{
    char buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];
    sprintf(buf, "#S %d %d %d %d %lu %lu\r\n",
            (int)s_cur, (int)s_wave_on, (int)s_wave_div,
            (int)(s_get_enabled ? (s_get_enabled() ? 1 : 0) : 0),
            (unsigned long)s_wave_drop, (unsigned long)s_rx_err);
    link_puts(buf);
}

static void reply_current(void)
{
    char buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];
    sprintf(buf, "#C %d %s\r\n", (int)s_cur, s_grp[s_cur].name);
    link_puts(buf);
}

static void cmd_select_group(uint8 gid)
{
    if (gid >= s_grp_num) { link_puts("#X badgid\r\n"); return; }

    if (s_grp[s_cur].on_stop) s_grp[s_cur].on_stop();
    s_run_by_me = 0;

    s_cur = gid;
    if (s_grp[s_cur].on_select) s_grp[s_cur].on_select();

    reply_current();
}

static void cmd_set_param(uint8 idx, float v)
{
    char  buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];
    uint8 gid, local;

    if (!idx_locate(idx, &gid, &local)) { link_puts("#X badidx\r\n"); return; }

    {
        const tune_param_t *p = &s_grp[gid].params[local];
        v = clamp_f(v, p->lo, p->hi);
        *(p->pval) = v;
        
        sprintf(buf, "#K p %d %.6f\r\n", (int)idx, (double)v);
        link_puts(buf);
    }
}

static void cmd_set_test(float v)
{
    char buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];
    const tune_group_t *g = &s_grp[s_cur];

    if (g->test_val == 0) { link_puts("#X notest\r\n"); return; }

    v = clamp_f(v, g->test_lo, g->test_hi);
    *(g->test_val) = v;

    if (g->on_test_change) g->on_test_change();

    sprintf(buf, "#K t %.6f\r\n", (double)v);
    link_puts(buf);
}

static void dump_start(dump_state_e first)
{
    s_dump   = first;
    s_dump_g = 0;
    s_dump_p = 0;
}

static void dump_pump(void)
{
    char buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];

    while (s_dump != DMP_IDLE)
    {
        if (tx_free() < (uint16)(PID_LINK_OUT_MAX + PID_LINK_WAVE_BYTES)) return;

        switch (s_dump)
        {
        case DMP_INFO:
            sprintf(buf, "#I %d %d %d %d %d\r\n",
                    (int)PID_LINK_PROTO_VER, (int)s_grp_num, (int)s_param_total,
                    (int)PID_LINK_WAVE_CH, (int)(200 / s_wave_div));
            link_puts(buf);
            s_dump = DMP_GROUP; s_dump_g = 0;
            break;

        case DMP_GROUP:
            if (s_dump_g >= s_grp_num) { s_dump = DMP_TEST; s_dump_g = 0; break; }
            {
                const tune_group_t *g = &s_grp[s_dump_g];
                sprintf(buf, "#G %d %s %d %s %s %s %s\r\n",
                        (int)s_dump_g, g->name, (int)g->param_num,
                        g->ch_name[0], g->ch_name[1], g->ch_name[2], g->ch_name[3]);
                link_puts(buf);
            }
            s_dump_g++;
            break;

        case DMP_TEST:
            if (s_dump_g >= s_grp_num) { s_dump = DMP_PARAM; s_dump_p = 0; break; }
            {
                const tune_group_t *g = &s_grp[s_dump_g];
                if (g->test_val != 0)
                {
                    sprintf(buf, "#T %d %s %.6f %.6f %.6f %.6f %d\r\n",
                            (int)s_dump_g, g->test_name, (double)(*(g->test_val)),
                            (double)g->test_lo, (double)g->test_hi,
                            (double)g->test_step, (int)g->test_decimals);
                    link_puts(buf);
                }
            }
            s_dump_g++;
            break;

        case DMP_PARAM:
            if (s_dump_p >= s_param_total) { s_dump = DMP_CUR; break; }
            {
                uint8 gid, local;
                if (idx_locate(s_dump_p, &gid, &local))
                {
                    const tune_param_t *p = &s_grp[gid].params[local];
                    sprintf(buf, "#P %d %d %s %.6f %.6f %.6f %.6f %d\r\n",
                            (int)s_dump_p, (int)gid, p->name, (double)(*(p->pval)),
                            (double)p->lo, (double)p->hi, (double)p->step,
                            (int)p->decimals);
                    link_puts(buf);
                }
            }
            s_dump_p++;
            break;

        case DMP_CUR:
            reply_current();
            s_dump = DMP_END;
            break;

        case DMP_END:
            sprintf(buf, "#E %d\r\n", (int)s_param_total);
            link_puts(buf);
            s_dump = DMP_IDLE;
            break;

        case DMP_MACRO:
            if (s_dump_p >= s_param_total)
            {
                link_puts("#E 0\r\n");
                s_dump = DMP_IDLE;
                break;
            }
            {
                uint8 gid, local;
                if (idx_locate(s_dump_p, &gid, &local))
                {
                    const tune_param_t *p = &s_grp[gid].params[local];
                    if (p->macro != 0)
                    {
                        sprintf(buf, "#M #define %-24s %.6ff\r\n",
                                p->macro, (double)(*(p->pval)));
                        link_puts(buf);
                    }
                }
            }
            s_dump_p++;
            break;

        default:
            s_dump = DMP_IDLE;
            break;
        }
    }
}

static void exec_line(const char *line)
{
    const char *p = skip_ws(line);

    if (*p == 0) return;
    s_active = 1;   

    if (p[0] == '?' && p[1] == 0) { dump_start(DMP_INFO); return; }

    if ((p[0] == 'g' || p[0] == 'G') && (p[1] == ' ' || p[1] == '\t'))
    {
        uint32 v;
        if (!parse_uint(p + 1, &v, 0)) { link_puts("#X badnum\r\n"); return; }
        cmd_select_group((uint8)v);
        return;
    }
    if ((p[0] == 'p' || p[0] == 'P') && (p[1] == ' ' || p[1] == '\t'))
    {
        uint32 idx;
        float  v;
        const char *q;
        if (!parse_uint(p + 1, &idx, &q))  { link_puts("#X badnum\r\n"); return; }
        if (!parse_float(q, &v, 0))        { link_puts("#X badnum\r\n"); return; }
        cmd_set_param((uint8)idx, v);
        return;
    }
    if ((p[0] == 't' || p[0] == 'T') && (p[1] == ' ' || p[1] == '\t'))
    {
        float v;
        if (!parse_float(p + 1, &v, 0)) { link_puts("#X badnum\r\n"); return; }
        cmd_set_test(v);
        return;
    }
    if ((p[0] == 'w' || p[0] == 'W') && (p[1] == ' ' || p[1] == '\t'))
    {
        uint32 v;
        char buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];
        if (!parse_uint(p + 1, &v, 0)) { link_puts("#X badnum\r\n"); return; }
        s_wave_on = (uint8)(v ? 1 : 0);
        sprintf(buf, "#K w %d\r\n", (int)s_wave_on);
        link_puts(buf);
        return;
    }
    if ((p[0] == 'r' || p[0] == 'R') && (p[1] == ' ' || p[1] == '\t'))
    {
        uint32 v;
        char buf[PID_LINK_OUT_MAX];
        if (!parse_uint(p + 1, &v, 0)) { link_puts("#X badnum\r\n"); return; }
        if (v < 1u || v > 50u)         { link_puts("#X baddiv\r\n"); return; }
        s_wave_div = (uint8)v;
        s_wave_cnt = 0;
        sprintf(buf, "#K r %d %d\r\n", (int)s_wave_div, (int)(200 / s_wave_div));
        link_puts(buf);
        return;
    }

    if (str_eq(p, "run"))
    {
        if (s_grp[s_cur].on_run) s_grp[s_cur].on_run();
        s_run_by_me = 1;
        link_puts("#K run\r\n");
        return;
    }
    if (str_eq(p, "stop"))
    {
        if (s_grp[s_cur].on_stop) s_grp[s_cur].on_stop();
        s_run_by_me = 0;
        link_puts("#K stop\r\n");
        return;
    }
    if (str_eq(p, "st"))   { reply_status(); return; }
    if (str_eq(p, "dump")) { dump_start(DMP_MACRO); return; }

    link_puts("#X badcmd\r\n");
}

void pid_link_init(const tune_group_t *groups, uint8 group_num,
                   uint8 (*get_enabled)(void), const char *banner)
{
    uint8 g;

    s_grp         = groups;
    s_grp_num     = (group_num > 16) ? 16 : group_num;
    s_get_enabled = get_enabled;

    s_param_total = 0;
    for (g = 0; g < s_grp_num; g++)
    {
        s_grp_base[g]  = s_param_total;
        s_param_total  = (uint8)(s_param_total + groups[g].param_num);
    }

    s_cur       = 0;
    s_wave_on   = 0;    
    s_wave_div  = PID_LINK_WAVE_DIV_DEF;
    s_wave_cnt  = 0;
    s_wave_drop = 0;
    s_rx_err    = 0;
    s_active    = 0;
    s_run_by_me = 0;
    s_silent_ms = 0;
    s_wdg_fire  = 0;
    s_dump      = DMP_IDLE;
    s_line_len  = 0;
    s_line_ovf  = 0;

    if (banner)
    {
        link_puts(banner);
        link_puts("\r\n");
    }
}

void pid_link_rx_byte(void)
{
    uint8 dat;

    while (uart_query_byte(PID_LINK_UART, &dat))
    {
        uint16 next = (uint16)((s_rx_head + 1) & RX_MASK);
        if (next == s_rx_tail) { s_rx_err++; break; }   

        s_rx[s_rx_head] = dat;
        s_rx_head       = next;
        s_silent_ms     = 0;                            
    }
}

void pid_link_tick(void)
{
    
    s_silent_ms += 5;
    if (s_run_by_me && s_silent_ms >= PID_LINK_WDG_MS) s_wdg_fire = 1;

    if (s_wave_on && s_grp && s_grp[s_cur].sample)
    {
        if (++s_wave_cnt >= s_wave_div)
        {
            s_wave_cnt = 0;

            if (tx_free() < PID_LINK_WAVE_BYTES)
            {
                s_wave_drop++;
            }
            else
            {
                float w[PID_LINK_WAVE_CH];
                uint8 i;
                s_grp[s_cur].sample(w);
                for (i = 0; i < PID_LINK_WAVE_CH; i++) push_f32(w[i]);
                tx_push(0x00); tx_push(0x00); tx_push(0x80); tx_push(0x7F);
            }
        }
    }

    tx_drain();
}

void pid_link_poll(void)
{
    if (!s_grp) return;

    if (s_wdg_fire)
    {
        s_wdg_fire = 0;
        if (s_grp[s_cur].on_stop) s_grp[s_cur].on_stop();
        s_run_by_me = 0;
        link_puts("#X timeout\r\n");
    }

    while (s_rx_tail != s_rx_head)
    {
        char c = (char)s_rx[s_rx_tail];
        s_rx_tail = (uint16)((s_rx_tail + 1) & RX_MASK);

        if (c == '\n' || c == '\r')
        {
            if (s_line_ovf)
            {
                link_puts("#X longline\r\n");
            }
            else if (s_line_len)
            {
                s_line[s_line_len] = 0;
                exec_line(s_line);
            }
            s_line_len = 0;
            s_line_ovf = 0;
            continue;
        }
        if (c == 8 || c == 127) { if (s_line_len) s_line_len--; continue; }

        if (s_line_len < (PID_LINK_LINE_MAX - 1)) s_line[s_line_len++] = (char)c;
        else                                       s_line_ovf = 1;
    }

    dump_pump();
}

uint8 pid_link_is_active(void)
{
    return s_active;
}

uint32 pid_link_get_drop(void)
{
    return s_wave_drop;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid_tune_table.h - PID参数表头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _PID_TUNE_TABLE_H_
#define _PID_TUNE_TABLE_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

void pid_tune_table_install(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid_tune_table.c - PID参数表实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "pid_tune_table.h"
#include "pid_link.h"
#include "pid_controller.h"
#include "chassis_controller.h"

static float s_test_wz  = 1.00f;    
static float s_test_yaw = 0.00f;    

static tune_param_t s_p_rate[5];
static tune_param_t s_p_hold[6];

static void rate_sample(float o[PID_LINK_WAVE_CH])
{
    chassis_yaw_probe_t pb;
    pid_t *p = chassis_get_yaw_rate_pid();
    chassis_get_yaw_probe(&pb);
    o[0] = pb.rate_target;
    o[1] = pb.rate_actual;
    o[2] = p->output;
    o[3] = p->integral;
}

static void hold_sample(float o[PID_LINK_WAVE_CH])
{
    chassis_yaw_probe_t pb;
    pid_t *p = chassis_get_yaw_hold_pid();
    chassis_get_yaw_probe(&pb);
    o[0] = pb.hold_target;
    o[1] = pb.hold_actual;
    o[2] = p->output;
    o[3] = p->integral;
}

static void common_stop(void)
{
    chassis_stop();
}

static void rate_test_change(void)
{
    if (chassis_is_enabled())
        chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, s_test_wz);
}
static void hold_test_change(void)
{
    if (chassis_is_enabled())
        chassis_set_yaw_target(s_test_yaw);
}

static void rate_select(void)
{
    chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE);
}
static void rate_run(void)
{
    chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE);
    chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, s_test_wz);
    chassis_set_enabled(true);
}

static void hold_select(void)
{
    chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
}
static void hold_run(void)
{
    chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
    chassis_set_yaw_target(s_test_yaw);
    chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
    chassis_set_enabled(true);
}

static uint8 tune_get_enabled(void)
{
    return (uint8)(chassis_is_enabled() ? 1 : 0);
}

static tune_group_t s_groups[2];

static void fill_group(tune_group_t *g, const char *name,
                       const char *c0, const char *c1,
                       const tune_param_t *params, uint8 pnum,
                       const char *tname, float *tval,
                       float tlo, float thi, float tstep, uint8 tdec,
                       void (*on_select)(void), void (*on_run)(void),
                       void (*on_test_change)(void),
                       void (*sample)(float[PID_LINK_WAVE_CH]))
{
    g->name       = name;
    g->ch_name[0] = c0;
    g->ch_name[1] = c1;
    g->ch_name[2] = "out";
    g->ch_name[3] = "integ";
    g->params     = params;
    g->param_num  = pnum;
    g->test_name  = tname;
    g->test_val   = tval;
    g->test_lo    = tlo;
    g->test_hi    = thi;
    g->test_step  = tstep;
    g->test_decimals = tdec;
    g->on_select  = on_select;
    g->on_run     = on_run;
    g->on_stop    = common_stop;
    g->on_test_change = on_test_change;
    g->sample     = sample;
}

void pid_tune_table_install(void)
{
    
    {
        pid_t *p = chassis_get_yaw_rate_pid();
        s_p_rate[0].name = "kp";   s_p_rate[0].pval = &p->kp;
        s_p_rate[0].lo = 0.0f; s_p_rate[0].hi = 0.500f; s_p_rate[0].step = 0.002f;  s_p_rate[0].decimals = 3; s_p_rate[0].macro = "YAW_PID_KP";
        s_p_rate[1].name = "ki";   s_p_rate[1].pval = &p->ki;
        s_p_rate[1].lo = 0.0f; s_p_rate[1].hi = 0.200f; s_p_rate[1].step = 0.001f;  s_p_rate[1].decimals = 3; s_p_rate[1].macro = "YAW_PID_KI";
        s_p_rate[2].name = "kd";   s_p_rate[2].pval = &p->kd;
        s_p_rate[2].lo = 0.0f; s_p_rate[2].hi = 0.020f; s_p_rate[2].step = 0.0001f; s_p_rate[2].decimals = 4; s_p_rate[2].macro = "YAW_PID_KD";
        s_p_rate[3].name = "ilim"; s_p_rate[3].pval = &p->integral_limit;
        s_p_rate[3].lo = 0.0f; s_p_rate[3].hi = 20.0f;  s_p_rate[3].step = 0.1f;    s_p_rate[3].decimals = 1; s_p_rate[3].macro = "YAW_PID_ILIMIT";
        s_p_rate[4].name = "olim"; s_p_rate[4].pval = &p->output_limit;
        s_p_rate[4].lo = 0.5f; s_p_rate[4].hi = 6.0f;   s_p_rate[4].step = 0.1f;    s_p_rate[4].decimals = 1; s_p_rate[4].macro = "YAW_PID_OLIMIT";
    }

    {
        pid_t *p = chassis_get_yaw_hold_pid();
        s_p_hold[0].name = "kp";   s_p_hold[0].pval = &p->kp;
        s_p_hold[0].lo = 0.0f; s_p_hold[0].hi = 1.000f; s_p_hold[0].step = 0.005f;  s_p_hold[0].decimals = 3; s_p_hold[0].macro = "YAW_HOLD_PID_KP";
        s_p_hold[1].name = "ki";   s_p_hold[1].pval = &p->ki;
        s_p_hold[1].lo = 0.0f; s_p_hold[1].hi = 0.100f; s_p_hold[1].step = 0.0005f; s_p_hold[1].decimals = 4; s_p_hold[1].macro = "YAW_HOLD_PID_KI";
        s_p_hold[2].name = "kd";   s_p_hold[2].pval = &p->kd;
        
        s_p_hold[2].lo = 0.0f; s_p_hold[2].hi = 5.000f; s_p_hold[2].step = 0.050f; s_p_hold[2].decimals = 2; s_p_hold[2].macro = "YAW_HOLD_PID_KD";
        s_p_hold[3].name = "ilim"; s_p_hold[3].pval = &p->integral_limit;
        s_p_hold[3].lo = 0.0f; s_p_hold[3].hi = 200.0f; s_p_hold[3].step = 1.0f;    s_p_hold[3].decimals = 1; s_p_hold[3].macro = "YAW_HOLD_PID_ILIMIT";
        s_p_hold[4].name = "olim"; s_p_hold[4].pval = &p->output_limit;
        s_p_hold[4].lo = 0.2f; s_p_hold[4].hi = 4.0f;   s_p_hold[4].step = 0.1f;    s_p_hold[4].decimals = 1; s_p_hold[4].macro = "YAW_HOLD_PID_OLIMIT";
        s_p_hold[5].name = "dz";   s_p_hold[5].pval = chassis_get_yaw_deadzone_ptr();
        s_p_hold[5].lo = 0.0f; s_p_hold[5].hi = 10.0f;  s_p_hold[5].step = 0.1f;    s_p_hold[5].decimals = 1; s_p_hold[5].macro = "YAW_HOLD_DEADZONE";
    }

    fill_group(&s_groups[0], "YawRate", "tgt_wz", "act_wz", s_p_rate, 5,
               "wz", &s_test_wz, -4.0f, 4.0f, 0.10f, 2,
               rate_select, rate_run, rate_test_change, rate_sample);
    fill_group(&s_groups[1], "YawHold", "tgt_deg", "act_deg", s_p_hold, 6,
               "yaw", &s_test_yaw, -180.0f, 180.0f, 5.0f, 1,
               hold_select, hold_run, hold_test_change, hold_sample);

    pid_link_init(s_groups, 2, tune_get_enabled, "#R mecanum pid tuner v2 (YAW only), send '?'");
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
odometry.h - 全局里程计头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _ODOMETRY_H_
#define _ODOMETRY_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define ODOM_UPDATE_PERIOD_MS   (5)         
#define ODOM_DT                 (0.005f)    

#define ODOM_FIELD_SIZE_X_DEFAULT (7.0f)    
#define ODOM_FIELD_SIZE_Y_DEFAULT (3.0f)    

#define ODOM_START_OFFSET_X_DEFAULT (3.5f)  
#define ODOM_START_OFFSET_Y_DEFAULT (0.0f)  

typedef enum
{
    ODOM_CAL_IDLE = 0,      
    ODOM_CAL_MEASURING,     
    ODOM_CAL_DONE,          
} odom_cal_state_enum;

typedef struct
{
    float x;                
    float y;                
    float yaw_deg;          

    float field_size_x;     
    float field_size_y;     

    float start_offset_x;   
    float start_offset_y;   
} odom_data_t;

void odometry_init(void);

void odometry_update(void);

void odometry_reset_to_start(void);

float odometry_get_x(void);         
float odometry_get_y(void);         
float odometry_get_yaw(void);       
const odom_data_t* odometry_get_data(void);  

void odometry_cal_start(void);

float odometry_cal_stop(void);

odom_cal_state_enum odometry_cal_get_state(void);

float odometry_cal_get_distance(void);

void odometry_set_field_size_x(float meters);
void odometry_set_field_size_y(float meters);
void odometry_set_start_offset_x(float meters);
void odometry_set_start_offset_y(float meters);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
odometry.c - 全局里程计实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "odometry.h"
#include "chassis_controller.h"
#include "encoder_manager.h"
#include "imu_manager.h"
#include <math.h>

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif
#define DEG2RAD(d)  ((d) * (M_PI / 180.0f))

static odom_data_t         g_odom;
static odom_cal_state_enum g_cal_state = ODOM_CAL_IDLE;
static float               g_cal_distance = 0.0f;   
static bool                g_initialized = false;

void odometry_init(void)
{
    g_odom.yaw_deg = 0.0f;

    g_odom.field_size_x   = ODOM_FIELD_SIZE_X_DEFAULT;
    g_odom.field_size_y   = ODOM_FIELD_SIZE_Y_DEFAULT;
    g_odom.start_offset_x = ODOM_START_OFFSET_X_DEFAULT;
    g_odom.start_offset_y = ODOM_START_OFFSET_Y_DEFAULT;

    g_odom.x = g_odom.start_offset_x;
    g_odom.y = g_odom.start_offset_y;

    g_cal_state    = ODOM_CAL_IDLE;
    g_cal_distance = 0.0f;
    g_initialized  = true;
}

void odometry_update(void)
{
    if (!g_initialized) return;

    float r = CHASSIS_WHEEL_DIAMETER / 2.0f;  
    float v_lf = encoder_motor_get_speed_rad_s(ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_FRONT)  * r;
    float v_lb = encoder_motor_get_speed_rad_s(ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_BACK)   * r;
    float v_rf = encoder_motor_get_speed_rad_s(ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_FRONT) * r;
    float v_rb = encoder_motor_get_speed_rad_s(ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_BACK)  * r;

    float vy_body = ( v_lf + v_rf + v_lb + v_rb) / 4.0f;   
    float vx_body = (-v_lf + v_rf + v_lb - v_rb) / 4.0f;   

    float yaw_deg = imu_get_yaw();
    float yaw_rad = DEG2RAD(yaw_deg);
    float cy = cosf(yaw_rad);
    float sy = sinf(yaw_rad);

    g_odom.yaw_deg = yaw_deg;

    float vx_global = vx_body * cy - vy_body * sy;
    float vy_global = vx_body * sy + vy_body * cy;

    g_odom.x += vx_global * ODOM_DT;
    g_odom.y += vy_global * ODOM_DT;

    if (g_cal_state == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING)
    {
        float v_body = sqrtf(vx_body * vx_body + vy_body * vy_body);
        g_cal_distance += v_body * ODOM_DT;
    }
}

void odometry_reset_to_start(void)
{
    g_odom.x = g_odom.start_offset_x;
    g_odom.y = g_odom.start_offset_y;
}

float odometry_get_x(void)   { return g_odom.x; }
float odometry_get_y(void)   { return g_odom.y; }
float odometry_get_yaw(void) { return g_odom.yaw_deg; }

const odom_data_t* odometry_get_data(void) { return &g_odom; }

void odometry_cal_start(void)
{
    g_cal_distance = 0.0f;
    g_cal_state    = ODOM_CAL_MEASURING;
}

float odometry_cal_stop(void)
{
    g_cal_state = ODOM_CAL_DONE;
    return g_cal_distance;
}

odom_cal_state_enum odometry_cal_get_state(void) { return g_cal_state; }

float odometry_cal_get_distance(void) { return g_cal_distance; }

void odometry_set_field_size_x(float meters)   { if (meters > 0.0f) g_odom.field_size_x = meters; }
void odometry_set_field_size_y(float meters)   { if (meters > 0.0f) g_odom.field_size_y = meters; }
void odometry_set_start_offset_x(float meters) { g_odom.start_offset_x = meters; }
void odometry_set_start_offset_y(float meters) { g_odom.start_offset_y = meters; }


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
vision_tracker.h - 视觉追踪头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _VISION_TRACKER_H_
#define _VISION_TRACKER_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define CAR_ZONE_X_MIN      3
#define CAR_ZONE_X_MAX      184
#define CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN      3
#define CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX      116
#define CAR_ZONE_CENTER_X   ((CAR_ZONE_X_MIN + CAR_ZONE_X_MAX) / 2)   
#define CAR_ZONE_CENTER_Y   ((CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN + CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX) / 2)   
#define CAR_ZONE_HALF_X     ((CAR_ZONE_X_MAX - CAR_ZONE_X_MIN) / 2)   
#define CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y     ((CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX - CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN) / 2)   

typedef enum
{
    CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE = 0,    
    CONTROL_MODE_VISION = 1,    
} control_mode_enum;

#define VISION_KP_X             (-0.040f)  

#define VISION_KP_Y             (0.040f)

#define VISION_MAX_SPEED        (1.30f)

#define VISION_MIN_SPEED        (0.70f)

#define VISION_ERR_NEAR     (10.0f)   
#define VISION_ERR_FAR      (30.0f)   

#define WALL_RATIO      (1.0f)                                   
#define WALL_SAFE_X     ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_X * 55 / 100))    
#define WALL_FULL_X     ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_X * 90 / 100))    
#define WALL_SAFE_Y     ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y * 55 / 100))    
#define WALL_FULL_Y     ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y * 90 / 100))    

#define SEARCH_WALL_RATIO   (1.0f)                                   
#define SEARCH_WALL_SAFE_X  ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_X * 65 / 100))    
#define SEARCH_WALL_FULL_X  ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_X * 95 / 100))    
#define SEARCH_WALL_SAFE_Y  ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y * 65 / 100))    
#define SEARCH_WALL_FULL_Y  ((float)(CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y * 95 / 100))    

#define VISION_ACCEL_LIMIT  (3.0f)

#define VISION_ESTOP_DECEL      (1.0f)

#define VISION_ESTOP_HOLD_TICKS (40u)

#define SEARCH_ACCEL_LIMIT  (1.5f)

#define SEARCH_REALIGN_DECEL  (4.0f)

#define SEARCH_RUN_DEFAULT_ON   (1)      
#define SEARCH_AXIS_DEFAULT     (1)      
#define SEARCH_SPEED_DEFAULT    (1.30f)  
#define SEARCH_MIN_SPEED_DEFAULT (0.80f) 
#define SEARCH_POS_KP           (3.0f)   
#define SEARCH_REACH_TOL        (0.15f)  
#define SEARCH_WALL_MARGIN      (0.20f)  
#define SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL        (0.15f)  

#define SEARCH_RESCUE_DEPTH     (0.20f)  
#define SEARCH_RESCUE_MIN       (0.15f)  

#define SEARCH_RESCUE_RELATCH_SAFEZONE  (1)

extern volatile uint8 g_nrf_estop_flag;

void vision_tracker_init(void);

void vision_tracker_update(void);

void vision_tracker_set_mode(control_mode_enum mode);

control_mode_enum vision_tracker_get_mode(void);

float vision_tracker_get_max_speed(void);

void vision_tracker_set_max_speed(float speed);

float vision_tracker_get_min_speed(void);

void vision_tracker_set_min_speed(float speed);

void  vision_search_set_enabled(uint8 en);
uint8 vision_search_get_enabled(void);

void  vision_search_set_axis(uint8 axis);
uint8 vision_search_get_axis(void);

void  vision_search_set_speed(float speed);
float vision_search_get_speed(void);

void  vision_search_set_min_speed(float speed);
float vision_search_get_min_speed(void);

void  vision_tracker_set_accel(float accel);
float vision_tracker_get_accel(void);

void  vision_search_set_accel(float accel);
float vision_search_get_accel(void);

uint8 vision_tracker_is_searching(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
vision_tracker.c - 视觉追踪实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "vision_tracker.h"
#include "chassis_controller.h"
#include "drone_uart_rx_v2.h"      
#include "odometry.h"           
#include <math.h>               

#define p_vision_cmd    (drone_uart_rx_get_vision_cmd())
#define p_car_error     (drone_uart_rx_get_car_error())

static control_mode_enum s_control_mode = CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE;

static float s_max_speed = VISION_MAX_SPEED;

static float s_min_speed = VISION_MIN_SPEED;

#define VISION_FILTER_ALPHA     (0.8f)  
static float s_filtered_vx = 0.0f;
static float s_filtered_vy = 0.0f;

static uint8 s_search_run = SEARCH_RUN_DEFAULT_ON;   
static uint8 s_search_axis = SEARCH_AXIS_DEFAULT;    
static float s_search_speed = SEARCH_SPEED_DEFAULT;  
static float s_search_min_speed = SEARCH_MIN_SPEED_DEFAULT; 
static uint8 s_sweep_to_high = 1;                    
static uint8 s_rescue_active = 0;                    
static uint8 s_search_realign = 1;                   
static uint8 s_prev_searching = 0;                   
static float s_scan_cross_target = 0.0f;             
static volatile uint8 s_searching_now = 0;           

static float s_accel_limit = VISION_ACCEL_LIMIT;     
static float s_search_accel = SEARCH_ACCEL_LIMIT;    
static float s_slew_vx = 0.0f;                        
static float s_slew_vy = 0.0f;                        
static uint16 s_estop_hold = 0u;                      

static float clamp_f(float val, float min_val, float max_val)
{
    if(val < min_val) return min_val;
    if(val > max_val) return max_val;
    return val;
}

static float wall_ramp(float d, float safe, float full)
{
    float t;
    if(d <= safe) return 0.0f;
    if(d >= full) return 1.0f;
    t = (d - safe) / (full - safe);
    return t;                       
}

static void slew_output_axes(float vx, float vy, float ax, float ay)
{
    if(ax > 0.0f)                                   
    {
        float step = ax * ODOM_DT;
        float dvx = vx - s_slew_vx;
        if(dvx >  step) dvx =  step;
        if(dvx < -step) dvx = -step;
        vx = s_slew_vx + dvx;
    }
    if(ay > 0.0f)                                   
    {
        float step = ay * ODOM_DT;
        float dvy = vy - s_slew_vy;
        if(dvy >  step) dvy =  step;
        if(dvy < -step) dvy = -step;
        vy = s_slew_vy + dvy;
    }
    s_slew_vx = vx;
    s_slew_vy = vy;
    chassis_set_velocity(vx, vy, 0.0f);
}

static void slew_output(float vx, float vy, float accel)
{
    slew_output_axes(vx, vy, accel, accel);
}

static void chassis_set_velocity_slew(float vx, float vy)
{
    slew_output(vx, vy, s_accel_limit);
}

static void chassis_set_velocity_slew_search(float vx, float vy)
{
    slew_output(vx, vy, s_search_accel);
}

static void chassis_set_velocity_realign(float vx, float vy)
{
    if(s_search_axis == 1) slew_output_axes(vx, vy, s_search_accel, SEARCH_REALIGN_DECEL); 
    else                   slew_output_axes(vx, vy, SEARCH_REALIGN_DECEL, s_search_accel); 
}

static void slew_reset(float vx, float vy)
{
    s_slew_vx = vx;
    s_slew_vy = vy;
}

static void search_wall_centering(float *wx, float *wy)
{
    *wx = 0.0f;
    *wy = 0.0f;

    if(!p_car_error->car_detected)
        return;                                  

    {
        float ox = p_car_error->car_error_x;     
        float oy = p_car_error->car_error_y;     
        float ax = (ox < 0.0f) ? -ox : ox;
        float ay = (oy < 0.0f) ? -oy : oy;
        float rx = wall_ramp(ax, SEARCH_WALL_SAFE_X, SEARCH_WALL_FULL_X);  
        float ry = wall_ramp(ay, SEARCH_WALL_SAFE_Y, SEARCH_WALL_FULL_Y);
        float vmax = SEARCH_WALL_RATIO * s_search_speed;                   
        
        float raw_wx = -ox * VISION_KP_X;
        float raw_wy =  oy * VISION_KP_Y;
        if(rx > 0.0f) *wx = (raw_wx >= 0.0f) ? (vmax * rx) : -(vmax * rx);
        if(ry > 0.0f) *wy = (raw_wy >= 0.0f) ? (vmax * ry) : -(vmax * ry);
    }
}

static float search_cross_relatch_target(float cf, float cp)
{
#if SEARCH_RESCUE_RELATCH_SAFEZONE
    float lo = SEARCH_WALL_MARGIN + SEARCH_RESCUE_DEPTH;
    float hi = cf - SEARCH_WALL_MARGIN - SEARCH_RESCUE_DEPTH;
    float ln = (cp > cf) ? hi : lo;                 
    return (hi > lo) ? clamp_f(ln, SEARCH_WALL_MARGIN, cf - SEARCH_WALL_MARGIN) : (cf * 0.5f);
#else
    (void)cp;
    return cf * 0.5f;                                
#endif
}

static void search_latch_cross_target(void)
{
    const odom_data_t *od = odometry_get_data();
    float fx = od->field_size_x;
    float fy = od->field_size_y;
    float x  = odometry_get_x();
    float y  = odometry_get_y();
    
    uint8 cross_out = (s_search_axis == 1) ? (x < 0.0f || x > fx)    
                                           : (y < 0.0f || y > fy);   
    float cf = (s_search_axis == 1) ? fx : fy;                          
    float cross_pos = (s_search_axis == 1) ? x : y;                     
    
    s_scan_cross_target = cross_out ? search_cross_relatch_target(cf, cross_pos) : cross_pos;
}

static void search_kill_reverse_scan_slew(void)
{
    const odom_data_t *od = odometry_get_data();
    float scan_pos = (s_search_axis == 1) ? odometry_get_y() : odometry_get_x();
    float scan_mid = (s_search_axis == 1) ? (od->field_size_y * 0.5f) : (od->field_size_x * 0.5f);
    float want = (scan_pos < scan_mid) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;   
    if(s_search_axis == 1)
    {
        if(s_slew_vy * want < 0.0f) s_slew_vy = 0.0f;    
    }
    else
    {
        if(s_slew_vx * want < 0.0f) s_slew_vx = 0.0f;    
    }
}

static void vision_search_update(void)
{
    const odom_data_t *od = odometry_get_data();
    float fx = od->field_size_x;
    float fy = od->field_size_y;
    float m  = SEARCH_WALL_MARGIN;
    float x  = odometry_get_x();
    float y  = odometry_get_y();
    float tx, ty;

    if(!s_rescue_active && (x < 0.0f || x > fx || y < 0.0f || y > fy))
    {
        s_rescue_active = 1;
        
        uint8 cross_out = (s_search_axis == 1) ? (x < 0.0f || x > fx)    
                                               : (y < 0.0f || y > fy);   
        if(cross_out)                   
        {
            
            s_scan_cross_target = search_cross_relatch_target((s_search_axis == 1) ? fx : fy,
                                                              (s_search_axis == 1) ? x  : y);
            s_search_realign = 1;
        }
        
        if(s_search_axis == 1)          
        {
            if(y > fy)      s_sweep_to_high = 0;    
            else if(y < 0)  s_sweep_to_high = 1;
        }
        else                            
        {
            if(x > fx)      s_sweep_to_high = 0;
            else if(x < 0)  s_sweep_to_high = 1;
        }
    }
    if(s_rescue_active)
    {
        
        if(x >= m && x <= fx - m && y >= m && y <= fy - m)
        {
            s_rescue_active = 0;
        }
        else
        {
            
            float dep    = SEARCH_RESCUE_DEPTH;
            float rfloor = (s_search_min_speed > SEARCH_RESCUE_MIN) ? s_search_min_speed : SEARCH_RESCUE_MIN;
            if(rfloor > s_search_speed) rfloor = s_search_speed;   

            float rx = x, ry = y;               
            uint8 pull_x = 0, pull_y = 0;
            float vx, vy;

            if(x < m)           { rx = m + dep;      pull_x = 1; }
            else if(x > fx - m) { rx = fx - m - dep; pull_x = 1; }
            if(pull_x) { float lo = m, hi = fx - m; rx = (hi > lo) ? clamp_f(rx, lo, hi) : fx * 0.5f; }

            if(y < m)           { ry = m + dep;      pull_y = 1; }
            else if(y > fy - m) { ry = fy - m - dep; pull_y = 1; }
            if(pull_y) { float lo = m, hi = fy - m; ry = (hi > lo) ? clamp_f(ry, lo, hi) : fy * 0.5f; }

            vx = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (rx - x), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);
            vy = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (ry - y), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);

            if(pull_x) { if(vx > 0.0f) { if(vx < rfloor) vx = rfloor; } else if(vx < 0.0f) { if(-vx < rfloor) vx = -rfloor; } }
            if(pull_y) { if(vy > 0.0f) { if(vy < rfloor) vy = rfloor; } else if(vy < 0.0f) { if(-vy < rfloor) vy = -rfloor; } }

            chassis_set_velocity_slew_search(vx, vy);  
            return;                     
        }
    }

    if(s_search_realign)
    {
        float cross_pos = (s_search_axis == 1) ? x : y;                      
        float cerr = cross_pos - s_scan_cross_target;
        if(cerr < 0.0f) cerr = -cerr;

        if(cerr > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL)
        {
            
            float avx, avy;
            if(s_search_axis == 1)  
            {
                avx = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (s_scan_cross_target - x), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);
                
                float scan_target = s_sweep_to_high ? (fy - m) : m;          
                avy = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (scan_target - y), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);
                if(cerr > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL * 2.0f)      avy = 0.0f;          
                else if(cerr > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL)        avy *= 0.5f;         
                
            }
            else                    
            {
                avy = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (s_scan_cross_target - y), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);
                float scan_target = s_sweep_to_high ? (fx - m) : m;
                avx = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (scan_target - x), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);
                if(cerr > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL * 2.0f)      avx = 0.0f;
                else if(cerr > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL)        avx *= 0.5f;
            }
            chassis_set_velocity_realign(avx, avy);
            return;                 
        }
        else
        {
            
            float scan_pos = (s_search_axis == 1) ? y : x;
            float scan_mid = (s_search_axis == 1) ? (fy * 0.5f) : (fx * 0.5f);
            s_sweep_to_high = (scan_pos < scan_mid) ? 1 : 0;
            s_search_realign = 0;   
        }
    }

    if(s_search_axis == 1)
    {
        
        float lo = m, hi = fy - m;
        if(hi < lo) hi = lo;                 
        tx = s_scan_cross_target;
        ty = s_sweep_to_high ? hi : lo;
        if(( s_sweep_to_high && y >= hi - SEARCH_REACH_TOL) ||
           (!s_sweep_to_high && y <= lo + SEARCH_REACH_TOL))
            s_sweep_to_high = !s_sweep_to_high;   
    }
    else
    {
        
        float lo = m, hi = fx - m;
        if(hi < lo) hi = lo;
        ty = s_scan_cross_target;
        tx = s_sweep_to_high ? hi : lo;
        if(( s_sweep_to_high && x >= hi - SEARCH_REACH_TOL) ||
           (!s_sweep_to_high && x <= lo + SEARCH_REACH_TOL))
            s_sweep_to_high = !s_sweep_to_high;
    }

    tx = clamp_f(tx, m, (fx - m > m) ? (fx - m) : m);
    ty = clamp_f(ty, m, (fy - m > m) ? (fy - m) : m);

    {
        float vx = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (tx - x), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);
        float vy = clamp_f(SEARCH_POS_KP * (ty - y), -s_search_speed, s_search_speed);

        float cross_err = (s_search_axis == 1) ? (tx - x) : (ty - y);
        float abs_cross_err = (cross_err < 0.0f) ? -cross_err : cross_err;
        uint8 off_line = (abs_cross_err > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL) ? 1u : 0u;

        if(abs_cross_err > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL * 2.0f)
        {
            
            if(s_search_axis == 1) vy = 0.0f; else vx = 0.0f;
        }
        else if(abs_cross_err > SEARCH_ALIGN_TOL)
        {
            
            if(s_search_axis == 1) vy *= 0.5f; else vx *= 0.5f;
        }

        {
            float floor = (s_search_min_speed < s_search_speed) ? s_search_min_speed : s_search_speed;
            if(floor > 0.0f)
            {
                if(s_search_axis == 1)          
                {
                    if(vy > 0.0f)      { if(vy < floor)  vy =  floor; }
                    else if(vy < 0.0f) { if(-vy < floor) vy = -floor; }
                }
                else                            
                {
                    if(vx > 0.0f)      { if(vx < floor)  vx =  floor; }
                    else if(vx < 0.0f) { if(-vx < floor) vx = -floor; }
                }
            }
        }

        {
            float wx, wy;
            float ceil = s_search_speed + SEARCH_WALL_RATIO * s_search_speed; 
            search_wall_centering(&wx, &wy);
            vx = clamp_f(vx + wx, -ceil, ceil);
            vy = clamp_f(vy + wy, -ceil, ceil);
        }

        if(off_line) chassis_set_velocity_realign(vx, vy);       
        else         chassis_set_velocity_slew_search(vx, vy);   
    }
}

void vision_tracker_init(void)
{
    s_control_mode = CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE;
    s_max_speed    = VISION_MAX_SPEED;
    s_min_speed    = VISION_MIN_SPEED;

    s_search_run    = SEARCH_RUN_DEFAULT_ON;
    s_search_axis   = SEARCH_AXIS_DEFAULT;
    s_search_speed  = SEARCH_SPEED_DEFAULT;
    s_search_min_speed = SEARCH_MIN_SPEED_DEFAULT;
    s_sweep_to_high = 1;
    s_rescue_active = 0;
    s_search_realign = 1;
    s_prev_searching = 0;
    s_scan_cross_target = 0.0f;

    s_accel_limit   = VISION_ACCEL_LIMIT;
    s_search_accel  = SEARCH_ACCEL_LIMIT;
    s_slew_vx       = 0.0f;
    s_slew_vy       = 0.0f;
    s_estop_hold    = 0u;
}

void vision_tracker_update(void)
{
    
    if(s_control_mode != CONTROL_MODE_VISION)
        return;

    if(g_nrf_estop_flag)
    {
        g_nrf_estop_flag = 0;
        s_estop_hold = VISION_ESTOP_HOLD_TICKS;
    }
    if(s_estop_hold > 0u)
    {
        s_estop_hold--;
        slew_output(0.0f, 0.0f, VISION_ESTOP_DECEL);   
        return;
    }

    if(drone_uart_rx_is_land_latched())
    {
        chassis_set_enabled(true);
        chassis_set_velocity_slew(0.0f, 0.0f);
        return;
    }

    if(!drone_uart_rx_is_takeoff_ready_latched())
    {
        chassis_set_enabled(true);
        chassis_set_velocity_slew(0.0f, 0.0f);
        return;
    }

    if(p_vision_cmd->valid)
    {
        
        uint8 was_searching = s_searching_now;
        s_searching_now = 0;   
        s_prev_searching = 0;  

        if(!chassis_is_enabled())
        {
            chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
            chassis_set_yaw_target(0.0f);
            chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
            chassis_set_enabled(true);
            was_searching = 1;       
            
            slew_reset(0.0f, 0.0f);
        }

        if(was_searching)
        {
            s_filtered_vx = 0.0f;
            s_filtered_vy = 0.0f;

            float dx = p_vision_cmd->vx;
            float dy = p_vision_cmd->vy;
            float target_vx =  dx * VISION_KP_X;  
            float target_vy = -dy * VISION_KP_Y;  

            if(target_vx * s_slew_vx < -0.01f) s_slew_vx = 0.0f;
            if(target_vy * s_slew_vy < -0.01f) s_slew_vy = 0.0f;
        }

        float dx = p_vision_cmd->vx; 
        float dy = p_vision_cmd->vy; 

        s_filtered_vx = VISION_FILTER_ALPHA * dx + (1.0f - VISION_FILTER_ALPHA) * s_filtered_vx;
        s_filtered_vy = VISION_FILTER_ALPHA * dy + (1.0f - VISION_FILTER_ALPHA) * s_filtered_vy;

        float pvx =  s_filtered_vx * VISION_KP_X;
        float pvy = -s_filtered_vy * VISION_KP_Y; 

        float err = sqrtf(s_filtered_vx * s_filtered_vx + s_filtered_vy * s_filtered_vy);

        float vx, vy;
        {
            
            float vmax    = WALL_RATIO * s_max_speed;    
            float ceil_xy = s_max_speed + vmax;          
            float rx = 0.0f, ry = 0.0f;                  
            float wx = 0.0f, wy = 0.0f;                  

            if(p_car_error->car_detected)
            {
                float ox = p_car_error->car_error_x;     
                float oy = p_car_error->car_error_y;     
                float ax = (ox < 0.0f) ? -ox : ox;       
                float ay = (oy < 0.0f) ? -oy : oy;       
                rx = wall_ramp(ax, WALL_SAFE_X, WALL_FULL_X);  
                ry = wall_ramp(ay, WALL_SAFE_Y, WALL_FULL_Y);
                
                float raw_wx = -ox * VISION_KP_X;
                float raw_wy =  oy * VISION_KP_Y;
                if(rx > 0.0f) wx = (raw_wx >= 0.0f) ? (vmax * rx) : -(vmax * rx);
                if(ry > 0.0f) wy = (raw_wy >= 0.0f) ? (vmax * ry) : -(vmax * ry);
            }

            float floor_base = (s_min_speed < s_max_speed) ? s_min_speed : s_max_speed;
            float fade       = ((1.0f - rx) < (1.0f - ry)) ? (1.0f - rx) : (1.0f - ry);
            float floor_v    = floor_base * fade;        

            float target;
            if(err >= VISION_ERR_FAR)            target = s_max_speed;               
            else if(err <= VISION_ERR_NEAR)      target = floor_v;                    
            else                                                                     
            {
                float t = (err - VISION_ERR_NEAR) / (VISION_ERR_FAR - VISION_ERR_NEAR);
                target = floor_v + t * (s_max_speed - floor_v);
            }

            float pmag = sqrtf(pvx * pvx + pvy * pvy);
            if(pmag > 1e-4f)
            {
                float k = target / pmag;
                vx = pvx * k;
                vy = pvy * k;
            }
            else
            {
                vx = 0.0f;                       
                vy = 0.0f;
            }

            vx += wx;
            vy += wy;
            vx = clamp_f(vx, -ceil_xy, ceil_xy);
            vy = clamp_f(vy, -ceil_xy, ceil_xy);
        }

        chassis_set_velocity_slew(vx, vy);
    }
    else
    {

        uint8 searching = p_vision_cmd->searching;

        if(searching && !s_prev_searching)
        {
            s_search_realign = 1;
            search_latch_cross_target();
            search_kill_reverse_scan_slew();   
        }
        s_prev_searching = searching;
        s_searching_now  = searching;   

        if(s_search_run && searching)
        {
            
            if(!chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
                chassis_set_yaw_target(0.0f);
                chassis_set_enabled(true);
            }
            vision_search_update();
        }
        else
        {
            
            chassis_set_velocity_slew(0.0f, 0.0f);
        }
    }
}

void vision_tracker_set_mode(control_mode_enum mode)
{
    if(s_control_mode == mode)
        return;

    chassis_stop();
    s_filtered_vx = 0.0f;
    s_filtered_vy = 0.0f;
    slew_reset(0.0f, 0.0f);     
    s_searching_now = 0;        
    g_nrf_estop_flag = 0;       
    s_estop_hold = 0u;          

    if(mode == CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE)
    {
        drone_uart_rx_clear_land_latch();
        drone_uart_rx_clear_takeoff_ready_latch();
    }

    s_control_mode = mode;
}

control_mode_enum vision_tracker_get_mode(void)
{
    return s_control_mode;
}

float vision_tracker_get_max_speed(void)
{
    return s_max_speed;
}

void vision_tracker_set_max_speed(float speed)
{
    if(speed < 0.0f)
        speed = 0.0f;
    s_max_speed = speed;    
}

float vision_tracker_get_min_speed(void)
{
    return s_min_speed;
}

void vision_tracker_set_min_speed(float speed)
{
    if(speed < 0.0f)
        speed = 0.0f;
    s_min_speed = speed;    
}

void  vision_search_set_enabled(uint8 en)   { s_search_run = en ? 1 : 0; }
uint8 vision_search_get_enabled(void)       { return s_search_run; }

void  vision_search_set_axis(uint8 axis)    { s_search_axis = axis ? 1 : 0; s_sweep_to_high = 1; s_search_realign = 1; search_latch_cross_target(); }
uint8 vision_search_get_axis(void)          { return s_search_axis; }

void  vision_search_set_speed(float speed)  { if(speed < 0.0f) speed = 0.0f; s_search_speed = speed; }
float vision_search_get_speed(void)         { return s_search_speed; }

void  vision_search_set_min_speed(float speed) { if(speed < 0.0f) speed = 0.0f; s_search_min_speed = speed; }
float vision_search_get_min_speed(void)        { return s_search_min_speed; }

void  vision_tracker_set_accel(float accel) { if(accel < 0.0f) accel = 0.0f; s_accel_limit = accel; }
float vision_tracker_get_accel(void)        { return s_accel_limit; }

void  vision_search_set_accel(float accel)  { if(accel < 0.0f) accel = 0.0f; s_search_accel = accel; }
float vision_search_get_accel(void)         { return s_search_accel; }

uint8 vision_tracker_is_searching(void)
{
    return s_searching_now;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
drone_uart_rx_v2.h - 无人机视觉串口接收头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _DRONE_UART_RX_H_
#define _DRONE_UART_RX_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

typedef struct
{
    float   vx;             
    float   vy;             
    uint8   valid;          
    uint8   land_cmd;       
    uint8   takeoff_ready;  
    uint8   beacon_count;   
    uint8   searching;      
    uint8   reserved[3];    
} vision_cmd_t;             

typedef struct
{
    float   car_error_x;    
    float   car_error_y;    
    uint8   car_detected;   
    uint8   reserved[3];    
} car_error_t;              

#define DRONE_RX_UART       UART_2
#define DRONE_RX_BAUD       115200
#define DRONE_RX_RX_PIN     UART2_RX_P10_0   
#define DRONE_RX_TX_PIN     UART2_TX_P10_1   

#define DRONE_RX_TIMEOUT_MS  200u   

void drone_uart_rx_init(void);

void drone_uart_rx_byte(void);

const vision_cmd_t *drone_uart_rx_get_vision_cmd(void);

const car_error_t *drone_uart_rx_get_car_error(void);

uint32 drone_uart_rx_get_frame_count(void);

uint32 drone_uart_rx_get_fps(void);

void drone_uart_rx_tick(void);

#define LAND_CONFIRM_FRAMES  (5u)   

uint8 drone_uart_rx_is_land_latched(void);

void drone_uart_rx_clear_land_latch(void);

#define TAKEOFF_CONFIRM_FRAMES  (5u)   

uint8 drone_uart_rx_is_takeoff_ready_latched(void);

void drone_uart_rx_clear_takeoff_ready_latch(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
drone_uart_rx_v2.c - 无人机视觉串口接收实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "drone_uart_rx_v2.h"
#include <string.h>

#define FRAME_HEADER1   (0xAAu)
#define FRAME_HEADER2   (0x55u)
#define FRAME_LEN       (25u)   
#define DATA_OFFSET     (2u)    
#define DATA_LEN        (22u)   

typedef enum
{
    RX_WAIT_H1 = 0,   
    RX_WAIT_H2,       
    RX_RECV_DATA,     
} rx_state_e;

static rx_state_e  s_state      = RX_WAIT_H1;
static uint8       s_buf[FRAME_LEN];
static uint8       s_idx        = 0u;           

static vision_cmd_t   s_vision_buf[2];
static car_error_t    s_car_buf[2];
static volatile uint8 s_active = 0u;   

static volatile uint32 s_last_rx_tick  = 0u;
static volatile uint32 s_tick          = 0u;
#define TIMEOUT_TICKS  (DRONE_RX_TIMEOUT_MS / 5u)   

static volatile uint32 s_frame_counter = 0u;

static volatile uint8 s_land_latched = 0u;   
static uint32         s_land_confirm = 0u;   

static volatile uint8 s_takeoff_ready_latched = 0u;   
static uint32         s_takeoff_confirm       = 0u;   
static uint32         s_takeoff_zero_confirm  = 0u;   

static volatile uint32 s_fps           = 0u;   
static volatile uint32 s_fps_count     = 0u;   
static          uint32 s_fps_last_tick = 0u;   

static uint8 calc_checksum(const uint8 *data, uint8 len)
{
    uint8 sum = 0u;
    for (uint8 i = 0u; i < len; i++)
        sum += data[i];
    return sum;
}

static uint8 float_sane(float v)
{
    if (v != v)                    return 0u;   
    if (v > 1.0e6f || v < -1.0e6f) return 0u;   
    return 1u;
}

static void parse_frame(void)
{
    
    if (calc_checksum(&s_buf[DATA_OFFSET], DATA_LEN) != s_buf[24])
        return;

    float vx, vy, car_error_x, car_error_y;
    memcpy(&vx,          &s_buf[2],  sizeof(float));   
    memcpy(&vy,          &s_buf[6],  sizeof(float));   
    memcpy(&car_error_x, &s_buf[15], sizeof(float));   
    memcpy(&car_error_y, &s_buf[19], sizeof(float));   

    if (!float_sane(vx) || !float_sane(vy) ||
        !float_sane(car_error_x) || !float_sane(car_error_y))
        return;

    uint8 w = (uint8)(s_active ^ 1u);

    s_vision_buf[w].vx             = vx;
    s_vision_buf[w].vy             = vy;
    s_vision_buf[w].valid          = s_buf[10];  
    s_vision_buf[w].land_cmd       = s_buf[11];  
    s_vision_buf[w].takeoff_ready  = s_buf[12];  
    s_vision_buf[w].beacon_count   = s_buf[13];  
    s_vision_buf[w].searching      = s_buf[14];  

    s_car_buf[w].car_error_x  = car_error_x;
    s_car_buf[w].car_error_y  = car_error_y;
    s_car_buf[w].car_detected = s_buf[23];       

    s_active = w;   

    if (s_buf[11])   
    {
        if (++s_land_confirm >= LAND_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
            s_land_latched = 1u;   
    }
    else
    {
        s_land_confirm = 0u;
    }

    if (s_buf[12])   
    {
        s_takeoff_zero_confirm = 0u;  
        if (++s_takeoff_confirm >= TAKEOFF_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
            s_takeoff_ready_latched = 1u;   
    }
    else   
    {
        s_takeoff_confirm = 0u;  
        
        if (s_takeoff_ready_latched)
        {
            if (++s_takeoff_zero_confirm >= TAKEOFF_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
            {
                s_takeoff_ready_latched = 0u;
                s_takeoff_zero_confirm  = 0u;
            }
        }
    }

    s_last_rx_tick = s_tick;
    s_frame_counter++;
    s_fps_count++;
}

void drone_uart_rx_init(void)
{
    memset(s_vision_buf, 0, sizeof(s_vision_buf));
    memset(s_car_buf, 0, sizeof(s_car_buf));
    s_active        = 0u;
    s_state         = RX_WAIT_H1;
    s_idx           = 0u;
    s_tick          = 0u;
    s_last_rx_tick  = 0u;
    s_frame_counter = 0u;
    s_fps           = 0u;
    s_fps_count     = 0u;
    s_fps_last_tick = 0u;
    s_land_latched  = 0u;   
    s_land_confirm  = 0u;
    s_takeoff_ready_latched = 0u;   
    s_takeoff_confirm       = 0u;
    s_takeoff_zero_confirm  = 0u;

    uart_init(DRONE_RX_UART, DRONE_RX_BAUD, DRONE_RX_TX_PIN, DRONE_RX_RX_PIN);

    uart_rx_interrupt(DRONE_RX_UART, 1);

    printf("[DRONE_RX] UART2 vision rx init OK (25-byte protocol, P10_0 RX / P10_1 TX)\r\n");
}

void drone_uart_rx_byte(void)
{
    
    uint8 byte = (uint8)Cy_SCB_ReadRxFifo(get_scb_module(DRONE_RX_UART));

    switch (s_state)
    {
        case RX_WAIT_H1:
            if (byte == FRAME_HEADER1)
            {
                s_buf[0] = byte;
                s_idx    = 1u;
                s_state  = RX_WAIT_H2;
            }
            break;

        case RX_WAIT_H2:
            if (byte == FRAME_HEADER2)
            {
                s_buf[1] = byte;
                s_idx    = 2u;
                s_state  = RX_RECV_DATA;
            }
            else if (byte == FRAME_HEADER1)
            {
                
                s_buf[0] = byte;
                s_idx    = 1u;
            }
            else
            {
                s_state = RX_WAIT_H1;
            }
            break;

        case RX_RECV_DATA:
            s_buf[s_idx++] = byte;
            if (s_idx >= FRAME_LEN)
            {
                parse_frame();
                s_state = RX_WAIT_H1;
                s_idx   = 0u;
            }
            break;

        default:
            s_state = RX_WAIT_H1;
            break;
    }
}

const vision_cmd_t *drone_uart_rx_get_vision_cmd(void)
{
    return &s_vision_buf[s_active];   
}

const car_error_t *drone_uart_rx_get_car_error(void)
{
    return &s_car_buf[s_active];
}

uint32 drone_uart_rx_get_frame_count(void)
{
    return s_frame_counter;
}

uint32 drone_uart_rx_get_fps(void)
{
    return s_fps;
}

void drone_uart_rx_tick(void)
{
    s_tick++;

    if ((s_tick - s_fps_last_tick) >= 200u)
    {
        s_fps           = s_fps_count;
        s_fps_count     = 0u;
        s_fps_last_tick = s_tick;
    }

    if ((s_tick - s_last_rx_tick) > TIMEOUT_TICKS)
    {
        
        s_vision_buf[s_active].valid      = 0u;
        s_vision_buf[s_active].searching  = 1u;
        
        s_car_buf[s_active].car_detected  = 0u;
    }
}

uint8 drone_uart_rx_is_land_latched(void)
{
    return s_land_latched;
}

void drone_uart_rx_clear_land_latch(void)
{
    
    s_land_latched = 0u;
    s_land_confirm = 0u;
}

uint8 drone_uart_rx_is_takeoff_ready_latched(void)
{
    return s_takeoff_ready_latched;
}

void drone_uart_rx_clear_takeoff_ready_latch(void)
{
    
    s_takeoff_ready_latched = 0u;
    s_takeoff_confirm       = 0u;
    s_takeoff_zero_confirm  = 0u;
}

#define CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y     ((CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX - CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN) / 2)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "zf_device_mt9v03x.h"
#include "zf_device_wifi_spi.h"
#include "camera_vision.h"

#define VISION_HEARTBEAT_ENABLE   0
#if (VISION_HEARTBEAT_ENABLE)
    #define CAM_HB(ch)   uart_write_byte(UART_1, (uint8)(ch))
#else
    #define CAM_HB(ch)   ((void)0)
#endif

extern vision_cmd_t g_vision_cmd;       
extern uint8        g_image_fresh_flag; 
extern uint8        g_m7_1_status;      

static uint8 s_image_copy[MT9V03X_H][MT9V03X_W];

static uint8 s_binary_image[MT9V03X_H][MT9V03X_W];

static uint8 s_edge_image[MT9V03X_H][MT9V03X_W];

static float s_binarize_threshold = VISION_BINARIZE_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT;

static float s_edge_threshold = VISION_EDGE_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT;

static float s_car_fill_max   = VISION_CAR_FILL_MAX;

static float s_car_fill_min   = VISION_CAR_FILL_MIN;

static float s_car_area_min   = VISION_CAR_AREA_MIN;

static float s_car_area_max   = VISION_CAR_AREA_MAX;

static float s_beacon_min_area  = VISION_BEACON_MIN_AREA;

static float s_beacon_max_area  = VISION_BEACON_MAX_AREA;

static float s_beacon_fill_min  = VISION_BEACON_FILL_MIN;

static float s_beacon_fill_max  = VISION_BEACON_FILL_MAX;

static float s_beacon_aspect_min = VISION_BEACON_ASPECT_MIN;

static float s_beacon_aspect_max = VISION_BEACON_ASPECT_MAX;

static float s_car_perim_min  = VISION_CAR_PERIM_MIN;

static float s_car_perim_max  = VISION_CAR_PERIM_MAX;

static float s_car_run_min    = VISION_CAR_RUN_MIN;

static float s_car_bbox_exclude_margin = VISION_CAR_BBOX_EXCLUDE_MARGIN;

static float s_beacon_min_area_near = VISION_BEACON_MIN_AREA_NEAR;

#define BEACON_HISTORY_RADIUS  5   
static int s_last_beacon_cx = -1; 
static int s_last_beacon_cy = -1; 
static int s_last_beacon_xmin = -1; 
static int s_last_beacon_xmax = -1;
static int s_last_beacon_ymin = -1;
static int s_last_beacon_ymax = -1;

static int   s_last_car_cx    = -1; 
static int   s_last_car_cy    = -1; 
static int   s_last_car_xmin  = -1; 
static int   s_last_car_xmax  = -1;
static int   s_last_car_ymin  = -1;
static int   s_last_car_ymax  = -1;

static const uint8 s_font5x7[][5] =
{
     {0x3E, 0x51, 0x49, 0x45, 0x3E},
     {0x00, 0x42, 0x7F, 0x40, 0x00},
     {0x42, 0x61, 0x51, 0x49, 0x46},
     {0x21, 0x41, 0x45, 0x4B, 0x31},
     {0x18, 0x14, 0x12, 0x7F, 0x10},
     {0x27, 0x45, 0x45, 0x45, 0x39},
     {0x3C, 0x4A, 0x49, 0x49, 0x30},
     {0x01, 0x71, 0x09, 0x05, 0x03},
     {0x36, 0x49, 0x49, 0x49, 0x36},
     {0x06, 0x49, 0x49, 0x29, 0x1E},
     {0x00, 0x36, 0x36, 0x00, 0x00},
     {0x63, 0x14, 0x08, 0x14, 0x63},
     {0x07, 0x08, 0x70, 0x08, 0x07},
     {0x08, 0x08, 0x3E, 0x08, 0x08},
     {0x08, 0x08, 0x08, 0x08, 0x08},
     {0x7E, 0x09, 0x09, 0x09, 0x7E},
     {0x7F, 0x09, 0x09, 0x09, 0x01},
     {0x7F, 0x09, 0x19, 0x29, 0x46},
     {0x7F, 0x09, 0x09, 0x09, 0x06},
     {0x3F, 0x40, 0x40, 0x40, 0x3F},
     {0x7F, 0x08, 0x08, 0x08, 0x7F},
     {0x3E, 0x41, 0x41, 0x41, 0x22},
     {0x61, 0x51, 0x49, 0x45, 0x43},   
};

static int font_index(char c)
{
    if(c >= '0' && c <= '9') return c - '0';
    if(c == ':') return 10;
    if(c == 'X') return 11;
    if(c == 'Y') return 12;
    if(c == '+') return 13;
    if(c == '-') return 14;
    if(c == 'A') return 15;
    if(c == 'F') return 16;
    if(c == 'R') return 17;
    if(c == 'P') return 18;
    if(c == 'U') return 19;
    if(c == 'H') return 20;
    if(c == 'C') return 21;
    if(c == 'Z') return 22;
    return -1;
}

static void draw_pixel(int r, int c, uint8 val)
{
    if(r >= 0 && r < MT9V03X_H && c >= 0 && c < MT9V03X_W)
        s_image_copy[r][c] = val;
}

static void draw_hline(int r, int c1, int c2, uint8 val)
{
    int c;
    if(c1 < 0) c1 = 0;
    if(c2 > MT9V03X_W - 1) c2 = MT9V03X_W - 1;
    for(c = c1; c <= c2; c++)             
        draw_pixel(r, c, val);
}

static void draw_vline(int c, int r1, int r2, uint8 val)
{
    int r;
    if(r1 < 0) r1 = 0;
    if(r2 > MT9V03X_H - 1) r2 = MT9V03X_H - 1;
    for(r = r1; r <= r2; r++)
        draw_pixel(r, c, val);
}

static void draw_rect(int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, uint8 val)
{
    draw_hline(y1, x1, x2, val);
    draw_hline(y2, x1, x2, val);
    draw_vline(x1, y1, y2, val);
    draw_vline(x2, y1, y2, val);
}

static void draw_cross(int cx, int cy, int r, uint8 val)
{
    draw_hline(cy, cx - r, cx + r, val);
    draw_vline(cx, cy - r, cy + r, val);
}

static void draw_char(int row, int col, char c, uint8 val)
{
    int idx = font_index(c);
    int x, y;
    if(idx < 0) return;
    for(x = 0; x < 5; x++)
    {
        uint8 col_data = s_font5x7[idx][x];
        for(y = 0; y < 7; y++)
        {
            if(col_data & (1 << y))
                draw_pixel(row + y, col + x, val);
        }
    }
}

static void draw_string(int row, int col, const char *str, uint8 val)
{
    while(*str)
    {
        draw_char(row, col, *str, val);
        col += 6;
        str++;
    }
}

static void compute_edge_image(uint8 thresh)
{
    int r, c;

    for(c = 0; c < MT9V03X_W; c++)
    {
        s_edge_image[0][c]              = 0;
        s_edge_image[MT9V03X_H - 1][c] = 0;
    }
    
    for(r = 0; r < MT9V03X_H; r++)
    {
        s_edge_image[r][0]              = 0;
        s_edge_image[r][MT9V03X_W - 1] = 0;
    }

    for(r = 1; r < MT9V03X_H - 1; r++)
    {
        for(c = 1; c < MT9V03X_W - 1; c++)
        {
            int gx  = (int)s_image_copy[r][c + 1] - (int)s_image_copy[r][c - 1];
            int gy  = (int)s_image_copy[r + 1][c] - (int)s_image_copy[r - 1][c];
            int mag = (gx < 0 ? -gx : gx) + (gy < 0 ? -gy : gy);
            s_edge_image[r][c] = (mag >= (int)thresh) ? 255 : 0;
        }
    }
}

typedef struct
{
    int x_min, x_max;   
    int y_min, y_max;   
    int cx, cy;          
    int area;            
    int score;           
    uint8 pass_flags;    
    uint8 valid;         
} beacon_t;

typedef struct
{
    beacon_t car;           
    beacon_t target;        
    uint8    beacon_count;  
    uint8    target_real;   
} vision_result_t;

#define MAX_LABELS  64

static uint8 s_label_parent[MAX_LABELS];

static uint8 uf_find(uint8 x)
{
    while(s_label_parent[x] != x)
    {
        s_label_parent[x] = s_label_parent[s_label_parent[x]]; 
        x = s_label_parent[x];
    }
    return x;
}

static void uf_union(uint8 a, uint8 b)
{
    uint8 ra = uf_find(a);
    uint8 rb = uf_find(b);
    if(ra != rb)
        s_label_parent[ra] = rb;
}

typedef struct
{
    uint8 c_start; 
    uint8 c_end;   
    uint8 label;   
} run_t;

#define MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW  94   

static run_t s_runs_prev[MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW]; 
static run_t s_runs_curr[MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW]; 

typedef struct
{
    int x_min, x_max, y_min, y_max;
    int area;
    int run_count; 
    uint8 used;
} label_stat_t;

static label_stat_t s_stats[MAX_LABELS];

typedef enum
{
    FIND_CAR_ONLY    = 0,  
    FIND_BEACON_ONLY = 1,  
} find_mode_e;

static void find_targets(const uint8 (*img)[MT9V03X_W], vision_result_t *vr, find_mode_e mode, uint8 skip_car_filter, int min_area_override, uint8 no_fallback)
{
    int r, c, i;
    int prev_cnt, curr_cnt;
    uint8 next_label;

    for(i = 0; i < MAX_LABELS; i++)
    {
        s_label_parent[i]      = (uint8)i;
        s_stats[i].used        = 0;
        s_stats[i].area        = 0;
        s_stats[i].run_count   = 0;
        s_stats[i].x_min       = MT9V03X_W;
        s_stats[i].x_max  = 0;
        s_stats[i].y_min  = MT9V03X_H;
        s_stats[i].y_max  = 0;
    }

    next_label = 1; 
    prev_cnt   = 0;

    for(r = 0; r < MT9V03X_H; r++)
    {
        
        curr_cnt = 0;
        c = 0;
        while(c < MT9V03X_W && curr_cnt < MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW)
        {
            
            while(c < MT9V03X_W && img[r][c] == 0) c++;
            if(c >= MT9V03X_W) break;

            s_runs_curr[curr_cnt].c_start = (uint8)c;
            while(c < MT9V03X_W && img[r][c] != 0) c++;
            s_runs_curr[curr_cnt].c_end   = (uint8)(c - 1);
            s_runs_curr[curr_cnt].label   = 0; 
            curr_cnt++;
        }

        for(i = 0; i < curr_cnt; i++)
        {
            int j;
            uint8 assigned = 0;
            for(j = 0; j < prev_cnt; j++)
            {
                
                if(s_runs_curr[i].c_start <= s_runs_prev[j].c_end &&
                   s_runs_curr[i].c_end   >= s_runs_prev[j].c_start)
                {
                    if(!assigned)
                    {
                        s_runs_curr[i].label = s_runs_prev[j].label;
                        assigned = 1;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        
                        uf_union(s_runs_curr[i].label, s_runs_prev[j].label);
                    }
                }
            }
            
            if(!assigned)
            {
                if(next_label < MAX_LABELS)
                    s_runs_curr[i].label = next_label++;
                else
                    s_runs_curr[i].label = MAX_LABELS - 1; 
            }
        }

        for(i = 0; i < curr_cnt; i++)
        {
            uint8 root = uf_find(s_runs_curr[i].label);
            int   area = s_runs_curr[i].c_end - s_runs_curr[i].c_start + 1;
            s_stats[root].used = 1;
            s_stats[root].area += area;
            s_stats[root].run_count++;  
            if(s_runs_curr[i].c_start < s_stats[root].x_min) s_stats[root].x_min = s_runs_curr[i].c_start;
            if(s_runs_curr[i].c_end   > s_stats[root].x_max) s_stats[root].x_max = s_runs_curr[i].c_end;
            if(r < s_stats[root].y_min) s_stats[root].y_min = r;
            if(r > s_stats[root].y_max) s_stats[root].y_max = r;
        }

        {
            run_t *tmp_ptr;
            int    tmp_cnt;
            
            tmp_cnt = prev_cnt;
            prev_cnt = curr_cnt;
            curr_cnt = tmp_cnt;
            for(i = 0; i < prev_cnt; i++)
                s_runs_prev[i] = s_runs_curr[i];
            (void)tmp_ptr; 
        }
    }

    for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
    {
        uint8 root = uf_find((uint8)i);
        if(root != i && s_stats[i].used)
        {
            s_stats[root].used       = 1;
            s_stats[root].area       += s_stats[i].area;
            s_stats[root].run_count  += s_stats[i].run_count;
            if(s_stats[i].x_min < s_stats[root].x_min) s_stats[root].x_min = s_stats[i].x_min;
            if(s_stats[i].x_max > s_stats[root].x_max) s_stats[root].x_max = s_stats[i].x_max;
            if(s_stats[i].y_min < s_stats[root].y_min) s_stats[root].y_min = s_stats[i].y_min;
            if(s_stats[i].y_max > s_stats[root].y_max) s_stats[root].y_max = s_stats[i].y_max;
            s_stats[i].used = 0; 
        }
    }

    if(mode == FIND_CAR_ONLY)
    {

        int best_score = -1;
        int best_dist2 = 0x7FFFFFFF;
        
        int ref_cx = (s_last_car_cx >= 0) ? s_last_car_cx : (MT9V03X_W / 2);
        int ref_cy = (s_last_car_cx >= 0) ? s_last_car_cy : (MT9V03X_H / 2);

        for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
        {
            int   cx, cy, dx, dy, dist2, score;
            int   w, h, bbox_area, perimeter, height_span;
            float fill_ratio, perim_ratio, avg_runs;

            if(!s_stats[i].used) continue;

            w = s_stats[i].x_max - s_stats[i].x_min + 1;
            h = s_stats[i].y_max - s_stats[i].y_min + 1;
            bbox_area = w * h;
            if(bbox_area <= 0) continue;

            cx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            cy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;

            if(cx < CAR_ZONE_X_MIN || cx > CAR_ZONE_X_MAX ||
               cy < CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN || cy > CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX)
                continue;

            if(vr->target.valid)
            {
                int margin = VISION_BEACON_BBOX_EXCLUDE_MARGIN;
                if(cx >= vr->target.x_min - margin && cx <= vr->target.x_max + margin &&
                   cy >= vr->target.y_min - margin && cy <= vr->target.y_max + margin)
                    continue;
            }

            if(s_stats[i].area < (int)s_car_area_min ||
               s_stats[i].area > (int)s_car_area_max)
                continue;

            {
                int r   = VISION_CAR_CENTER_CHECK_R;
                int cx0 = cx - r, cx1 = cx + r;
                int cy0 = cy - r, cy1 = cy + r;
                int bx, by, white = 0, total = 0;
                if(cx0 < 0) cx0 = 0;
                if(cy0 < 0) cy0 = 0;
                if(cx1 >= MT9V03X_W) cx1 = MT9V03X_W - 1;
                if(cy1 >= MT9V03X_H) cy1 = MT9V03X_H - 1;
                for(by = cy0; by <= cy1; by++)
                    for(bx = cx0; bx <= cx1; bx++)
                    { total++; if(s_binary_image[by][bx]) white++; }
                if(total > 0 && (float)white / (float)total > VISION_CAR_CENTER_WHITE_RATIO)
                    continue;   
            }

            score = 0;
            {
                uint8 flags = 0;

                fill_ratio = (float)s_stats[i].area / (float)bbox_area;
                if(fill_ratio >= s_car_fill_min &&
                   fill_ratio <  s_car_fill_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 0); } 

                perimeter = 2 * (w + h);
                perim_ratio = (perimeter > 0) ? ((float)s_stats[i].area / (float)perimeter) : 0.0f;
                if(perim_ratio >= s_car_perim_min &&
                   perim_ratio <= s_car_perim_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 1); } 

                height_span = (s_stats[i].y_max - s_stats[i].y_min + 1);
                avg_runs = (height_span > 0) ? ((float)s_stats[i].run_count / (float)height_span) : 0.0f;
                if(avg_runs >= s_car_run_min)
                { score += 2; flags |= (1 << 2); } 

                if(s_last_car_cx >= 0)
                {
                    int hdx = cx - s_last_car_cx;
                    int hdy = cy - s_last_car_cy;
                    if(hdx * hdx + hdy * hdy <=
                       VISION_CAR_HISTORY_RADIUS * VISION_CAR_HISTORY_RADIUS)
                    { score += 2; flags |= (1 << 3); } 
                }

                dx = cx - ref_cx;
                dy = cy - ref_cy;
                dist2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;

                if(score > best_score || (score == best_score && dist2 < best_dist2))
                {
                    best_score         = score;
                    best_dist2         = dist2;
                    vr->car.x_min      = s_stats[i].x_min;
                    vr->car.x_max      = s_stats[i].x_max;
                    vr->car.y_min      = s_stats[i].y_min;
                    vr->car.y_max      = s_stats[i].y_max;
                    vr->car.cx         = cx;
                    vr->car.cy         = cy;
                    vr->car.area       = s_stats[i].area;
                    vr->car.score      = score;
                    vr->car.pass_flags = flags;
                    vr->car.valid      = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if(!vr->car.valid && s_last_car_cx >= 0)
        {
            vr->car.x_min = s_last_car_xmin;
            vr->car.x_max = s_last_car_xmax;
            vr->car.y_min = s_last_car_ymin;
            vr->car.y_max = s_last_car_ymax;
            vr->car.cx    = s_last_car_cx;
            vr->car.cy    = s_last_car_cy;
            vr->car.area       = 0;
            vr->car.score      = 0;
            vr->car.pass_flags = 0;
            vr->car.valid      = 1;
        }

        if(vr->car.valid && best_score >= 0)
        {
            s_last_car_cx   = vr->car.cx;
