            s_last_car_cy   = vr->car.cy;
            s_last_car_xmin = vr->car.x_min;
            s_last_car_xmax = vr->car.x_max;
            s_last_car_ymin = vr->car.y_min;
            s_last_car_ymax = vr->car.y_max;
        }
    }
    else 
    {

        int ref_cx = vr->car.valid ? vr->car.cx : (MT9V03X_W / 2);
        int ref_cy = vr->car.valid ? vr->car.cy : (MT9V03X_H / 2);
        int best_score  = -1;
        int best_dist2  = 0x7FFFFFFF;
        int found_any   = 0; 
        int min_dist2_to_car = 0x7FFFFFFF; 

        for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
        {
            int pcx, pcy, pdx, pdy, pd2, area_min;
            if(!s_stats[i].used) continue;
            pcx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            pcy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;
            if(!skip_car_filter && s_last_car_cx >= 0)
            {
                int margin = (int)s_car_bbox_exclude_margin;
                if(pcx >= s_last_car_xmin - margin && pcx <= s_last_car_xmax + margin &&
                   pcy >= s_last_car_ymin - margin && pcy <= s_last_car_ymax + margin)
                    continue;
            }
            area_min = (min_area_override >= 0) ? min_area_override : (int)s_beacon_min_area;
            if(s_stats[i].area < area_min || s_stats[i].area > (int)s_beacon_max_area)
                continue;
            pdx = pcx - ref_cx;
            pdy = pcy - ref_cy;
            pd2 = pdx * pdx + pdy * pdy;
            if(pd2 < min_dist2_to_car) min_dist2_to_car = pd2;
        }

        for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
        {
            int   cx, cy, dx, dy, dist2;
            int   w, h, bbox_area;
            float fill_ratio;
            int   score = 0;
            uint8 flags = 0;

            if(!s_stats[i].used) continue;

            cx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            cy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;

            if(!skip_car_filter && s_last_car_cx >= 0)
            {
                int margin = (int)s_car_bbox_exclude_margin;
                if(cx >= s_last_car_xmin - margin && cx <= s_last_car_xmax + margin &&
                   cy >= s_last_car_ymin - margin && cy <= s_last_car_ymax + margin)
                    continue;
            }

            {
                int area_min = (min_area_override >= 0) ? min_area_override : (int)s_beacon_min_area;
                if(s_stats[i].area < area_min ||
                   s_stats[i].area > (int)s_beacon_max_area)
                    continue;
            }

            found_any = 1; 

            w = s_stats[i].x_max - s_stats[i].x_min + 1;
            h = s_stats[i].y_max - s_stats[i].y_min + 1;
            bbox_area = w * h;
            if(bbox_area > 0)
            {
                fill_ratio = (float)s_stats[i].area / (float)bbox_area;
                if(fill_ratio >= s_beacon_fill_min &&
                   fill_ratio <= s_beacon_fill_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 0); } 
            }

            if(w > 0 && h > 0)
            {
                float aspect = (float)w / (float)h;
                if(aspect >= s_beacon_aspect_min && aspect <= s_beacon_aspect_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 1); } 
            }

            if(s_last_beacon_cx >= 0)
            {
                int hdx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2 - s_last_beacon_cx;
                int hdy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2 - s_last_beacon_cy;
                if(hdx * hdx + hdy * hdy <=
                   BEACON_HISTORY_RADIUS * BEACON_HISTORY_RADIUS)
                { score += 3; flags |= (1 << 2); } 
            }

            {
                int cdx = cx - ref_cx;
                int cdy = cy - ref_cy;
                int cd2 = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;
                if(cd2 == min_dist2_to_car)
                { score += 2; flags |= (1 << 3); } 
            }

            cx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            cy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;
            dx = cx - ref_cx;
            dy = cy - ref_cy;
            dist2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;

            {
                int margin = VISION_BEACON_ISOLATION_MARGIN;
                int ex1 = s_stats[i].x_min - margin;
                int ex2 = s_stats[i].x_max + margin;
                int ey1 = s_stats[i].y_min - margin;
                int ey2 = s_stats[i].y_max + margin;
                int j;
                for(j = 1; j < next_label; j++)
                {
                    if(j == i || !s_stats[j].used) continue;
                    if(s_stats[j].area <= VISION_BEACON_ISOLATION_AREA) continue;
                    
                    if(s_stats[j].x_max >= ex1 && s_stats[j].x_min <= ex2 &&
                       s_stats[j].y_max >= ey1 && s_stats[j].y_min <= ey2)
                    {
                        score -= 2;
                        break; 
                    }
                }
            }

            if(score >= 2)
                vr->beacon_count++;

            if(score > best_score || (score == best_score && dist2 < best_dist2))
            {
                best_score          = score;
                best_dist2          = dist2;
                vr->target.x_min    = s_stats[i].x_min;
                vr->target.x_max    = s_stats[i].x_max;
                vr->target.y_min    = s_stats[i].y_min;
                vr->target.y_max    = s_stats[i].y_max;
                vr->target.cx       = cx;
                vr->target.cy       = cy;
                vr->target.area     = s_stats[i].area;
                vr->target.score    = score;
                vr->target.pass_flags = flags;
                vr->target.valid    = 1;
            }
        }

        vr->target_real = (uint8)found_any;

        if(!found_any && s_last_beacon_cx >= 0 && !no_fallback)
        {
            vr->target.cx    = s_last_beacon_cx;
            vr->target.cy    = s_last_beacon_cy;
            vr->target.x_min = s_last_beacon_xmin;
            vr->target.x_max = s_last_beacon_xmax;
            vr->target.y_min = s_last_beacon_ymin;
            vr->target.y_max = s_last_beacon_ymax;
            vr->target.valid = 1;
            vr->target.score = 0;
        }

        if(vr->target.valid)
        {
            s_last_beacon_cx = vr->target.cx;
            s_last_beacon_cy = vr->target.cy;
            if(found_any)
            {
                s_last_beacon_xmin = vr->target.x_min;
                s_last_beacon_xmax = vr->target.x_max;
                s_last_beacon_ymin = vr->target.y_min;
                s_last_beacon_ymax = vr->target.y_max;
            }
        }
    }
}

static int int_to_str(int val, char *buf)
{
    int          len = 0;
    int          tmp;
    char         tmp_buf[12];    
    int          i;
    unsigned int uval;

    if(val >= 0)
    {
        buf[len++] = '+';
        uval = (unsigned int)val;
    }
    else
    {
        buf[len++] = '-';
        uval = (unsigned int)(-(val + 1)) + 1u;  
    }

    tmp = 0;
    if(uval == 0)
    {
        tmp_buf[tmp++] = '0';
    }
    else
    {
        while(uval > 0 && tmp < (int)sizeof(tmp_buf))
        {
            tmp_buf[tmp++] = (char)('0' + uval % 10);
            uval /= 10;
        }
    }

    for(i = tmp - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        buf[len++] = tmp_buf[i];

    buf[len] = '\0';
    return len;
}

static uint8 s_stomp_counter = 0;

typedef enum
{
    VS_INIT,           
    VS_FAR_TRACKING,   
    VS_NEAR_STOMPING,  
} vision_state_e;

static vision_state_e s_vision_state       = VS_INIT; 
static uint8          s_near_confirm_cnt   = 0;        
static uint8          s_stomp_detect_cnt   = 0;        

#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)

static uint8  s_search_active       = 0;      
static uint8  s_prev_display_class  = 0xFF;   
static uint8  s_flip_hist[VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N] = {0}; 
static uint8  s_flip_idx            = 0;      
static uint16 s_flip_sum            = 0;      
static uint16 s_no_beacon_frames    = 0;      

static uint8  s_realhit_hist[VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N] = {0};
static uint8  s_realhit_idx         = 0;
static uint16 s_realhit_sum         = 0;
#endif

uint8 camera_vision_init(void)
{
#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    
    while(wifi_spi_init(VISION_WIFI_SSID, VISION_WIFI_PASSWORD))
    {
        system_delay_ms(100);
    }

    if(1 != WIFI_SPI_AUTO_CONNECT)
    {
        while(wifi_spi_socket_connect(
            "TCP",
            VISION_TCP_IP,
            VISION_TCP_PORT,
            VISION_LOCAL_PORT))
        {
            system_delay_ms(100);
        }
    }
#endif 

    mt9v03x_init();

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    
    seekfree_assistant_interface_init(SEEKFREE_ASSISTANT_WIFI_SPI);

    seekfree_assistant_camera_information_config(
        SEEKFREE_ASSISTANT_MT9V03X,
        s_image_copy[0],
        MT9V03X_W,
        MT9V03X_H
    );
#endif 

    return 0;
}

uint8 camera_vision_update(void)
{
    if(!mt9v03x_finish_flag) return 0;   
    mt9v03x_finish_flag = 0;

    memcpy(s_image_copy[0], mt9v03x_image[0], MT9V03X_IMAGE_SIZE);

#if (VISION_HEARTBEAT_ENABLE)
    {
        static uint32 s_hb_frame = 0;
        if(((++s_hb_frame) & 0x3F) == 0)
        {
            char hb[12];
            uart_write_byte(UART_1, '\r');
            uart_write_byte(UART_1, '\n');
            int_to_str((int)s_hb_frame, hb);
            uart_write_string(UART_1, hb);
            uart_write_byte(UART_1, ':');
        }
    }
#endif
    CAM_HB('A');

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    seekfree_assistant_data_analysis();
    CAM_HB('P');   

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)

    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0])
    {
        s_edge_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[0];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1])
    {
        s_car_fill_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[1] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2])
    {
        s_car_area_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[2];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3])
    {
        s_car_area_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[3];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4])
    {
        s_car_perim_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[4] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5])
    {
        s_car_perim_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[5] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6])
    {
        s_car_fill_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[6] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7])
    {
        s_car_run_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[7] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7] = 0;
    }

#elif (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)

    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0])
    {
        s_binarize_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[0];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1])
    {
        s_beacon_min_area = seekfree_assistant_parameter[1];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2])
    {
        s_beacon_max_area = seekfree_assistant_parameter[2];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3])
    {
        s_beacon_fill_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[3] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4])
    {
        s_beacon_fill_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[4] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5])
    {
        s_beacon_aspect_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[5] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6])
    {
        s_beacon_aspect_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[6] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6] = 0;
    }

#else

    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0])
    {
        s_binarize_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[0];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1])
    {
        s_edge_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[1];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2])
    {
        s_car_fill_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[2] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3])
    {
        s_car_area_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[3];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4])
    {
        s_car_area_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[4];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5])
    {
        s_beacon_max_area = seekfree_assistant_parameter[5];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6])
    {
        s_car_perim_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[6] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7])
    {
        s_car_perim_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[7] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7] = 0;
    }
#endif 

#endif 

    {
        int r, c;
        uint8 thresh = (uint8)s_binarize_threshold;
        for(r = 0; r < MT9V03X_H; r++)
            for(c = 0; c < MT9V03X_W; c++)
                s_binary_image[r][c] = (s_image_copy[r][c] >= thresh) ? 255 : 0;
    }

    compute_edge_image((uint8)s_edge_threshold);

    {
        vision_result_t vr;
        
        memset(&vr, 0, sizeof(vr));

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        if(s_vision_state == VS_NEAR_STOMPING)
        {
            
            find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_binary_image, &vr, FIND_BEACON_ONLY, 1, (int)s_beacon_min_area_near, 1);
            find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_edge_image,   &vr, FIND_CAR_ONLY,    0, -1, 0);
        }
        else 
        {
            
            find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_edge_image, &vr, FIND_CAR_ONLY, 0, -1, 0);
            if(vr.car.valid)
                find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_binary_image, &vr, FIND_BEACON_ONLY, 0, -1, 0);
        }
#elif (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
        
        find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_edge_image, &vr, FIND_CAR_ONLY, 0, -1, 0);
#else 
        
        find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_binary_image, &vr, FIND_BEACON_ONLY, 1, -1, 0);
#endif

#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        {
            uint8 cls  = (s_vision_state == VS_NEAR_STOMPING) ? 1 : 0;   
            uint8 flip = (s_prev_display_class != 0xFF && cls != s_prev_display_class) ? 1 : 0;
            s_prev_display_class = cls;

            s_flip_sum -= s_flip_hist[s_flip_idx];
            s_flip_hist[s_flip_idx] = flip;
            s_flip_sum += flip;
            s_flip_idx = (uint8)((s_flip_idx + 1) % VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N);

            {
                uint8 real = vr.target_real ? 1 : 0;
                s_realhit_sum -= s_realhit_hist[s_realhit_idx];
                s_realhit_hist[s_realhit_idx] = real;
                s_realhit_sum += real;
                s_realhit_idx = (uint8)((s_realhit_idx + 1) % VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N);
            }

            if(vr.target.valid)
                s_no_beacon_frames = 0;
            else if(s_no_beacon_frames < 0xFFFF)
                s_no_beacon_frames++;

            if(!s_search_active)
            {
                
                if(s_flip_sum > VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_THRESHOLD ||
                   s_no_beacon_frames >= VISION_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_FRAMES ||
                   s_realhit_sum < VISION_SEARCH_REALHIT_ENTER)
                    s_search_active = 1;
            }
            else
            {
                
                if(s_realhit_sum >= VISION_SEARCH_REALHIT_EXIT)
                {
                    int _i;
                    s_search_active = 0;
                    
                    s_flip_sum = 0;
                    for(_i = 0; _i < VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N; _i++) s_flip_hist[_i] = 0;
                    s_no_beacon_frames = 0;
                }
            }
        }
#endif

        CAM_HB('F');

#if   (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
        
        memcpy(s_image_copy[0], s_edge_image[0], MT9V03X_IMAGE_SIZE);
#elif (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)
        
        memcpy(s_image_copy[0], s_binary_image[0], MT9V03X_IMAGE_SIZE);
#endif

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        {
            char state_ch = 'C';
            if(s_vision_state == VS_NEAR_STOMPING) state_ch = 'X';
            draw_char(2, 2, state_ch, 255);
        }
#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE)
        
        if(s_search_active) draw_char(2, MT9V03X_W - 7, 'Z', 255);
        
        {
            char jbuf[12];
            int_to_str((int)s_flip_sum, jbuf);
            draw_string(2, 12, (jbuf[0] == '+') ? jbuf + 1 : jbuf, 255);  
        }
        
        {
            char rbuf[12];
            int_to_str((int)s_realhit_sum, rbuf);
            draw_string(2, MT9V03X_W - 7 - 2 - 12, (rbuf[0] == '+') ? rbuf + 1 : rbuf, 255); 
        }
#endif
#endif

        if(vr.target.valid)
        {
            
            int ref_cx = vr.car.valid ? vr.car.cx : (MT9V03X_W / 2);
            int ref_cy = vr.car.valid ? vr.car.cy : (MT9V03X_H / 2);
            int dx = vr.target.cx - ref_cx;
            int dy = vr.target.cy - ref_cy;

            s_stomp_counter = 0;

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE != 2)
            if(vr.car.valid)
            {
                draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 1, vr.car.y_min - 1,
                          vr.car.x_max + 1, vr.car.y_max + 1, 255);
                draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 4, vr.car.y_min - 4,
                          vr.car.x_max + 4, vr.car.y_max + 4, 255);
#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
                
                {
                    char score_buf[8];
                    int  slen = 0;
                    int  text_row = vr.car.y_min - 9;
                    int  text_col = vr.car.x_min - 4;
                    if(text_row < 0) text_row = vr.car.y_max + 5;
                    if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;
                    score_buf[slen++] = '0' + (char)(vr.car.score);
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 0)) score_buf[slen++] = 'F';
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 1)) score_buf[slen++] = 'P';
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 2)) score_buf[slen++] = 'U';
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 3)) score_buf[slen++] = 'H';
                    score_buf[slen] = '\0';
                    draw_string(text_row, text_col, score_buf, 255);
                }
#endif
            }
#endif

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE != 1)
            draw_rect(vr.target.x_min - 2, vr.target.y_min - 2,
                      vr.target.x_max + 2, vr.target.y_max + 2, 255);
            draw_cross(vr.target.cx, vr.target.cy, 8, 255);

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)
            
            {
                char score_buf[8];
                int  slen = 0;
                int  text_row = vr.target.y_min - 9;
                int  text_col = vr.target.x_min - 2;
                if(text_row < 0) text_row = vr.target.y_max + 5;
                if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;
                score_buf[slen++] = '0' + (char)(vr.target.score < 0 ? 0 : vr.target.score);
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 0)) score_buf[slen++] = 'F';
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 1)) score_buf[slen++] = 'R';
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 2)) score_buf[slen++] = 'H';
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 3)) score_buf[slen++] = 'C';
                score_buf[slen] = '\0';
                draw_string(text_row, text_col, score_buf, 255);
            }
#endif

            {
                char str_x[12], str_y[12];
                char label[20];
                int  pos = 0;
                int  text_row, text_col;
                const int LABEL_MAX = (int)sizeof(label) - 1;    

                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = 'X';
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = ':';
                int_to_str(dx, str_x);
                { int k = 0; while(str_x[k] && pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = str_x[k++]; }
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = ' ';
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = 'Y';
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = ':';
                int_to_str(dy, str_y);
                { int k = 0; while(str_y[k] && pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = str_y[k++]; }
                label[pos] = '\0';

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)
                text_row = vr.target.y_min - 19;
#else
                text_row = vr.target.y_min - 10;
#endif
                text_col = vr.target.x_min - 2;
                if(text_row < 0)
                    text_row = vr.target.y_max + 4;
                if(text_col + (int)(pos * 6) > MT9V03X_W)
                    text_col = MT9V03X_W - pos * 6 - 1;
                if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;

                draw_string(text_row, text_col, label, 255);
            }
#endif

            g_vision_cmd.vx             = (float)dx;
            g_vision_cmd.vy             = (float)dy;
            g_vision_cmd.valid          = 1;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_stomped = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_count   = vr.beacon_count;
#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
            
            if(s_search_active) g_vision_cmd.valid = 0;
#endif
            SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(vision_cmd_t));
        }
        else if(vr.car.valid)
        {
            
#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE != 2)
            
            draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 1, vr.car.y_min - 1,
                      vr.car.x_max + 1, vr.car.y_max + 1, 255);
            draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 4, vr.car.y_min - 4,
                      vr.car.x_max + 4, vr.car.y_max + 4, 255);
#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
            
            {
                char score_buf[8];
                int  slen = 0;
                int  text_row = vr.car.y_min - 9;
                int  text_col = vr.car.x_min - 4;
                if(text_row < 0) text_row = vr.car.y_max + 5;
                if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;
                score_buf[slen++] = '0' + (char)(vr.car.score);
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 0)) score_buf[slen++] = 'F';
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 1)) score_buf[slen++] = 'P';
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 2)) score_buf[slen++] = 'U';
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 3)) score_buf[slen++] = 'H';
                score_buf[slen] = '\0';
                draw_string(text_row, text_col, score_buf, 255);
            }
#endif
#endif

            s_stomp_counter++;

            if(s_stomp_counter >= VISION_STOMP_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
            {
                
                s_stomp_counter = 0;
                g_vision_cmd.valid          = 0;
                g_vision_cmd.beacon_stomped = 1;
                g_vision_cmd.beacon_count   = 0;
                SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(vision_cmd_t));
                
                s_last_beacon_cx = -1;
                s_last_beacon_cy = -1;
                s_last_beacon_xmin = -1;
                s_last_beacon_xmax = -1;
                s_last_beacon_ymin = -1;
                s_last_beacon_ymax = -1;
            }
            else
            {
                
            }
        }
        else
        {
            
            s_stomp_counter = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.valid          = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_stomped = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_count   = 0;
            SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(vision_cmd_t));
        }

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
    {
        switch(s_vision_state)
        {
            case VS_INIT:
                
                if(vr.car.valid)
                    s_vision_state = VS_FAR_TRACKING;
                break;

            case VS_FAR_TRACKING:
                
                if(vr.target.valid && vr.target.area >= (int)s_beacon_min_area_near && vr.car.valid)
                {
                    int adx = vr.target.cx - vr.car.cx;
                    int ady = vr.target.cy - vr.car.cy;
                    if(adx < 0) adx = -adx;
                    if(ady < 0) ady = -ady;
                    if(adx < VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD && ady < VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD)
                    {
                        s_near_confirm_cnt++;
                        if(s_near_confirm_cnt >= VISION_NEAR_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
                        {
                            
                            s_vision_state     = VS_NEAR_STOMPING;
                            s_near_confirm_cnt = 0;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                        s_near_confirm_cnt = 0;
                }
                else
                    s_near_confirm_cnt = 0;
                break;

            case VS_NEAR_STOMPING:
                
                if(vr.target.valid && vr.target.area >= (int)s_beacon_min_area_near)
                {
                    s_stomp_detect_cnt = 0;
                    if(vr.car.valid)
                    {
                        int adx = vr.target.cx - vr.car.cx;
                        int ady = vr.target.cy - vr.car.cy;
                        if(adx < 0) adx = -adx;
                        if(ady < 0) ady = -ady;
                        if(adx >= VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD || ady >= VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD)
                            s_vision_state = VS_FAR_TRACKING;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    
                    s_stomp_detect_cnt++;
                    if(s_stomp_detect_cnt >= 3)
                    {
                        s_stomp_detect_cnt = 0;
                        s_vision_state     = VS_FAR_TRACKING;
                    }
                }
                break;

            default:
                break;
        }
    }
#endif 

    {
        extern car_error_t    g_car_error;    
        extern drone_target_t g_drone_target; 

        float center_x = (float)(MT9V03X_W / 2);
        float center_y = (float)(MT9V03X_H / 2);

        if(vr.car.valid) {
            g_car_error.car_error_x  = (float)vr.car.cx - center_x;  
            g_car_error.car_error_y  = (float)vr.car.cy - center_y;  
            g_car_error.car_detected = 1;
        } else {
            
            g_car_error.car_detected = 0;
        }

        int drone_skip_beacon = 0;
#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        drone_skip_beacon = s_search_active;
#endif
        if(!drone_skip_beacon && vr.target.valid) {
            g_drone_target.err_x = (float)vr.target.cx - center_x;      
            g_drone_target.err_y = (float)vr.target.cy - center_y;
            g_drone_target.valid = 1;
        } else if(!drone_skip_beacon && s_last_beacon_cx >= 0) {
            g_drone_target.err_x = (float)s_last_beacon_cx - center_x;  
            g_drone_target.err_y = (float)s_last_beacon_cy - center_y;
            g_drone_target.valid = 1;
        } else if(vr.car.valid) {
            g_drone_target.err_x = (float)vr.car.cx - center_x;         
            g_drone_target.err_y = (float)vr.car.cy - center_y;
            g_drone_target.valid = 1;
        } else {
            g_drone_target.valid = 0;
        }

        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_car_error,    sizeof(car_error_t));
        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_drone_target, sizeof(drone_target_t));
    }

    } 

#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
    
    {
        extern search_state_t g_search_state;
        g_search_state.searching = s_search_active;
        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_search_state, sizeof(search_state_t));
    }
#endif

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    CAM_HB('S');                        
    seekfree_assistant_camera_send();
    CAM_HB('D');                        
#endif 

    g_image_fresh_flag = 1;
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_image_fresh_flag, 1);

    return 1;   
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
remote_control.h - 遥控控制头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _REMOTE_CONTROL_H_
#define _REMOTE_CONTROL_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define USE_NRF_REMOTE

#ifdef USE_NRF_REMOTE
#define NRF_POS_GAIN        0.003f      
#define NRF_MAX_SPEED       1.5f        
#define NRF_DEADZONE        50          
#define NRF_TIMEOUT_TICKS   40          
#define NRF_CH_MID          1500        
#endif

#ifdef USE_NRF_REMOTE
#define RC_POS_GAIN         NRF_POS_GAIN
#define RC_MAX_SPEED        NRF_MAX_SPEED
#define RC_DEADZONE         NRF_DEADZONE
#define RC_TIMEOUT_TICKS    NRF_TIMEOUT_TICKS
#define RC_CH_MID           NRF_CH_MID
#endif

typedef struct {
    float   vx;             
    float   vy;             
    uint8   connected;      
    uint32  last_rx_tick;   
    
    uint32  nrf_irq_cnt;    
    uint32  nrf_parse_ok;   
    uint32  nrf_parse_fail; 
} remote_control_t;

extern remote_control_t g_remote_ctrl;

void remote_control_init(void);

void remote_control_update(void);

void remote_control_set_drive_enabled(uint8 en);

uint8 remote_control_is_connected(void);

#define ENABLE_VISION_CMD   0

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
remote_control.c - 遥控控制实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "remote_control.h"
#include "nrf24l01_driver.h"
#include "chassis_controller.h"

remote_control_t g_remote_ctrl = {0};   
static uint32    s_tick         = 0;    
static uint8     s_drive_en     = 0;    

static uint32 s_nrf_irq_cnt     = 0u;   
static uint32 s_nrf_parse_ok    = 0u;   
static uint32 s_nrf_parse_fail  = 0u;   
#define NRF_STATS_PRINT_INTERVAL  200u  

static void parse_and_update(void);

void remote_control_init(void)
{
#ifdef USE_NRF_REMOTE
    
    printf("Initializing NRF24L01 remote control...\r\n");
    if(nrf24l01_init() != 0)
    {
        printf("NRF24L01 Init Failed!\r\n");
        return;
    }
    printf("NRF24L01 Init OK\r\n");
#endif

    chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);

    g_remote_ctrl.vx           = 0.0f;
    g_remote_ctrl.vy           = 0.0f;
    g_remote_ctrl.connected    = 0;
    g_remote_ctrl.last_rx_tick = 0;

    s_drive_en = 0;

    printf("Remote control initialized!\r\n");
}

#ifdef USE_NRF_REMOTE

static void parse_and_update(void)
{
    
    if(g_nrf_rx_buf[0] != 0xAA ||
       g_nrf_rx_buf[1] != 0xAF ||
       g_nrf_rx_buf[2] != 0x03)
    {
        s_nrf_parse_fail++;
        
        return; 
    }

    s_nrf_parse_ok++;

    uint16 rol = ((uint16)g_nrf_rx_buf[8]  << 8) | g_nrf_rx_buf[9];   
    uint16 pit = ((uint16)g_nrf_rx_buf[10] << 8) | g_nrf_rx_buf[11];  

    int16 offset_vx = (int16)rol - RC_CH_MID;
    int16 offset_vy = (int16)pit - RC_CH_MID;

    if(offset_vx > -RC_DEADZONE && offset_vx < RC_DEADZONE) offset_vx = 0;
    if(offset_vy > -RC_DEADZONE && offset_vy < RC_DEADZONE) offset_vy = 0;

    g_remote_ctrl.vx = -RC_POS_GAIN * offset_vx;  
    g_remote_ctrl.vy =  RC_POS_GAIN * offset_vy;

    if(g_remote_ctrl.vx >  RC_MAX_SPEED) g_remote_ctrl.vx =  RC_MAX_SPEED;
    if(g_remote_ctrl.vx < -RC_MAX_SPEED) g_remote_ctrl.vx = -RC_MAX_SPEED;
    if(g_remote_ctrl.vy >  RC_MAX_SPEED) g_remote_ctrl.vy =  RC_MAX_SPEED;
    if(g_remote_ctrl.vy < -RC_MAX_SPEED) g_remote_ctrl.vy = -RC_MAX_SPEED;

    g_remote_ctrl.last_rx_tick = s_tick;
    g_remote_ctrl.connected    = 1;

}
#endif 

void remote_control_update(void)
{
    
    s_tick++;

#ifdef USE_NRF_REMOTE
    
    if(g_nrf_rx_flag)
    {
        g_nrf_rx_flag = 0;
        s_nrf_irq_cnt++;
        parse_and_update();
    }

    {
        static uint8 decay_active = 0u;
        #define DECAY_FACTOR     0.97f     
        #define DECAY_FLOOR      0.02f     

        if(g_remote_ctrl.connected)
        {
            
            decay_active = 0u;
        }
        else
        {
            uint32 gap = s_tick - g_remote_ctrl.last_rx_tick;

            if(gap > NRF_TIMEOUT_TICKS)
            {
                
                if(!decay_active)
                {
                    decay_active = 1u;
                    
                }

                g_remote_ctrl.vx *= DECAY_FACTOR;
                g_remote_ctrl.vy *= DECAY_FACTOR;

                float abs_vx = (g_remote_ctrl.vx >= 0.0f) ? g_remote_ctrl.vx : -g_remote_ctrl.vx;
                float abs_vy = (g_remote_ctrl.vy >= 0.0f) ? g_remote_ctrl.vy : -g_remote_ctrl.vy;
                if(abs_vx < DECAY_FLOOR) g_remote_ctrl.vx = 0.0f;
                if(abs_vy < DECAY_FLOOR) g_remote_ctrl.vy = 0.0f;
            }
        }
    }

#endif

    if(s_drive_en && chassis_is_enabled())
    {
        chassis_set_velocity(g_remote_ctrl.vx, g_remote_ctrl.vy, 0.0f);
    }
}

uint8 remote_control_is_connected(void)
{
    return g_remote_ctrl.connected;
}

void remote_control_set_drive_enabled(uint8 en)
{
    s_drive_en = en;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
nrf24l01_driver.h - NRF24L01驱动头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _NRF24L01_DRIVER_H_
#define _NRF24L01_DRIVER_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define ENABLE_NRF_REMOTE   1   

#if ENABLE_NRF_REMOTE

#define NRF_CE_PIN      P01_1   
#define NRF_IRQ_PIN     P02_4   

#define CONFIG          0x00    
#define EN_AA           0x01    
#define EN_RXADDR       0x02    
#define SETUP_AW        0x03    
#define SETUP_RETR      0x04    
#define RF_CH           0x05    
#define RF_SETUP        0x06    
#define STATUS          0x07    
#define RX_ADDR_P0      0x0A    
#define TX_ADDR         0x10    
#define RX_PW_P0        0x11    
#define NRF_FIFO_STATUS 0x17    
#define DYNPD           0x1C    
#define FEATRUE         0x1D    

#define NRF_READ_REG    0x00    
#define NRF_WRITE_REG   0x20    
#define RD_RX_PLOAD     0x61    
#define FLUSH_TX        0xE1    
#define FLUSH_RX        0xE2    
#define NOP             0xFF    

#define PRIM_RX         0       
#define PWR_UP          1       
#define CRCO            2       
#define EN_CRC          3       
#define MAX_RT          4       
#define TX_DS           5       
#define RX_DR           6       
#define IRQ_ALL         ((1<<RX_DR)|(1<<TX_DS)|(1<<MAX_RT))  

#define NRF_PACKET_LEN  32      

extern volatile uint8 g_nrf_rx_buf[NRF_PACKET_LEN];  
extern volatile uint8 g_nrf_rx_flag;                  

uint8 nrf24l01_init       (void);                                       
uint8 nrf24l01_check      (void);                                       
void  nrf24l01_write_reg  (uint8 reg, uint8 val);                       
uint8 nrf24l01_read_reg   (uint8 reg);                                  
void  nrf24l01_write_buf  (uint8 reg, const uint8 *buf, uint8 len);     
void  nrf24l01_read_buf   (uint8 reg, uint8 *buf, uint8 len);           
void  nrf24l01_irq_handler(void);                                       

#endif 

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
nrf24l01_driver.c - NRF24L01驱动实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "nrf24l01_driver.h"

#if ENABLE_NRF_REMOTE

volatile uint8 g_nrf_rx_buf[NRF_PACKET_LEN] = {0};  
volatile uint8 g_nrf_rx_flag = 0;                    

void nrf24l01_write_reg(uint8 reg, uint8 val)
{
    uint8 tx[2] = {NRF_WRITE_REG | reg, val};
    uint8 rx[2];

    gpio_low(P02_3);  
    spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, tx, rx, 2);
    gpio_high(P02_3); 
}

uint8 nrf24l01_read_reg(uint8 reg)
{
    uint8 tx[2] = {NRF_READ_REG | reg, NOP};
    uint8 rx[2] = {0};

    gpio_low(P02_3);  
    spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, tx, rx, 2);
    gpio_high(P02_3); 

    return rx[1];
}

void nrf24l01_write_buf(uint8 reg, const uint8 *buf, uint8 len)
{
    uint8 tx[33];
    uint8 rx[33];
    tx[0] = NRF_WRITE_REG | reg;
    memcpy(&tx[1], buf, len);

    gpio_low(P02_3);  
    spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, tx, rx, (uint32)(len + 1));
    gpio_high(P02_3); 

}

void nrf24l01_read_buf(uint8 reg, uint8 *buf, uint8 len)
{
    uint8 tx[33];
    uint8 rx[33] = {0};
    uint8 i;

    tx[0] = NRF_READ_REG | reg;
    for(i = 1; i <= len; i++)
        tx[i] = 0xFF;

    gpio_low(P02_3);  
    spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, tx, rx, (uint32)(len + 1));
    gpio_high(P02_3); 

    memcpy(buf, &rx[1], len);
}

uint8 nrf24l01_check(void)
{
    uint8 test[5] = {0xA5, 0xA5, 0xA5, 0xA5, 0xA5};
    uint8 read[5] = {0};
    uint8 i;

    nrf24l01_write_buf(TX_ADDR, test, 5);
    nrf24l01_read_buf(TX_ADDR, read, 5);

    for(i = 0; i < 5; i++)
    {
        if(test[i] != read[i])
        {
            
            return 1;  
        }
    }
    
    return 0;  
}

uint8 nrf24l01_init(void)
{
    uint8 addr[5] = {0xAA, 0xBB, 0xCC, 0x00, 0x01};
    uint8 flush_tx[1] = {FLUSH_TX};
    uint8 flush_rx[1] = {FLUSH_RX};
    uint8 dummy[1];

    spi_init(SPI_0, SPI_MODE0, 2000000,
             SPI0_CLK_P02_2, SPI0_MOSI_P02_1,
             SPI0_MISO_P02_0, SPI_CS_NULL);  

    gpio_init(P02_3, GPO, GPIO_HIGH, GPO_PUSH_PULL);

    gpio_init(P01_1, GPO, GPIO_LOW, GPO_PUSH_PULL);

    exti_init(P02_4, EXTI_TRIGGER_FALLING);

    gpio_low(P01_1);
    system_delay_ms(20);

    if(nrf24l01_check() != 0)
        return 1;  

    nrf24l01_write_reg(CONFIG, (1 << EN_CRC) | (1 << CRCO) | (1 << PWR_UP));
    system_delay_ms(10);

    nrf24l01_write_reg(0x1D, 0x00);  
    nrf24l01_write_reg(0x1C, 0x00);  

    nrf24l01_write_reg(EN_AA,      0x01);              
    nrf24l01_write_reg(EN_RXADDR,  0x01);              
    nrf24l01_write_reg(SETUP_AW,   0x03);              
    nrf24l01_write_reg(SETUP_RETR, 0x1A);              
    nrf24l01_write_reg(RF_CH,      5);                 
    nrf24l01_write_reg(RF_SETUP,   0x0E);              
    nrf24l01_write_reg(RX_PW_P0,   NRF_PACKET_LEN);   

    nrf24l01_write_buf(TX_ADDR,    addr, 5);
    nrf24l01_write_buf(RX_ADDR_P0, addr, 5);

    gpio_low(P02_3);  
    spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, flush_tx, dummy, 1);
    gpio_high(P02_3); 

    gpio_low(P02_3);  
    spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, flush_rx, dummy, 1);
    gpio_high(P02_3); 

    nrf24l01_write_reg(STATUS, IRQ_ALL);

    nrf24l01_write_reg(CONFIG,
        nrf24l01_read_reg(CONFIG) | (1 << PRIM_RX));
    gpio_high(P01_1);
    system_delay_us(130);  

    return 0;  
}

void nrf24l01_irq_handler(void)
{

    uint8 status = nrf24l01_read_reg(STATUS);

    if(status & (1 << RX_DR))
    {
        
        uint8 tx[NRF_PACKET_LEN + 1];
        uint8 rx[NRF_PACKET_LEN + 1];
        uint8 i;

        tx[0] = RD_RX_PLOAD;
        memset(&tx[1], NOP, NRF_PACKET_LEN);

        gpio_low(P02_3);  
        spi_transfer_8bit(SPI_0, tx, rx, NRF_PACKET_LEN + 1);
        gpio_high(P02_3); 

        for(i = 0; i < NRF_PACKET_LEN; i++)
            g_nrf_rx_buf[i] = rx[i + 1];

        g_nrf_rx_flag = 1;
    }

    nrf24l01_write_reg(STATUS, status);
}

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
menu_system.h - 菜单系统头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _MENU_SYSTEM_H_
#define _MENU_SYSTEM_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"
#include "key_driver.h"

#define MENU_SPEED_MAX          (100)       
#define MENU_SPEED_MIN          (-100)      
#define MENU_DATA_REFRESH_MS    (100)       

typedef enum
{
    MENU_PAGE_MAIN = 0,             
    MENU_PAGE_SPEED_CTRL,           
    MENU_PAGE_SPEED_EDIT,           
    MENU_PAGE_ENCODER_VIEW,         
    MENU_PAGE_IMU_VIEW,             
    MENU_PAGE_CHASSIS_CTRL,         
    MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL,          
    MENU_PAGE_VISION_SPEED_EDIT,    
    MENU_PAGE_VISION_MINSPD_EDIT,   
    MENU_PAGE_VISION_ACCEL_EDIT,    
    MENU_PAGE_PID_SELECT,           
    MENU_PAGE_PID_TUNING,           
    MENU_PAGE_ODOM,                 
    MENU_PAGE_ODOM_START,           
    MENU_PAGE_SEARCH,               
    MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_SPEED_EDIT,    
    MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT,   
    MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT,    
    MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_RX_VIEW,       
} menu_page_enum;

void menu_system_init(void);

void menu_system_update(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter);

menu_page_enum menu_system_get_page(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
menu_system.c - 菜单系统实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "menu_system.h"
#include "key_driver.h"
#include "motor_control.h"
#include "encoder_manager.h"
#include "imu_manager.h"
#include "chassis_controller.h"
#include "pid_controller.h"
#include "remote_control.h"
#include "vision_tracker.h"
#include "drone_uart_rx_v2.h"    

#include "odometry.h"
#include "pid_link.h"       

#define LINE_HEIGHT         (16)        
#define TITLE_Y             (0)         
#define ITEM_START_Y        (20)        
#define MAIN_ITEM_START_Y   (16)        
#define HINT_Y              (112)       
#define TITLE_BG_COLOR      RGB565_BLUE
#define TITLE_FG_COLOR      RGB565_WHITE
#define SELECT_BG_COLOR     RGB565_GREEN
#define SELECT_FG_COLOR     RGB565_BLACK
#define NORMAL_BG_COLOR     RGB565_BLACK
#define NORMAL_FG_COLOR     RGB565_WHITE
#define HINT_FG_COLOR       RGB565_YELLOW
#define VALUE_FG_COLOR      RGB565_CYAN

#define MAIN_MENU_ITEMS     (8)
#define MAIN_MENU_VISIBLE   (7)     

static const char *main_menu_labels[MAIN_MENU_ITEMS] =
{
    "Remote Ctrl",      
    "Search",           
    "Odometry",         
    "Speed Control",    
    "Encoder Data",     
    "IMU Data",         
    "Chassis Ctrl",     
    "PID Tuning",       
};

#define SPEED_MENU_ITEMS    (7)
static const char *speed_menu_labels[SPEED_MENU_ITEMS] =
{
    "All",
    "LF",
    "LB",
    "RF",
    "RB",
    "[Stop All]",
    "[Back]",
};

#define SPEED_EDIT_ALL      (-1)
#define SPEED_IDX_APPLY     (5)
#define SPEED_IDX_BACK      (6)

static const int8 speed_motor_map[SPEED_MENU_ITEMS] =
{
    SPEED_EDIT_ALL,             
    MOTOR_LEFT_FRONT,           
    MOTOR_LEFT_BACK,            
    MOTOR_RIGHT_FRONT,          
    MOTOR_RIGHT_BACK,           
    -2,                         
    -3,                         
};

static const int16 speed_step_table[] = {100, 50, 25, 10, 5, 1};
#define SPEED_STEP_COUNT    (sizeof(speed_step_table) / sizeof(speed_step_table[0]))
#define SPEED_STEP_DEFAULT  (3)     

typedef struct
{
    menu_page_enum  current_page;       
    uint8           cursor;             
    bool            need_redraw;        

    uint8           refresh_counter;

    int16           speed_values[MOTOR_NUM];    
    int8            editing_motor;      
    int16           editing_value;      
    uint8           speed_step_index;   
} menu_state_t;

static menu_state_t g_menu;

static void draw_title(const char *title)
{
    tft180_set_color(TITLE_FG_COLOR, TITLE_BG_COLOR);
    
    tft180_show_string(0, TITLE_Y, "                    ");
    
    tft180_show_string(4, TITLE_Y, title);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void draw_hint(const char *hint)
{
    tft180_set_color(HINT_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, hint);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void show_line_bounded(uint16 x, uint16 y, const char *str)
{
    char tmp[21];
    uint8 max_len = (uint8)((tft180_width_max - x) / 8u);   
    if (max_len > 20) max_len = 20;
    uint8 len = 0;
    while (str[len] != '\0' && len < max_len)
    {
        tmp[len] = str[len];
        len++;
    }
    tmp[len] = '\0';
    tft180_show_string(x, y, tmp);
}

static void draw_menu_item(uint8 y, const char *label, bool selected)
{
    if (selected)
    {
        tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    }
    else
    {
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }

    tft180_show_string(0, y, "                    ");

    if (selected)
    {
        tft180_show_string(0, y, ">");
        tft180_show_string(8, y, label);
    }
    else
    {
        tft180_show_string(8, y, label);
    }

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void draw_menu_item_with_value(uint8 y, const char *label, int16 value, bool selected)
{
    char buf[22];

    if (selected)
    {
        tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    }
    else
    {
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }

    tft180_show_string(0, y, "                    ");

    sprintf(buf, "%s%s:%5d", selected ? ">" : " ", label, value);
    tft180_show_string(0, y, buf);

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static bool has_key_event(key_event_enum ev)
{
    return (ev == KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS ||
            ev == KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS  ||
            ev == KEY_EVENT_LONG_REPEAT);
}

static void page_main_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("System Menu");

    uint8 top = (g_menu.cursor < MAIN_MENU_VISIBLE) ? 0 : (uint8)(g_menu.cursor - (MAIN_MENU_VISIBLE - 1));
    for (uint8 i = 0; i < MAIN_MENU_VISIBLE && (top + i) < MAIN_MENU_ITEMS; i++)
    {
        draw_menu_item(MAIN_ITEM_START_Y + i * LINE_HEIGHT,
                       main_menu_labels[top + i], ((top + i) == g_menu.cursor));
    }
}

static void page_main_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor > 0)
        {
            g_menu.cursor--;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < MAIN_MENU_ITEMS - 1)
        {
            g_menu.cursor++;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {

        switch (g_menu.cursor)
        {
            case 0:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL;
                
                g_menu.cursor = 5;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 1:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 2:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_ODOM;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 3:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SPEED_CTRL;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                
                for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
                {
                    g_menu.speed_values[i] = motor_get_speed((motor_id_enum)i);
                }
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 4:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_ENCODER_VIEW;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 5:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_IMU_VIEW;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 6:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_CHASSIS_CTRL;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case 7:     
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_PID_SELECT;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;
        }
    }
}

#define SPEED_ITEM_START_Y  (16)    

static void page_speed_ctrl_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Speed Control");

    for (uint8 i = 0; i < SPEED_MENU_ITEMS; i++)
    {
        uint8 y = SPEED_ITEM_START_Y + i * LINE_HEIGHT;

        if (i <= 4)  
        {
            int16 val;
            if (i == 0)
            {
                
                val = g_menu.speed_values[0];
            }
            else
            {
                val = g_menu.speed_values[i - 1];
            }
            draw_menu_item_with_value(y, speed_menu_labels[i], val, (i == g_menu.cursor));
        }
        else  
        {
            draw_menu_item(y, speed_menu_labels[i], (i == g_menu.cursor));
        }
    }
    
}

static void page_speed_ctrl_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor > 0)
        {
            g_menu.cursor--;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < SPEED_MENU_ITEMS - 1)
        {
            g_menu.cursor++;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor <= 4)
        {
            
            g_menu.editing_motor = speed_motor_map[g_menu.cursor];

            if (g_menu.editing_motor == SPEED_EDIT_ALL)
            {
                g_menu.editing_value = g_menu.speed_values[0];
            }
            else
            {
                g_menu.editing_value = g_menu.speed_values[g_menu.editing_motor];
            }

            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SPEED_EDIT;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == SPEED_IDX_APPLY)
        {
            
            motor_stop_all();
            for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
            {
                g_menu.speed_values[i] = 0;
            }
            
            tft180_set_color(RGB565_RED, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
            tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, " All Motors Stopped!");
            tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
            system_delay_ms(500);
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == SPEED_IDX_BACK)
        {
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
            g_menu.cursor = 3;  
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }
}

static void page_speed_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();

    const char *name;
    if (g_menu.editing_motor == SPEED_EDIT_ALL)
    {
        name = "All Motors";
    }
    else
    {
        name = speed_menu_labels[g_menu.editing_motor + 1];
    }
    draw_title(name);

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %5d >  ", g_menu.editing_value);
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%d~%d", MENU_SPEED_MIN, MENU_SPEED_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Step:  %d", speed_step_table[g_menu.speed_step_index]);
    tft180_show_string(8, 88, buf);

    draw_hint("-  +  OK  hold=Step");
}

static void page_speed_edit_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;

    int16 step = speed_step_table[g_menu.speed_step_index];

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        g_menu.editing_value -= step;
        if (g_menu.editing_value < MENU_SPEED_MIN)
        {
            g_menu.editing_value = MENU_SPEED_MIN;
        }
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        g_menu.editing_value += step;
        if (g_menu.editing_value > MENU_SPEED_MAX)
        {
            g_menu.editing_value = MENU_SPEED_MAX;
        }
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (ev_enter == KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS)
    {
        g_menu.speed_step_index = (g_menu.speed_step_index + 1) % SPEED_STEP_COUNT;

        char sbuf[22];
        sprintf(sbuf, "Step:  %-5d", speed_step_table[g_menu.speed_step_index]);
        tft180_show_string(8, 88, sbuf);
        return;
    }

    if (ev_enter == KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS)
    {
        if (g_menu.editing_motor == SPEED_EDIT_ALL)
        {
            
            for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
            {
                g_menu.speed_values[i] = g_menu.editing_value;
                motor_set_speed((motor_id_enum)i, g_menu.editing_value);
            }
        }
        else
        {
            
            g_menu.speed_values[g_menu.editing_motor] = g_menu.editing_value;
            motor_set_speed((motor_id_enum)g_menu.editing_motor, g_menu.editing_value);
        }

        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SPEED_CTRL;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        return;
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        char buf[22];
        tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "  < %5d >  ", g_menu.editing_value);
        tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }
}

static void speed_ctrl_periodic_send(void)
{
    if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_SPEED_EDIT)
    {
        if (g_menu.editing_motor == SPEED_EDIT_ALL)
        {
            
            for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
                motor_set_speed((motor_id_enum)i, g_menu.editing_value);
        }
        else
        {
            
            for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
            {
                int16 v = (i == (uint8)g_menu.editing_motor) ? g_menu.editing_value
                                                             : g_menu.speed_values[i];
                motor_set_speed((motor_id_enum)i, v);
            }
        }
    }
    else   
    {
        
        for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
            motor_set_speed((motor_id_enum)i, g_menu.speed_values[i]);
    }
}

static float s_encoder_mileage[ENCODER_MOTOR_NUM];

static void page_encoder_view_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Encoder Data");
    draw_hint("   K3=Back");
    
    for (uint8 i = 0; i < ENCODER_MOTOR_NUM; i++)
        s_encoder_mileage[i] = 0.0f;
}

static void page_encoder_view_refresh(void)
{
    char buf[32];
    const encoder_data_t *data;

    for (uint8 i = 0; i < ENCODER_MOTOR_NUM; i++)
    {
        s_encoder_mileage[i] += encoder_motor_get_speed_rad_s((encoder_motor_index_enum)i)
                              * 0.1f * (CHASSIS_WHEEL_DIAMETER / 2.0f);
    }

    data = encoder_motor_get_data(ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_FRONT);
    sprintf(buf, "LF:%+5.1f %5.2fm", data->speed_rad_s, s_encoder_mileage[ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_FRONT]);
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y, buf);

    data = encoder_motor_get_data(ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_BACK);
    sprintf(buf, "LB:%+5.1f %5.2fm", data->speed_rad_s, s_encoder_mileage[ENCODER_MOTOR_LEFT_BACK]);
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT, buf);

    data = encoder_motor_get_data(ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_FRONT);
    sprintf(buf, "RF:%+5.1f %5.2fm", data->speed_rad_s, s_encoder_mileage[ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_FRONT]);
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2, buf);

    data = encoder_motor_get_data(ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_BACK);
    sprintf(buf, "RB:%+5.1f %5.2fm", data->speed_rad_s, s_encoder_mileage[ENCODER_MOTOR_RIGHT_BACK]);
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 3, buf);
}

static void page_encoder_view_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    (void)ev_up;
    (void)ev_down;

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
        g_menu.cursor = 4;  
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }
}

static void page_imu_view_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("IMU Data");
    draw_hint("   K3=Back");
}

static void page_imu_view_refresh(void)
{
    char buf[22];

#if ENABLE_FUSION_AHRS
    sprintf(buf, "Yaw:%6.2f B:%5.3f", imu_get_yaw(), imu_get_gyro_bias_z());
#else
    sprintf(buf, "Yaw: %7.2f  ", imu_get_yaw());
#endif
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Gz: %7.1f d/s", imu_get_gyro_z());
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Gx: %7.1f d/s", imu_get_gyro_x());
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Gy: %7.1f d/s", imu_get_gyro_y());
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 3, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Ax: %6.3f g", imu_get_acc_x());
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 4, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Ay: %6.3f g", imu_get_acc_y());
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 5, buf);
}

static void page_imu_view_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    (void)ev_up;
    (void)ev_down;

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
        g_menu.cursor = 5;  
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }
}

#define CHASSIS_IDX_VX          (0)
#define CHASSIS_IDX_VY          (1)
#define CHASSIS_IDX_WZ          (2)     
#define CHASSIS_IDX_MODE        (3)     
#define CHASSIS_IDX_RANGE       (4)     
#define CHASSIS_IDX_STARTSTOP   (5)
#define CHASSIS_IDX_BACK        (6)

#define CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_RATE   (6)     
#define CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_HOLD   (7)     
#define CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_OF(m)  ((m) == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE ? CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_HOLD : CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_RATE)

#define CHASSIS_VXY_STEP        (0.05f) 
#define CHASSIS_WZ_STEP         (0.1f)  
#define CHASSIS_YAW_STEP        (5.0f)  
#define CHASSIS_VXY_MAX         (1.0f)
#define CHASSIS_WZ_MAX          (3.0f)

static float chassis_edit_vx = 0.0f;
static float chassis_edit_vy = 0.0f;
static float chassis_edit_wz = 0.0f;
static float chassis_edit_yaw = 0.0f;          
static chassis_yaw_mode_enum chassis_edit_mode = CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE;
static chassis_angle_range_enum chassis_edit_range = CHASSIS_ANGLE_NORMAL;

static void page_chassis_ctrl_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Chassis Control");

    char buf[22];
    uint8 line = 0;  

    sprintf(buf, "%sVx: %+5.2f m/s", (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VX) ? ">" : " ", chassis_edit_vx);
    if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VX) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
    line++;

    sprintf(buf, "%sVy: %+5.2f m/s", (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VY) ? ">" : " ", chassis_edit_vy);
    if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VY) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
    line++;

    if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE)
        sprintf(buf, "%sWz: %+5.2f r/s", (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_WZ) ? ">" : " ", chassis_edit_wz);
    else
        sprintf(buf, "%sYaw:%+7.1f deg", (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_WZ) ? ">" : " ", chassis_edit_yaw);
    if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_WZ) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
    line++;

    {
        const char *mode_str = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE) ? "Hold" : "Rate";
        sprintf(buf, "%sMode: %s", (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_MODE) ? ">" : " ", mode_str);
        if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_MODE) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
        line++;
    }

    if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE)
    {
        const char *range_str = (chassis_edit_range == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN) ? "Multi" : "Normal";
        sprintf(buf, "%sRange: %s", (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_RANGE) ? ">" : " ", range_str);
        if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_RANGE) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
        line++;
    }

    uint8 ss_idx = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE) ? CHASSIS_IDX_STARTSTOP : (CHASSIS_IDX_STARTSTOP - 1);
    uint8 bk_idx = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE) ? CHASSIS_IDX_BACK : (CHASSIS_IDX_BACK - 1);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
    {
        const char *action_label = chassis_is_enabled() ? "[Stop]" : "[Start]";
        if (g_menu.cursor == ss_idx) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line,
                           (g_menu.cursor == ss_idx) ? ">" : " ");
        tft180_show_string(8, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, action_label);
    }
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == bk_idx) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(80, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line,
                           (g_menu.cursor == bk_idx) ? ">[Back]" : " [Back]");
    }

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    tft180_set_color(HINT_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "Status: %s", chassis_is_enabled() ? "RUNNING" : "STOPPED");
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void page_chassis_ctrl_refresh(void)
{
    if (!chassis_is_enabled()) return;

    char buf[32];   
    chassis_status_t st = chassis_get_status();

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "A:%+.2f %+.2f %+.1f", st.actual_vx, st.actual_vy, st.actual_wz);
    
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, HINT_Y, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void page_chassis_ctrl_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;
    uint8 item_num = CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_OF(chassis_edit_mode);
    
    uint8 ss_idx = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE) ? CHASSIS_IDX_STARTSTOP : (CHASSIS_IDX_STARTSTOP - 1);
    uint8 bk_idx = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE) ? CHASSIS_IDX_BACK : (CHASSIS_IDX_BACK - 1);
    float yaw_limit = (chassis_edit_range == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN) ? CHASSIS_MULTITURN_LIMIT : 180.0f;

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VX)
        {
            chassis_edit_vx -= CHASSIS_VXY_STEP;
            if (chassis_edit_vx < -CHASSIS_VXY_MAX) chassis_edit_vx = -CHASSIS_VXY_MAX;
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VY)
        {
            chassis_edit_vy -= CHASSIS_VXY_STEP;
            if (chassis_edit_vy < -CHASSIS_VXY_MAX) chassis_edit_vy = -CHASSIS_VXY_MAX;
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_WZ)
        {
            if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE)
            {
                chassis_edit_wz -= CHASSIS_WZ_STEP;
                if (chassis_edit_wz < -CHASSIS_WZ_MAX) chassis_edit_wz = -CHASSIS_WZ_MAX;
            }
            else
            {
                chassis_edit_yaw -= CHASSIS_YAW_STEP;
                if (chassis_edit_yaw < -yaw_limit) chassis_edit_yaw = -yaw_limit;
            }
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_MODE)
        {
            chassis_edit_mode = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE)
                                ? CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE : CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE;
            
            if (g_menu.cursor >= CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_OF(chassis_edit_mode))
                g_menu.cursor = CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_OF(chassis_edit_mode) - 1;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE && g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_RANGE)
        {
            chassis_edit_range = (chassis_edit_range == CHASSIS_ANGLE_NORMAL)
                                 ? CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN : CHASSIS_ANGLE_NORMAL;
            
            float new_limit = (chassis_edit_range == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN) ? CHASSIS_MULTITURN_LIMIT : 180.0f;
            if (chassis_edit_yaw > new_limit) chassis_edit_yaw = new_limit;
            if (chassis_edit_yaw < -new_limit) chassis_edit_yaw = -new_limit;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else
        {
            
            g_menu.cursor--;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VX)
        {
            chassis_edit_vx += CHASSIS_VXY_STEP;
            if (chassis_edit_vx > CHASSIS_VXY_MAX) chassis_edit_vx = CHASSIS_VXY_MAX;
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_VY)
        {
            chassis_edit_vy += CHASSIS_VXY_STEP;
            if (chassis_edit_vy > CHASSIS_VXY_MAX) chassis_edit_vy = CHASSIS_VXY_MAX;
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_WZ)
        {
            if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE)
            {
                chassis_edit_wz += CHASSIS_WZ_STEP;
                if (chassis_edit_wz > CHASSIS_WZ_MAX) chassis_edit_wz = CHASSIS_WZ_MAX;
            }
            else
            {
                chassis_edit_yaw += CHASSIS_YAW_STEP;
                if (chassis_edit_yaw > yaw_limit) chassis_edit_yaw = yaw_limit;
            }
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_MODE)
        {
            chassis_edit_mode = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE)
                                ? CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE : CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE;
            if (g_menu.cursor >= CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_OF(chassis_edit_mode))
                g_menu.cursor = CHASSIS_ITEM_NUM_OF(chassis_edit_mode) - 1;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE && g_menu.cursor == CHASSIS_IDX_RANGE)
        {
            chassis_edit_range = (chassis_edit_range == CHASSIS_ANGLE_NORMAL)
                                 ? CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN : CHASSIS_ANGLE_NORMAL;
            float new_limit = (chassis_edit_range == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN) ? CHASSIS_MULTITURN_LIMIT : 180.0f;
            if (chassis_edit_yaw > new_limit) chassis_edit_yaw = new_limit;
            if (chassis_edit_yaw < -new_limit) chassis_edit_yaw = -new_limit;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else
        {
            
            if (g_menu.cursor < item_num - 1)
            {
                g_menu.cursor++;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
            }
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        
        uint8 last_edit_idx = (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE) ? CHASSIS_IDX_RANGE : CHASSIS_IDX_MODE;
        if (g_menu.cursor <= last_edit_idx)
        {
            g_menu.cursor++;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ss_idx)
        {
            if (chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                chassis_stop();
            }
            else
            {
                
                chassis_set_angle_range(chassis_edit_range);
                chassis_set_yaw_mode(chassis_edit_mode);
                if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE)
                {
                    chassis_set_yaw_target(chassis_edit_yaw);
                    chassis_set_velocity(chassis_edit_vx, chassis_edit_vy, 0.0f);
                }
                else
                {
                    chassis_set_velocity(chassis_edit_vx, chassis_edit_vy, chassis_edit_wz);
                }
                chassis_set_enabled(true);
            }
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == bk_idx)
        {
            if (chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                chassis_stop();
            }
            
            chassis_set_angle_range(CHASSIS_ANGLE_NORMAL);
            chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE);
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
            g_menu.cursor = 6;  
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        
        if (chassis_is_enabled())
        {
            if (chassis_edit_mode == CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE)
            {
                chassis_set_yaw_target(chassis_edit_yaw);
                chassis_set_velocity(chassis_edit_vx, chassis_edit_vy, 0.0f);
            }
            else
            {
                chassis_set_velocity(chassis_edit_vx, chassis_edit_vy, chassis_edit_wz);
            }
        }
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }
}

static control_mode_enum s_remote_page_mode = CONTROL_MODE_VISION;

#define REMOTE_IDX_RX       0   
#define REMOTE_IDX_MAXSPD   1   
#define REMOTE_IDX_MINSPD   2   
#define REMOTE_IDX_ACCEL    3   
#define REMOTE_IDX_MODE     4   
#define REMOTE_IDX_START    5   
#define REMOTE_IDX_BACK     6
#define REMOTE_IDX_MAX      6   

#define VISION_SPEED_EDIT_STEP  (0.05f) 
#define VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MIN   (0.05f) 
#define VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MAX   (3.00f) 

#define VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP (0.05f) 
#define VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN  (0.05f) 
#define VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX  (1.50f) 

#define VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_STEP  (0.10f) 
#define VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN   (0.00f) 
#define VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX   (5.00f) 

static void draw_remote_rx_line(void)
{
    char buf[22];
    if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_RX) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "%sRx:%ufps %s", (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_RX) ? ">" : " ",
            (unsigned)drone_uart_rx_get_fps(),
            remote_control_is_connected() ? "CON" : "DIS");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 0, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 0, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void page_remote_ctrl_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Remote Control");

    char buf[22];
    uint8 line = 0;

    draw_remote_rx_line();
    line++;

    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MAXSPD) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "%sMaxSpd: %.2f m/s", (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MAXSPD) ? ">" : " ",
                vision_tracker_get_max_speed());
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
        line++;
    }

    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MINSPD) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "%sMinSpd: %.2f m/s", (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MINSPD) ? ">" : " ",
                vision_tracker_get_min_speed());
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
        line++;
    }

    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_ACCEL) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "%sAccel: %.2f m/ss", (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_ACCEL) ? ">" : " ",
                vision_tracker_get_accel());
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, buf);
        line++;
    }

    {
        char seg[10];
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");

        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MODE) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(seg, "%sMd:%s", (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MODE) ? ">" : " ",
                (s_remote_page_mode == CONTROL_MODE_VISION) ? "Vis" : "RC");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, seg);
        line++;
    }

    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, "                    ");
    {
        const char *action_label = chassis_is_enabled() ? "[Stop]" : "[Start]";
        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_START) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line,
                           (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_START) ? ">" : " ");
        tft180_show_string(8, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line, action_label);
    }
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_BACK) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(80, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * line,
                           (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_BACK) ? ">[Back]" : " [Back]");
    }

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    tft180_set_color(HINT_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    const char *hint_str = chassis_is_enabled()
        ? (s_remote_page_mode == CONTROL_MODE_VISION ? "Mode: VISION  " : "Mode: YAW HOLD")
        : "K12=move K3=select";
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, hint_str);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void page_remote_ctrl_refresh(void)
{
    char buf[32];   

    draw_remote_rx_line();

    if (chassis_is_enabled())
    {
        if (s_remote_page_mode == CONTROL_MODE_VISION)
        {

            const vision_cmd_t *cmd = drone_uart_rx_get_vision_cmd();
            const char *land_str = drone_uart_rx_is_land_latched() ? " LOCK"
                                 : (cmd->land_cmd ? " land" : "");
            sprintf(buf, "x%+4.0f y%+4.0f %s%s",
                    cmd->vx, cmd->vy, cmd->valid ? "OK" : "--",
                    land_str);
        }
        else
        {
            
            chassis_status_t st = chassis_get_status();
            sprintf(buf, "A:%+.2f %+.2f %+.1f", st.actual_vx, st.actual_vy, st.actual_wz);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        chassis_status_t st = chassis_get_status();
        sprintf(buf, "A:%+.2f %+.2f %+.1f", st.actual_vx, st.actual_vy, st.actual_wz);
    }

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    
    tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, HINT_Y, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void page_remote_ctrl_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor > 0)
        {
            g_menu.cursor--;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < REMOTE_IDX_MAX)
        {
            g_menu.cursor++;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_RX)
        {
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_RX_VIEW;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MAXSPD)
        {
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_VISION_SPEED_EDIT;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MINSPD)
        {
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_VISION_MINSPD_EDIT;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_ACCEL)
        {
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_VISION_ACCEL_EDIT;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_MODE)
        {
            
            s_remote_page_mode = (s_remote_page_mode == CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE)
                                 ? CONTROL_MODE_VISION : CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_START)
        {
            if (chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                
                remote_control_set_drive_enabled(0);
                vision_tracker_set_mode(CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE);
                chassis_stop();
            }
            else if (s_remote_page_mode == CONTROL_MODE_VISION)
            {
                
                chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
                chassis_set_yaw_target(0.0f);
                chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
                chassis_set_enabled(true);

                vision_tracker_set_mode(CONTROL_MODE_VISION);
            }
            else
            {
                
                vision_tracker_set_mode(CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE);

                chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
                chassis_set_yaw_target(0.0f);
                chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
                chassis_set_enabled(true);

                remote_control_set_drive_enabled(1);
            }
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == REMOTE_IDX_BACK)
        {
            if (chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                chassis_stop();
            }
            
            remote_control_set_drive_enabled(0);
            vision_tracker_set_mode(CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE);
            chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE);
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
            g_menu.cursor = 0;  
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }
}

static void page_remote_rx_view_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Drone RX Data");
    draw_hint("   K3=Back");
}

static void page_remote_rx_view_refresh(void)
{
    char buf[22];
    const vision_cmd_t *cmd = drone_uart_rx_get_vision_cmd();
    const car_error_t  *cer = drone_uart_rx_get_car_error();

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "FPS:%u", (unsigned)drone_uart_rx_get_fps());
