    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y, buf);

    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "v:%d s:%d b:%d", cmd->valid, cmd->searching, cmd->beacon_count);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT, buf);

    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "vx%+.0f vy%+.0f", cmd->vx, cmd->vy);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2, buf);

    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "ex%+.0f ey%+.0f", cer->car_error_x, cer->car_error_y);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 3, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 3, buf);

    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "car:%d", cer->car_detected);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 4, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 4, buf);

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
}

static void page_remote_rx_view_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    (void)ev_up;
    (void)ev_down;

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL;
        g_menu.cursor = REMOTE_IDX_RX;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }
}

static void page_vision_speed_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Max Track Speed");

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_tracker_get_max_speed());
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%.2f~%.2f", VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MIN, VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Step:  %.2f", VISION_SPEED_EDIT_STEP);
    tft180_show_string(8, 88, buf);

    draw_hint("K1- K2+ K3=OK");
}

static void page_vision_speed_edit_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;
    float v = vision_tracker_get_max_speed();

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        v -= VISION_SPEED_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v < VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MIN) v = VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MIN;
        vision_tracker_set_max_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        v += VISION_SPEED_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v > VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MAX) v = VISION_SPEED_EDIT_MAX;
        vision_tracker_set_max_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL;
        g_menu.cursor = REMOTE_IDX_MAXSPD;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        return;
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        char buf[22];
        tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_tracker_get_max_speed());
        tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }
}

static void page_vision_minspd_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Min Track Speed");

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_tracker_get_min_speed());
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%.2f~%.2f", VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN, VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "Step:  %.2f", VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP);
    tft180_show_string(8, 88, buf);

    draw_hint("K1- K2+ K3=OK");
}

{
    bool value_changed = false;
    float v = vision_tracker_get_min_speed();

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        v -= VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v < VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN) v = VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN;
        vision_tracker_set_min_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        v += VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v > VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX) v = VISION_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX;
        vision_tracker_set_min_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL;
        g_menu.cursor = REMOTE_IDX_MINSPD;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        return;
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        char buf[22];
        tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_tracker_get_min_speed());
        tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }
}

static void page_vision_accel_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Track Accel");

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/ss > ", vision_tracker_get_accel());
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%.1f~%.1f", VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN, VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);
    
    if (vision_tracker_get_accel() <= 0.001f)
        tft180_show_string(8, 88, "Step:0.10 (0=OFF)");
    else
        tft180_show_string(8, 88, "Step:  0.10      ");

    draw_hint("K1- K2+ K3=OK");
}

static void page_vision_accel_edit_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;
    float v = vision_tracker_get_accel();

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        v -= VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v < VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN) v = VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN;
        vision_tracker_set_accel(v);
        value_changed = true;

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        v += VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v > VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX) v = VISION_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX;
        vision_tracker_set_accel(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL;

        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }
}

#define PID_TUNE_PARAM_NUM      (3)     
#define PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT      (2)     
#define PID_TUNE_ITEM_NUM_BASE  (6)     
#define PID_TUNE_ITEM_NUM_HOLD  (7)     
#define PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD        (3)
#define PID_TUNE_IDX_DZ         (4)     

#define PID_TUNE_IDX_RUNSTOP_OF(idx)  ((idx) == 1 ? 5 : 4)
#define PID_TUNE_IDX_BACK_OF(idx)     ((idx) == 1 ? 6 : 5)
#define PID_TUNE_ITEM_NUM_OF(idx)     ((idx) == 1 ? PID_TUNE_ITEM_NUM_HOLD : PID_TUNE_ITEM_NUM_BASE)

#define PID_SELECT_ITEM_NUM     (PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT + 1)  

#define PID_KP_STEP             (0.001f)
#define PID_KI_STEP             (0.001f)
#define PID_KD_STEP             (0.001f)
#define PID_SPD_WZ_STEP         (0.1f)      
#define PID_SPD_YAW_DEG_STEP    (5.0f)      
#define PID_DZ_STEP             (0.5f)      

static uint8 pid_tune_index = 0;        
static float pid_tune_test_wz = 1.0f;   
static float pid_tune_test_yaw_deg = 0.0f;  
static const char *pid_tune_names[PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT] = {
    "YAW Rate", "YAW Hold"
};

static pid_t* get_tuning_pid(void)
{
    
    if (pid_tune_index == 0) return chassis_get_yaw_rate_pid();
    else                      return chassis_get_yaw_hold_pid();
}

static void sync_yaw_mode_for_tuning(void)
{
    if (pid_tune_index == 1)
    {
        chassis_set_angle_range(chassis_edit_range);
        chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);
    }
    else  
    {
        chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE);
    }
}

static void page_pid_select_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("PID Select");

    char buf[22];

    for (uint8 i = 0; i < PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT; i++)
    {
        uint16 y = ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * i;

        sprintf(buf, "%s%s", (g_menu.cursor == i) ? ">" : " ", pid_tune_names[i]);
        if (g_menu.cursor == i) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, y, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, y, buf);
    }

    {
        uint16 y = ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2;
        if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, y, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, y, (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT) ? ">[Back]" : " [Back]");
    }

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    draw_hint("K12=move K3=select");
}

static void page_pid_select_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor > 0) g_menu.cursor--;
        else g_menu.cursor = PID_SELECT_ITEM_NUM - 1;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < PID_SELECT_ITEM_NUM - 1) g_menu.cursor++;
        else g_menu.cursor = 0;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < PID_TUNE_PID_COUNT)
        {
            
            pid_tune_index = g_menu.cursor;
            sync_yaw_mode_for_tuning();
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_PID_TUNING;
            g_menu.cursor = 0;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else
        {
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
            g_menu.cursor = 7;  
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }
}

static void page_pid_tuning_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();

    char buf[22];
    sprintf(buf, "PID: %s", pid_tune_names[pid_tune_index]);
    draw_title(buf);

    pid_t *p = get_tuning_pid();

    sprintf(buf, "%sKp:%6.3f", (g_menu.cursor == 0) ? ">" : " ", p->kp);
    if (g_menu.cursor == 0) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "%sKi:%6.4f", (g_menu.cursor == 1) ? ">" : " ", p->ki);
    if (g_menu.cursor == 1) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT, buf);

    sprintf(buf, "%sKd:%6.4f", (g_menu.cursor == 2) ? ">" : " ", p->kd);
    if (g_menu.cursor == 2) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 2, buf);

    if (pid_tune_index == 0)
        sprintf(buf, "%sSpd:%5.2f r/s", (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD) ? ">" : " ", pid_tune_test_wz);
    else
        sprintf(buf, "%sTgt:%+7.1f", (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD) ? ">" : " ", pid_tune_test_yaw_deg);
    if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
    else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 3, "                    ");
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 3, buf);

    uint8 btn_row = 4;  
    if (pid_tune_index == 1)
    {
        sprintf(buf, "%sDZ:%4.1f deg", (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_DZ) ? ">" : " ", chassis_get_yaw_deadzone());
        if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_DZ) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 4, "                    ");
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * 4, buf);
        btn_row = 5;
    }

    uint8 idx_rs = PID_TUNE_IDX_RUNSTOP_OF(pid_tune_index);
    uint8 idx_bk = PID_TUNE_IDX_BACK_OF(pid_tune_index);
    tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * btn_row, "                    ");
    {
        const char *rs_label = chassis_is_enabled() ? "[Stop]" : "[Run]";
        if (g_menu.cursor == idx_rs) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(0, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * btn_row,
                           (g_menu.cursor == idx_rs) ? ">" : " ");
        tft180_show_string(8, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * btn_row, rs_label);
    }
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == idx_bk) tft180_set_color(SELECT_FG_COLOR, SELECT_BG_COLOR);
        else tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        tft180_show_string(80, ITEM_START_Y + LINE_HEIGHT * btn_row,
                           (g_menu.cursor == idx_bk) ? ">[Back]" : " [Back]");
    }

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    draw_hint("K12=adj K3=v LK3=go");
}

static void page_pid_tuning_refresh(void)
{
    char buf[32];   
    pid_t *p = get_tuning_pid();

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    if (pid_tune_index == 0)
    {
        
        chassis_status_t st = chassis_get_status();
        float gyro_z = -imu_get_gyro_z() * 0.01745329f;
        sprintf(buf, "T:%+5.2f A:%+5.2f", st.wz, gyro_z);
        tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
        show_line_bounded(0, HINT_Y, buf);
    }
    else
    {
        
        float actual_yaw = (chassis_get_angle_range() == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN)
                           ? chassis_get_yaw_accumulated() : imu_get_yaw();
        sprintf(buf, "T:%+7.1f A:%+7.1f", pid_tune_test_yaw_deg, actual_yaw);
        tft180_show_string(0, HINT_Y, "                    ");
        show_line_bounded(0, HINT_Y, buf);
    }

    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    if (!pid_link_is_active())
    {
        chassis_debug_print(pid_tune_index);
    }
}

static void page_pid_tuning_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    pid_t *p = get_tuning_pid();
    bool value_changed = false;

    uint8 idx_rs = PID_TUNE_IDX_RUNSTOP_OF(pid_tune_index);
    uint8 idx_bk = PID_TUNE_IDX_BACK_OF(pid_tune_index);
    uint8 item_num = PID_TUNE_ITEM_NUM_OF(pid_tune_index);

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == 0)          { p->kp -= PID_KP_STEP; if (p->kp < 0.0f) p->kp = 0.0f; value_changed = true; }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == 1)     { p->ki -= PID_KI_STEP; if (p->ki < 0.0f) p->ki = 0.0f; value_changed = true; }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == 2)     { p->kd -= PID_KD_STEP; if (p->kd < 0.0f) p->kd = 0.0f; value_changed = true; }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD)
        {
            if (pid_tune_index == 0)       { pid_tune_test_wz -= PID_SPD_WZ_STEP; if (pid_tune_test_wz < 0.1f)  pid_tune_test_wz = 0.1f; }
            else                           { float lim = (chassis_get_angle_range() == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN) ? CHASSIS_MULTITURN_LIMIT : 180.0f; pid_tune_test_yaw_deg -= PID_SPD_YAW_DEG_STEP; if (pid_tune_test_yaw_deg < -lim) pid_tune_test_yaw_deg = -lim; }
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_DZ && pid_tune_index == 1)
        {
            float dz = chassis_get_yaw_deadzone() - PID_DZ_STEP;
            if (dz < 0.0f) dz = 0.0f;
            chassis_set_yaw_deadzone(dz);
            value_changed = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == 0)          { p->kp += PID_KP_STEP; value_changed = true; }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == 1)     { p->ki += PID_KI_STEP; value_changed = true; }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == 2)     { p->kd += PID_KD_STEP; value_changed = true; }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD)
        {
            if (pid_tune_index == 0)       { pid_tune_test_wz += PID_SPD_WZ_STEP; if (pid_tune_test_wz > 3.0f) pid_tune_test_wz = 3.0f; }
            else                           { float lim = (chassis_get_angle_range() == CHASSIS_ANGLE_MULTITURN) ? CHASSIS_MULTITURN_LIMIT : 180.0f; pid_tune_test_yaw_deg += PID_SPD_YAW_DEG_STEP; if (pid_tune_test_yaw_deg > lim) pid_tune_test_yaw_deg = lim; }
            value_changed = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_DZ && pid_tune_index == 1)
        {
            float dz = chassis_get_yaw_deadzone() + PID_DZ_STEP;
            if (dz > 30.0f) dz = 30.0f;
            chassis_set_yaw_deadzone(dz);
            value_changed = true;
        }
    }

    if (ev_enter == KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS)
    {
        g_menu.cursor = (g_menu.cursor + 1) % item_num;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }

    if (ev_enter == KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS)
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == idx_rs)
        {
            if (chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                chassis_stop();
            }
            else
            {
                sync_yaw_mode_for_tuning();
                if (pid_tune_index == 0)
                    chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, pid_tune_test_wz);
                else
                {
                    
                    chassis_set_yaw_target(pid_tune_test_yaw_deg);
                    chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
                }
                chassis_set_enabled(true);
            }
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == idx_bk)
        {
            if (chassis_is_enabled())
            {
                chassis_stop();
            }
            
            chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_RATE_MODE);
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_PID_SELECT;
            g_menu.cursor = pid_tune_index;  
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        
        if (g_menu.cursor == PID_TUNE_IDX_SPD && chassis_is_enabled())
        {
            if (pid_tune_index == 0)
                chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, pid_tune_test_wz);
            else
                chassis_set_yaw_target(pid_tune_test_yaw_deg);
        }
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
    }
}

#define ODOM_DATA_Y         (16)                 
#define ODOM_ITEM_START_Y   (16 + 16)            

static float odom_start_edit_x = 0.0f;      
static float odom_start_edit_y = 0.0f;      
static float odom_field_edit_x = 0.0f;      
static float odom_field_edit_y = 0.0f;      

static float odom_meas_speed = 0.15f;
#define ODOM_MEAS_SPEED_STEP    (0.05f)     
#define ODOM_MEAS_SPEED_MIN     (0.10f)     
#define ODOM_MEAS_SPEED_MAX     (0.30f)     

#define ODOM_IDX_SPD        (0)     
#define ODOM_IDX_CALY       (1)     
#define ODOM_IDX_CALX       (2)     
#define ODOM_IDX_RESET      (3)     
#define ODOM_IDX_START      (4)     
#define ODOM_IDX_BACK       (5)     
#define ODOM_ITEM_NUM       (6)

static uint8 odom_cal_axis = 0;

static void odom_cal_stop_and_halt(void)
{
    odometry_cal_stop();
    chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
    chassis_set_enabled(false);     
}

static void odom_cal_start_drive(uint8 axis)
{
    odom_cal_axis = axis;
    vision_tracker_set_mode(CONTROL_MODE_REMOTE);   
    chassis_set_yaw_mode(CHASSIS_YAW_HOLD_MODE);    
    chassis_set_enabled(true);
    if (axis == 0) chassis_set_velocity(0.0f, odom_meas_speed, 0.0f);  
    else           chassis_set_velocity(odom_meas_speed, 0.0f, 0.0f);  
    odometry_cal_start();
}

static void page_odom_draw_items(void)
{
    char buf[22];
    const odom_data_t *od = odometry_get_data();
    bool measuring = (odometry_cal_get_state() == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING);

    sprintf(buf, "MeasSpd:%.2f", odom_meas_speed);
    draw_menu_item(ODOM_ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_IDX_SPD * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_IDX_SPD));

    if (measuring && odom_cal_axis == 0) sprintf(buf, "MeasY: STOP");
    else                                 sprintf(buf, "MeasY  Y=%.2f", od->field_size_y);
    draw_menu_item(ODOM_ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_IDX_CALY * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_IDX_CALY));

    if (measuring && odom_cal_axis == 1) sprintf(buf, "MeasX: STOP");
    else                                 sprintf(buf, "MeasX  X=%.2f", od->field_size_x);
    draw_menu_item(ODOM_ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_IDX_CALX * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_IDX_CALX));

    draw_menu_item(ODOM_ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_IDX_RESET * LINE_HEIGHT, "Reset Here", (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_IDX_RESET));

    draw_menu_item(ODOM_ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_IDX_START * LINE_HEIGHT, "Field/Start>", (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_IDX_START));

    draw_menu_item(ODOM_ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_IDX_BACK * LINE_HEIGHT, "[Back]", (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_IDX_BACK));
}

static void page_odom_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Odometry");
    page_odom_draw_items();
}

static void page_odom_refresh(void)
{
    char buf[32];   
    sprintf(buf, "X%+.1f Y%+.1f D%.2f",
            odometry_get_x(), odometry_get_y(), odometry_cal_get_distance());
    
    tft180_show_string(0, ODOM_DATA_Y, "                    ");
    show_line_bounded(0, ODOM_DATA_Y, buf);
}

static void page_odom_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor > 0) { g_menu.cursor--; g_menu.need_redraw = true; }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < ODOM_ITEM_NUM - 1) { g_menu.cursor++; g_menu.need_redraw = true; }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        switch (g_menu.cursor)
        {
            case ODOM_IDX_SPD:      
                odom_meas_speed += ODOM_MEAS_SPEED_STEP;
                if (odom_meas_speed > ODOM_MEAS_SPEED_MAX + 0.001f)
                    odom_meas_speed = ODOM_MEAS_SPEED_MIN;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case ODOM_IDX_CALY:     
                if (odometry_cal_get_state() == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING)
                {
                    if (odom_cal_axis == 0)     
                        odometry_set_field_size_y(odometry_cal_get_distance());
                    odom_cal_stop_and_halt();
                }
                else
                {
                    odom_cal_start_drive(0);    
                }
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case ODOM_IDX_CALX:     
                if (odometry_cal_get_state() == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING)
                {
                    if (odom_cal_axis == 1)     
                        odometry_set_field_size_x(odometry_cal_get_distance());
                    odom_cal_stop_and_halt();
                }
                else
                {
                    odom_cal_start_drive(1);    
                }
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case ODOM_IDX_RESET:    
                if (odometry_cal_get_state() == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING)
                    odom_cal_stop_and_halt();       
                imu_reset_yaw();                    
                odometry_reset_to_start();          
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case ODOM_IDX_START:    
                if (odometry_cal_get_state() == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING)
                    odom_cal_stop_and_halt();       
                {
                    
                    const odom_data_t *od = odometry_get_data();
                    odom_field_edit_x = od->field_size_x;
                    odom_field_edit_y = od->field_size_y;
                    odom_start_edit_x = od->start_offset_x;
                    odom_start_edit_y = od->start_offset_y;
                }
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_ODOM_START;
                g_menu.cursor = 0;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case ODOM_IDX_BACK:     
                if (odometry_cal_get_state() == ODOM_CAL_MEASURING)
                    odom_cal_stop_and_halt();       
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
                g_menu.cursor = 2;  
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;
        }
    }
}

#define ODOM_START_IDX_FX       (0)     
#define ODOM_START_IDX_FY       (1)     
#define ODOM_START_IDX_X        (2)     
#define ODOM_START_IDX_Y        (3)     
#define ODOM_START_IDX_APPLY    (4)
#define ODOM_START_IDX_BACK     (5)
#define ODOM_START_ITEM_NUM     (6)
#define ODOM_START_STEP         (0.1f)      
#define ODOM_START_MAX          (7.0f)      
#define ODOM_FIELD_STEP         (0.1f)      
#define ODOM_FIELD_MIN          (1.0f)      
#define ODOM_FIELD_MAX          (7.0f)      

static void page_odom_start_draw(void)
{
    char buf[22];
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Field & Start");

    sprintf(buf, "FieldX: %4.2f m", odom_field_edit_x);
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_START_IDX_FX * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_FX));
    
    sprintf(buf, "FieldY: %4.2f m", odom_field_edit_y);
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_START_IDX_FY * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_FY));
    
    sprintf(buf, "StartX: %4.2f m", odom_start_edit_x);
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_START_IDX_X * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_X));
    
    sprintf(buf, "StartY: %4.2f m", odom_start_edit_y);
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_START_IDX_Y * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_Y));
    
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_START_IDX_APPLY * LINE_HEIGHT, "[Apply+Reset]", (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_APPLY));
    
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + ODOM_START_IDX_BACK * LINE_HEIGHT, "[Back]", (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_BACK));

    draw_hint("K1+ K2- K3=OK");
}

static void page_odom_start_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_FX)
        {
            odom_field_edit_x += ODOM_FIELD_STEP;
            if (odom_field_edit_x > ODOM_FIELD_MAX) odom_field_edit_x = ODOM_FIELD_MAX;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_FY)
        {
            odom_field_edit_y += ODOM_FIELD_STEP;
            if (odom_field_edit_y > ODOM_FIELD_MAX) odom_field_edit_y = ODOM_FIELD_MAX;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_X)
        {
            odom_start_edit_x += ODOM_START_STEP;
            if (odom_start_edit_x > ODOM_START_MAX) odom_start_edit_x = ODOM_START_MAX;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_Y)
        {
            odom_start_edit_y += ODOM_START_STEP;
            if (odom_start_edit_y > ODOM_START_MAX) odom_start_edit_y = ODOM_START_MAX;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor > 0)
        {
            g_menu.cursor--;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_FX)
        {
            odom_field_edit_x -= ODOM_FIELD_STEP;
            if (odom_field_edit_x < ODOM_FIELD_MIN) odom_field_edit_x = ODOM_FIELD_MIN;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_FY)
        {
            odom_field_edit_y -= ODOM_FIELD_STEP;
            if (odom_field_edit_y < ODOM_FIELD_MIN) odom_field_edit_y = ODOM_FIELD_MIN;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_X)
        {
            odom_start_edit_x -= ODOM_START_STEP;
            if (odom_start_edit_x < 0.0f) odom_start_edit_x = 0.0f;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_Y)
        {
            odom_start_edit_y -= ODOM_START_STEP;
            if (odom_start_edit_y < 0.0f) odom_start_edit_y = 0.0f;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor < ODOM_START_ITEM_NUM - 1)
        {
            g_menu.cursor++;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }

    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor <= ODOM_START_IDX_Y)   
        {
            g_menu.cursor++;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else if (g_menu.cursor == ODOM_START_IDX_APPLY)
        {
            
            odometry_set_field_size_x(odom_field_edit_x);
            odometry_set_field_size_y(odom_field_edit_y);
            odometry_set_start_offset_x(odom_start_edit_x);
            odometry_set_start_offset_y(odom_start_edit_y);
            odometry_reset_to_start();
            
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_ODOM;
            g_menu.cursor = ODOM_IDX_START;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
        else 
        {
            g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_ODOM;
            g_menu.cursor = ODOM_IDX_START;
            g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        }
    }
}

#define SEARCH_IDX_RUN      (0)
#define SEARCH_IDX_AXIS     (1)
#define SEARCH_IDX_SPEED    (2)
#define SEARCH_IDX_SPDMIN   (3)
#define SEARCH_IDX_ACCEL    (4)
#define SEARCH_IDX_BACK     (5)
#define SEARCH_PAGE_ITEM_NUM (6)

#define SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_STEP    (0.05f) 
#define SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MIN     (0.05f) 
#define SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MAX     (3.00f) 

#define SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP (0.05f) 
#define SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN  (0.00f) 
#define SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX  (1.00f) 

#define SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_STEP  (0.10f) 
#define SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN   (0.00f) 
#define SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX   (5.00f) 

static void page_search_draw(void)
{
    char buf[22];
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Search");

    sprintf(buf, "Run:   %s", vision_search_get_enabled() ? "ON" : "OFF");
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + SEARCH_IDX_RUN * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == SEARCH_IDX_RUN));

    sprintf(buf, "Sweep: %s", vision_search_get_axis() ? "Y axis" : "X axis");
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + SEARCH_IDX_AXIS * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == SEARCH_IDX_AXIS));

    sprintf(buf, "SpdMax: %.2f m/s", vision_search_get_speed());
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + SEARCH_IDX_SPEED * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == SEARCH_IDX_SPEED));

    sprintf(buf, "SpdMin: %.2f m/s", vision_search_get_min_speed());
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + SEARCH_IDX_SPDMIN * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == SEARCH_IDX_SPDMIN));

    sprintf(buf, "Accel: %.2f", vision_search_get_accel());
    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + SEARCH_IDX_ACCEL * LINE_HEIGHT, buf, (g_menu.cursor == SEARCH_IDX_ACCEL));

    draw_menu_item(ITEM_START_Y + SEARCH_IDX_BACK * LINE_HEIGHT, "[Back]", (g_menu.cursor == SEARCH_IDX_BACK));

    draw_hint("K12=move K3=set");
}

static void page_search_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor > 0) { g_menu.cursor--; g_menu.need_redraw = true; }
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        if (g_menu.cursor < SEARCH_PAGE_ITEM_NUM - 1) { g_menu.cursor++; g_menu.need_redraw = true; }
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        switch (g_menu.cursor)
        {
            case SEARCH_IDX_RUN:    
                vision_search_set_enabled(!vision_search_get_enabled());
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case SEARCH_IDX_AXIS:   
                vision_search_set_axis(!vision_search_get_axis());
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case SEARCH_IDX_SPEED:  
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_SPEED_EDIT;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case SEARCH_IDX_SPDMIN: 
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case SEARCH_IDX_ACCEL:  
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT;
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;

            case SEARCH_IDX_BACK:   
                g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
                g_menu.cursor = 1;  
                g_menu.need_redraw = true;
                break;
        }
    }
}

static void page_search_speed_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Patrol Speed");

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_search_get_speed());
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%.2f~%.2f", SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MIN, SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);
    sprintf(buf, "Step:  %.2f", SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_STEP);
    tft180_show_string(8, 88, buf);

    draw_hint("K1- K2+ K3=OK");
}

static void page_search_speed_edit_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;
    float v = vision_search_get_speed();

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        v -= SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v < SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MIN) v = SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MIN;
        vision_search_set_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        v += SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v > SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MAX) v = SEARCH_SPD_EDIT_MAX;
        vision_search_set_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH;
        g_menu.cursor = SEARCH_IDX_SPEED;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        return;
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        char buf[22];
        tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_search_get_speed());
        tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }
}

static void page_search_minspd_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Patrol MinSpd");

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_search_get_min_speed());
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%.2f~%.2f", SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN, SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);
    
    sprintf(buf, "Max=%.2f 0=OFF", vision_search_get_speed());
    tft180_show_string(8, 88, buf);

    draw_hint("K1- K2+ K3=OK");
}

static void page_search_minspd_edit_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;
    float v = vision_search_get_min_speed();

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        v -= SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v < SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN) v = SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MIN;
        vision_search_set_min_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        v += SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v > SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX) v = SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT_MAX;
        vision_search_set_min_speed(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH;
        g_menu.cursor = SEARCH_IDX_SPDMIN;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        return;
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        char buf[22];
        tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/s >  ", vision_search_get_min_speed());
        tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    }
}

static void page_search_accel_edit_draw(void)
{
    tft180_clear();
    draw_title("Accel Limit");

    char buf[22];

    tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/ss > ", vision_search_get_accel());
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);

    sprintf(buf, "Range:%.1f~%.1f", SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN, SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX);
    tft180_show_string(8, 72, buf);
    
    if (vision_search_get_accel() <= 0.001f)
        tft180_show_string(8, 88, "Step:0.10 (0=OFF)");
    else
        tft180_show_string(8, 88, "Step:  0.10      ");

    draw_hint("K1- K2+ K3=OK");
}

static void page_search_accel_edit_handle(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    bool value_changed = false;
    float v = vision_search_get_accel();

    if (has_key_event(ev_up))
    {
        v -= SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v < SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN) v = SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MIN;
        vision_search_set_accel(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_down))
    {
        v += SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_STEP;
        if (v > SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX) v = SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT_MAX;
        vision_search_set_accel(v);
        value_changed = true;
    }
    if (has_key_event(ev_enter))
    {
        g_menu.current_page = MENU_PAGE_SEARCH;
        g_menu.cursor = SEARCH_IDX_ACCEL;
        g_menu.need_redraw = true;
        return;
    }

    if (value_changed)
    {
        char buf[22];
        tft180_set_color(VALUE_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        sprintf(buf, "  < %.2f m/ss > ", vision_search_get_accel());
        tft180_show_string(0, 48, buf);
        tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
        
        if (vision_search_get_accel() <= 0.001f)
            tft180_show_string(8, 88, "Step:0.10 (0=OFF)");
        else
            tft180_show_string(8, 88, "Step:  0.10      ");
    }
}

void menu_system_init(void)
{
    
    tft180_set_dir(TFT180_CROSSWISE);
    tft180_set_color(NORMAL_FG_COLOR, NORMAL_BG_COLOR);
    tft180_init();
    tft180_clear();

    g_menu.current_page     = MENU_PAGE_MAIN;
    g_menu.cursor           = 0;
    g_menu.need_redraw      = true;
    g_menu.refresh_counter  = 0;
    g_menu.editing_motor    = 0;
    g_menu.editing_value    = 0;
    g_menu.speed_step_index = SPEED_STEP_DEFAULT;

    for (uint8 i = 0; i < MOTOR_NUM; i++)
    {
        g_menu.speed_values[i] = 0;
    }
}

void menu_system_update(key_event_enum ev_up, key_event_enum ev_down, key_event_enum ev_enter)
{
    
    switch (g_menu.current_page)
    {
        case MENU_PAGE_MAIN:
            page_main_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_SPEED_CTRL:
            page_speed_ctrl_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            speed_ctrl_periodic_send();   
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_SPEED_EDIT:
            page_speed_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            speed_ctrl_periodic_send();   
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_ENCODER_VIEW:
            page_encoder_view_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_IMU_VIEW:
            page_imu_view_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_CHASSIS_CTRL:
            page_chassis_ctrl_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL:
            page_remote_ctrl_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_RX_VIEW:
            page_remote_rx_view_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_VISION_SPEED_EDIT:
            page_vision_speed_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_VISION_MINSPD_EDIT:
            page_vision_minspd_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_VISION_ACCEL_EDIT:
            page_vision_accel_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_PID_SELECT:
            page_pid_select_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_PID_TUNING:
            page_pid_tuning_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_ODOM:
            page_odom_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_ODOM_START:
            page_odom_start_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH:
            page_search_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_SPEED_EDIT:
            page_search_speed_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT:
            page_search_minspd_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
        case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT:
            page_search_accel_edit_handle(ev_up, ev_down, ev_enter);
            break;
    }

    if (g_menu.need_redraw)
    {
        g_menu.need_redraw = false;

        switch (g_menu.current_page)
        {
            case MENU_PAGE_MAIN:
                page_main_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_SPEED_CTRL:
                page_speed_ctrl_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_SPEED_EDIT:
                page_speed_edit_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_ENCODER_VIEW:
                page_encoder_view_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;     
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_IMU_VIEW:
                page_imu_view_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_CHASSIS_CTRL:
                page_chassis_ctrl_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL:
                page_remote_ctrl_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_RX_VIEW:
                page_remote_rx_view_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_VISION_SPEED_EDIT:
                page_vision_speed_edit_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_VISION_MINSPD_EDIT:
                page_vision_minspd_edit_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_VISION_ACCEL_EDIT:
                page_vision_accel_edit_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_PID_SELECT:
                page_pid_select_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_PID_TUNING:
                page_pid_tuning_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_ODOM:
                page_odom_draw();
                g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;     
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_ODOM_START:
                page_odom_start_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH:
                page_search_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_SPEED_EDIT:
                page_search_speed_edit_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_MINSPD_EDIT:
                page_search_minspd_edit_draw();
                break;
            case MENU_PAGE_SEARCH_ACCEL_EDIT:
                page_search_accel_edit_draw();
                break;
        }
    }

    #define DATA_REFRESH_INTERVAL   (MENU_DATA_REFRESH_MS / KEY_SCAN_PERIOD_MS)

    if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_ENCODER_VIEW ||
        g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_IMU_VIEW    ||
        g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_CHASSIS_CTRL ||
        g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL ||
        g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_RX_VIEW ||
        g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_PID_TUNING  ||
        g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_ODOM)
    {
        g_menu.refresh_counter++;
        if (g_menu.refresh_counter >= DATA_REFRESH_INTERVAL)
        {
            g_menu.refresh_counter = 0;

            if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_ENCODER_VIEW)
            {
                page_encoder_view_refresh();
            }
            else if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_IMU_VIEW)
            {
                page_imu_view_refresh();
            }
            else if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_CHASSIS_CTRL)
            {
                page_chassis_ctrl_refresh();
            }
            else if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_CTRL)
            {
                page_remote_ctrl_refresh();
            }
            else if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_REMOTE_RX_VIEW)
            {
                page_remote_rx_view_refresh();
            }
            else if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_PID_TUNING)
            {
                page_pid_tuning_refresh();
            }
            else if (g_menu.current_page == MENU_PAGE_ODOM)
            {
                page_odom_refresh();
            }
        }
    }
}

menu_page_enum menu_system_get_page(void)
{
    return g_menu.current_page;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
key_driver.h - 按键驱动头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _KEY_DRIVER_H_
#define _KEY_DRIVER_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define KEY_UP_PIN          (P20_0)      
#define KEY_DOWN_PIN        (P20_1)      
#define KEY_ENTER_PIN       (P20_2)      

#define KEY_SCAN_PERIOD_MS      (20)    
#define KEY_DEBOUNCE_COUNT      (2)     
#define KEY_LONG_PRESS_COUNT    (25)    
#define KEY_REPEAT_COUNT        (10)    

typedef enum
{
    KEY_ID_UP = 0,                      
    KEY_ID_DOWN,                        
    KEY_ID_ENTER,                       
    KEY_ID_NUM                          
} key_id_enum;

typedef enum
{
    KEY_EVENT_NONE = 0,                 
    KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS,              
    KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS,               
    KEY_EVENT_LONG_REPEAT,              
} key_event_enum;

void key_driver_init(void);

void key_driver_scan_all(void);

key_event_enum key_driver_get_event(key_id_enum id);

void key_driver_clear_all(void);

bool key_driver_is_pressed(key_id_enum id);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
key_driver.c - 按键驱动实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "key_driver.h"

static const gpio_pin_enum key_pin_table[KEY_ID_NUM] =
{
    KEY_UP_PIN,         
    KEY_DOWN_PIN,       
    KEY_ENTER_PIN,      
};

typedef enum
{
    KEY_STATE_IDLE = 0,         
    KEY_STATE_DEBOUNCE,         
    KEY_STATE_PRESSED,          
} key_fsm_state_enum;

typedef struct
{
    key_fsm_state_enum  state;          
    uint8               debounce_cnt;   
    uint16              hold_count;     
    bool                long_triggered; 
    key_event_enum      event;          
} key_context_t;

static key_context_t g_keys[KEY_ID_NUM];

static bool key_read_raw(key_id_enum id)
{
    if (id >= KEY_ID_NUM) return false;
    return (gpio_get_level(key_pin_table[id]) == 0);
}

static void key_process_one(key_id_enum id)
{
    key_context_t *k = &g_keys[id];
    bool pressed = key_read_raw(id);

    switch (k->state)
    {
        
        case KEY_STATE_IDLE:
            if (pressed)
            {
                k->state = KEY_STATE_DEBOUNCE;
                k->debounce_cnt = 1;
            }
            break;

        case KEY_STATE_DEBOUNCE:
            if (pressed)
            {
                k->debounce_cnt++;
                if (k->debounce_cnt >= KEY_DEBOUNCE_COUNT)
                {
                    
                    k->state = KEY_STATE_PRESSED;
                    k->hold_count = 0;
                    k->long_triggered = false;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                
                k->state = KEY_STATE_IDLE;
            }
            break;

        case KEY_STATE_PRESSED:
            if (pressed)
            {
                k->hold_count++;

                if (!k->long_triggered)
                {
                    
                    if (k->hold_count >= KEY_LONG_PRESS_COUNT)
                    {
                        k->event = KEY_EVENT_LONG_PRESS;
                        k->long_triggered = true;
                        k->hold_count = 0;  
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    
                    if (k->hold_count >= KEY_REPEAT_COUNT)
                    {
                        k->event = KEY_EVENT_LONG_REPEAT;
                        k->hold_count = 0;  
                    }
                }
            }
            else
            {
                
                if (!k->long_triggered)
                {
                    
                    k->event = KEY_EVENT_SHORT_PRESS;
                }

                k->state = KEY_STATE_IDLE;
                k->hold_count = 0;
                k->long_triggered = false;
            }
            break;

        default:
            k->state = KEY_STATE_IDLE;
            break;
    }
}

void key_driver_init(void)
{
    
    for (uint8 i = 0; i < KEY_ID_NUM; i++)
    {
        gpio_init(key_pin_table[i], GPI, 1, GPI_PULL_UP);
    }

    for (uint8 i = 0; i < KEY_ID_NUM; i++)
    {
        g_keys[i].state          = KEY_STATE_IDLE;
        g_keys[i].debounce_cnt   = 0;
        g_keys[i].hold_count     = 0;
        g_keys[i].long_triggered = false;
        g_keys[i].event          = KEY_EVENT_NONE;
    }
}

void key_driver_scan_all(void)
{
    for (uint8 i = 0; i < KEY_ID_NUM; i++)
    {
        key_process_one((key_id_enum)i);
    }
}

key_event_enum key_driver_get_event(key_id_enum id)
{
    if (id >= KEY_ID_NUM) return KEY_EVENT_NONE;

    key_event_enum ev = g_keys[id].event;
    g_keys[id].event = KEY_EVENT_NONE;  
    return ev;
}

void key_driver_clear_all(void)
{
    for (uint8 i = 0; i < KEY_ID_NUM; i++)
    {
        g_keys[i].event = KEY_EVENT_NONE;
    }
}

bool key_driver_is_pressed(key_id_enum id)
{
    if (id >= KEY_ID_NUM) return false;
    return key_read_raw(id);
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
blind_box.h - 盲盒模块头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _BLIND_BOX_H_
#define _BLIND_BOX_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define BLIND_BOX_DEFAULT_EN    (0)       
#define BB_T_ARM_MS             (1000U)   
#define BB_T_Z_DEFAULT_S        (8.0f)    
#define BB_ESTOP_HOLD_MS        (100U)    

typedef enum
{
    BB_STATE_UNARMED = 0,   
    BB_STATE_PRE_ARMED,     
    BB_STATE_ARMED,         
    BB_STATE_LANDING,       
} bb_state_enum;

void blind_box_init(void);

void blind_box_tick(void);

void blind_box_on_nrf_frame(void);

uint8 blind_box_is_landing(void);

uint8 blind_box_get_land_flag(void);

bb_state_enum blind_box_get_state(void);

char blind_box_get_state_char(void);

float blind_box_get_z_elapsed_s(void);

void  blind_box_set_enabled(uint8 en);
uint8 blind_box_get_enabled(void);

void  blind_box_set_tz(float tz_s);
float blind_box_get_tz(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
blind_box.c - 盲盒模块实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "blind_box.h"
#include "nrf24l01_driver.h"
#include "vision_tracker.h"
#include "chassis_controller.h"

#define BB_TICK_MS              (5U)                          
#define BB_T_ARM_TICKS          (BB_T_ARM_MS / BB_TICK_MS)    
#define BB_ESTOP_HOLD_TICKS     (BB_ESTOP_HOLD_MS / BB_TICK_MS) 

static volatile bb_state_enum s_state = BB_STATE_UNARMED;   
static volatile uint8  s_enable       = BLIND_BOX_DEFAULT_EN; 
static volatile float  s_tz_s         = BB_T_Z_DEFAULT_S;     
static volatile uint32 s_tick         = 0;                    
static volatile uint32 s_last_pkt_tick = 0;                   
static volatile uint8  s_pkt_seen     = 0;                    
static uint32 s_z_accum_ms = 0;                               

void blind_box_init(void)
{
    s_state         = BB_STATE_UNARMED;
    s_enable        = BLIND_BOX_DEFAULT_EN;
    s_tz_s          = BB_T_Z_DEFAULT_S;
    s_tick          = 0;
    s_last_pkt_tick = 0;
    s_pkt_seen      = 0;
    s_z_accum_ms    = 0;
}

void blind_box_on_nrf_frame(void)
{
    if(!s_enable) return;

#if ENABLE_NRF_REMOTE
    if(g_nrf_rx_buf[0] != 0xAA ||
       g_nrf_rx_buf[1] != 0xAF ||
       g_nrf_rx_buf[2] != 0x03)
    {
        return; 
    }
#endif

    s_last_pkt_tick = s_tick;
    s_pkt_seen      = 1;
}

void blind_box_tick(void)
{
    s_tick++;   

    if(!s_enable)
    {
        
        s_state      = BB_STATE_UNARMED;
        s_pkt_seen   = 0;
        s_z_accum_ms = 0;
        return;
    }

    switch(s_state)
    {
        case BB_STATE_UNARMED:
            
            if(s_pkt_seen)
            {
                s_state = BB_STATE_PRE_ARMED;
            }
            break;

        case BB_STATE_PRE_ARMED:
            
            if((s_tick - s_last_pkt_tick) >= BB_T_ARM_TICKS)
            {
                s_state      = BB_STATE_ARMED;
                s_z_accum_ms = 0;
            }
            break;

        case BB_STATE_ARMED:
        {
            
            if((s_tick - s_last_pkt_tick) < BB_ESTOP_HOLD_TICKS)
            {
                s_z_accum_ms = 0;
                break;
            }

            if(vision_tracker_get_mode() == CONTROL_MODE_VISION &&
               chassis_is_enabled() &&
               vision_tracker_is_searching())
            {
                s_z_accum_ms += BB_TICK_MS;
                if((float)s_z_accum_ms >= s_tz_s * 1000.0f)
                {
                    s_state = BB_STATE_LANDING;   
                }
            }
            else
            {
                s_z_accum_ms = 0;
            }
            break;
        }

        case BB_STATE_LANDING:
            
            break;

        default:
            s_state = BB_STATE_UNARMED;
            break;
    }
}

uint8 blind_box_is_landing(void)
{
    return (s_enable && s_state == BB_STATE_LANDING) ? 1U : 0U;
}

uint8 blind_box_get_land_flag(void)
{
    return blind_box_is_landing();
}

bb_state_enum blind_box_get_state(void)
{
    return s_state;
}

char blind_box_get_state_char(void)
{
    switch(s_state)
    {
        case BB_STATE_PRE_ARMED: return 'P';
        case BB_STATE_ARMED:     return 'A';
        case BB_STATE_LANDING:   return 'L';
        case BB_STATE_UNARMED:
        default:                 return 'U';
    }
}

float blind_box_get_z_elapsed_s(void)
{
    return (float)s_z_accum_ms / 1000.0f;
}

void blind_box_set_enabled(uint8 en)
{
    s_enable = en ? 1U : 0U;
}

uint8 blind_box_get_enabled(void)
{
    return s_enable;
}

void blind_box_set_tz(float tz_s)
{
    if(tz_s < 1.0f)  tz_s = 1.0f;
    if(tz_s > 30.0f) tz_s = 30.0f;
    s_tz_s = tz_s;
}

float blind_box_get_tz(void)
{
    return s_tz_s;
}

B.8　无人机侧完整源代码
项目说明：
  基于 CYT4BB7 双核(M7_0 + M7_1)的无人机飞控系统
  功能：四旋翼飞行控制、视觉识别、光流定位、自动起飞降落
  M7_0：飞行控制、姿态解算、传感器融合、遥控处理
  M7_1：摄像头视觉识别、WiFi图传、目标识别

================================================================================


================================================================================
                                   第一部分：主函数代码                                   
================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
main_cm7_0.c - M7_0 核心主函数
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "display_config.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include "imu/IMU.h"
#include "imu/ALL_DATA.h"
#include "protocol/anonym_protocol.h"
#include "remote_control/remote_control.h"
#include "menu/menu.h"
#include "shared_data.h"
#include "buzzer/buzzer.h"
#if VOFA_STREAM_ENABLE
#include "protocol/vofa.h"
#endif
#include "control/motor_uart_out.h"
#include "control/attitude_ctrl.h"
#include "tof/tof.h"
#include "flow/flow.h"
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart.h"
#include "code1/shared_memory.h"
#include "local_camera_shared.h"
#include "battery/battery.h"
#include "blind_box/air_blind_box.h"
#include "auto_takeoff/air_auto_takeoff.h"

#define M7_0_MENU_TICK_MODE   (1)   

volatile vision_data_t g_vision_data = {0};

static void local_camera_target_update(uint32 now)
{
    static uint32 last_frame_seq = 0U;
    static uint32 last_frame_ms = 0U;
    volatile uint32 *frame_seq = (volatile uint32 *)LOCAL_CAMERA_FRAME_SEQ_ADDR;
    volatile drone_target_t *target = (volatile drone_target_t *)SHARED_DRONE_TARGET_ADDR;
    volatile search_state_t *search = (volatile search_state_t *)SHARED_SEARCH_FLAG_ADDR;

    SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)frame_seq, sizeof(*frame_seq));
    if(*frame_seq != last_frame_seq)
    {
        last_frame_seq = *frame_seq;
        last_frame_ms = now;
        SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)target, sizeof(*target));
        g_vision_data.error_x_px = target->err_x;
        g_vision_data.error_y_px = target->err_y;
        g_vision_data.detected = target->valid;
        g_vision_data.valid = target->valid;
        
        SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)search, sizeof(*search));
        g_vision_data.searching = search->searching;
        g_vision_data.last_update_ms = now;
        g_vision_data.frame_count++;
    }
    else if((now - last_frame_ms) > VISION_TIMEOUT_MS)
    {
        g_vision_data.detected = 0U;
        g_vision_data.valid = 0U;
        g_vision_data.searching = 0U;   
    }
}

static void shared_flightdata_update(void)
{
    
    g_shared_flight_data.roll_deg   = SystemIMU.angle.roll;
    g_shared_flight_data.pitch_deg  = SystemIMU.angle.pitch;
    g_shared_flight_data.yaw_deg    = SystemIMU.angle.yaw;
    g_shared_flight_data.gyro_z_dps = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[2];

    g_shared_flight_data.land_cmd   = air_blind_box_is_landing();

    g_shared_flight_data.takeoff_ready = air_auto_takeoff_is_ready_to_send();

    SCB_CleanDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)&g_shared_flight_data,
                            (int32_t)sizeof(g_shared_flight_data));
}

int main(void)
{
    clock_init(SYSTEM_CLOCK_250M);
    debug_init();
    buzzer_init();

    IMU_gyro_offest_Init_All();

    attitude_ctrl_init();
    motor_uart_out_init();

    air_blind_box_init();

    air_auto_takeoff_init();

    remote_control_init();

    tof_init();

    flow_init();

    pit_ms_init(PIT_CH0, 1);   
    pit_enable(PIT_CH0);

#if 0
#if !PID_TUNER_ENABLE
    Anonym_ProtocolInit();
#endif
#endif

    menu_init();
#if VOFA_STREAM_ENABLE
    vofa_init();
#endif

    uint32 last_shared_ms = 0;

    while(true)
    {
        uint32 now = system_get_ms();

#if VOFA_STREAM_ENABLE
        vofa_pid_tuning_stream_tick(now);
#endif

        local_camera_target_update(now);

        air_auto_takeoff_tick(now);

        air_blind_box_tick(now);

        if(now - last_shared_ms >= 10U)
        {
            last_shared_ms = now;
            shared_flightdata_update();
        }

#if   (M7_0_MENU_TICK_MODE == 0)
        menu_tick();                              
#elif (M7_0_MENU_TICK_MODE == 1)
        
        {
            static uint32 s_last_menu_ms = 0;
            if(now - s_last_menu_ms >= 50U)
            {
                s_last_menu_ms = now;
                menu_tick();
            }
        }
#else 
        
#endif
    }
}

void pit0_ch0_isr()
{
    pit_isr_flag_clear(PIT_CH0);

    Attitude_Update();
    
    motor_uart_out_flush();

    tof_update_1ms(system_get_ms());

    flow_update_1ms(system_get_ms());

    attitude_ctrl_tick_1ms();

    static uint8 rc_cnt = 0;
    if(++rc_cnt >= 10) {
        rc_cnt = 0;
        remote_control_control_tick();
    }
}

void gpio_18_exti_isr() {}
void gpio_19_exti_isr() {}
void gpio_20_exti_isr() {}
void gpio_21_exti_isr() {}
void gpio_22_exti_isr() {}
void gpio_23_exti_isr() {}

#if DEBUG_UART_USE_INTERRUPT

    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
main_cm7_1.c - M7_1 核心主函数
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "camera_vision.h"
#include "shared_memory.h"
#include "local_camera_shared.h"
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart_tx.h"

#pragma location = SHARED_VISION_CMD_ADDR
__no_init vision_cmd_t g_vision_cmd;

#pragma location = SHARED_IMG_FRESH_ADDR
__no_init uint8 g_image_fresh_flag;

#pragma location = SHARED_M7_1_STATUS_ADDR
__no_init uint8 g_m7_1_status;

#pragma location = SHARED_CAR_ERROR_ADDR
__no_init car_error_t g_car_error;

#pragma location = SHARED_DRONE_TARGET_ADDR
__no_init drone_target_t g_drone_target;

#pragma location = SHARED_SEARCH_FLAG_ADDR
__no_init search_state_t g_search_state;

#pragma location = LOCAL_CAMERA_FRAME_SEQ_ADDR
__no_init uint32 g_local_camera_frame_seq;

static void vision_shared_init(void)
{
    memset(&g_vision_cmd, 0, sizeof(g_vision_cmd));
    g_image_fresh_flag = 0U;
    g_m7_1_status = (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_INIT;
    memset(&g_car_error, 0, sizeof(g_car_error));
    memset(&g_drone_target, 0, sizeof(g_drone_target));
    memset(&g_search_state, 0, sizeof(g_search_state));
    g_local_camera_frame_seq = 0U;

    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(g_vision_cmd));
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_image_fresh_flag, 1);
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_m7_1_status, 1);
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_car_error, sizeof(g_car_error));
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_drone_target, sizeof(g_drone_target));
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_search_state, sizeof(g_search_state));
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_local_camera_frame_seq, sizeof(g_local_camera_frame_seq));
}

int main(void)
{
    clock_init(SYSTEM_CLOCK_250M);

    vision_shared_init();

    vision_uart_tx_init();

    if(camera_vision_init() != 0U)
    {
        g_m7_1_status = (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_ERROR;
        g_drone_target.valid = 0U;
        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_m7_1_status, 1);
        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_drone_target, sizeof(g_drone_target));

        while(true)
        {
            system_delay_ms(10);
        }
    }

    g_m7_1_status = (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_RUNNING;
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_m7_1_status, 1);

    while(true)
    {
        
        if(camera_vision_update())
        {
            g_local_camera_frame_seq++;
            SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_local_camera_frame_seq, sizeof(g_local_camera_frame_seq));

            vision_uart_tx_send();
        }
    }
}

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

void attitude_ctrl_init(void);
