--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
attitude_ctrl.c - 姿态控制实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "control/attitude_ctrl.h"
#include "control/pid.h"
#include "control/mixer_x.h"
#include "control/motor_uart_out.h"
#include "remote_control/remote_control.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include "protocol/vofa.h"
#include "tof/tof.h"
#include "flow/flow.h"
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart.h"
#include "battery/battery.h"
#include "blind_box/air_blind_box.h"
#include "auto_takeoff/air_auto_takeoff.h"
#include "shared_data.h"
#include <math.h>

#define CTRL_THR_MAX          (10000)
#define CTRL_THR_MIN          (0)

#define HEIGHT_GATE_DIFF_M        (0.40f)
#define HEIGHT_FREEZE_TMAX_MS     (1000U)

#define CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG     (10.0f)
#define CTRL_ANGLE_MEAS_MIN_DEG    (-30.0f)
#define CTRL_ANGLE_MEAS_MAX_DEG    (30.0f)
#define CTRL_ANGLE_MAX_STEP_DEG    (10.0f)
#define FLOW_HORIZONTAL_ASSIST_EN  (1)

#define FLOW_CORR_SLEW_DEG_PER_TICK   (0.15f)

volatile float g_trim_roll_deg  = 4.1f;
volatile float g_trim_pitch_deg = -0.7f;
volatile float g_vision_en         = 1.0f;    

volatile uint8 g_vision_tracking_en = 1U;

volatile float g_vision_kp_x       = 0.02f;   
volatile float g_vision_kp_y       = 0.02f;   
volatile float g_vision_deadband_x = 0.0f;    
volatile float g_vision_deadband_y = 0.0f;    
volatile float g_vision_max_tilt   = 15.0f;    
volatile float g_vision_lpf_alpha  = 0.15f;   
volatile float g_vision_sign_x     = -1.0f;   
volatile float g_vision_sign_y     = -1.0f;   
volatile float g_vision_kd_x       = 0.0f;    
volatile float g_vision_kd_y       = 0.0f;    
volatile float g_flow_pos_kp   = 0.0f;    
volatile float g_flow_pos_vmax = 2.8f;    
volatile float g_vision_roll_target_dbg  = 0.0f;
volatile float g_vision_pitch_target_dbg = 0.0f;

volatile float g_land_rate     = 0.5f;    
volatile float g_land_cut_h    = 0.15f;   
volatile float g_land_still_ms = 300.0f;  
volatile uint8 g_land_phase_dbg = 0U;     

typedef struct
{
    float last_valid_deg;
    uint8 initialized;
    uint8 fault_count;
} angle_guard_t;

static angle_guard_t s_roll_angle_guard;
static angle_guard_t s_pitch_angle_guard;
static float s_flow_pitch_corr = 0.0f;
static float s_flow_roll_corr  = 0.0f;

typedef enum { LAND_NONE = 0, LAND_DESCEND = 1, LAND_CUT = 2 } land_phase_t;
#define LAND_STILL_VZ_MPS   (0.10f)   

static land_phase_t s_land_phase          = LAND_NONE;
static float        s_land_thr            = 0.0f;   
static uint8        s_land_thr_valid      = 0U;     
static uint32       s_land_still_start_ms = 0U;     
static int16        s_prev_thr_total      = 0;      

static inline float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static inline int16 clamp_i16(int32 v, int16 lo, int16 hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return (int16)v;
}

static float guard_angle_measure(angle_guard_t *guard, float raw_angle_deg)
{
    float limited_angle_deg;
    float angle_step_deg;

    if(!isfinite(raw_angle_deg))
    {
        guard->fault_count++;
        return guard->last_valid_deg;
    }

    limited_angle_deg = clampf(raw_angle_deg, CTRL_ANGLE_MEAS_MIN_DEG, CTRL_ANGLE_MEAS_MAX_DEG);

    if(!guard->initialized)
    {
        guard->last_valid_deg = limited_angle_deg;
        guard->initialized = 1U;
        guard->fault_count = 0U;
        return limited_angle_deg;
    }

    angle_step_deg = limited_angle_deg - guard->last_valid_deg;
    if(fabsf(angle_step_deg) > CTRL_ANGLE_MAX_STEP_DEG)
    {
        guard->fault_count++;
        return guard->last_valid_deg;
    }

    guard->last_valid_deg = limited_angle_deg;
    guard->fault_count = 0U;
    return limited_angle_deg;
}

static int16 map_thr_from_rc(uint16 thr_us)
{
    
    int32 t = (int32)thr_us - 100;
    if(t < 0) t = 0;
    if(t > 100) t = 100;
    return (int16)(t * 100);
}

static float wrap_deg_180(float deg)
{
    while(deg > 180.0f) deg -= 360.0f;
    while(deg < -180.0f) deg += 360.0f;
    return deg;
}

void attitude_ctrl_init(void)
{
    PID_Reset(&RollPID);
    PID_Reset(&PitchPID);
    PID_Reset(&YawPID);
    PID_Reset(&RollRatePID);
    PID_Reset(&PitchRatePID);
    PID_Reset(&YawRatePID);
    PID_Reset(&HeightPID);
    PID_Reset(&HeightSpeedPID);
    PID_Reset(&FlowSpeedPIDX);
    PID_Reset(&FlowSpeedPIDY);
    PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDX);
    PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDY);

    s_flow_pitch_corr = 0.0f;
    s_flow_roll_corr  = 0.0f;

    motor_uart_out_set_cached((mixer_out_t){0,0,0,0});
}

void attitude_ctrl_tick_1ms(void)
{
    
    static uint8 s_prev_unlocked = 0U;
    if(g_rc_flags.unlocked && !s_prev_unlocked)
    {
        flow_reset_pos();
    }
    
    if(!g_rc_flags.unlocked && s_prev_unlocked)
    {
        air_blind_box_reset();

        if(g_auto_takeoff_en >= 0.5f)
        {
            air_auto_takeoff_reset();
        }
        s_land_phase          = LAND_NONE;
        s_land_thr_valid      = 0U;
        s_land_still_start_ms = 0U;
        g_land_phase_dbg      = 0U;
    }
    s_prev_unlocked = g_rc_flags.unlocked ? 1U : 0U;

    float target_roll_deg  = g_trim_roll_deg  + g_rc_cmd.roll_cmd;
    float target_pitch_deg = g_trim_pitch_deg + g_rc_cmd.pitch_cmd;

    target_roll_deg = clampf(target_roll_deg, -CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG, CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG);
    target_pitch_deg = clampf(target_pitch_deg, -CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG, CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG);

    int16 thr;
    auto_takeoff_state_t at_state = air_auto_takeoff_get_state();
    if(at_state == AT_ABORT)
    {
        
        extern volatile float g_takeoff_abort_throttle;
        thr = (int16)g_takeoff_abort_throttle;
    }
    else if(g_auto_height_control)
    {

        thr = (int16)air_auto_takeoff_get_throttle();
    }
    else
    {
        
        thr = map_thr_from_rc(g_rc_cmd.throttle_us);
    }
    thr = clamp_i16(thr, CTRL_THR_MIN, CTRL_THR_MAX);
    g_vofa_thr_cmd = (float)thr;

    float roll_meas_deg = guard_angle_measure(&s_roll_angle_guard, SystemIMU.angle.roll);
    float pitch_meas_deg = guard_angle_measure(&s_pitch_angle_guard, SystemIMU.angle.pitch);

    float target_yaw_deg = 0.0f;
    float yaw_meas_deg = wrap_deg_180(SystemIMU.angle.yaw);
    float yaw_err_deg = wrap_deg_180(target_yaw_deg - yaw_meas_deg);

    g_vofa_roll_angle_target = target_roll_deg;         
    g_vofa_roll_angle_current = roll_meas_deg;
    g_vofa_pitch_angle_target = target_pitch_deg;        
    g_vofa_pitch_angle_current = pitch_meas_deg;
    g_vofa_yaw_angle_target = target_yaw_deg;            
    g_vofa_yaw_angle_current = yaw_meas_deg;

    if(!g_rc_flags.unlocked)
    {
        PID_Reset(&RollPID);
        PID_Reset(&PitchPID);
        PID_Reset(&YawPID);
        PID_Reset(&RollRatePID);
        PID_Reset(&PitchRatePID);
        PID_Reset(&YawRatePID);
        PID_Reset(&HeightPID);
        PID_Reset(&HeightSpeedPID);
        PID_Reset(&FlowSpeedPIDX);
        PID_Reset(&FlowSpeedPIDY);
        PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDX);
        PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDY);
        motor_uart_out_set_cached((mixer_out_t){0,0,0,0});

        g_vofa_roll_rate_target = RollRatePID.derivative;    
        g_vofa_roll_rate_current = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0];
        g_vofa_pitch_rate_target = PitchRatePID.derivative;   
        g_vofa_pitch_rate_current = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1];
        g_vofa_h_outer_target = 0.0f;
        g_vofa_h_outer_current = g_tof_state.height_m;
        g_vofa_h_inner_target = 0.0f;
        g_vofa_h_inner_current = g_tof_state.vz_mps;
        g_vofa_z_velocity = g_tof_state.vz_mps;
        g_vofa_flow_hold_on = 0.0f;   
        g_vofa_tracking_on  = 0.0f;
        s_flow_pitch_corr = 0.0f;     
        s_flow_roll_corr  = 0.0f;
        return;
    }

    {
        uint32 land_now_ms = system_get_ms();
        switch(s_land_phase)
        {
            case LAND_NONE:
                if(air_blind_box_is_landing() && g_rc_flags.unlocked && (g_rc_cmd.height_hold != 0U))
                {
                    s_land_phase          = LAND_DESCEND;
                    s_land_still_start_ms = 0U;
                }
                break;

            case LAND_DESCEND:
                
                if(s_land_thr_valid && s_land_thr <= 0.5f)
                {
                    s_land_phase = LAND_CUT;
                }
                
                else if(g_tof_state.height_m < g_land_cut_h && fabsf(g_tof_state.vz_mps) < LAND_STILL_VZ_MPS)
                {
                    if(s_land_still_start_ms == 0U) s_land_still_start_ms = land_now_ms;
                    if((land_now_ms - s_land_still_start_ms) >= (uint32)g_land_still_ms)
                    {
                        s_land_phase = LAND_CUT;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    s_land_still_start_ms = 0U;   
                }
                break;

            case LAND_CUT:
                g_rc_flags.unlocked = 0U;   
                break;

            default:
                s_land_phase = LAND_NONE;
                break;
        }
        g_land_phase_dbg = (uint8)s_land_phase;
    }

    float flow_roll_corr_deg  = 0.0f;
    float flow_pitch_corr_deg = 0.0f;
    float flow_pitch_corr_target = 0.0f;  
    float flow_roll_corr_target  = 0.0f; 

    uint8 flow_hold_on   = (FLOW_HORIZONTAL_ASSIST_EN != 0) && (g_vision_en > 0.5f) && (g_rc_cmd.pos_hold != 0U)
                           && (air_auto_takeoff_horizontal_ok() != 0U);
    uint8 track_on       = flow_hold_on && (g_vision_tracking_en == 1U) && g_vision_data.valid;
    
    if(s_land_phase == LAND_DESCEND || s_land_phase == LAND_CUT)
    {
        track_on = 0U;
    }
    uint8 roll_centered  = (fabsf(g_rc_cmd.roll_cmd)  < 0.1f);   
    uint8 pitch_centered = (fabsf(g_rc_cmd.pitch_cmd) < 0.1f);

    g_vofa_flow_hold_on = flow_hold_on ? 1.0f : 0.0f;   
    g_vofa_tracking_on  = track_on     ? 1.0f : 0.0f;   

    if(flow_hold_on)
    {
        float vx_target = 0.0f;   
        float vy_target = 0.0f;   

        if(track_on)
        {
            
            float ex = g_vision_data.error_x_px;   
            float ey = g_vision_data.error_y_px;   
            float ex_ds = (fabsf(ex) >= g_vision_deadband_x) ? copysignf(fabsf(ex) - g_vision_deadband_x, ex) : 0.0f;
            float ey_ds = (fabsf(ey) >= g_vision_deadband_y) ? copysignf(fabsf(ey) - g_vision_deadband_y, ey) : 0.0f;

            PID_Update(&FlowPosPIDX, 0.0f, ex_ds);
            PID_Update(&FlowPosPIDY, 0.0f, ey_ds);
            vx_target = g_vision_sign_x * FlowPosPIDX.output;
            vy_target = g_vision_sign_y * FlowPosPIDY.output;
            
            vx_target = clampf(vx_target, -g_flow_pos_vmax, g_flow_pos_vmax);
            vy_target = clampf(vy_target, -g_flow_pos_vmax, g_flow_pos_vmax);
            flow_reset_pos();
        }
        else
        {
            
            PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDX);
            PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDY);

            if(!pitch_centered || !roll_centered) flow_reset_pos();

            vx_target = clampf(-g_flow_pos_kp * g_flow_state.pos_x, -g_flow_pos_vmax, g_flow_pos_vmax);
            vy_target = clampf(-g_flow_pos_kp * g_flow_state.pos_y, -g_flow_pos_vmax, g_flow_pos_vmax);
        }
        PID_Update(&FlowSpeedPIDX, vx_target, g_flow_state.deb_vx);
        PID_Update(&FlowSpeedPIDY, vy_target, g_flow_state.deb_vy);

        flow_pitch_corr_target = clampf(FlowSpeedPIDX.output, -g_vision_max_tilt, g_vision_max_tilt);
        flow_roll_corr_target  = clampf(FlowSpeedPIDY.output, -g_vision_max_tilt, g_vision_max_tilt);

        g_vofa_flow_speed_x_target  = vx_target;                    
        g_vofa_flow_speed_x_current = g_flow_state.deb_vx;
        g_vofa_flow_speed_y_target  = vy_target;
        g_vofa_flow_speed_y_current = g_flow_state.deb_vy;
    }
    else
    {
        PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDX);
        PID_Reset(&FlowPosPIDY);
        PID_Reset(&FlowSpeedPIDX);
        PID_Reset(&FlowSpeedPIDY);
        flow_reset_pos();
        g_vofa_flow_speed_x_target  = 0.0f;
        g_vofa_flow_speed_x_current = g_flow_state.deb_vx;
        g_vofa_flow_speed_y_target  = 0.0f;
        g_vofa_flow_speed_y_current = g_flow_state.deb_vy;
    }

    s_flow_pitch_corr += clampf(flow_pitch_corr_target - s_flow_pitch_corr,
                                -FLOW_CORR_SLEW_DEG_PER_TICK, FLOW_CORR_SLEW_DEG_PER_TICK);
    s_flow_roll_corr  += clampf(flow_roll_corr_target  - s_flow_roll_corr,
                                -FLOW_CORR_SLEW_DEG_PER_TICK, FLOW_CORR_SLEW_DEG_PER_TICK);
    flow_pitch_corr_deg = s_flow_pitch_corr;
    flow_roll_corr_deg  = s_flow_roll_corr;

    g_vision_pitch_target_dbg = flow_pitch_corr_deg;   
    g_vision_roll_target_dbg  = flow_roll_corr_deg;

    target_roll_deg  = clampf(target_roll_deg  + flow_roll_corr_deg,  -CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG, CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG);
    target_pitch_deg = clampf(target_pitch_deg + flow_pitch_corr_deg, -CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG, CTRL_MAX_TILT_DEG);

    g_vofa_roll_angle_target  = target_roll_deg;
    g_vofa_pitch_angle_target = target_pitch_deg;

    PID_Update(&RollPID, target_roll_deg, roll_meas_deg);
    PID_Update(&PitchPID, target_pitch_deg, pitch_meas_deg);

    PID_Update(&YawPID, 0.0f, -yaw_err_deg);

    float roll_rate_cmd_dps = RollPID.output;
    float pitch_rate_cmd_dps = PitchPID.output;
    float yaw_rate_cmd_dps = YawPID.output;

    PID_Update(&RollRatePID, roll_rate_cmd_dps, SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0]);
    PID_Update(&PitchRatePID, pitch_rate_cmd_dps, SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1]);
    PID_Update(&YawRatePID, yaw_rate_cmd_dps, SystemIMU.gyro_deg[2]);

    float u_roll = RollRatePID.output;
    float u_pitch = PitchRatePID.output;
    float u_yaw = YawRatePID.output;

    uint8 height_ctrl_on = (g_rc_cmd.height_hold != 0U) || (g_auto_height_control != 0U);

    float height_output = 0.0f;
    if(height_ctrl_on)
    {
        
        static float target_height = 0.0f;
        static uint8 height_ctrl_last = 0;

        if(g_auto_height_control)
        {
            
            target_height = air_auto_takeoff_get_target_height();
        }
        else if(!height_ctrl_last)
        {
            
            target_height = g_tof_state.height_m;
        }
        height_ctrl_last = height_ctrl_on;

        static uint8  s_h_freeze_active   = 0U;
        static uint32 s_h_freeze_start_ms = 0U;
        static float  s_h_last_output     = 0.0f;
        float height_diff = fabsf(target_height - g_tof_state.height_m);
        uint32 h_gate_now_ms = system_get_ms();

        if(height_diff > HEIGHT_GATE_DIFF_M)
        {
            if(!s_h_freeze_active)
            {
                s_h_freeze_active   = 1U;
                s_h_freeze_start_ms = h_gate_now_ms;
            }
            if((h_gate_now_ms - s_h_freeze_start_ms) >= HEIGHT_FREEZE_TMAX_MS)
            {
                s_h_freeze_active = 0U;   
            }
        }
        else
        {
            s_h_freeze_active = 0U;       
        }

        if(s_h_freeze_active)
        {
            
            height_output = s_h_last_output;
        }
        else
        {
            
            PID_Update(&HeightPID, target_height, g_tof_state.height_m);
            PID_Update(&HeightSpeedPID, HeightPID.output, g_tof_state.vz_mps);
            height_output = HeightSpeedPID.output;
            s_h_last_output = height_output;   
        }

        g_vofa_h_outer_target = target_height;
        g_vofa_h_outer_current = g_tof_state.height_m;
        g_vofa_h_inner_target = HeightPID.output;

        float acc_z_mps2 = g_vofa_smooth_acc_z;  
        static float acc_velocity = 0.0f;         
        static uint32 last_ms = 0;

        if(last_ms == 0) last_ms = system_get_ms();
        float dt = (system_get_ms() - last_ms) / 1000.0f; 
        last_ms = system_get_ms();

        acc_velocity += acc_z_mps2 * dt;

        acc_velocity *= 0.995f;  

        g_vofa_h_inner_current = acc_velocity;    
        g_vofa_z_velocity = acc_velocity;         
        g_vofa_height_hold_enabled = 1.0f;       
    }
    else
    {
        
        PID_Reset(&HeightPID);
        PID_Reset(&HeightSpeedPID);
        g_vofa_h_outer_target = g_tof_state.height_m;
        g_vofa_h_outer_current = g_tof_state.height_m;
        g_vofa_h_inner_target = 0.0f;

        float acc_z_mps2 = g_vofa_smooth_acc_z;
        static float acc_velocity_idle = 0.0f;
        static uint32 last_ms_idle = 0;

        if(last_ms_idle == 0) last_ms_idle = system_get_ms();
        float dt = (system_get_ms() - last_ms_idle) / 1000.0f;
        last_ms_idle = system_get_ms();

        acc_velocity_idle += acc_z_mps2 * dt;
        acc_velocity_idle *= 0.995f;  

        g_vofa_h_inner_current = acc_velocity_idle;
        g_vofa_z_velocity = acc_velocity_idle;
        g_vofa_height_hold_enabled = 0.0f;       
    }

    g_vofa_roll_rate_target = roll_rate_cmd_dps;
    g_vofa_roll_rate_current = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0];
    g_vofa_pitch_rate_target = pitch_rate_cmd_dps;
    g_vofa_pitch_rate_current = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1];
    g_vofa_yaw_rate_target = yaw_rate_cmd_dps;
    g_vofa_yaw_rate_current = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[2];

    g_vofa_tof_init_status = (float)g_tof_init_ret;
    g_vofa_tof_working = (g_tof_state.raw_mm > 0U && g_tof_state.raw_mm < 2000U) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;

    float pitch_rad = SystemIMU.angle.pitch * 0.0174533f;
    float roll_rad  = SystemIMU.angle.roll  * 0.0174533f;
    float earth_acc_z_g = SystemIMU.acc_g[2] * cosf(pitch_rad) * cosf(roll_rad) - 1.0f;
    g_vofa_smooth_acc_z = earth_acc_z_g * 9.80665f;

    if(s_land_phase == LAND_DESCEND || s_land_phase == LAND_CUT || at_state == AT_ABORT)
    {
        PID_Reset(&HeightPID);
        PID_Reset(&HeightSpeedPID);
        height_output = 0.0f;
    }

    int16 thr_total;
    if(s_land_phase == LAND_DESCEND || s_land_phase == LAND_CUT)
    {

        if(!s_land_thr_valid)
        {
            s_land_thr       = (float)s_prev_thr_total;   
            s_land_thr_valid = 1U;
        }
        s_land_thr -= g_land_rate;                        
        if(s_land_thr < 0.0f) s_land_thr = 0.0f;
        thr_total = (int16)s_land_thr;
    }
    else
    {
        thr_total = (int16)((int32)thr + (int32)height_output);
    }
    thr_total = clamp_i16(thr_total, CTRL_THR_MIN, CTRL_THR_MAX);
    g_vofa_thr_total = (float)thr_total;
    s_prev_thr_total = thr_total;                         

    float vol_factor = battery_get_vol_factor();
    mixer_out_t out = mixer_x_mix(thr_total, u_roll, u_pitch, u_yaw, vol_factor);

    motor_uart_out_set_cached(out);
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid.h - PID控制器头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef CODE_CONTROL_PID_H_
#define CODE_CONTROL_PID_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define ROLL_INTEGRAL_DEADBAND_DEG   (0.8f)
#define PITCH_INTEGRAL_DEADBAND_DEG  (0.8f)

typedef struct
{
    float kp;
    float ki;
    float kd;

    float prev_error;
    float intergral;
    float derivative;
    float output;

    float LimitIntegralMax;
    float LimitIntegralMin;
    float LimitOutputMax;
    float LimitOutputMin;
    float IntegralThreshold;
    float IntegralDeadband;
    float alpha;
} PIDControllerType_t;

void PID_Reset(PIDControllerType_t* pid);

void PID_Update(PIDControllerType_t* pid, float target, float current);

extern PIDControllerType_t RollRatePID;
extern PIDControllerType_t PitchRatePID;
extern PIDControllerType_t RollPID;
extern PIDControllerType_t PitchPID;
extern PIDControllerType_t YawRatePID;
extern PIDControllerType_t YawPID;
extern PIDControllerType_t HeightPID;
extern PIDControllerType_t HeightSpeedPID;
extern PIDControllerType_t FlowSpeedPIDX;
extern PIDControllerType_t FlowSpeedPIDY;

extern PIDControllerType_t FlowPosPIDX;
extern PIDControllerType_t FlowPosPIDY;

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
pid.c - PID控制器实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "control/pid.h"

#include <math.h>

void PID_Reset(PIDControllerType_t* pid)
{
    pid->intergral = 0.0f;
    pid->output = 0.0f;
    pid->prev_error = 0.0f;
    pid->derivative = 0.0f;
}

void PID_Update(PIDControllerType_t* pid, float target, float current)
{
    float error = target - current;
    float abs_err = fabsf(error);

    if(abs_err >= pid->IntegralDeadband)
    {
        
        float integral_factor = (abs_err < pid->IntegralThreshold || pid->IntegralThreshold <= 0.0f)
                                 ? 1.0f : (pid->IntegralThreshold / abs_err);
        pid->intergral += integral_factor * error;
    }

    float tmp_derivative = (error - pid->prev_error);
    pid->derivative = pid->alpha * tmp_derivative + (1.0f - pid->alpha) * pid->derivative;
    pid->prev_error = error;

    pid->output = pid->kp * error + pid->ki * pid->intergral + pid->kd * pid->derivative;

    if(pid->output > pid->LimitOutputMax)
    {
        pid->output = pid->LimitOutputMax;
        if(error > 0.0f) pid->intergral *= 0.9f;
    }
    if(pid->output < pid->LimitOutputMin)
    {
        pid->output = pid->LimitOutputMin;
        if(error < 0.0f) pid->intergral *= 0.9f;
    }

    if(pid->intergral > pid->LimitIntegralMax) pid->intergral = pid->LimitIntegralMax;
    if(pid->intergral < pid->LimitIntegralMin) pid->intergral = pid->LimitIntegralMin;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
mixer_x.h - X型混控头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef CODE_CONTROL_MIXER_X_H_
#define CODE_CONTROL_MIXER_X_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

typedef struct
{
    int16 m1;
    int16 m2;
    int16 m3;
    int16 m4;
} mixer_out_t;

mixer_out_t mixer_x_mix(int16 thr, float u_roll, float u_pitch, float u_yaw, float vol_factor);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
mixer_x.c - X型混控实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "control/mixer_x.h"

static inline int16 clamp_i16(int32 v, int16 lo, int16 hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return (int16)v;
}

mixer_out_t mixer_x_mix(int16 thr, float u_roll, float u_pitch, float u_yaw, float vol_factor)
{

    float m1 = (float)thr - u_pitch - u_roll + u_yaw;
    float m2 = (float)thr + u_pitch - u_roll - u_yaw;
    float m3 = (float)thr - u_pitch + u_roll - u_yaw;
    float m4 = (float)thr + u_pitch + u_roll + u_yaw;

    mixer_out_t out;
    out.m1 = clamp_i16((int32)m1, 0, 10000);
    out.m2 = clamp_i16((int32)m2, 0, 10000);
    out.m3 = clamp_i16((int32)m3, 0, 10000);
    out.m4 = clamp_i16((int32)m4, 0, 10000);
    return out;
}

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
motor_uart_out.c - 电机串口输出实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "control/motor_uart_out.h"
#include "esc_uart/small_driver_uart_control.h"

static volatile int16 s_m1 = 0;
static volatile int16 s_m2 = 0;
static volatile int16 s_m3 = 0;
static volatile int16 s_m4 = 0;

static inline int16 clamp_i16(int32 v, int16 lo, int16 hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return (int16)v;
}

void motor_uart_out_init(void)
{
    small_driver_uart_init();
}

void motor_uart_out_set_cached(mixer_out_t out)
{
    s_m1 = out.m1;
    s_m2 = out.m2;
    s_m3 = out.m3;
    s_m4 = out.m4;
}

void motor_uart_out_flush(void)
{
    int16 m1 = clamp_i16(s_m1, 0, 10000);
    int16 m2 = clamp_i16(s_m2, 0, 10000);
    int16 m3 = clamp_i16(s_m3, 0, 10000);
    int16 m4 = clamp_i16(s_m4, 0, 10000);

    small_driver_set_duty(m1, m2, m3, m4);
}

{
    float pitch;
    float roll;
    float yaw;
} IMU_euler_t;

{
    float gyro_x, gyro_y, gyro_z;
    float acc_x, acc_y, acc_z;
    float mag_x, mag_y, mag_z;
} IMU_param_t;

extern volatile IMU_euler_t IMU_Euler;
extern volatile IMU_param_t IMU_Data;

void IMU_gyro_offest_Init_All(void);

void IMU_Data_Collect(void);

void IMU_YAW_integral(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
IMU.c - IMU模块实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "imu/IMU.h"
#include "imu/ALL_DATA.h"

volatile IMU_euler_t IMU_Euler;
volatile IMU_param_t IMU_Data;

_st_Mpu imu963ra_data;
_st_AngE Angle;

static void imu_compat_sync_from_system(void)
{
    IMU_Euler.roll  = SystemIMU.angle.roll;
    IMU_Euler.pitch = SystemIMU.angle.pitch;
    IMU_Euler.yaw   = SystemIMU.angle.yaw;

    IMU_Data.acc_x = SystemIMU.acc_g[0];
    IMU_Data.acc_y = SystemIMU.acc_g[1];
    IMU_Data.acc_z = SystemIMU.acc_g[2];

    IMU_Data.gyro_x = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0];
    IMU_Data.gyro_y = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1];
    IMU_Data.gyro_z = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[2];

    IMU_Data.mag_x = 0.0f;
    IMU_Data.mag_y = 0.0f;
    IMU_Data.mag_z = 0.0f;

    imu963ra_data.gyroX = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0];
    imu963ra_data.gyroY = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1];
    imu963ra_data.gyroZ = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[2];

    imu963ra_data.accX = SystemIMU.acc_g[0];
    imu963ra_data.accY = SystemIMU.acc_g[1];
    imu963ra_data.accZ = SystemIMU.acc_g[2];

    imu963ra_data.magX = 0.0f;
    imu963ra_data.magY = 0.0f;
    imu963ra_data.magZ = 0.0f;

    Angle.roll  = SystemIMU.angle.roll;
    Angle.pitch = SystemIMU.angle.pitch;
    Angle.yaw   = SystemIMU.angle.yaw;
}

void IMU_gyro_offest_Init_All(void)
{
    Attitude_Init();
    imu_compat_sync_from_system();
}

void IMU_Data_Collect(void)
{
    Attitude_Update();
    imu_compat_sync_from_system();
}

void IMU_YAW_integral(void)
{
    Attitude_Update();
    imu_compat_sync_from_system();
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Attitude_Est.h - 姿态估计头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef CODE_ATTITUDE_EST_H_
#define CODE_ATTITUDE_EST_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define IMU_TYPE_660RA              1
#define IMU_TYPE_660RB              2
#define IMU_TYPE_963RA              3

#define ACTIVE_IMU  IMU_TYPE_963RA                                  

#if ACTIVE_IMU == IMU_TYPE_660RA
    #define IMU_INIT()             imu660ra_init()
    #define IMU_GET_ACC()          imu660ra_get_acc()
    #define IMU_GET_GYRO()         imu660ra_get_gyro()
    #define IMU_ACC_X              imu660ra_acc_x
    #define IMU_ACC_Y              imu660ra_acc_y
    #define IMU_ACC_Z              imu660ra_acc_z
    #define IMU_GYRO_X             imu660ra_gyro_x
    #define IMU_GYRO_Y             imu660ra_gyro_y
    #define IMU_GYRO_Z             imu660ra_gyro_z
    #define IMU_ACC_SCALE          imu660ra_transition_factor[0]
    #define IMU_GYRO_SCALE         imu660ra_transition_factor[1]

#elif ACTIVE_IMU == IMU_TYPE_660RB
    #define IMU_INIT()             imu660rb_init()
    #define IMU_GET_ACC()          imu660rb_get_acc()
    #define IMU_GET_GYRO()         imu660rb_get_gyro()
    #define IMU_ACC_X              (imu660rb_acc_y)
    #define IMU_ACC_Y              (-imu660rb_acc_x)
    #define IMU_ACC_Z              (imu660rb_acc_z)

    #define IMU_GYRO_X             (imu660rb_gyro_y)
    #define IMU_GYRO_Y             (-imu660rb_gyro_x)
    #define IMU_GYRO_Z             (imu660rb_gyro_z)

    #define IMU_ACC_SCALE          4098.0f
    #define IMU_GYRO_SCALE         28.6f

#elif ACTIVE_IMU == IMU_TYPE_963RA
    #define IMU_INIT()             imu963ra_init()
    #define IMU_GET_ACC()          imu963ra_get_acc()
    #define IMU_GET_GYRO()         imu963ra_get_gyro()

    #define IMU_ACC_X              imu963ra_acc_x
    #define IMU_ACC_Y              imu963ra_acc_y
    #define IMU_ACC_Z              imu963ra_acc_z
    #define IMU_GYRO_X             imu963ra_gyro_x
    #define IMU_GYRO_Y             imu963ra_gyro_y
    #define IMU_GYRO_Z             imu963ra_gyro_z

    #define IMU_ACC_SCALE          1.0f
    #define IMU_GYRO_SCALE         1.0f
#endif

#define AHRS_DT             0.001f          

#define AHRS_KP             1.0f            
#define AHRS_KI             0.008f          

#define ACC_TRUST_FULL_G    0.10f           
#define ACC_TRUST_ZERO_G    0.50f           
#define ACC_TRUST_MIN       0.10f           

#define GYRO_CALIB_COUNT    1000            
#define GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ    150             
#define ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ     80              

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI              3.1415926535f
#endif

#define RTOD              (57.2957795f)
#define DTOR              (0.01745329f)

typedef struct 
{
    float roll;
    float pitch;
    float yaw;
    float total_yaw;
} EulerAngle_t;

typedef struct 
{
    uint8_t imu_ready;
    
    float q0, q1, q2, q3;
    float exInt, eyInt, ezInt;
    float gyro_bias[3];
    float acc_bias[3];
    float gyro_deadzone[3];
    float roll_offset;
    float pitch_offset;

    float last_roll;
    float last_pitch;
    float last_yaw;

    float acc_g[3];
    float gyro_rad[3];

    EulerAngle_t angle;
    float gyro_deg[3];
    float acc_earth[3];
} Attitude_Handle_t;

extern Attitude_Handle_t SystemIMU;

void Attitude_Init(void);
void Attitude_Update(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Attitude_Est.c - 姿态估计实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "Attitude_Est.h"
#include "Filter.h"
#include <math.h>

Attitude_Handle_t SystemIMU;

static float InvSqrt(float x);
static void IMU_Read_And_Preprocess(Attitude_Handle_t *imu);
static void Mahony_Update(Attitude_Handle_t *imu);
static void Post_Processing_Filter(Attitude_Handle_t *imu);

void Attitude_Init(void)
{
    Attitude_Handle_t *imu = &SystemIMU;

    if(IMU_INIT())
    {
        zf_log(0, "IMU Init Failed! System will run without IMU.");
        imu->imu_ready = 0;
        imu->q0 = 1.0f;
        imu->q1 = 0.0f;
        imu->q2 = 0.0f;
        imu->q3 = 0.0f;
        imu->exInt = 0.0f;
        imu->eyInt = 0.0f;
        imu->ezInt = 0.0f;
        
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&gyro_lpf[0], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&gyro_lpf[1], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&gyro_lpf[2], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&acc_lpf[0], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ);
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&acc_lpf[1], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ);
        Filter_Bessel_Init(&acc_lpf[2], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ);
        return;
    }

    imu->imu_ready = 1;

    imu->q0 = 1.0f;
    imu->q1 = 0.0f;
    imu->q2 = 0.0f;
    imu->q3 = 0.0f;

    imu->exInt = 0.0f;
    imu->eyInt = 0.0f;
    imu->ezInt = 0.0f;

    float gx_sum = 0.0f, gy_sum = 0.0f, gz_sum = 0.0f;
    float gz_min = 100000.0f;
    float gz_max = -100000.0f;

    for(int i = 0; i < GYRO_CALIB_COUNT; i++)
    {
        IMU_GET_GYRO();

#if ACTIVE_IMU == IMU_TYPE_963RA
        float gx_dps = imu963ra_gyro_transition(IMU_GYRO_X);
        float gy_dps = imu963ra_gyro_transition(IMU_GYRO_Y);
        float gz_dps = imu963ra_gyro_transition(IMU_GYRO_Z);
#else
        float gyro_scale = IMU_GYRO_SCALE;
        float gx_dps = (float)IMU_GYRO_X / gyro_scale;
        float gy_dps = (float)IMU_GYRO_Y / gyro_scale;
        float gz_dps = (float)IMU_GYRO_Z / gyro_scale;
#endif

        gx_sum += gx_dps;
        gy_sum += gy_dps;
        gz_sum += gz_dps;

        if(gz_dps > gz_max) gz_max = gz_dps;
        if(gz_dps < gz_min) gz_min = gz_dps;

        system_delay_ms(2);
    }

    imu->gyro_bias[0] = (gx_sum / (float)GYRO_CALIB_COUNT) * DTOR;
    imu->gyro_bias[1] = (gy_sum / (float)GYRO_CALIB_COUNT) * DTOR;
    imu->gyro_bias[2] = (gz_sum / (float)GYRO_CALIB_COUNT) * DTOR;

    float noise_amplitude_z = (gz_max - gz_min);
    imu->gyro_deadzone[2] = ((noise_amplitude_z / 2.0f) * 3.0f) * DTOR;
    if(imu->gyro_deadzone[2] > (0.5f * DTOR))
    {
        imu->gyro_deadzone[2] = 0.5f * DTOR;
    }

    Filter_Bessel_Init(&gyro_lpf[0], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);
    Filter_Bessel_Init(&gyro_lpf[1], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);
    Filter_Bessel_Init(&gyro_lpf[2], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, GYRO_CUTOFF_FREQ);

    Filter_Bessel_Init(&acc_lpf[0], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ);
    Filter_Bessel_Init(&acc_lpf[1], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ);
    Filter_Bessel_Init(&acc_lpf[2], 1.0f / AHRS_DT, ACC_CUTOFF_FREQ);

    imu->roll_offset = 0.0f;
    imu->pitch_offset = 0.0f;
    imu->acc_bias[0] = 0.0f;
    imu->acc_bias[1] = 0.0f;
    imu->acc_bias[2] = 0.0f;
}

void Attitude_Update(void)
{
    Attitude_Handle_t *imu = &SystemIMU;

    IMU_Read_And_Preprocess(imu);

    Filter_Bessel_Apply(&gyro_lpf[0], SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0]);
    Filter_Bessel_Apply(&gyro_lpf[1], SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1]);
    Filter_Bessel_Apply(&gyro_lpf[2], SystemIMU.gyro_deg[2]);

    imu->gyro_rad[0] = gyro_lpf[0].out * DTOR;
    imu->gyro_rad[1] = gyro_lpf[1].out * DTOR;
    imu->gyro_rad[2] = gyro_lpf[2].out * DTOR;

    if(fabsf(imu->gyro_rad[2]) <= imu->gyro_deadzone[2])
    {
        imu->gyro_rad[2] = 0.0f;
    }
    else
    {
        if(imu->gyro_rad[2] > 0.0f)
            imu->gyro_rad[2] -= imu->gyro_deadzone[2];
        else
            imu->gyro_rad[2] += imu->gyro_deadzone[2];
    }

    Mahony_Update(imu);
    Post_Processing_Filter(imu);
}

static void IMU_Read_And_Preprocess(Attitude_Handle_t *imu)
{
    IMU_GET_ACC();
    IMU_GET_GYRO();

#if ACTIVE_IMU == IMU_TYPE_963RA
    float cur_ax = imu963ra_acc_transition(IMU_ACC_X);
    float cur_ay = imu963ra_acc_transition(IMU_ACC_Y);
    float cur_az = imu963ra_acc_transition(IMU_ACC_Z);
#else
    float acc_scale  = IMU_ACC_SCALE;
    float cur_ax = (float)IMU_ACC_X / acc_scale;
    float cur_ay = (float)IMU_ACC_Y / acc_scale;
    float cur_az = (float)IMU_ACC_Z / acc_scale;
#endif

    Filter_Bessel_Apply(&acc_lpf[0], cur_ax);
    Filter_Bessel_Apply(&acc_lpf[1], cur_ay);
    Filter_Bessel_Apply(&acc_lpf[2], cur_az);

    imu->acc_g[0] = acc_lpf[0].out;
    imu->acc_g[1] = acc_lpf[1].out;
    imu->acc_g[2] = acc_lpf[2].out;

#if ACTIVE_IMU == IMU_TYPE_963RA
    float gx_dps = imu963ra_gyro_transition(IMU_GYRO_X);
    float gy_dps = imu963ra_gyro_transition(IMU_GYRO_Y);
    float gz_dps = imu963ra_gyro_transition(IMU_GYRO_Z);

    imu->gyro_rad[0] = gx_dps * DTOR - imu->gyro_bias[0];
    imu->gyro_rad[1] = gy_dps * DTOR - imu->gyro_bias[1];
    imu->gyro_rad[2] = gz_dps * DTOR - imu->gyro_bias[2];
#else
    float gyro_scale = IMU_GYRO_SCALE;
    imu->gyro_rad[0] = ((float)IMU_GYRO_X / gyro_scale) * DTOR - imu->gyro_bias[0];
    imu->gyro_rad[1] = ((float)IMU_GYRO_Y / gyro_scale) * DTOR - imu->gyro_bias[1];
    imu->gyro_rad[2] = ((float)IMU_GYRO_Z / gyro_scale) * DTOR - imu->gyro_bias[2];
#endif

    imu->gyro_deg[0] = imu->gyro_rad[0] * RTOD;
    imu->gyro_deg[1] = imu->gyro_rad[1] * RTOD;
    imu->gyro_deg[2] = imu->gyro_rad[2] * RTOD;
}

static void Mahony_Update(Attitude_Handle_t *imu)
{
    float ax = imu->acc_g[0];
    float ay = imu->acc_g[1];
    float az = imu->acc_g[2];
    float gx = imu->gyro_rad[0];
    float gy = imu->gyro_rad[1];
    float gz = imu->gyro_rad[2];

    float norm;
    float vx, vy, vz;
    float ex, ey;

    norm = InvSqrt(ax*ax + ay*ay + az*az);
    if(norm == 0.0f) return;

    float acc_mag_g = 1.0f / norm;                        
    float acc_dev   = fabsf(acc_mag_g - 1.0f);
    float acc_trust;
    if(acc_dev <= ACC_TRUST_FULL_G)      acc_trust = 1.0f;
    else if(acc_dev >= ACC_TRUST_ZERO_G) acc_trust = ACC_TRUST_MIN;
    else acc_trust = 1.0f - (1.0f - ACC_TRUST_MIN) *
                     (acc_dev - ACC_TRUST_FULL_G) / (ACC_TRUST_ZERO_G - ACC_TRUST_FULL_G);

    ax *= norm; ay *= norm; az *= norm;

    float q0 = imu->q0, q1 = imu->q1, q2 = imu->q2, q3 = imu->q3;
    vx = 2.0f*(q1*q3 - q0*q2);
    vy = 2.0f*(q0*q1 + q2*q3);
    vz = q0*q0 - q1*q1 - q2*q2 + q3*q3;

    ex = (ay*vz - az*vy);
    ey = (az*vx - ax*vz);

    imu->exInt += ex * AHRS_KI * AHRS_DT * acc_trust;
    imu->eyInt += ey * AHRS_KI * AHRS_DT * acc_trust;

    gx += AHRS_KP * acc_trust * ex + imu->exInt;
    gy += AHRS_KP * acc_trust * ey + imu->eyInt;

    float halfT = 0.5f * AHRS_DT;

    float q0_last = q0, q1_last = q1, q2_last = q2, q3_last = q3;
    imu->q0 += (-q1_last*gx - q2_last*gy - q3_last*gz) * halfT;
    imu->q1 += ( q0_last*gx + q2_last*gz - q3_last*gy) * halfT;
    imu->q2 += ( q0_last*gy - q1_last*gz + q3_last*gx) * halfT;
    imu->q3 += ( q0_last*gz + q1_last*gy - q2_last*gx) * halfT;

    norm = InvSqrt(imu->q0*imu->q0 + imu->q1*imu->q1 + imu->q2*imu->q2 + imu->q3*imu->q3);
    imu->q0 *= norm; imu->q1 *= norm; imu->q2 *= norm; imu->q3 *= norm;
}

static void Post_Processing_Filter(Attitude_Handle_t *imu)
{
    float temp_pitch = asinf(-2.0f*imu->q1*imu->q3 + 2.0f*imu->q0*imu->q2) * RTOD - imu->pitch_offset;
    float temp_roll  = atan2f(2.0f*imu->q2*imu->q3 + 2.0f*imu->q0*imu->q1, -2.0f*imu->q1*imu->q1 - 2.0f*imu->q2*imu->q2 + 1.0f) * RTOD - imu->roll_offset;
    float temp_yaw   = atan2f(2.0f*imu->q1*imu->q2 + 2.0f*imu->q0*imu->q3, -2.0f*imu->q2*imu->q2 - 2.0f*imu->q3*imu->q3 + 1.0f) * RTOD;

    imu->angle.roll  = temp_roll;
    imu->angle.pitch = temp_pitch;

    float yaw_diff = temp_yaw - imu->last_yaw;
    if(yaw_diff < -180.0f) yaw_diff += 360.0f;
    if(yaw_diff >  180.0f) yaw_diff -= 360.0f;

    imu->angle.yaw = temp_yaw;
    imu->angle.total_yaw += yaw_diff;
    imu->last_yaw = temp_yaw;
}

static float InvSqrt(float x)
{
    float halfx = 0.5f * x;
    float y = x;
    long i = *(long*)&y;
    i = 0x5f3759df - (i>>1);
    y = *(float*)&i;
    y = y * (1.5f - (halfx * y * y));
    return y;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Filter.h - 滤波器头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef __FILTER_H__
#define __FILTER_H__

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif

typedef struct {
    float b0, b1, b2, a1, a2; 
    float x1, x2;             
    float y1, y2;             
    float out;                
} Bessel_Filter_t;

extern Bessel_Filter_t gyro_lpf[3];         
extern Bessel_Filter_t acc_lpf[3];          

void Filter_Bessel_Init(Bessel_Filter_t* filter, float f_sample, float f_cutoff);

float Filter_Bessel_Apply(Bessel_Filter_t* filter, float input);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Filter.c - 滤波器实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "Filter.h"

Bessel_Filter_t gyro_lpf[3];        
Bessel_Filter_t acc_lpf[3];         

void Filter_Bessel_Init(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float f_sample, float f_cutoff)
{
    
    float Q = 0.57735027f; 

    float omega_0 = 2.0f * M_PI * f_cutoff / f_sample;

    float cos_omega = cosf(omega_0);
    float alpha = sinf(omega_0) / (2.0f * Q);

    float a0 = 1.0f + alpha;

    filter->b0 = ((1.0f - cos_omega) / 2.0f) / a0;
    filter->b1 =  (1.0f - cos_omega)         / a0;
    filter->b2 = ((1.0f - cos_omega) / 2.0f) / a0;
    filter->a1 = (-2.0f * cos_omega)         / a0;
    filter->a2 =  (1.0f - alpha)             / a0;

    filter->x1 = 0.0f; filter->x2 = 0.0f;
    filter->y1 = 0.0f; filter->y2 = 0.0f;
    filter->out = 0.0f;
}

float Filter_Bessel_Apply(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float input)
{
    
    filter->out = filter->b0 * input + 
                  filter->b1 * filter->x1 + 
                  filter->b2 * filter->x2 - 
                  filter->a1 * filter->y1 - 
                  filter->a2 * filter->y2;

    filter->x2 = filter->x1;
    filter->x1 = input;
    filter->y2 = filter->y1;
    filter->y1 = filter->out;

    return filter->out;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
flow.h - 光流传感器头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef CODE_FLOW_FLOW_H_
#define CODE_FLOW_FLOW_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define OPTICAL_FLOW_CUTFREQ    5.0f        

#define FLOW_RAW_MAX     6.0f    
#define FLOW_SLEW_MAX    2.0f    

#define FLOW_BIAS_CAL_FRAMES    300U   

#define FLOW_BIAS_WARMUP        20U

typedef struct
{
    int16 raw_x_integral;
    int16 raw_y_integral;

    float flow_vx;
    float flow_vy;

    float filtered_vx;
    float filtered_vy;

    float bias_vx, bias_vy;    
    float deb_vx,  deb_vy;     
    float pos_x,   pos_y;      
    uint8 bias_calibrated;     
    uint16 bias_cnt;           

    uint8 valid;
    uint32 last_update_ms;
} flow_state_t;

typedef struct {
    float b0, b1, b2, a1, a2; 
    float x1, x2;             
    float y1, y2;             
    float out;                
} Bessel_Filter_t;

extern float OpticalFlowX_Buffer[5];       
extern uint8 pIndex_OpticalFlowX_Buffer;    
extern float OpticalFlowY_Buffer[5];       
extern uint8 pIndex_OpticalFlowY_Buffer;    
extern Bessel_Filter_t OpticalFlow_lpf[2]; 

extern volatile flow_state_t g_flow_state;

void flow_init(void);
void flow_update_1ms(uint32 now_ms);

void flow_reset_pos(void);

void Flow_Filter_Bessel_Init(Bessel_Filter_t* filter, float f_sample, float f_cutoff);
float Flow_Filter_Bessel_Apply(Bessel_Filter_t* filter, float input);
float Filter_Median_Apply(float* Source_Buffer, uint8* pIndex, uint8 Buffer_Size, float new_data);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
flow.c - 光流传感器实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "flow/flow.h"
#include "zf_device_upflow302.h"
#include "zf_device_type.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include <math.h>

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif

volatile flow_state_t g_flow_state;

static inline float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

float OpticalFlowX_Buffer[5];           
uint8 pIndex_OpticalFlowX_Buffer;       
float OpticalFlowY_Buffer[5];           
uint8 pIndex_OpticalFlowY_Buffer;       
Bessel_Filter_t OpticalFlow_lpf[2];     

void flow_init(void)
{
    upflow302_receive_init();
    set_wireless_type(WIRELESS_UART, upflow302_receive_callback);

    g_flow_state.raw_x_integral = 0;
    g_flow_state.raw_y_integral = 0;
    g_flow_state.flow_vx = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.flow_vy = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.filtered_vx = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.filtered_vy = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.bias_vx = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.bias_vy = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.deb_vx = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.deb_vy = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.pos_x = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.pos_y = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.bias_calibrated = 0U;
    g_flow_state.bias_cnt = 0U;
    g_flow_state.valid = 0U;
    g_flow_state.last_update_ms = system_get_ms();

    Flow_Filter_Bessel_Init(&OpticalFlow_lpf[0], 50.0f, OPTICAL_FLOW_CUTFREQ);
    Flow_Filter_Bessel_Init(&OpticalFlow_lpf[1], 50.0f, OPTICAL_FLOW_CUTFREQ);

    pIndex_OpticalFlowX_Buffer = 0;
    pIndex_OpticalFlowY_Buffer = 0;
}

void flow_update_1ms(uint32 now_ms)
{
    if(upflow302_finsh_flag)
    {
        upflow302_finsh_flag = 0U;

        g_flow_state.raw_x_integral = upflow302_receive.upflow302_x;
        g_flow_state.raw_y_integral = upflow302_receive.upflow302_y;

        uint8 frame_ok = (upflow302_receive.upflow302_valid == 245U) ? 1U : 0U;
        g_flow_state.valid = frame_ok;
        if(frame_ok == 0U)
        {
            g_flow_state.last_update_ms = now_ms;
            return;   
        }

        float flow_rad_x = (float)g_flow_state.raw_y_integral / 200.0f;   
        float flow_rad_y = (float)g_flow_state.raw_x_integral / 200.0f;   

        flow_rad_x = clampf(flow_rad_x, -FLOW_RAW_MAX, FLOW_RAW_MAX);
        flow_rad_y = clampf(flow_rad_y, -FLOW_RAW_MAX, FLOW_RAW_MAX);

        float temp_median_x = Filter_Median_Apply(OpticalFlowX_Buffer, &pIndex_OpticalFlowX_Buffer,
                                                  sizeof(OpticalFlowX_Buffer) / sizeof(OpticalFlowX_Buffer[0]), flow_rad_x);
        float temp_median_y = Filter_Median_Apply(OpticalFlowY_Buffer, &pIndex_OpticalFlowY_Buffer,
                                                  sizeof(OpticalFlowY_Buffer) / sizeof(OpticalFlowY_Buffer[0]), flow_rad_y);

        static float last_med_x = 0.0f, last_med_y = 0.0f;
        static uint8 slew_inited = 0U;
        if(slew_inited == 0U) { last_med_x = temp_median_x; last_med_y = temp_median_y; slew_inited = 1U; }
        temp_median_x = last_med_x + clampf(temp_median_x - last_med_x, -FLOW_SLEW_MAX, FLOW_SLEW_MAX);
        temp_median_y = last_med_y + clampf(temp_median_y - last_med_y, -FLOW_SLEW_MAX, FLOW_SLEW_MAX);
        last_med_x = temp_median_x;
        last_med_y = temp_median_y;

        float gyro_rad_x = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[0] * 0.0055f;  
        float gyro_rad_y = SystemIMU.gyro_deg[1] * 0.0055f;  

        float comp_rad_x = temp_median_x + gyro_rad_x;
        float comp_rad_y = temp_median_y + gyro_rad_y;

        Flow_Filter_Bessel_Apply(&OpticalFlow_lpf[0], comp_rad_x);
        Flow_Filter_Bessel_Apply(&OpticalFlow_lpf[1], comp_rad_y);

        g_flow_state.flow_vx = comp_rad_x;
        g_flow_state.flow_vy = comp_rad_y;
        g_flow_state.filtered_vx = OpticalFlow_lpf[0].out;
        g_flow_state.filtered_vy = OpticalFlow_lpf[1].out;

        if(g_flow_state.bias_calibrated == 0U)
        {
            g_flow_state.bias_cnt++;
            g_flow_state.deb_vx = 0.0f;
            g_flow_state.deb_vy = 0.0f;
            if(g_flow_state.bias_cnt > FLOW_BIAS_WARMUP)
            {
                g_flow_state.bias_vx += g_flow_state.filtered_vx;
                g_flow_state.bias_vy += g_flow_state.filtered_vy;
            }
            if(g_flow_state.bias_cnt >= (FLOW_BIAS_WARMUP + FLOW_BIAS_CAL_FRAMES))
            {
                g_flow_state.bias_vx /= (float)FLOW_BIAS_CAL_FRAMES;
                g_flow_state.bias_vy /= (float)FLOW_BIAS_CAL_FRAMES;
                g_flow_state.bias_calibrated = 1U;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            const float dt = 0.02f;   
            g_flow_state.deb_vx = g_flow_state.filtered_vx - g_flow_state.bias_vx;
            g_flow_state.deb_vy = g_flow_state.filtered_vy - g_flow_state.bias_vy;
            g_flow_state.pos_x += g_flow_state.deb_vx * dt;
            g_flow_state.pos_y += g_flow_state.deb_vy * dt;
        }

        g_flow_state.last_update_ms = now_ms;
    }
}

void flow_reset_pos(void)
{
    g_flow_state.pos_x = 0.0f;
    g_flow_state.pos_y = 0.0f;
}

void Flow_Filter_Bessel_Init(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float f_sample, float f_cutoff)
{
    
    float Q = 0.57735027f;

    float omega_0 = 2.0f * M_PI * f_cutoff / f_sample;

    float cos_omega = cosf(omega_0);
    float alpha = sinf(omega_0) / (2.0f * Q);

    float a0 = 1.0f + alpha;

    filter->b0 = ((1.0f - cos_omega) / 2.0f) / a0;
    filter->b1 =  (1.0f - cos_omega)         / a0;
    filter->b2 = ((1.0f - cos_omega) / 2.0f) / a0;
    filter->a1 = (-2.0f * cos_omega)         / a0;
    filter->a2 =  (1.0f - alpha)             / a0;

    filter->x1 = 0.0f; filter->x2 = 0.0f;
    filter->y1 = 0.0f; filter->y2 = 0.0f;
    filter->out = 0.0f;
}

float Flow_Filter_Bessel_Apply(Bessel_Filter_t *filter, float input)
{
    
    filter->out = filter->b0 * input +
                  filter->b1 * filter->x1 +
                  filter->b2 * filter->x2 -
                  filter->a1 * filter->y1 -
                  filter->a2 * filter->y2;

    filter->x2 = filter->x1;
    filter->x1 = input;
    filter->y2 = filter->y1;
    filter->y1 = filter->out;

    return filter->out;
}

#define MAX_MEDIAN_LEN  5

float Filter_Median_Apply(float* Source_Buffer, uint8* pIndex, uint8 Buffer_Size, float new_data)
{
    
    if (Buffer_Size > MAX_MEDIAN_LEN)
    {
        Buffer_Size = MAX_MEDIAN_LEN;
    }

    Source_Buffer[*pIndex] = new_data;
    (*pIndex) ++;
    if(*pIndex >= Buffer_Size)
    {
        *pIndex = 0;
    }

    float temp_arr[MAX_MEDIAN_LEN];
    for(uint8 i = 0; i < Buffer_Size; i++)
    {
        temp_arr[i] = Source_Buffer[i];
    }

    for(uint8 i = 0; i < Buffer_Size - 1; i++)
    {
        for(uint8 j = 0; j < Buffer_Size - 1 - i; j++)
        {
            if(temp_arr[j] > temp_arr[j + 1])
            {
                float temp = temp_arr[j];
                temp_arr[j] = temp_arr[j + 1];
                temp_arr[j + 1] = temp;
            }
        }
    }

    return temp_arr[Buffer_Size / 2];
}

    float height_m;          
    float vz_mps;            

    uint16 raw_mm;           
    float tof_height_m;      
    float tof_vz_mps;        

    uint32 last_update_ms;
} tof_state_t;

extern volatile tof_state_t g_tof_state;
extern volatile uint8 g_tof_init_ret;

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
tof.c - TOF测距实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "tof/tof.h"
#include "zf_device_dl1b.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include <math.h>

#define TOF_RAW_READ_PERIOD_MS   (5U)      
#define TOF_RAW_DT_S             (0.005f)  
#define TOF_LPF_H_A              (0.6f)    
#define TOF_LPF_VZ_A             (0.5f)    
#define TOF_COMP_A               (0.98f)   

volatile tof_state_t g_tof_state;
volatile uint8 g_tof_init_ret = 1U;

static inline float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static inline uint16 median3_u16(uint16 a, uint16 b, uint16 c)
{
    uint16 t;
    if(a > b) { t = a; a = b; b = t; }
    if(b > c) { t = b; b = c; c = t; }
    if(a > b) { t = a; a = b; b = t; }
    return b;
}

uint8 tof_init(void)
{
    
    uint8 ret = dl1b_init();
    g_tof_init_ret = ret;

    g_tof_state.height_m = 0.0f;
    g_tof_state.vz_mps = 0.0f;
    g_tof_state.raw_mm = 0U;
    g_tof_state.tof_height_m = 0.0f;
    g_tof_state.tof_vz_mps = 0.0f;
    g_tof_state.last_update_ms = system_get_ms();

    return ret;
}

void tof_update_1ms(uint32 now_ms)
{
    
    static uint8 cnt_ms = 0U;
    static float target_tof_h = 0.0f;
    static float smooth_raw_h = 0.0f;
    static float last_target_h = 0.0f;
    static float latest_tof_vz = 0.0f;

    static uint16 raw_hist[3] = {0U, 0U, 0U};
    static uint8  raw_hist_cnt = 0U;

    if(++cnt_ms >= TOF_RAW_READ_PERIOD_MS)
    {
        cnt_ms = 0U;

        dl1b_get_distance();

        uint16 raw_dist = dl1b_distance_mm;
        g_tof_state.raw_mm = raw_dist;

        if(raw_dist > 0U && raw_dist < 2000U)
        {

            raw_hist[2] = raw_hist[1];
            raw_hist[1] = raw_hist[0];
            raw_hist[0] = raw_dist;
            uint16 med_dist;
            if(raw_hist_cnt < 2U)
            {
                raw_hist_cnt++;
                med_dist = raw_dist;   
            }
            else
            {
                med_dist = median3_u16(raw_hist[0], raw_hist[1], raw_hist[2]);
            }

            float raw_h = ((int32)med_dist - 50) / 1000.0f;

            smooth_raw_h = TOF_LPF_H_A * smooth_raw_h + (1.0f - TOF_LPF_H_A) * raw_h;

            const float max_h_step = 0.25f;
            if(fabsf(smooth_raw_h - target_tof_h) <= max_h_step)
            {
                target_tof_h = smooth_raw_h;
            }

            float raw_vz = (target_tof_h - last_target_h) / TOF_RAW_DT_S;
            last_target_h = target_tof_h;

            latest_tof_vz = TOF_LPF_VZ_A * latest_tof_vz + (1.0f - TOF_LPF_VZ_A) * raw_vz;

            latest_tof_vz = clampf(latest_tof_vz, -2.5f, 2.5f);

            g_tof_state.tof_height_m = target_tof_h;
            g_tof_state.tof_vz_mps = latest_tof_vz;
        }
        else
        {
            
        }
    }

    const float dt = 0.001f;

    float pitch_rad = SystemIMU.angle.pitch * 0.0174533f;
    float roll_rad  = SystemIMU.angle.roll  * 0.0174533f;
    
    float earth_acc_z_g = SystemIMU.acc_g[2] * cosf(pitch_rad) * cosf(roll_rad) - 1.0f;
    float acc_z_mps2 = earth_acc_z_g * 9.80665f;

    acc_z_mps2 = clampf(acc_z_mps2, -10.0f, 10.0f);

    static float smooth_acc_z = 0.0f;
    smooth_acc_z = 0.8f * smooth_acc_z + 0.2f * acc_z_mps2;

    static float est_velocity = 0.0f;
    static float est_height = 0.0f;

    est_velocity = TOF_COMP_A * (est_velocity + smooth_acc_z * dt) + (1.0f - TOF_COMP_A) * g_tof_state.tof_vz_mps;
    est_height   = TOF_COMP_A * (est_height + est_velocity * dt) + (1.0f - TOF_COMP_A) * g_tof_state.tof_height_m;

    if(fabsf(est_velocity) < 0.01f) est_velocity = 0.0f;

    g_tof_state.vz_mps = est_velocity;
    g_tof_state.height_m = est_height;
    g_tof_state.last_update_ms = now_ms;
}

uint8 tof_get_init_status(void)
{
    if(g_tof_state.raw_mm > 0U && g_tof_state.raw_mm < 2000U)
    {
        return 0U; 
    }
    return g_tof_init_ret; 
}

uint8 tof_is_working(void)
{
    return (g_tof_state.raw_mm > 0U && g_tof_state.raw_mm < 2000U) ? 1U : 0U;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
small_driver_uart_control.h - 电调串口控制头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef SMALL_DRIVER_UART_CONTROL_H_
#define SMALL_DRIVER_UART_CONTROL_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#define SMALL_DRIVER_UART      (UART_2)
#define SMALL_DRIVER_BAUDRATE  (460800)
#define SMALL_DRIVER_RX        (UART2_RX_P10_0)
#define SMALL_DRIVER_TX        (UART2_TX_P10_1)

typedef struct
{
    uint8 send_data_buffer[11];      
    uint8 receive_data_buffer[11];   
    uint8 receive_data_count;        
    uint8 sum_check_data;            
    int16 receive_speed_data[4];     
} small_device_value_struct;

extern small_device_value_struct motor_value;

void uart_control_callback(void);
void small_driver_set_duty(int16 motor_duty_1, int16 motor_duty_2, int16 motor_duty_3, int16 motor_duty_4);
void small_driver_get_speed(void);
void small_driver_uart_init(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
small_driver_uart_control.c - 电调串口控制实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "small_driver_uart_control.h"
#include "protocol/vofa.h"

small_device_value_struct motor_value;    

static void small_driver_init(void)
{
    memset(motor_value.send_data_buffer, 0, sizeof(motor_value.send_data_buffer));
    memset(motor_value.receive_data_buffer, 0, sizeof(motor_value.receive_data_buffer));
    memset(motor_value.receive_speed_data, 0, sizeof(motor_value.receive_speed_data));
    motor_value.receive_data_count = 0;
    motor_value.sum_check_data = 0;
}

void uart_control_callback(void)
{
    uint8 receive_data = 0;

    if(!uart_query_byte(SMALL_DRIVER_UART, &receive_data))
    {
        return;
    }

    if(motor_value.receive_data_count == 0)
    {
        if(receive_data != 0xA5)
        {
            return;
        }

        motor_value.receive_data_buffer[0] = receive_data;
        motor_value.receive_data_count = 1;
        return;
    }

    if(motor_value.receive_data_count >= sizeof(motor_value.receive_data_buffer))
    {
        motor_value.receive_data_count = 0;
        return;
    }

    motor_value.receive_data_buffer[motor_value.receive_data_count++] = receive_data;

    if(motor_value.receive_data_count == sizeof(motor_value.receive_data_buffer))
    {
        motor_value.sum_check_data = 0;
        for(int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
            motor_value.sum_check_data += motor_value.receive_data_buffer[i];
        }

        if(motor_value.sum_check_data == motor_value.receive_data_buffer[10])
        {
            for(int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                
                motor_value.receive_speed_data[i] =
                    (int16)(((int32)motor_value.receive_data_buffer[i * 2 + 2] << 8) |
                            (int32)motor_value.receive_data_buffer[i * 2 + 3]);
            }
            motor_speed1 =  motor_value.receive_speed_data[0];
            motor_speed2 =  motor_value.receive_speed_data[1];
            motor_speed3 =  motor_value.receive_speed_data[2];
            motor_speed4 =  motor_value.receive_speed_data[3];
        }

        motor_value.receive_data_count = 0;
        memset(motor_value.receive_data_buffer, 0, sizeof(motor_value.receive_data_buffer));
    }
}

void small_driver_set_duty(int16 motor_duty_1, int16 motor_duty_2, int16 motor_duty_3, int16 motor_duty_4)
{
    
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[0] = 0xA5;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[1] = 0x01;

    motor_value.send_data_buffer[2] = (uint8)((motor_duty_1 & 0xFF00) >> 8);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[3] = (uint8)(motor_duty_1 & 0x00FF);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[4] = (uint8)((motor_duty_2 & 0xFF00) >> 8);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[5] = (uint8)(motor_duty_2 & 0x00FF);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[6] = (uint8)((motor_duty_3 & 0xFF00) >> 8);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[7] = (uint8)(motor_duty_3 & 0x00FF);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[8] = (uint8)((motor_duty_4 & 0xFF00) >> 8);
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[9] = (uint8)(motor_duty_4 & 0x00FF);

    motor_value.send_data_buffer[10] = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
    {
        motor_value.send_data_buffer[10] += motor_value.send_data_buffer[i];
    }

    uart_write_buffer(SMALL_DRIVER_UART, motor_value.send_data_buffer, 11);
}

void small_driver_get_speed(void)
{
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[0] = 0xA5;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[1] = 0x02;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[2] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[3] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[4] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[5] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[6] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[7] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[8] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[9] = 0x00;
    motor_value.send_data_buffer[10] = 0xA7;

    uart_write_buffer(SMALL_DRIVER_UART, motor_value.send_data_buffer, 11);
}

void small_driver_uart_init(void)
{
    uart_init(SMALL_DRIVER_UART, SMALL_DRIVER_BAUDRATE, SMALL_DRIVER_TX, SMALL_DRIVER_RX);
    uart_rx_interrupt(SMALL_DRIVER_UART, 1);

    small_driver_init();
    small_driver_set_duty(0, 0, 0, 0);
    small_driver_get_speed();
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
camera_vision.h - 摄像头视觉识别头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _CAMERA_VISION_H_
#define _CAMERA_VISION_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "shared_memory.h"

#define VISION_WIFI_ENABLE                 (1)      

#define VISION_WIFI_SKIP_WHEN_BUSY         (1)      

#define VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE             (0)

#define VISION_BINARIZE_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT  (190)   
#define VISION_EDGE_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT      (150)   

#define VISION_CAR_FILL_MAX                (1.00f) 
#define VISION_CAR_FILL_MIN                (0.20f) 

#define VISION_CAR_AREA_MIN                (20)    
#define VISION_CAR_AREA_MAX                (110)   

#define VISION_CAR_PERIM_MIN               (0.55f) 
#define VISION_CAR_PERIM_MAX               (1.80f) 

#define VISION_CAR_RUN_MIN                 (2.00f) 

#define VISION_CAR_CENTER_CHECK_R          (1)     
#define VISION_CAR_CENTER_WHITE_RATIO      (0.30f) 

#define VISION_BEACON_MIN_AREA             (2)     
#define VISION_BEACON_MAX_AREA             (90)    

#define VISION_BEACON_FILL_MIN             (0.40f) 
#define VISION_BEACON_FILL_MAX             (0.90f) 

#define VISION_BEACON_ASPECT_MIN           (1.00f) 
#define VISION_BEACON_ASPECT_MAX           (1.80f) 

#define VISION_STOMP_CONFIRM_FRAMES        (15)    

#define VISION_BEACON_BBOX_EXCLUDE_MARGIN  (2)     

#define VISION_BEACON_ISOLATION_MARGIN     (25)    
#define VISION_BEACON_ISOLATION_AREA       (35)    

#define VISION_CAR_BBOX_EXCLUDE_MARGIN     (8)     

#define VISION_BEACON_MIN_AREA_NEAR        (20)    

#define VISION_CAR_HISTORY_RADIUS          (4)     

#define VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD       (35)   
#define VISION_NEAR_CONFIRM_FRAMES  (3)    

#define VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE   (1)   
#define VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N    (30)  
#define VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_THRESHOLD (15)  
#define VISION_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_FRAMES  (45)  

#define VISION_SEARCH_REALHIT_ENTER   (4)   
#define VISION_SEARCH_REALHIT_EXIT    (7)  

#define VISION_WIFI_SSID        "iQ"            
#define VISION_WIFI_PASSWORD    "123456789"     
#define VISION_TCP_IP           "192.168.80.30" 
#define VISION_TCP_PORT         "8086"          
#define VISION_LOCAL_PORT       "6666"          

uint8 camera_vision_init(void);

uint8 camera_vision_update(void);

#endif
