camera_vision.c - 摄像头视觉识别实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "zf_device_mt9v03x.h"
#include "zf_device_wifi_spi.h"
#include "camera_vision.h"
#include "shared_memory.h"

#define VISION_HEARTBEAT_ENABLE   0
#if (VISION_HEARTBEAT_ENABLE)
    #define CAM_HB(ch)   uart_write_byte(UART_1, (uint8)(ch))
#else
    #define CAM_HB(ch)   ((void)0)
#endif

extern vision_cmd_t g_vision_cmd;       
extern uint8        g_image_fresh_flag; 
extern uint8        g_m7_1_status;      

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)

static uint8 s_wifi_ready = 0U;
#endif

static uint8 s_image_copy[MT9V03X_H][MT9V03X_W];

static uint8 s_binary_image[MT9V03X_H][MT9V03X_W];

static uint8 s_edge_image[MT9V03X_H][MT9V03X_W];

static float s_binarize_threshold = VISION_BINARIZE_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT;

static float s_edge_threshold = VISION_EDGE_THRESHOLD_DEFAULT;

static float s_car_fill_max   = VISION_CAR_FILL_MAX;

static float s_car_fill_min   = VISION_CAR_FILL_MIN;

static float s_car_area_min   = VISION_CAR_AREA_MIN;

static float s_car_area_max   = VISION_CAR_AREA_MAX;

static float s_beacon_min_area  = VISION_BEACON_MIN_AREA;

static float s_beacon_max_area  = VISION_BEACON_MAX_AREA;

static float s_beacon_fill_min  = VISION_BEACON_FILL_MIN;

static float s_beacon_fill_max  = VISION_BEACON_FILL_MAX;

static float s_beacon_aspect_min = VISION_BEACON_ASPECT_MIN;

static float s_beacon_aspect_max = VISION_BEACON_ASPECT_MAX;

static float s_car_perim_min  = VISION_CAR_PERIM_MIN;

static float s_car_perim_max  = VISION_CAR_PERIM_MAX;

static float s_car_run_min    = VISION_CAR_RUN_MIN;

static float s_car_bbox_exclude_margin = VISION_CAR_BBOX_EXCLUDE_MARGIN;

static float s_beacon_min_area_near = VISION_BEACON_MIN_AREA_NEAR;

#define BEACON_HISTORY_RADIUS  5   
static int s_last_beacon_cx = -1; 
static int s_last_beacon_cy = -1; 
static int s_last_beacon_xmin = -1; 
static int s_last_beacon_xmax = -1;
static int s_last_beacon_ymin = -1;
static int s_last_beacon_ymax = -1;

static int   s_last_car_cx    = -1; 
static int   s_last_car_cy    = -1; 
static int   s_last_car_xmin  = -1; 
static int   s_last_car_xmax  = -1;
static int   s_last_car_ymin  = -1;
static int   s_last_car_ymax  = -1;

static const uint8 s_font5x7[][5] =
{
     {0x3E, 0x51, 0x49, 0x45, 0x3E},
     {0x00, 0x42, 0x7F, 0x40, 0x00},
     {0x42, 0x61, 0x51, 0x49, 0x46},
     {0x21, 0x41, 0x45, 0x4B, 0x31},
     {0x18, 0x14, 0x12, 0x7F, 0x10},
     {0x27, 0x45, 0x45, 0x45, 0x39},
     {0x3C, 0x4A, 0x49, 0x49, 0x30},
     {0x01, 0x71, 0x09, 0x05, 0x03},
     {0x36, 0x49, 0x49, 0x49, 0x36},
     {0x06, 0x49, 0x49, 0x29, 0x1E},
     {0x00, 0x36, 0x36, 0x00, 0x00},
     {0x63, 0x14, 0x08, 0x14, 0x63},
     {0x07, 0x08, 0x70, 0x08, 0x07},
     {0x08, 0x08, 0x3E, 0x08, 0x08},
     {0x08, 0x08, 0x08, 0x08, 0x08},
     {0x7E, 0x09, 0x09, 0x09, 0x7E},
     {0x7F, 0x09, 0x09, 0x09, 0x01},
     {0x7F, 0x09, 0x19, 0x29, 0x46},
     {0x7F, 0x09, 0x09, 0x09, 0x06},
     {0x3F, 0x40, 0x40, 0x40, 0x3F},
     {0x7F, 0x08, 0x08, 0x08, 0x7F},
     {0x3E, 0x41, 0x41, 0x41, 0x22},
     {0x61, 0x51, 0x49, 0x45, 0x43},   
};

static int font_index(char c)
{
    if(c >= '0' && c <= '9') return c - '0';
    if(c == ':') return 10;
    if(c == 'X') return 11;
    if(c == 'Y') return 12;
    if(c == '+') return 13;
    if(c == '-') return 14;
    if(c == 'A') return 15;
    if(c == 'F') return 16;
    if(c == 'R') return 17;
    if(c == 'P') return 18;
    if(c == 'U') return 19;
    if(c == 'H') return 20;
    if(c == 'C') return 21;
    if(c == 'Z') return 22;
    return -1;
}

static void draw_pixel(int r, int c, uint8 val)
{
    if(r >= 0 && r < MT9V03X_H && c >= 0 && c < MT9V03X_W)
        s_image_copy[r][c] = val;
}

static void draw_hline(int r, int c1, int c2, uint8 val)
{
    int c;
    if(c1 < 0) c1 = 0;
    if(c2 > MT9V03X_W - 1) c2 = MT9V03X_W - 1;
    for(c = c1; c <= c2; c++)             
        draw_pixel(r, c, val);
}

static void draw_vline(int c, int r1, int r2, uint8 val)
{
    int r;
    if(r1 < 0) r1 = 0;
    if(r2 > MT9V03X_H - 1) r2 = MT9V03X_H - 1;
    for(r = r1; r <= r2; r++)
        draw_pixel(r, c, val);
}

static void draw_rect(int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, uint8 val)
{
    draw_hline(y1, x1, x2, val);
    draw_hline(y2, x1, x2, val);
    draw_vline(x1, y1, y2, val);
    draw_vline(x2, y1, y2, val);
}

static void draw_cross(int cx, int cy, int r, uint8 val)
{
    draw_hline(cy, cx - r, cx + r, val);
    draw_vline(cx, cy - r, cy + r, val);
}

static void draw_char(int row, int col, char c, uint8 val)
{
    int idx = font_index(c);
    int x, y;
    if(idx < 0) return;
    for(x = 0; x < 5; x++)
    {
        uint8 col_data = s_font5x7[idx][x];
        for(y = 0; y < 7; y++)
        {
            if(col_data & (1 << y))
                draw_pixel(row + y, col + x, val);
        }
    }
}

static void draw_string(int row, int col, const char *str, uint8 val)
{
    while(*str)
    {
        draw_char(row, col, *str, val);
        col += 6;
        str++;
    }
}

static void compute_edge_image(uint8 thresh)
{
    int r, c;

    for(c = 0; c < MT9V03X_W; c++)
    {
        s_edge_image[0][c]              = 0;
        s_edge_image[MT9V03X_H - 1][c] = 0;
    }
    
    for(r = 0; r < MT9V03X_H; r++)
    {
        s_edge_image[r][0]              = 0;
        s_edge_image[r][MT9V03X_W - 1] = 0;
    }

    for(r = 1; r < MT9V03X_H - 1; r++)
    {
        for(c = 1; c < MT9V03X_W - 1; c++)
        {
            int gx  = (int)s_image_copy[r][c + 1] - (int)s_image_copy[r][c - 1];
            int gy  = (int)s_image_copy[r + 1][c] - (int)s_image_copy[r - 1][c];
            int mag = (gx < 0 ? -gx : gx) + (gy < 0 ? -gy : gy);
            s_edge_image[r][c] = (mag >= (int)thresh) ? 255 : 0;
        }
    }
}

typedef struct
{
    int x_min, x_max;   
    int y_min, y_max;   
    int cx, cy;          
    int area;            
    int score;           
    uint8 pass_flags;    
    uint8 valid;         
} beacon_t;

typedef struct
{
    beacon_t car;           
    beacon_t target;        
    uint8    beacon_count;  
    uint8    target_real;   
} vision_result_t;

#define MAX_LABELS  64

static uint8 s_label_parent[MAX_LABELS];

static uint8 uf_find(uint8 x)
{
    while(s_label_parent[x] != x)
    {
        s_label_parent[x] = s_label_parent[s_label_parent[x]]; 
        x = s_label_parent[x];
    }
    return x;
}

static void uf_union(uint8 a, uint8 b)
{
    uint8 ra = uf_find(a);
    uint8 rb = uf_find(b);
    if(ra != rb)
        s_label_parent[ra] = rb;
}

typedef struct
{
    uint8 c_start; 
    uint8 c_end;   
    uint8 label;   
} run_t;

#define MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW  94   

static run_t s_runs_prev[MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW]; 
static run_t s_runs_curr[MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW]; 

typedef struct
{
    int x_min, x_max, y_min, y_max;
    int area;
    int run_count; 
    uint8 used;
} label_stat_t;

static label_stat_t s_stats[MAX_LABELS];

typedef enum
{
    FIND_CAR_ONLY    = 0,  
    FIND_BEACON_ONLY = 1,  
} find_mode_e;

static void find_targets(const uint8 (*img)[MT9V03X_W], vision_result_t *vr, find_mode_e mode, uint8 skip_car_filter, int min_area_override, uint8 no_fallback)
{
    int r, c, i;
    int prev_cnt, curr_cnt;
    uint8 next_label;

    for(i = 0; i < MAX_LABELS; i++)
    {
        s_label_parent[i]      = (uint8)i;
        s_stats[i].used        = 0;
        s_stats[i].area        = 0;
        s_stats[i].run_count   = 0;
        s_stats[i].x_min       = MT9V03X_W;
        s_stats[i].x_max  = 0;
        s_stats[i].y_min  = MT9V03X_H;
        s_stats[i].y_max  = 0;
    }

    next_label = 1; 
    prev_cnt   = 0;

    for(r = 0; r < MT9V03X_H; r++)
    {
        
        curr_cnt = 0;
        c = 0;
        while(c < MT9V03X_W && curr_cnt < MAX_RUNS_PER_ROW)
        {
            
            while(c < MT9V03X_W && img[r][c] == 0) c++;
            if(c >= MT9V03X_W) break;

            s_runs_curr[curr_cnt].c_start = (uint8)c;
            while(c < MT9V03X_W && img[r][c] != 0) c++;
            s_runs_curr[curr_cnt].c_end   = (uint8)(c - 1);
            s_runs_curr[curr_cnt].label   = 0; 
            curr_cnt++;
        }

        for(i = 0; i < curr_cnt; i++)
        {
            int j;
            uint8 assigned = 0;
            for(j = 0; j < prev_cnt; j++)
            {
                
                if(s_runs_curr[i].c_start <= s_runs_prev[j].c_end &&
                   s_runs_curr[i].c_end   >= s_runs_prev[j].c_start)
                {
                    if(!assigned)
                    {
                        s_runs_curr[i].label = s_runs_prev[j].label;
                        assigned = 1;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        
                        uf_union(s_runs_curr[i].label, s_runs_prev[j].label);
                    }
                }
            }
            
            if(!assigned)
            {
                if(next_label < MAX_LABELS)
                    s_runs_curr[i].label = next_label++;
                else
                    s_runs_curr[i].label = MAX_LABELS - 1; 
            }
        }

        for(i = 0; i < curr_cnt; i++)
        {
            uint8 root = uf_find(s_runs_curr[i].label);
            int   area = s_runs_curr[i].c_end - s_runs_curr[i].c_start + 1;
            s_stats[root].used = 1;
            s_stats[root].area += area;
            s_stats[root].run_count++;  
            if(s_runs_curr[i].c_start < s_stats[root].x_min) s_stats[root].x_min = s_runs_curr[i].c_start;
            if(s_runs_curr[i].c_end   > s_stats[root].x_max) s_stats[root].x_max = s_runs_curr[i].c_end;
            if(r < s_stats[root].y_min) s_stats[root].y_min = r;
            if(r > s_stats[root].y_max) s_stats[root].y_max = r;
        }

        {
            run_t *tmp_ptr;
            int    tmp_cnt;
            
            tmp_cnt = prev_cnt;
            prev_cnt = curr_cnt;
            curr_cnt = tmp_cnt;
            for(i = 0; i < prev_cnt; i++)
                s_runs_prev[i] = s_runs_curr[i];
            (void)tmp_ptr; 
        }
    }

    for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
    {
        uint8 root = uf_find((uint8)i);
        if(root != i && s_stats[i].used)
        {
            s_stats[root].used       = 1;
            s_stats[root].area       += s_stats[i].area;
            s_stats[root].run_count  += s_stats[i].run_count;
            if(s_stats[i].x_min < s_stats[root].x_min) s_stats[root].x_min = s_stats[i].x_min;
            if(s_stats[i].x_max > s_stats[root].x_max) s_stats[root].x_max = s_stats[i].x_max;
            if(s_stats[i].y_min < s_stats[root].y_min) s_stats[root].y_min = s_stats[i].y_min;
            if(s_stats[i].y_max > s_stats[root].y_max) s_stats[root].y_max = s_stats[i].y_max;
            s_stats[i].used = 0; 
        }
    }

    if(mode == FIND_CAR_ONLY)
    {

        int best_score = -1;
        int best_dist2 = 0x7FFFFFFF;
        
        int ref_cx = (s_last_car_cx >= 0) ? s_last_car_cx : (MT9V03X_W / 2);
        int ref_cy = (s_last_car_cx >= 0) ? s_last_car_cy : (MT9V03X_H / 2);

        for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
        {
            int   cx, cy, dx, dy, dist2, score;
            int   w, h, bbox_area, perimeter, height_span;
            float fill_ratio, perim_ratio, avg_runs;

            if(!s_stats[i].used) continue;

            w = s_stats[i].x_max - s_stats[i].x_min + 1;
            h = s_stats[i].y_max - s_stats[i].y_min + 1;
            bbox_area = w * h;
            if(bbox_area <= 0) continue;

            cx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            cy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;

            if(cx < CAR_ZONE_X_MIN || cx > CAR_ZONE_X_MAX ||
               cy < CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN || cy > CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX)
                continue;

            if(vr->target.valid)
            {
                int margin = VISION_BEACON_BBOX_EXCLUDE_MARGIN;
                if(cx >= vr->target.x_min - margin && cx <= vr->target.x_max + margin &&
                   cy >= vr->target.y_min - margin && cy <= vr->target.y_max + margin)
                    continue;
            }

            if(s_stats[i].area < (int)s_car_area_min ||
               s_stats[i].area > (int)s_car_area_max)
                continue;

            {
                int r   = VISION_CAR_CENTER_CHECK_R;
                int cx0 = cx - r, cx1 = cx + r;
                int cy0 = cy - r, cy1 = cy + r;
                int bx, by, white = 0, total = 0;
                if(cx0 < 0) cx0 = 0;
                if(cy0 < 0) cy0 = 0;
                if(cx1 >= MT9V03X_W) cx1 = MT9V03X_W - 1;
                if(cy1 >= MT9V03X_H) cy1 = MT9V03X_H - 1;
                for(by = cy0; by <= cy1; by++)
                    for(bx = cx0; bx <= cx1; bx++)
                    { total++; if(s_binary_image[by][bx]) white++; }
                if(total > 0 && (float)white / (float)total > VISION_CAR_CENTER_WHITE_RATIO)
                    continue;   
            }

            score = 0;
            {
                uint8 flags = 0;

                fill_ratio = (float)s_stats[i].area / (float)bbox_area;
                if(fill_ratio >= s_car_fill_min &&
                   fill_ratio <  s_car_fill_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 0); } 

                perimeter = 2 * (w + h);
                perim_ratio = (perimeter > 0) ? ((float)s_stats[i].area / (float)perimeter) : 0.0f;
                if(perim_ratio >= s_car_perim_min &&
                   perim_ratio <= s_car_perim_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 1); } 

                height_span = (s_stats[i].y_max - s_stats[i].y_min + 1);
                avg_runs = (height_span > 0) ? ((float)s_stats[i].run_count / (float)height_span) : 0.0f;
                if(avg_runs >= s_car_run_min)
                { score += 2; flags |= (1 << 2); } 

                if(s_last_car_cx >= 0)
                {
                    int hdx = cx - s_last_car_cx;
                    int hdy = cy - s_last_car_cy;
                    if(hdx * hdx + hdy * hdy <=
                       VISION_CAR_HISTORY_RADIUS * VISION_CAR_HISTORY_RADIUS)
                    { score += 2; flags |= (1 << 3); } 
                }

                dx = cx - ref_cx;
                dy = cy - ref_cy;
                dist2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;

                if(score > best_score || (score == best_score && dist2 < best_dist2))
                {
                    best_score         = score;
                    best_dist2         = dist2;
                    vr->car.x_min      = s_stats[i].x_min;
                    vr->car.x_max      = s_stats[i].x_max;
                    vr->car.y_min      = s_stats[i].y_min;
                    vr->car.y_max      = s_stats[i].y_max;
                    vr->car.cx         = cx;
                    vr->car.cy         = cy;
                    vr->car.area       = s_stats[i].area;
                    vr->car.score      = score;
                    vr->car.pass_flags = flags;
                    vr->car.valid      = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if(!vr->car.valid && s_last_car_cx >= 0)
        {
            vr->car.x_min = s_last_car_xmin;
            vr->car.x_max = s_last_car_xmax;
            vr->car.y_min = s_last_car_ymin;
            vr->car.y_max = s_last_car_ymax;
            vr->car.cx    = s_last_car_cx;
            vr->car.cy    = s_last_car_cy;
            vr->car.area       = 0;
            vr->car.score      = 0;
            vr->car.pass_flags = 0;
            vr->car.valid      = 1;
        }

        if(vr->car.valid && best_score >= 0)
        {
            s_last_car_cx   = vr->car.cx;
            s_last_car_cy   = vr->car.cy;
            s_last_car_xmin = vr->car.x_min;
            s_last_car_xmax = vr->car.x_max;
            s_last_car_ymin = vr->car.y_min;
            s_last_car_ymax = vr->car.y_max;
        }
    }
    else 
    {

        int ref_cx = vr->car.valid ? vr->car.cx : (MT9V03X_W / 2);
        int ref_cy = vr->car.valid ? vr->car.cy : (MT9V03X_H / 2);
        int best_score  = -1;
        int best_dist2  = 0x7FFFFFFF;
        int found_any   = 0; 
        int min_dist2_to_car = 0x7FFFFFFF; 

        for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
        {
            int pcx, pcy, pdx, pdy, pd2, area_min;
            if(!s_stats[i].used) continue;
            pcx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            pcy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;
            if(!skip_car_filter && s_last_car_cx >= 0)
            {
                int margin = (int)s_car_bbox_exclude_margin;
                if(pcx >= s_last_car_xmin - margin && pcx <= s_last_car_xmax + margin &&
                   pcy >= s_last_car_ymin - margin && pcy <= s_last_car_ymax + margin)
                    continue;
            }
            area_min = (min_area_override >= 0) ? min_area_override : (int)s_beacon_min_area;
            if(s_stats[i].area < area_min || s_stats[i].area > (int)s_beacon_max_area)
                continue;
            pdx = pcx - ref_cx;
            pdy = pcy - ref_cy;
            pd2 = pdx * pdx + pdy * pdy;
            if(pd2 < min_dist2_to_car) min_dist2_to_car = pd2;
        }

        for(i = 1; i < next_label; i++)
        {
            int   cx, cy, dx, dy, dist2;
            int   w, h, bbox_area;
            float fill_ratio;
            int   score = 0;
            uint8 flags = 0;

            if(!s_stats[i].used) continue;

            cx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            cy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;

            if(!skip_car_filter && s_last_car_cx >= 0)
            {
                int margin = (int)s_car_bbox_exclude_margin;
                if(cx >= s_last_car_xmin - margin && cx <= s_last_car_xmax + margin &&
                   cy >= s_last_car_ymin - margin && cy <= s_last_car_ymax + margin)
                    continue;
            }

            {
                int area_min = (min_area_override >= 0) ? min_area_override : (int)s_beacon_min_area;
                if(s_stats[i].area < area_min ||
                   s_stats[i].area > (int)s_beacon_max_area)
                    continue;
            }

            found_any = 1; 

            w = s_stats[i].x_max - s_stats[i].x_min + 1;
            h = s_stats[i].y_max - s_stats[i].y_min + 1;
            bbox_area = w * h;
            if(bbox_area > 0)
            {
                fill_ratio = (float)s_stats[i].area / (float)bbox_area;
                if(fill_ratio >= s_beacon_fill_min &&
                   fill_ratio <= s_beacon_fill_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 0); } 
            }

            if(w > 0 && h > 0)
            {
                float aspect = (float)w / (float)h;
                if(aspect >= s_beacon_aspect_min && aspect <= s_beacon_aspect_max)
                { score++; flags |= (1 << 1); } 
            }

            if(s_last_beacon_cx >= 0)
            {
                int hdx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2 - s_last_beacon_cx;
                int hdy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2 - s_last_beacon_cy;
                if(hdx * hdx + hdy * hdy <=
                   BEACON_HISTORY_RADIUS * BEACON_HISTORY_RADIUS)
                { score += 3; flags |= (1 << 2); } 
            }

            {
                int cdx = cx - ref_cx;
                int cdy = cy - ref_cy;
                int cd2 = cdx * cdx + cdy * cdy;
                if(cd2 == min_dist2_to_car)
                { score += 2; flags |= (1 << 3); } 
            }

            cx = (s_stats[i].x_min + s_stats[i].x_max) / 2;
            cy = (s_stats[i].y_min + s_stats[i].y_max) / 2;
            dx = cx - ref_cx;
            dy = cy - ref_cy;
            dist2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;

            {
                int margin = VISION_BEACON_ISOLATION_MARGIN;
                int ex1 = s_stats[i].x_min - margin;
                int ex2 = s_stats[i].x_max + margin;
                int ey1 = s_stats[i].y_min - margin;
                int ey2 = s_stats[i].y_max + margin;
                int j;
                for(j = 1; j < next_label; j++)
                {
                    if(j == i || !s_stats[j].used) continue;
                    if(s_stats[j].area <= VISION_BEACON_ISOLATION_AREA) continue;
                    
                    if(s_stats[j].x_max >= ex1 && s_stats[j].x_min <= ex2 &&
                       s_stats[j].y_max >= ey1 && s_stats[j].y_min <= ey2)
                    {
                        score -= 2;
                        break; 
                    }
                }
            }

            if(score >= 2)
                vr->beacon_count++;

            if(score > best_score || (score == best_score && dist2 < best_dist2))
            {
                best_score          = score;
                best_dist2          = dist2;
                vr->target.x_min    = s_stats[i].x_min;
                vr->target.x_max    = s_stats[i].x_max;
                vr->target.y_min    = s_stats[i].y_min;
                vr->target.y_max    = s_stats[i].y_max;
                vr->target.cx       = cx;
                vr->target.cy       = cy;
                vr->target.area     = s_stats[i].area;
                vr->target.score    = score;
                vr->target.pass_flags = flags;
                vr->target.valid    = 1;
            }
        }

        vr->target_real = (uint8)found_any;

        if(!found_any && s_last_beacon_cx >= 0 && !no_fallback)
        {
            vr->target.cx    = s_last_beacon_cx;
            vr->target.cy    = s_last_beacon_cy;
            vr->target.x_min = s_last_beacon_xmin;
            vr->target.x_max = s_last_beacon_xmax;
            vr->target.y_min = s_last_beacon_ymin;
            vr->target.y_max = s_last_beacon_ymax;
            vr->target.valid = 1;
            vr->target.score = 0;
        }

        if(vr->target.valid)
        {
            s_last_beacon_cx = vr->target.cx;
            s_last_beacon_cy = vr->target.cy;
            if(found_any)
            {
                s_last_beacon_xmin = vr->target.x_min;
                s_last_beacon_xmax = vr->target.x_max;
                s_last_beacon_ymin = vr->target.y_min;
                s_last_beacon_ymax = vr->target.y_max;
            }
        }
    }
}

static int int_to_str(int val, char *buf)
{
    int          len = 0;
    int          tmp;
    char         tmp_buf[12];    
    int          i;
    unsigned int uval;

    if(val >= 0)
    {
        buf[len++] = '+';
        uval = (unsigned int)val;
    }
    else
    {
        buf[len++] = '-';
        uval = (unsigned int)(-(val + 1)) + 1u;  
    }

    tmp = 0;
    if(uval == 0)
    {
        tmp_buf[tmp++] = '0';
    }
    else
    {
        while(uval > 0 && tmp < (int)sizeof(tmp_buf))
        {
            tmp_buf[tmp++] = (char)('0' + uval % 10);
            uval /= 10;
        }
    }

    for(i = tmp - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        buf[len++] = tmp_buf[i];

    buf[len] = '\0';
    return len;
}

static uint8 s_stomp_counter = 0;

typedef enum
{
    VS_INIT,           
    VS_FAR_TRACKING,   
    VS_NEAR_STOMPING,  
} vision_state_e;

static vision_state_e s_vision_state       = VS_INIT; 
static uint8          s_near_confirm_cnt   = 0;        
static uint8          s_stomp_detect_cnt   = 0;        

#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)

static uint8  s_search_active       = 0;      
static uint8  s_prev_display_class  = 0xFF;   
static uint8  s_flip_hist[VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N] = {0}; 
static uint8  s_flip_idx            = 0;      
static uint16 s_flip_sum            = 0;      
static uint16 s_no_beacon_frames    = 0;      

static uint8  s_realhit_hist[VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N] = {0};
static uint8  s_realhit_idx         = 0;
static uint16 s_realhit_sum         = 0;
#endif

uint8 camera_vision_init(void)
{
    
    mt9v03x_init();

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    s_wifi_ready = 0U;

    if(wifi_spi_init(VISION_WIFI_SSID, VISION_WIFI_PASSWORD) == 0U)
    {
        if((WIFI_SPI_AUTO_CONNECT == 1) ||
           (wifi_spi_socket_connect(
               "TCP",
               VISION_TCP_IP,
               VISION_TCP_PORT,
               VISION_LOCAL_PORT) == 0U))
        {
            seekfree_assistant_interface_init(SEEKFREE_ASSISTANT_WIFI_SPI);
            seekfree_assistant_camera_information_config(
                SEEKFREE_ASSISTANT_MT9V03X,
                s_image_copy[0],
                MT9V03X_W,
                MT9V03X_H
            );
            s_wifi_ready = 1U;
        }
    }
#endif 

    return 0;
}

uint8 camera_vision_update(void)
{
    if(!mt9v03x_finish_flag) return 0;   

    __disable_irq();
    
    memcpy(s_image_copy[0], mt9v03x_image[0], MT9V03X_IMAGE_SIZE);
    mt9v03x_finish_flag = 0;
    __enable_irq();

#if (VISION_HEARTBEAT_ENABLE)
    {
        static uint32 s_hb_frame = 0;
        if(((++s_hb_frame) & 0x3F) == 0)
        {
            char hb[12];
            uart_write_byte(UART_1, '\r');
            uart_write_byte(UART_1, '\n');
            int_to_str((int)s_hb_frame, hb);
            uart_write_string(UART_1, hb);
            uart_write_byte(UART_1, ':');
        }
    }
#endif
    CAM_HB('A');

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    if(s_wifi_ready)
    {
        seekfree_assistant_data_analysis();
        CAM_HB('P');   

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)

    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0])
    {
        s_edge_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[0];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1])
    {
        s_car_fill_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[1] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2])
    {
        s_car_area_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[2];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3])
    {
        s_car_area_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[3];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4])
    {
        s_car_perim_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[4] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5])
    {
        s_car_perim_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[5] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6])
    {
        s_car_fill_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[6] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7])
    {
        s_car_run_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[7] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7] = 0;
    }

#elif (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)

    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0])
    {
        s_binarize_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[0];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1])
    {
        s_beacon_min_area = seekfree_assistant_parameter[1];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2])
    {
        s_beacon_max_area = seekfree_assistant_parameter[2];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3])
    {
        s_beacon_fill_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[3] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4])
    {
        s_beacon_fill_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[4] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5])
    {
        s_beacon_aspect_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[5] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6])
    {
        s_beacon_aspect_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[6] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6] = 0;
    }

#else

    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0])
    {
        s_binarize_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[0];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[0] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1])
    {
        s_edge_threshold = seekfree_assistant_parameter[1];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[1] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2])
    {
        s_car_fill_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[2] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[2] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3])
    {
        s_car_area_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[3];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[3] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4])
    {
        s_car_area_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[4];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[4] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5])
    {
        s_beacon_max_area = seekfree_assistant_parameter[5];
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[5] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6])
    {
        s_car_perim_min = seekfree_assistant_parameter[6] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[6] = 0;
    }
    
    if(seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7])
    {
        s_car_perim_max = seekfree_assistant_parameter[7] / 100.0f;
        seekfree_assistant_parameter_update_flag[7] = 0;
    }
#endif 
    }
#endif 

    {
        int r, c;
        uint8 thresh = (uint8)s_binarize_threshold;
        for(r = 0; r < MT9V03X_H; r++)
            for(c = 0; c < MT9V03X_W; c++)
                s_binary_image[r][c] = (s_image_copy[r][c] >= thresh) ? 255 : 0;
    }

    compute_edge_image((uint8)s_edge_threshold);

    {
        vision_result_t vr;
        
        memset(&vr, 0, sizeof(vr));

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        if(s_vision_state == VS_NEAR_STOMPING)
        {
            
            find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_binary_image, &vr, FIND_BEACON_ONLY, 1, (int)s_beacon_min_area_near, 1);
            find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_edge_image,   &vr, FIND_CAR_ONLY,    0, -1, 0);
        }
        else 
        {
            
            find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_edge_image, &vr, FIND_CAR_ONLY, 0, -1, 0);
            if(vr.car.valid)
                find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_binary_image, &vr, FIND_BEACON_ONLY, 0, -1, 0);
        }
#elif (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
        
        find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_edge_image, &vr, FIND_CAR_ONLY, 0, -1, 0);
#else 
        
        find_targets((const uint8 (*)[MT9V03X_W])s_binary_image, &vr, FIND_BEACON_ONLY, 1, -1, 0);
#endif

#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        {
            uint8 cls  = (s_vision_state == VS_NEAR_STOMPING) ? 1 : 0;   
            uint8 flip = (s_prev_display_class != 0xFF && cls != s_prev_display_class) ? 1 : 0;
            s_prev_display_class = cls;

            s_flip_sum -= s_flip_hist[s_flip_idx];
            s_flip_hist[s_flip_idx] = flip;
            s_flip_sum += flip;
            s_flip_idx = (uint8)((s_flip_idx + 1) % VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N);

            {
                uint8 real = vr.target_real ? 1 : 0;
                s_realhit_sum -= s_realhit_hist[s_realhit_idx];
                s_realhit_hist[s_realhit_idx] = real;
                s_realhit_sum += real;
                s_realhit_idx = (uint8)((s_realhit_idx + 1) % VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N);
            }

            if(vr.target.valid)
                s_no_beacon_frames = 0;
            else if(s_no_beacon_frames < 0xFFFF)
                s_no_beacon_frames++;

            if(!s_search_active)
            {
                
                if(s_flip_sum > VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_THRESHOLD ||
                   s_no_beacon_frames >= VISION_SEARCH_TIMEOUT_FRAMES ||
                   s_realhit_sum < VISION_SEARCH_REALHIT_ENTER)
                    s_search_active = 1;
            }
            else
            {
                
                if(s_realhit_sum >= VISION_SEARCH_REALHIT_EXIT)
                {
                    int _i;
                    s_search_active = 0;
                    
                    s_flip_sum = 0;
                    for(_i = 0; _i < VISION_SEARCH_JITTER_WIN_N; _i++) s_flip_hist[_i] = 0;
                    s_no_beacon_frames = 0;
                }
            }
        }
#endif

        CAM_HB('F');

#if   (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
        
        memcpy(s_image_copy[0], s_edge_image[0], MT9V03X_IMAGE_SIZE);
#elif (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)
        
        memcpy(s_image_copy[0], s_binary_image[0], MT9V03X_IMAGE_SIZE);
#endif

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        {
            char state_ch = 'C';
            if(s_vision_state == VS_NEAR_STOMPING) state_ch = 'X';
            draw_char(2, 2, state_ch, 255);
        }
#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE)
        
        if(s_search_active) draw_char(2, MT9V03X_W - 7, 'Z', 255);
        
        {
            char jbuf[12];
            int_to_str((int)s_flip_sum, jbuf);
            draw_string(2, 12, (jbuf[0] == '+') ? jbuf + 1 : jbuf, 255);  
        }
        
        {
            char rbuf[12];
            int_to_str((int)s_realhit_sum, rbuf);
            draw_string(2, MT9V03X_W - 7 - 2 - 12, (rbuf[0] == '+') ? rbuf + 1 : rbuf, 255); 
        }
#endif
#endif

        if(vr.target.valid)
        {
            
            int ref_cx = vr.car.valid ? vr.car.cx : (MT9V03X_W / 2);
            int ref_cy = vr.car.valid ? vr.car.cy : (MT9V03X_H / 2);
            int dx = vr.target.cx - ref_cx;
            int dy = vr.target.cy - ref_cy;

            s_stomp_counter = 0;

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE != 2)
            if(vr.car.valid)
            {
                draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 1, vr.car.y_min - 1,
                          vr.car.x_max + 1, vr.car.y_max + 1, 255);
                draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 4, vr.car.y_min - 4,
                          vr.car.x_max + 4, vr.car.y_max + 4, 255);
#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
                
                {
                    char score_buf[8];
                    int  slen = 0;
                    int  text_row = vr.car.y_min - 9;
                    int  text_col = vr.car.x_min - 4;
                    if(text_row < 0) text_row = vr.car.y_max + 5;
                    if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;
                    score_buf[slen++] = '0' + (char)(vr.car.score);
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 0)) score_buf[slen++] = 'F';
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 1)) score_buf[slen++] = 'P';
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 2)) score_buf[slen++] = 'U';
                    if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 3)) score_buf[slen++] = 'H';
                    score_buf[slen] = '\0';
                    draw_string(text_row, text_col, score_buf, 255);
                }
#endif
            }
#endif

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE != 1)
            draw_rect(vr.target.x_min - 2, vr.target.y_min - 2,
                      vr.target.x_max + 2, vr.target.y_max + 2, 255);
            draw_cross(vr.target.cx, vr.target.cy, 8, 255);

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)
            
            {
                char score_buf[8];
                int  slen = 0;
                int  text_row = vr.target.y_min - 9;
                int  text_col = vr.target.x_min - 2;
                if(text_row < 0) text_row = vr.target.y_max + 5;
                if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;
                score_buf[slen++] = '0' + (char)(vr.target.score < 0 ? 0 : vr.target.score);
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 0)) score_buf[slen++] = 'F';
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 1)) score_buf[slen++] = 'R';
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 2)) score_buf[slen++] = 'H';
                if(vr.target.pass_flags & (1 << 3)) score_buf[slen++] = 'C';
                score_buf[slen] = '\0';
                draw_string(text_row, text_col, score_buf, 255);
            }
#endif

            {
                char str_x[12], str_y[12];
                char label[20];
                int  pos = 0;
                int  text_row, text_col;
                const int LABEL_MAX = (int)sizeof(label) - 1;    

                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = 'X';
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = ':';
                int_to_str(dx, str_x);
                { int k = 0; while(str_x[k] && pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = str_x[k++]; }
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = ' ';
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = 'Y';
                if(pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = ':';
                int_to_str(dy, str_y);
                { int k = 0; while(str_y[k] && pos < LABEL_MAX) label[pos++] = str_y[k++]; }
                label[pos] = '\0';

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 2)
                text_row = vr.target.y_min - 19;
#else
                text_row = vr.target.y_min - 10;
#endif
                text_col = vr.target.x_min - 2;
                if(text_row < 0)
                    text_row = vr.target.y_max + 4;
                if(text_col + (int)(pos * 6) > MT9V03X_W)
                    text_col = MT9V03X_W - pos * 6 - 1;
                if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;

                draw_string(text_row, text_col, label, 255);
            }
#endif

            g_vision_cmd.vx             = (float)dx;
            g_vision_cmd.vy             = (float)dy;
            g_vision_cmd.valid          = 1;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_stomped = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_count   = vr.beacon_count;
#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
            
            if(s_search_active) g_vision_cmd.valid = 0;
#endif
            SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(vision_cmd_t));
        }
        else if(vr.car.valid)
        {
            
#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE != 2)
            
            draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 1, vr.car.y_min - 1,
                      vr.car.x_max + 1, vr.car.y_max + 1, 255);
            draw_rect(vr.car.x_min - 4, vr.car.y_min - 4,
                      vr.car.x_max + 4, vr.car.y_max + 4, 255);
#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 1)
            
            {
                char score_buf[8];
                int  slen = 0;
                int  text_row = vr.car.y_min - 9;
                int  text_col = vr.car.x_min - 4;
                if(text_row < 0) text_row = vr.car.y_max + 5;
                if(text_col < 0) text_col = 0;
                score_buf[slen++] = '0' + (char)(vr.car.score);
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 0)) score_buf[slen++] = 'F';
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 1)) score_buf[slen++] = 'P';
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 2)) score_buf[slen++] = 'U';
                if(vr.car.pass_flags & (1 << 3)) score_buf[slen++] = 'H';
                score_buf[slen] = '\0';
                draw_string(text_row, text_col, score_buf, 255);
            }
#endif
#endif

            s_stomp_counter++;

            if(s_stomp_counter >= VISION_STOMP_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
            {
                
                s_stomp_counter = 0;
                g_vision_cmd.valid          = 0;
                g_vision_cmd.beacon_stomped = 1;
                g_vision_cmd.beacon_count   = 0;
                SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(vision_cmd_t));
                
                s_last_beacon_cx = -1;
                s_last_beacon_cy = -1;
                s_last_beacon_xmin = -1;
                s_last_beacon_xmax = -1;
                s_last_beacon_ymin = -1;
                s_last_beacon_ymax = -1;
            }
            else
            {
                
            }
        }
        else
        {
            
            s_stomp_counter = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.valid          = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_stomped = 0;
            g_vision_cmd.beacon_count   = 0;
            SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_vision_cmd, sizeof(vision_cmd_t));
        }

#if (VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
    {
        switch(s_vision_state)
        {
            case VS_INIT:
                
                if(vr.car.valid)
                    s_vision_state = VS_FAR_TRACKING;
                break;

            case VS_FAR_TRACKING:
                
                if(vr.target.valid && vr.target.area >= (int)s_beacon_min_area_near && vr.car.valid)
                {
                    int adx = vr.target.cx - vr.car.cx;
                    int ady = vr.target.cy - vr.car.cy;
                    if(adx < 0) adx = -adx;
                    if(ady < 0) ady = -ady;
                    if(adx < VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD && ady < VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD)
                    {
                        s_near_confirm_cnt++;
                        if(s_near_confirm_cnt >= VISION_NEAR_CONFIRM_FRAMES)
                        {
                            
                            s_vision_state     = VS_NEAR_STOMPING;
                            s_near_confirm_cnt = 0;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                        s_near_confirm_cnt = 0;
                }
                else
                    s_near_confirm_cnt = 0;
                break;

            case VS_NEAR_STOMPING:
                
                if(vr.target.valid && vr.target.area >= (int)s_beacon_min_area_near)
                {
                    s_stomp_detect_cnt = 0;
                    if(vr.car.valid)
                    {
                        int adx = vr.target.cx - vr.car.cx;
                        int ady = vr.target.cy - vr.car.cy;
                        if(adx < 0) adx = -adx;
                        if(ady < 0) ady = -ady;
                        if(adx >= VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD || ady >= VISION_NEAR_THRESHOLD)
                            s_vision_state = VS_FAR_TRACKING;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    
                    s_stomp_detect_cnt++;
                    if(s_stomp_detect_cnt >= 3)
                    {
                        s_stomp_detect_cnt = 0;
                        s_vision_state     = VS_FAR_TRACKING;
                    }
                }
                break;

            default:
                break;
        }
    }
#endif 

    {
        extern car_error_t    g_car_error;    
        extern drone_target_t g_drone_target; 

        float center_x = (float)(MT9V03X_W / 2);
        float center_y = (float)(MT9V03X_H / 2);

        if(vr.car.valid) {
            g_car_error.car_error_x  = (float)vr.car.cx - center_x;  
            g_car_error.car_error_y  = (float)vr.car.cy - center_y;  
            g_car_error.car_detected = 1;
        } else {
            
            g_car_error.car_detected = 0;
        }

        int drone_skip_beacon = 0;
#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
        drone_skip_beacon = s_search_active;
#endif
        if(!drone_skip_beacon && vr.target.valid) {
            g_drone_target.err_x = (float)vr.target.cx - center_x;      
            g_drone_target.err_y = (float)vr.target.cy - center_y;
            g_drone_target.valid = 1;
        } else if(!drone_skip_beacon && s_last_beacon_cx >= 0) {
            g_drone_target.err_x = (float)s_last_beacon_cx - center_x;  
            g_drone_target.err_y = (float)s_last_beacon_cy - center_y;
            g_drone_target.valid = 1;
        } else if(vr.car.valid) {
            g_drone_target.err_x = (float)vr.car.cx - center_x;         
            g_drone_target.err_y = (float)vr.car.cy - center_y;
            g_drone_target.valid = 1;
        } else {
            g_drone_target.valid = 0;
        }

        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_car_error,    sizeof(car_error_t));
        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_drone_target, sizeof(drone_target_t));
    }

    } 

#if (VISION_BEACON_SEARCH_ENABLE && VISION_SEND_IMAGE_MODE == 0)
    
    {
        extern search_state_t g_search_state;
        g_search_state.searching = s_search_active;
        SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_search_state, sizeof(search_state_t));
    }
#endif

#if (1 == VISION_WIFI_ENABLE)
    if(s_wifi_ready)
    {
        CAM_HB('S');                    
#if (VISION_WIFI_SKIP_WHEN_BUSY)
        
        if(gpio_get_level(WIFI_SPI_INT_PIN) != 0)
#endif
        seekfree_assistant_camera_send();
        CAM_HB('D');                    
    }
#endif 

    g_image_fresh_flag = 1;
    SCB_CleanInvalidateDCache_by_Addr(&g_image_fresh_flag, 1);

    return 1;   
}

shared_memory.h - 双核共享内存头文件

#ifndef _SHARED_MEMORY_H_
#define _SHARED_MEMORY_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define SHARED_MEM_BASE             (0x28001000UL)

#define SHARED_VISION_CMD_ADDR      (SHARED_MEM_BASE + 0x00UL)

#define SHARED_IMG_FRESH_ADDR       (SHARED_MEM_BASE + 0x10UL)

#define SHARED_M7_1_STATUS_ADDR     (SHARED_MEM_BASE + 0x11UL)

#define SHARED_CAR_ERROR_ADDR       (SHARED_MEM_BASE + 0x20UL)

#define SHARED_DRONE_TARGET_ADDR    (SHARED_MEM_BASE + 0x30UL)

#define SHARED_SEARCH_FLAG_ADDR     (SHARED_MEM_BASE + 0x40UL)

#define CAR_ZONE_X_MIN      3
#define CAR_ZONE_X_MAX      184
#define CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN      3
#define CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX      116
#define CAR_ZONE_CENTER_X   ((CAR_ZONE_X_MIN + CAR_ZONE_X_MAX) / 2)   
#define CAR_ZONE_CENTER_Y   ((CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN + CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX) / 2)   
#define CAR_ZONE_HALF_X     ((CAR_ZONE_X_MAX - CAR_ZONE_X_MIN) / 2)   
#define CAR_ZONE_HALF_Y     ((CAR_ZONE_Y_MAX - CAR_ZONE_Y_MIN) / 2)   

typedef struct
{
    float   vx;             
    float   vy;             
    uint8   valid;          
    uint8   beacon_stomped; 
    uint8   beacon_count;   
    uint8   reserved[5];    
} vision_cmd_t;             

typedef struct
{
    float   car_error_x;    
    float   car_error_y;    
    uint8   car_detected;   
    uint8   reserved[3];    
} car_error_t;              

typedef struct
{
    float   err_x;          
    float   err_y;          
    uint8   valid;          
    uint8   reserved[3];    
} drone_target_t;           

typedef struct
{
    uint8   searching;      
    uint8   reserved[31];   
} search_state_t;           

typedef enum
{
    M7_1_STATUS_INIT    = 0x00, 
    M7_1_STATUS_RUNNING = 0x01, 
    M7_1_STATUS_ERROR   = 0xFF, 
} m7_1_status_enum;

#endif

vision_uart_tx.h - 视觉串口发送头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _VISION_UART_TX_H_
#define _VISION_UART_TX_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define VISION_TX_UART_INDEX      (UART_5)
#define VISION_TX_UART_BAUD       (115200)
#define VISION_TX_UART_TX_PIN     (UART5_TX_P02_1)
#define VISION_TX_UART_RX_PIN     (UART5_RX_P02_0)

#define VISION_TX_FRAME_LEN       (25U)   
#define VISION_TX_DATA_LEN        (22U)   

void vision_uart_tx_init(void);

void vision_uart_tx_send(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
vision_uart_tx.c - 视觉串口发送实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart_tx.h"
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "zf_driver_uart.h"
#include "shared_memory.h"
#include "shared_data.h"
#include <string.h>

extern vision_cmd_t   g_vision_cmd;
extern search_state_t g_search_state;
extern car_error_t    g_car_error;

#define VISION_TX_HEADER1 (0xAAU)
#define VISION_TX_HEADER2 (0x55U)

static uint8 s_tx_buf[VISION_TX_FRAME_LEN];

static uint8 vision_tx_calc_checksum(const uint8 *data, uint16 len)
{
    uint8 sum = 0;
    for(uint16 i = 0; i < len; i++)
    {
        sum += data[i];
    }
    return sum;
}

void vision_uart_tx_init(void)
{
    
    uart_init(VISION_TX_UART_INDEX, VISION_TX_UART_BAUD,
              VISION_TX_UART_TX_PIN, VISION_TX_UART_RX_PIN);
}

void vision_uart_tx_send(void)
{
    uint16 idx = 2U;   

    s_tx_buf[0] = VISION_TX_HEADER1;
    s_tx_buf[1] = VISION_TX_HEADER2;

    memcpy(&s_tx_buf[idx], &g_vision_cmd.vx, 4); idx += 4;
    memcpy(&s_tx_buf[idx], &g_vision_cmd.vy, 4); idx += 4;

    s_tx_buf[idx++] = g_vision_cmd.valid;
    
    SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)SHARED_FLIGHT_DATA_ADDR, (int32_t)sizeof(Shared_FlightData_t));
    s_tx_buf[idx++] = g_shared_flight_data.land_cmd;
    
    s_tx_buf[idx++] = g_shared_flight_data.takeoff_ready;
    s_tx_buf[idx++] = g_vision_cmd.beacon_count;
    s_tx_buf[idx++] = g_search_state.searching;   

    memcpy(&s_tx_buf[idx], &g_car_error.car_error_x, 4); idx += 4;
    memcpy(&s_tx_buf[idx], &g_car_error.car_error_y, 4); idx += 4;
    s_tx_buf[idx++] = g_car_error.car_detected;

    s_tx_buf[24] = vision_tx_calc_checksum(&s_tx_buf[2], VISION_TX_DATA_LEN);

    uart_write_buffer(VISION_TX_UART_INDEX, s_tx_buf, VISION_TX_FRAME_LEN);
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
remote_control.h - 遥控器头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _REMOTE_CONTROL_H_
#define _REMOTE_CONTROL_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define RC_CHANNEL_NUM             6

#define SBUS_VALUE_MIN             (200)    
#define SBUS_VALUE_MAX             (1800)   
#define SBUS_VALUE_MID             (1000)   
#define SBUS_VALUE_RANGE           (SBUS_VALUE_MAX - SBUS_VALUE_MIN)

#define RC_OUTPUT_MIN              (100)    
#define RC_OUTPUT_MAX              (200)    
#define RC_OUTPUT_MID              (150)    
#define RC_OUTPUT_RANGE            (RC_OUTPUT_MAX - RC_OUTPUT_MIN)

#define RC_ROLL_PITCH_CTRL_EN     (1)

#define RC_CH1_PITCH_RAW_MIN      (200)    
#define RC_CH1_PITCH_RAW_MID      (996)    
#define RC_CH1_PITCH_RAW_MAX      (1780)   

#define RC_CH2_ROLL_RAW_MIN       (216)    
#define RC_CH2_ROLL_RAW_MID       (1008)   
#define RC_CH2_ROLL_RAW_MAX       (1816)   

#define RC_RP_RAW_DEADBAND        (25)

#define RC_RP_CTRL_ANGLE          (5.0f)

#define RC_CH4_YAW_MIN            (100)    
#define RC_CH4_YAW_MID            (149)    
#define RC_CH4_YAW_MAX            (198)    

#define RC_MAX_YAW_RATE           (45.0f)  

typedef struct
{
    uint16 ch[RC_CHANNEL_NUM];       
    uint16 ch_value[RC_CHANNEL_NUM]; 
} remote_control_t;

extern remote_control_t remote_data;

typedef struct
{
    uint8 unlocked;       
    uint8 unlock_stage;   
} rc_control_flags_t;

typedef struct
{
    float roll_cmd;       
    float pitch_cmd;      
    float yaw_cmd;        
    uint16 throttle_us;   
    uint8 height_hold;    
    uint8 pos_hold;       
} rc_control_command_t;

extern rc_control_flags_t g_rc_flags;
extern rc_control_command_t g_rc_cmd;

extern uint8 g_motor_linear_mode;

void remote_control_init(void);
void remote_control_update(void);
void remote_control_control_tick(void);
const rc_control_flags_t* rc_control_get_flags(void);
const rc_control_command_t* rc_control_get_command(void);
void rc_get_calibrated_mid(float* ch1_mid, float* ch2_mid);

uint8 remote_control_get_signal_valid(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
remote_control.c - 遥控器实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "remote_control.h"
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "zf_device_uart_receiver.h"
#include "buzzer/buzzer.h"

remote_control_t    remote_data = {
    .ch       = {SBUS_VALUE_MID, SBUS_VALUE_MID, SBUS_VALUE_MIN, SBUS_VALUE_MID, SBUS_VALUE_MIN, SBUS_VALUE_MIN},
    .ch_value = {150, 150, RC_OUTPUT_MIN, 150, 100, 100},
};

rc_control_flags_t   g_rc_flags = {0};
rc_control_command_t g_rc_cmd   = {0};

uint8 g_motor_linear_mode = 1;

static uint32 last_signal_ms = 0;
static uint8 s_unlock_prev = 0U;
static uint8 s_buzzer_active = 0U;
static uint32 s_buzzer_off_ms = 0U;

static inline uint16 clamp_u16(uint16 v, uint16 lo, uint16 hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static inline float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static inline float deadzone_filter(float value, float threshold)
{
    if(value > threshold)  return value - threshold;
    if(value < -threshold) return value + threshold;
    return 0.0f;
}

static float map_channel_delta_angle(uint16 ch_value, uint16 ch_min, uint16 ch_mid, uint16 ch_max, uint16 deadband, float neg_max_angle, float pos_max_angle)
{
    if((ch_value >= (uint16)(ch_mid - deadband)) && (ch_value <= (uint16)(ch_mid + deadband)))
    {
        return 0.0f;
    }

    if(ch_value < ch_mid)
    {
        if(ch_mid > ch_min)
        {
            float ratio = (float)(ch_mid - ch_value) / (float)(ch_mid - ch_min);
            return clampf(neg_max_angle * ratio, fminf(neg_max_angle, 0.0f), fmaxf(neg_max_angle, 0.0f));
        }
    }
    else
    {
        if(ch_max > ch_mid)
        {
            float ratio = (float)(ch_value - ch_mid) / (float)(ch_max - ch_mid);
            return clampf(pos_max_angle * ratio, fminf(pos_max_angle, 0.0f), fmaxf(pos_max_angle, 0.0f));
        }
    }

    return 0.0f;
}

static float map_channel_to_angle(uint16 ch_value, uint16 ch_min, uint16 ch_mid, uint16 ch_max, float max_angle)
{
    float result = 0.0f;
    
    if(ch_value < ch_mid)
    {
        
        if(ch_mid > ch_min)
        {
            result = -max_angle * (float)(ch_mid - ch_value) / (float)(ch_mid - ch_min);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        
        if(ch_max > ch_mid)
        {
            result = max_angle * (float)(ch_value - ch_mid) / (float)(ch_max - ch_mid);
        }
    }
    
    return clampf(result, -max_angle, max_angle);
}

static uint16 sbus_map_to_range(uint16 sbus_raw, uint16 out_min, uint16 out_range)
{
    
    if(sbus_raw < SBUS_VALUE_MIN) { sbus_raw = SBUS_VALUE_MIN; }
    if(sbus_raw > SBUS_VALUE_MAX) { sbus_raw = SBUS_VALUE_MAX; }

    uint32 mapped = (uint32)out_min
                  + (uint32)(sbus_raw - SBUS_VALUE_MIN) * out_range / SBUS_VALUE_RANGE;

    return (uint16)mapped;
}

void remote_control_init(void)
{
    
    uart_receiver_init();

    remote_data.ch[0]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;
    remote_data.ch[1]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;
    remote_data.ch[2]       = SBUS_VALUE_MIN;
    remote_data.ch[3]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;
    remote_data.ch[4]       = SBUS_VALUE_MIN;
    remote_data.ch[5]       = SBUS_VALUE_MIN;
    remote_data.ch_value[0] = 150;
    remote_data.ch_value[1] = 150;
    remote_data.ch_value[2] = RC_OUTPUT_MIN;
    remote_data.ch_value[3] = 150;
    remote_data.ch_value[4] = 100;
    remote_data.ch_value[5] = 100;

    g_rc_flags.unlocked     = 0;
    g_rc_flags.unlock_stage = 0;
    g_rc_cmd.roll_cmd       = 0.0f;
    g_rc_cmd.pitch_cmd      = 0.0f;
    g_rc_cmd.throttle_us    = remote_data.ch_value[2];
    g_rc_cmd.height_hold    = 0;

    last_signal_ms = system_get_ms();
    s_unlock_prev = 0U;
    s_buzzer_active = 0U;
    s_buzzer_off_ms = 0U;

    buzzer_init();
    buzzer_off();
}

void remote_control_update(void)
{
}

void remote_control_control_tick(void)
{
    
    if(uart_receiver.finsh_flag)
    {
        uart_receiver.finsh_flag = 0;

        last_signal_ms = system_get_ms();

        if(uart_receiver.state)
        {
            
            for(uint8 i = 0; i < RC_CHANNEL_NUM; i++)
            {
                uint16 sbus_raw = uart_receiver.channel[i];
                remote_data.ch[i] = sbus_raw;

                if(i == 0)
                {
                    
                    uint16 reversed_sbus = SBUS_VALUE_MIN + SBUS_VALUE_MAX - sbus_raw;
                    remote_data.ch_value[i] = clamp_u16(
                        sbus_map_to_range(reversed_sbus, RC_OUTPUT_MIN, RC_OUTPUT_RANGE),
                        RC_OUTPUT_MIN, RC_OUTPUT_MAX);
                }
                else
                {
                    remote_data.ch_value[i] = clamp_u16(
                        sbus_map_to_range(sbus_raw, RC_OUTPUT_MIN, RC_OUTPUT_RANGE),
                        RC_OUTPUT_MIN, RC_OUTPUT_MAX);
                }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            
            remote_data.ch[0]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;
            remote_data.ch[1]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;
            remote_data.ch[2]       = SBUS_VALUE_MIN;
            remote_data.ch[3]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;
            remote_data.ch[4]       = SBUS_VALUE_MAX;
            remote_data.ch[5]       = SBUS_VALUE_MID;

            remote_data.ch_value[0] = RC_OUTPUT_MID; 
            remote_data.ch_value[1] = RC_OUTPUT_MID; 
            remote_data.ch_value[2] = RC_OUTPUT_MIN; 
            remote_data.ch_value[3] = RC_OUTPUT_MID; 
            remote_data.ch_value[4] = RC_OUTPUT_MAX;
            remote_data.ch_value[5] = RC_OUTPUT_MID; 
        }
    }

    if(uart_receiver.state)
    {

        uint16 ch6   = remote_data.ch_value[5];
        uint16 thr   = remote_data.ch_value[2];
        uint16 pitch = remote_data.ch_value[1];

        if(!g_rc_flags.unlocked)
        {
            if(ch6 < 130U && thr < 110U)
            {
                
                g_rc_flags.unlock_stage = 1;
            }
            else
            {
                g_rc_flags.unlock_stage = 0;
            }

            if(g_rc_flags.unlock_stage == 1 && pitch < 130U)
            {
                g_rc_flags.unlocked = 1;
                g_rc_flags.unlock_stage = 0;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            
            if(ch6 > 140U)
            {
                g_rc_flags.unlocked = 0;
                g_rc_flags.unlock_stage = 0;
            }
        }
    }

#if RC_ROLL_PITCH_CTRL_EN
    g_rc_cmd.pitch_cmd = map_channel_delta_angle(
        remote_data.ch[0],            
        RC_CH1_PITCH_RAW_MIN,
        RC_CH1_PITCH_RAW_MID,
        RC_CH1_PITCH_RAW_MAX,
        RC_RP_RAW_DEADBAND,
        -RC_RP_CTRL_ANGLE,            
        RC_RP_CTRL_ANGLE              
    );

    g_rc_cmd.roll_cmd = map_channel_delta_angle(
        remote_data.ch[1],            
        RC_CH2_ROLL_RAW_MIN,
        RC_CH2_ROLL_RAW_MID,
        RC_CH2_ROLL_RAW_MAX,
        RC_RP_RAW_DEADBAND,
        -RC_RP_CTRL_ANGLE,
        RC_RP_CTRL_ANGLE
    );
#else
    g_rc_cmd.pitch_cmd = 0.0f;
    g_rc_cmd.roll_cmd  = 0.0f;
#endif

    g_rc_cmd.throttle_us = remote_data.ch_value[2];

    float yaw_rate = map_channel_to_angle(
        remote_data.ch_value[3], 
        RC_CH4_YAW_MIN, 
        RC_CH4_YAW_MID, 
        RC_CH4_YAW_MAX, 
        RC_MAX_YAW_RATE
    );
    g_rc_cmd.yaw_cmd = deadzone_filter(yaw_rate, 3.0f);

    uint16 ch5 = remote_data.ch_value[4];
    uint8 sw5;
    if(ch5 < 125U)       sw5 = 0U;   
    else if(ch5 < 175U)  sw5 = 1U;   
    else                 sw5 = 2U;   

    g_rc_cmd.pos_hold    = (sw5 == 0U) ? 1U : 0U;                 
    g_rc_cmd.height_hold = (sw5 == 0U || sw5 == 1U) ? 1U : 0U;    

    if(!s_unlock_prev && g_rc_flags.unlocked)
    {
        buzzer_on();
        s_buzzer_active = 1U;
        s_buzzer_off_ms = system_get_ms() + 500U;
    }
    s_unlock_prev = g_rc_flags.unlocked;

    if(s_buzzer_active)
    {
        if((int32)(system_get_ms() - s_buzzer_off_ms) >= 0)
        {
            buzzer_off();
            s_buzzer_active = 0U;
        }
    }

}

const rc_control_flags_t* rc_control_get_flags(void)
{
    return &g_rc_flags;
}

const rc_control_command_t* rc_control_get_command(void)
{
    return &g_rc_cmd;
}

void rc_get_calibrated_mid(float* ch1_mid, float* ch2_mid)
{
    if(ch1_mid) { *ch1_mid = (float)RC_OUTPUT_MID; }
    if(ch2_mid) { *ch2_mid = (float)RC_OUTPUT_MID; }
}

uint8 remote_control_get_signal_valid(void)
{
    uint32 now = system_get_ms();
    return (now - last_signal_ms) < 200 ? 1 : 0;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
air_auto_takeoff.h - 自动起飞头文件
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#ifndef _AIR_AUTO_TAKEOFF_H_
#define _AIR_AUTO_TAKEOFF_H_

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

typedef enum
{
    AT_OFF = 0,          
    AT_RISING,           
    AT_STABILIZING,      
    AT_HEIGHT_LOCK,      
    AT_BEEP_WAIT,        
    AT_TRACKING,         
    AT_ABORT,            
} auto_takeoff_state_t;

extern volatile float g_auto_takeoff_en;         
extern volatile float g_takeoff_target_h;        
extern volatile float g_takeoff_target_h_max;    
extern volatile float g_takeoff_throttle;        
extern volatile float g_takeoff_max_time_s;      
extern volatile float g_takeoff_stable_time_ms;  
extern volatile float g_takeoff_beep_time_ms;    
extern volatile float g_takeoff_beep_delay_ms;   
extern volatile float g_takeoff_max_roll_deg;    
extern volatile float g_takeoff_max_pitch_deg;   
extern volatile float g_takeoff_abort_throttle;  
extern volatile float g_takeoff_stable_vz_mps;   

extern volatile uint8 g_auto_height_control;     

void air_auto_takeoff_init(void);

void air_auto_takeoff_tick(uint32 now_ms);

void air_auto_takeoff_reset(void);

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_is_active(void);

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_is_in_progress(void);

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_horizontal_ok(void);

float air_auto_takeoff_get_throttle(void);

float air_auto_takeoff_get_target_height(void);

auto_takeoff_state_t air_auto_takeoff_get_state(void);

char air_auto_takeoff_get_state_char(void);

float air_auto_takeoff_get_elapsed_s(void);

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_is_ready_to_send(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
air_auto_takeoff.c - 自动起飞实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "auto_takeoff/air_auto_takeoff.h"
#include "remote_control/remote_control.h"
#include "tof/tof.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include "buzzer/buzzer.h"
#include "blind_box/air_blind_box.h"
#include <math.h>

#define AT_TRIGGER_H             (1.20f)    
#define AT_HOLD_H                (1.40f)    
#define AT_RAMP_MPS              (0.30f)    
#define AT_LEASH_M               (0.15f)    
#define AT_SOFTSTART_MS          (400.0f)   
#define AT_SOFTSTART_FLOOR_FRAC  (0.80f)    

volatile float g_auto_takeoff_en         = 0.0f;    
volatile float g_takeoff_target_h        = 1.2f;    
volatile float g_takeoff_target_h_max    = 1.5f;    
volatile float g_takeoff_throttle        = 53.0f;   
volatile float g_takeoff_max_time_s      = 30.0f;   
volatile float g_takeoff_stable_time_ms  = 2000.0f; 
volatile float g_takeoff_beep_time_ms    = 3000.0f; 
volatile float g_takeoff_beep_delay_ms   = 500.0f;  
volatile float g_takeoff_max_roll_deg    = 30.0f;   
volatile float g_takeoff_max_pitch_deg   = 30.0f;   
volatile float g_takeoff_abort_throttle  = 4000.0f; 
volatile float g_takeoff_stable_vz_mps   = 0.1f;    

volatile uint8 g_auto_height_control = 0U;  

static auto_takeoff_state_t s_state = AT_OFF;
static uint32 s_phase_start_ms = 0U;        
static float  s_target_h       = 0.0f;      
static uint32 s_rising_start_ms = 0U;       
static uint8  s_last_pos_hold  = 0U;        
static uint32 s_stable_start_ms = 0U;       
static uint8  s_beep_started   = 0U;        
static uint8  s_tracking_started = 0U;      
static uint32 s_attitude_fault_start_ms = 0U; 
static uint8  s_attitude_fault_active = 0U;   
static uint32 s_last_tick_ms   = 0U;        
static uint8  s_first_tick     = 1U;        

static uint8 check_attitude_fault(void)
{
    float roll_abs  = fabsf(SystemIMU.angle.roll);
    float pitch_abs = fabsf(SystemIMU.angle.pitch);

    if(roll_abs > g_takeoff_max_roll_deg || pitch_abs > g_takeoff_max_pitch_deg)
    {
        return 1U;
    }
    return 0U;
}

static void fast_beep_tick(uint32 now_ms)
{
    static uint32 s_beep_toggle_ms = 0;

    if(now_ms - s_beep_toggle_ms >= 100U)  
    {
        static uint8 s_beep_on = 0U;
        if(s_beep_on)
        {
            buzzer_off();
            s_beep_on = 0U;
        }
        else
        {
            buzzer_on();
            s_beep_on = 1U;
        }
        s_beep_toggle_ms = now_ms;
    }
}

static void enter_abort_state(void)
{
    s_state = AT_ABORT;
    s_phase_start_ms = 0U;

    buzzer_on();  
}

void air_auto_takeoff_init(void)
{
    s_state              = AT_OFF;
    s_phase_start_ms     = 0U;
    s_target_h           = 0.0f;
    s_rising_start_ms    = 0U;
    s_last_pos_hold      = 0U;
    s_stable_start_ms    = 0U;
    s_beep_started       = 0U;
    s_tracking_started   = 0U;
    s_attitude_fault_start_ms = 0U;
    s_attitude_fault_active   = 0U;
    s_last_tick_ms       = 0U;
    s_first_tick         = 1U;
    g_auto_height_control = 0U;
}

void air_auto_takeoff_tick(uint32 now_ms)
{
    
    uint32 dt = s_first_tick ? 0U : (now_ms - s_last_tick_ms);
    s_last_tick_ms = now_ms;
    s_first_tick   = 0U;

    if(g_auto_takeoff_en < 0.5f)
    {
        if(s_state != AT_OFF)
        {
            air_auto_takeoff_reset();
        }
        return;
    }

    uint8 unlocked = g_rc_flags.unlocked;
    uint8 pos_hold = (g_rc_cmd.pos_hold != 0U);

    uint8 pos_hold_rising_edge = (pos_hold && !s_last_pos_hold);
    s_last_pos_hold = pos_hold;

    if(s_state == AT_OFF && unlocked && pos_hold_rising_edge)
    {
        s_state = AT_RISING;
        s_phase_start_ms  = now_ms;
        s_rising_start_ms = now_ms;             
        s_target_h        = g_tof_state.height_m; 
        s_stable_start_ms = 0U;
        s_beep_started = 0U;
        s_tracking_started = 0U;
        s_attitude_fault_start_ms = 0U;
        s_attitude_fault_active = 0U;
        g_auto_height_control = 1U;             
        return;
    }

    if((s_state == AT_STABILIZING || s_state == AT_BEEP_WAIT || s_state == AT_TRACKING) && !pos_hold)
    {
        
        return;
    }

    if(s_state != AT_OFF && s_state != AT_ABORT)
    {
        if(check_attitude_fault())
        {
            if(!s_attitude_fault_active)
            {
                s_attitude_fault_active = 1U;
                s_attitude_fault_start_ms = now_ms;
            }
            else if(now_ms - s_attitude_fault_start_ms >= 1000U)
            {
                enter_abort_state();
                return;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            s_attitude_fault_active = 0U;
            s_attitude_fault_start_ms = 0U;
        }
    }

    if(s_state == AT_RISING)
    {
        float ramped = s_target_h + AT_RAMP_MPS * ((float)dt / 1000.0f);
        float leash  = g_tof_state.height_m + AT_LEASH_M;
        float t = ramped;
        if(t > leash)     t = leash;
        if(t > AT_HOLD_H) t = AT_HOLD_H;
        s_target_h = t;
    }

    switch(s_state)
    {
        case AT_OFF:
            break;  

        case AT_RISING:
        {
            uint32 elapsed_ms = now_ms - s_phase_start_ms;
            if((float)elapsed_ms / 1000.0f >= g_takeoff_max_time_s)
            {
                enter_abort_state();
                break;
            }
            
            if(g_tof_state.height_m >= AT_TRIGGER_H)
            {
                s_target_h = AT_HOLD_H;             
                s_state = AT_BEEP_WAIT;
                s_phase_start_ms  = now_ms;
                s_beep_started = 0U;
                s_tracking_started = 0U;
            }
            break;
        }

        case AT_STABILIZING:
            
            s_target_h = AT_HOLD_H;
            s_state = AT_BEEP_WAIT;
            s_phase_start_ms  = now_ms;
            s_beep_started = 0U;
            s_tracking_started = 0U;
            break;

        case AT_BEEP_WAIT:
        {
            uint32 elapsed_ms = now_ms - s_phase_start_ms;

            if(!s_beep_started)
            {
                buzzer_on();
                s_beep_started = 1U;
            }

            if(!s_tracking_started && (float)elapsed_ms >= g_takeoff_beep_delay_ms)
            {
                s_tracking_started = 1U;
                s_state = AT_TRACKING;
                
            }

            if(s_beep_started && (float)elapsed_ms >= g_takeoff_beep_time_ms)
            {
                buzzer_off();
            }
            break;
        }

        case AT_TRACKING:
        {

            uint32 total_elapsed_ms = now_ms - s_phase_start_ms + (uint32)g_takeoff_beep_delay_ms;
            if(s_beep_started && (float)total_elapsed_ms >= g_takeoff_beep_time_ms)
            {
                buzzer_off();
                s_beep_started = 0U;
            }
            break;
        }

        case AT_ABORT:
            fast_beep_tick(now_ms);  
            break;

        case AT_HEIGHT_LOCK:  
        default:
            s_state = AT_OFF;
            g_auto_height_control = 0U;
            break;
    }
}

void air_auto_takeoff_reset(void)
{
    
    s_state              = AT_OFF;
    s_phase_start_ms     = 0U;
    s_stable_start_ms    = 0U;
    s_beep_started       = 0U;
    s_tracking_started   = 0U;
    s_target_h           = 0.0f;
    s_rising_start_ms    = 0U;
    s_last_pos_hold      = 0U;
    s_attitude_fault_start_ms = 0U;
    s_attitude_fault_active   = 0U;
    g_auto_height_control = 0U;
    buzzer_off();  
}

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_is_active(void)
{
    return (s_state != AT_OFF && s_state != AT_ABORT) ? 1U : 0U;
}

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_is_in_progress(void)
{
    
    return (s_state == AT_RISING ||
            s_state == AT_STABILIZING ||
            s_state == AT_HEIGHT_LOCK ||
            s_state == AT_BEEP_WAIT) ? 1U : 0U;
}

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_horizontal_ok(void)
{
    
    if(g_auto_takeoff_en < 0.5f)
    {
        return 1U;
    }
    return (s_state == AT_TRACKING) ? 1U : 0U;
}

float air_auto_takeoff_get_throttle(void)
{
    
    float climb = g_takeoff_throttle * 100.0f;  

    if(s_state == AT_RISING && s_rising_start_ms != 0U)
    {
        uint32 el = system_get_ms() - s_rising_start_ms;
        if((float)el < AT_SOFTSTART_MS)
        {
            float k = (float)el / AT_SOFTSTART_MS;  
            return climb * (AT_SOFTSTART_FLOOR_FRAC + (1.0f - AT_SOFTSTART_FLOOR_FRAC) * k);
        }
    }
    return climb;
}

float air_auto_takeoff_get_target_height(void)
{
    
    return s_target_h;
}

auto_takeoff_state_t air_auto_takeoff_get_state(void)
{
    return s_state;
}

char air_auto_takeoff_get_state_char(void)
{
    switch(s_state)
    {
        case AT_RISING:       return 'R';
        case AT_STABILIZING:  return 'S';
        case AT_HEIGHT_LOCK:  return 'H';
        case AT_BEEP_WAIT:    return 'B';
        case AT_TRACKING:     return 'T';
        case AT_ABORT:        return 'X';
        case AT_OFF:
        default:              return 'O';
    }
}

float air_auto_takeoff_get_elapsed_s(void)
{
    if(s_state == AT_OFF || s_phase_start_ms == 0U)
    {
        return 0.0f;
    }

    uint32 now_ms = system_get_ms();
    uint32 elapsed_ms = now_ms - s_phase_start_ms;
    return (float)elapsed_ms / 1000.0f;
}

uint8 air_auto_takeoff_is_ready_to_send(void)
{
    
    if(g_auto_takeoff_en < 0.5f)
    {
        return 1U;
    }

    return (s_state == AT_TRACKING) ? 1U : 0U;
}

#ifndef _AIR_BLIND_BOX_H_
#define _AIR_BLIND_BOX_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

extern volatile float g_bb_en;      
extern volatile float g_bb_arm_s;   
extern volatile float g_bb_tz_s;    

typedef enum
{
    BB_OFF = 0,     
    BB_ARMING,      
    BB_READY,       
    BB_LANDING,     
} air_bb_state_enum;

void air_blind_box_init(void);

void air_blind_box_tick(uint32 now_ms);

uint8 air_blind_box_is_landing(void);

void air_blind_box_reset(void);

air_bb_state_enum air_blind_box_get_state(void);
char  air_blind_box_get_state_char(void);   
float air_blind_box_get_z_elapsed_s(void);  
float air_blind_box_get_arm_elapsed_s(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
air_blind_box.c - 盲盒任务实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "blind_box/air_blind_box.h"
#include "remote_control/remote_control.h"
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart.h"
#include "auto_takeoff/air_auto_takeoff.h"

volatile float g_bb_en    = 0.0f;   
volatile float g_bb_arm_s = 5.0f;   
volatile float g_bb_tz_s  = 8.0f;   

#define BB_VISION_FRESH_MS   (200U)

static air_bb_state_enum s_state       = BB_OFF;
static uint32 s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;      
static uint32 s_z_accum_ms   = 0U;      
static uint32 s_last_ms      = 0U;      
static uint8  s_first_tick   = 1U;      

void air_blind_box_init(void)
{
    s_state        = BB_OFF;
    s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;
    s_z_accum_ms   = 0U;
    s_last_ms      = 0U;
    s_first_tick   = 1U;
}

void air_blind_box_tick(uint32 now_ms)
{
    
    uint32 dt = s_first_tick ? 0U : (now_ms - s_last_ms);
    s_last_ms    = now_ms;
    s_first_tick = 0U;

    if(g_bb_en < 0.5f)
    {
        s_state        = BB_OFF;
        s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;
        s_z_accum_ms   = 0U;
        return;
    }

    uint8 arm_cond = (g_rc_cmd.pos_hold != 0U) && (g_rc_flags.unlocked != 0U) && !air_auto_takeoff_is_in_progress();

    switch(s_state)
    {
        case BB_OFF:
            
            s_state        = BB_ARMING;
            s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;
            s_z_accum_ms   = 0U;
            break;

        case BB_ARMING:
            if(!arm_cond)
            {
                
                s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;
                break;
            }
            s_arm_accum_ms += dt;
            if((float)s_arm_accum_ms >= g_bb_arm_s * 1000.0f)
            {
                s_state      = BB_READY;
                s_z_accum_ms = 0U;
            }
            break;

        case BB_READY:
        {
            if(!arm_cond)
            {
                
                s_state        = BB_ARMING;
                s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;
                s_z_accum_ms   = 0U;
                break;
            }

            uint8 vision_fresh = ((now_ms - g_vision_data.last_update_ms) < BB_VISION_FRESH_MS) ? 1U : 0U;

            if(g_vision_data.searching && vision_fresh)
            {
                s_z_accum_ms += dt;
                if((float)s_z_accum_ms >= g_bb_tz_s * 1000.0f)
                {
                    s_state = BB_LANDING;   
                }
            }
            else
            {
                
                s_z_accum_ms = 0U;
            }
            break;
        }

        case BB_LANDING:
            
            break;

        default:
            s_state = BB_OFF;
            break;
    }
}

uint8 air_blind_box_is_landing(void)
{
    return (g_bb_en > 0.5f && s_state == BB_LANDING) ? 1U : 0U;
}

void air_blind_box_reset(void)
{
    
    s_state        = BB_ARMING;
    s_arm_accum_ms = 0U;
    s_z_accum_ms   = 0U;
}

air_bb_state_enum air_blind_box_get_state(void)
{
    return s_state;
}

char air_blind_box_get_state_char(void)
{
    switch(s_state)
    {
        case BB_ARMING:  return 'A';
        case BB_READY:   return 'R';
        case BB_LANDING: return 'L';
        case BB_OFF:
        default:         return 'O';
    }
}

float air_blind_box_get_z_elapsed_s(void)
{
    return (float)s_z_accum_ms / 1000.0f;
}

float air_blind_box_get_arm_elapsed_s(void)
{
    return (float)s_arm_accum_ms / 1000.0f;
}

#ifndef BATTERY_H_

extern volatile uint16 g_battery_raw;
extern volatile float  g_battery_raw_vol;

void  battery_init(void);     
void  battery_update(void);   
float battery_get_voltage(void);
float battery_get_vol_factor(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
battery.c - 电池管理实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "battery.h"
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "zf_driver_adc.h"

#define BATTERY_ADC_CHANNEL   (ADC0_CH21_P07_5)   
#define BATTERY_ADC_RES       (ADC_12BIT)         
#define BATTERY_ADC_MAX       (4095.0f)           
#define BATTERY_ADC_VREF      (3.3f)              
#define BATTERY_ADC_SAMPLES   (8U)                

#define BATTERY_DIV_RATIO     ((200.0f + 20.0f) / 20.0f)

#define BATTERY_REF_VOLTAGE   (10.7f)

volatile float g_battery_voltage = 0.0f;

volatile uint16 g_battery_raw     = 0;
volatile float  g_battery_raw_vol = 0.0f;

void battery_init(void)
{
    adc_init(BATTERY_ADC_CHANNEL, BATTERY_ADC_RES);
}

void battery_update(void)
{
    
    uint16 raw = adc_mean_filter_convert(BATTERY_ADC_CHANNEL, BATTERY_ADC_SAMPLES);
    g_battery_raw = raw;   

    float vadc    = (float)raw / BATTERY_ADC_MAX * BATTERY_ADC_VREF;
    float new_vol = vadc * BATTERY_DIV_RATIO;
    g_battery_raw_vol = new_vol;   

    if(new_vol > 7.0f && new_vol < 14.0f)
    {
        g_battery_voltage = 0.9f * g_battery_voltage + 0.1f * new_vol;
    }
}

float battery_get_voltage(void)
{
    return g_battery_voltage;
}

float battery_get_vol_factor(void)
{
    float vol_factor = 1.0f;

    if(g_battery_voltage > 7.0f)
    {
        vol_factor = BATTERY_REF_VOLTAGE / g_battery_voltage;
        if(vol_factor > 1.15f) vol_factor = 1.15f;
        if(vol_factor < 0.85f) vol_factor = 0.85f;
    }
    return vol_factor;
}

#ifndef _BUZZER_H_
#define _BUZZER_H_

#include "zf_common_typedef.h"

#define BUZZER_PIN                 (P19_4)                     

void buzzer_init(void);                                        
void buzzer_on(void);                                          
void buzzer_off(void);                                         
void buzzer_toggle(void);                                      
void buzzer_beep(uint16_t duration_ms);                        

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
buzzer.c - 蜂鸣器实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "buzzer.h"

void buzzer_init(void)
{
    
    gpio_init(BUZZER_PIN, GPO, GPIO_LOW, GPO_PUSH_PULL);
}

void buzzer_on(void)
{
    gpio_high(BUZZER_PIN);
}

void buzzer_off(void)
{
    gpio_low(BUZZER_PIN);
}

void buzzer_toggle(void)
{
    if(gpio_get_level(BUZZER_PIN))
    {
        gpio_low(BUZZER_PIN);
    }
    else
    {
        gpio_high(BUZZER_PIN);
    }
}

void buzzer_beep(uint16_t duration_ms)
{
    buzzer_on();
    system_delay_ms(duration_ms);
    buzzer_off();
}
