--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
menu.c - 菜单系统实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "menu.h"
#include "display_config.h"
#include "zf_common_headfile.h"
#include "zf_driver_gpio.h"
#include "zf_driver_flash.h"
#include "zf_device_tft180.h"
#include "buzzer/buzzer.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include "remote_control/remote_control.h"
#include "shared_data.h"
#include "tof/tof.h"
#include "flow/flow.h"
#include "zf_device_dl1b.h"
#include "control/pid.h"
#include "control/attitude_ctrl.h"
#include "blind_box/air_blind_box.h"
#include "auto_takeoff/air_auto_takeoff.h"
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart.h"
#include "code1/shared_memory.h"
#include "local_camera_shared.h"

#pragma location = 0x28037c70
__no_init volatile uint8 wifi_diag_stage;
#pragma location = 0x28037c71
__no_init volatile uint8 wifi_diag_mode;
#pragma location = 0x28037c72
__no_init volatile uint8 wifi_diag_init_retry;
#pragma location = 0x28037c73
__no_init volatile uint8 wifi_diag_tcp_retry;
#pragma location = 0x28037c74
__no_init volatile uint32 wifi_diag_send_cnt;
#pragma location = 0x28037c78
__no_init volatile uint8 wifi_diag_heartbeat;
#pragma location = 0x28037c7C
__no_init volatile uint32 wifi_diag_frame_cnt;

typedef struct
{
    uint32 magic; 
    uint32 cfsr;
    uint32 hfsr;
    uint32 mmfar;
    uint32 bfar;
    uint32 afsr;
    uint32 sp;
    uint32 lr;
    uint32 pc;
    uint32 psr;
} HardFaultInfo_t;

#pragma location = 0x28037CA0
__no_init volatile HardFaultInfo_t g_hf1;

#define KEY_NEXT_PIN     (P20_2)   
#define KEY_ENTER_PIN    (P20_1)   
#define KEY_INC_PIN      (P20_3)   
#define KEY_DEC_PIN      (P21_6)   
#define KEY_BACK_PIN     (P21_5)   

#define MENU_FLASH_SECTOR    (0U)
#define MENU_FLASH_PAGE      (0U)
#define MENU_FLASH_MAGIC     (0x4D4E5031UL)
#define MENU_FLASH_LAYOUT_VERSION   (0x000BU)   
#define MENU_FLASH_PARAM_VERSION    (0x038FU)   
#define MENU_FLASH_WORDS     (71U)

typedef enum
{
    MENU_ITEM_SUBMENU = 0,
    MENU_ITEM_PARAM_FLOAT,
    MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_IMU,
    MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_REMOTE,
    MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_TOF,
    MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_FLOW,
    MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_VISION,
    MENU_ITEM_ACTION,
    MENU_ITEM_DISPLAY_RC_CH,
} menu_item_type_t;

typedef struct menu_item menu_item_t;

struct menu_item
{
    const char* name;
    menu_item_type_t type;
    const menu_item_t* parent;
    const menu_item_t* children;
    uint8 child_count;

    volatile float* fval;  
    float* flash_saved;    
    float step;          
    float minv;          
    float maxv;          

    void (*action)(void);
    uint8 ch_index;  
};

typedef enum
{
    UI_LIST = 0,         
    UI_EDIT_FLOAT,        
    UI_MONITOR_IMU,       
    UI_MONITOR_REMOTE,    
    UI_MONITOR_TOF,       
    UI_MONITOR_FLOW,      
    UI_MONITOR_VISION,    
} ui_mode_t;

#define MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH (10U)
static const menu_item_t* g_menu_stack[MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH];
static uint8              g_menu_depth = 0;
static uint8              g_cur_index = 0;       
static ui_mode_t          g_mode = UI_LIST;

static volatile float*    g_edit_ptr = NULL; 
static float              g_edit_value = 0.0f; 
static float              g_edit_step = 0.01f; 
static float              g_edit_minv = -10000.0f; 
static float              g_edit_maxv = 10000.0f; 
static const char*        g_edit_name = NULL;

#define INLINE_PARAM_MAX_COUNT (3U)
static uint8              g_inline_edit_active = 0;
static float              g_inline_edit_values[INLINE_PARAM_MAX_COUNT] = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};

static uint32             g_last_draw_ms = 0;

#define KEY_DEBOUNCE_MS        (20U)   
#define MENU_KEY_PRESS_LEVEL        (0U)    
#define MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL      (1U)    
#define KEY_REPEAT_START_MS    (600U)  
#define KEY_REPEAT_MS          (150U)  
#define KEY_LONGPRESS_MS       (800U)  

typedef struct
{
    gpio_pin_enum pin;
    uint8 last_level;
    uint8 stable_level;
    uint32 last_change_ms;

    uint8 is_pressed; 
    uint32 press_start_ms; 

    uint32 next_repeat_ms;
} key_ctx_t;

static key_ctx_t key_next  = {KEY_NEXT_PIN,  MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, 0, 0, 0, 0};
static key_ctx_t key_enter = {KEY_ENTER_PIN, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, 0, 0, 0, 0};
static key_ctx_t key_inc   = {KEY_INC_PIN,   MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, 0, 0, 0, 0};
static key_ctx_t key_dec   = {KEY_DEC_PIN,   MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, 0, 0, 0, 0};
static key_ctx_t key_back  = {KEY_BACK_PIN,  MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, 0, 0, 0, 0};

typedef enum
{
    KEYEV_NONE = 0,
    KEYEV_NEXT,
    KEYEV_INC,
    KEYEV_DEC,
    KEYEV_ENTER_SHORT,
    KEYEV_ENTER_LONG,
    KEYEV_BACK,
} key_event_t;

static void key_init_one(key_ctx_t* k)
{
    gpio_init(k->pin, GPI, MENU_KEY_RELEASE_LEVEL, GPI_PULL_UP);
    k->last_level = gpio_get_level(k->pin);
    k->stable_level = k->last_level;
    k->last_change_ms = system_get_ms();
    k->is_pressed = 0;
    k->press_start_ms = 0;
    k->next_repeat_ms = 0;
}

static key_event_t key_scan_next_repeat(key_ctx_t* k, uint32 now_ms, key_event_t press_ev)
{
    uint8 level = gpio_get_level(k->pin); 
    if(level != k->last_level) 
    {
        k->last_level = level;
        k->last_change_ms = now_ms; 
    }
    
    if((now_ms - k->last_change_ms) >= KEY_DEBOUNCE_MS && k->stable_level != k->last_level) 
    {
        k->stable_level = k->last_level; 
        if(k->stable_level == MENU_KEY_PRESS_LEVEL) 
        {
            k->is_pressed = 1;
            k->press_start_ms = now_ms;
            k->next_repeat_ms = now_ms + KEY_REPEAT_START_MS;
            return press_ev;
        }
        else
        {
            
            k->is_pressed = 0;
            k->next_repeat_ms = 0;
        }
    }

    if(k->is_pressed && k->stable_level == MENU_KEY_PRESS_LEVEL && k->next_repeat_ms != 0 && now_ms >= k->next_repeat_ms)
    {
        k->next_repeat_ms = now_ms + KEY_REPEAT_MS; 
        return press_ev;
    }
    return KEYEV_NONE;
}

static key_event_t key_scan_enter(key_ctx_t* k, uint32 now_ms)
{
    uint8 level = gpio_get_level(k->pin);
    if(level != k->last_level)
    {
        k->last_level = level;
        k->last_change_ms = now_ms;
    }
    
    if((now_ms - k->last_change_ms) >= KEY_DEBOUNCE_MS && k->stable_level != k->last_level)
    {
        k->stable_level = k->last_level;
        if(k->stable_level == MENU_KEY_PRESS_LEVEL)
        {
            k->is_pressed = 1;
            k->press_start_ms = now_ms;
        }
        else
        {
            
            if(k->is_pressed)
            {
                uint32 dur = now_ms - k->press_start_ms;
                k->is_pressed = 0;
                if(dur >= KEY_LONGPRESS_MS) return KEYEV_ENTER_LONG; 
                else return KEYEV_ENTER_SHORT; 
            }
            k->is_pressed = 0;
        }
    }
    return KEYEV_NONE; 
}

static key_event_t key_scan_back(key_ctx_t* k, uint32 now_ms)
{
    uint8 level = gpio_get_level(k->pin);
    if(level != k->last_level)
    {
        k->last_level = level;
        k->last_change_ms = now_ms;
    }
    
    if((now_ms - k->last_change_ms) >= KEY_DEBOUNCE_MS && k->stable_level != k->last_level)
    {
        k->stable_level = k->last_level;
        if(k->stable_level == MENU_KEY_PRESS_LEVEL)
        {
            k->is_pressed = 1;
            k->press_start_ms = now_ms;
        }
        else
        {
            
            if(k->is_pressed)
            {
                k->is_pressed = 0;
                return KEYEV_BACK;
            }
            k->is_pressed = 0;
        }
    }
    return KEYEV_NONE;
}

static key_event_t keys_poll_once(void)
{
    uint32 now = system_get_ms();

    key_event_t ev; 
    ev = key_scan_next_repeat(&key_next, now, KEYEV_NEXT); 
    if(ev) return ev;
    ev = key_scan_next_repeat(&key_inc, now, KEYEV_INC); 
    if(ev) return ev;
    ev = key_scan_next_repeat(&key_dec, now, KEYEV_DEC); 
    if(ev) return ev;
    ev = key_scan_enter(&key_enter, now); 
    if(ev) return ev;
    ev = key_scan_back(&key_back, now); 
    if(ev) return ev;
    return KEYEV_NONE;
}

static void menu_params_apply_from_buffer(void)
{
    RollPID.kp = flash_union_buffer[3].float_type;
    RollPID.ki = flash_union_buffer[4].float_type;
    RollPID.kd = flash_union_buffer[5].float_type;
    
    PitchPID.kp = flash_union_buffer[6].float_type;
    PitchPID.ki = flash_union_buffer[7].float_type;
    PitchPID.kd = flash_union_buffer[8].float_type;
    
    YawPID.kp = flash_union_buffer[9].float_type;
    YawPID.ki = flash_union_buffer[10].float_type;
    YawPID.kd = flash_union_buffer[11].float_type;
    
    RollRatePID.kp = flash_union_buffer[12].float_type;
    RollRatePID.ki = flash_union_buffer[13].float_type;
    RollRatePID.kd = flash_union_buffer[14].float_type;
    
    PitchRatePID.kp = flash_union_buffer[15].float_type;
    PitchRatePID.ki = flash_union_buffer[16].float_type;
    PitchRatePID.kd = flash_union_buffer[17].float_type;
    
    YawRatePID.kp = flash_union_buffer[18].float_type;
    YawRatePID.ki = flash_union_buffer[19].float_type;
    YawRatePID.kd = flash_union_buffer[20].float_type;
    
    HeightPID.kp = flash_union_buffer[21].float_type;
    HeightPID.ki = flash_union_buffer[22].float_type;
    HeightPID.kd = flash_union_buffer[23].float_type;
    
    HeightSpeedPID.kp = flash_union_buffer[24].float_type;
    HeightSpeedPID.ki = flash_union_buffer[25].float_type;
    HeightSpeedPID.kd = flash_union_buffer[26].float_type;
    
    FlowSpeedPIDX.kp = flash_union_buffer[27].float_type;
    FlowSpeedPIDX.ki = flash_union_buffer[28].float_type;
    FlowSpeedPIDX.kd = flash_union_buffer[29].float_type;
    
    FlowSpeedPIDY.kp = flash_union_buffer[30].float_type;
    FlowSpeedPIDY.ki = flash_union_buffer[31].float_type;
    FlowSpeedPIDY.kd = flash_union_buffer[32].float_type;

    FlowSpeedPIDX.ki = 0.0f;
    FlowSpeedPIDY.ki = 0.0f;

    g_trim_roll_deg  = flash_union_buffer[33].float_type;
    g_trim_pitch_deg = flash_union_buffer[34].float_type;

    g_vision_en         = flash_union_buffer[35].float_type;
    g_vision_kp_x       = flash_union_buffer[36].float_type;
    g_vision_kp_y       = flash_union_buffer[37].float_type;
    g_vision_deadband_x = flash_union_buffer[38].float_type;
    g_vision_deadband_y = flash_union_buffer[39].float_type;
    g_vision_max_tilt   = flash_union_buffer[40].float_type;
    g_vision_lpf_alpha  = flash_union_buffer[41].float_type;
    g_vision_sign_x     = flash_union_buffer[42].float_type;
    g_vision_sign_y     = flash_union_buffer[43].float_type;
    g_vision_kd_x       = flash_union_buffer[44].float_type;
    g_vision_kd_y       = flash_union_buffer[45].float_type;

    FlowPosPIDX.kp = flash_union_buffer[46].float_type;
    FlowPosPIDX.ki = flash_union_buffer[47].float_type;
    FlowPosPIDX.kd = flash_union_buffer[48].float_type;
    FlowPosPIDY.kp = flash_union_buffer[49].float_type;
    FlowPosPIDY.ki = flash_union_buffer[50].float_type;
    FlowPosPIDY.kd = flash_union_buffer[51].float_type;

    g_flow_pos_kp   = flash_union_buffer[52].float_type;
    g_flow_pos_vmax = flash_union_buffer[53].float_type;
    
    g_bb_en         = flash_union_buffer[54].float_type;
    g_bb_arm_s      = flash_union_buffer[55].float_type;
    g_bb_tz_s       = flash_union_buffer[56].float_type;
    g_land_rate     = flash_union_buffer[57].float_type;
    g_land_cut_h    = flash_union_buffer[58].float_type;
    g_land_still_ms = flash_union_buffer[59].float_type;

    g_auto_takeoff_en         = flash_union_buffer[60].float_type;
    g_takeoff_target_h        = flash_union_buffer[61].float_type;
    g_takeoff_target_h_max    = flash_union_buffer[62].float_type;
    g_takeoff_throttle        = flash_union_buffer[63].float_type;  
    g_takeoff_max_time_s      = flash_union_buffer[64].float_type;
    g_takeoff_stable_time_ms  = flash_union_buffer[65].float_type;
    g_takeoff_beep_time_ms    = flash_union_buffer[66].float_type;
    g_takeoff_beep_delay_ms   = flash_union_buffer[67].float_type;
    g_takeoff_max_roll_deg    = flash_union_buffer[68].float_type;
    g_takeoff_max_pitch_deg   = flash_union_buffer[69].float_type;
    g_takeoff_abort_throttle  = flash_union_buffer[70].float_type;
}

static uint8 menu_params_load(void)
{

    if(!flash_check(MENU_FLASH_SECTOR, MENU_FLASH_PAGE))
    {
        return 0;
    }

    flash_read_page_to_buffer(MENU_FLASH_SECTOR, MENU_FLASH_PAGE, MENU_FLASH_WORDS);
    if(flash_union_buffer[0].uint32_type != MENU_FLASH_MAGIC) return 0;
    if(flash_union_buffer[1].uint16_type != MENU_FLASH_LAYOUT_VERSION) return 0;
    if(flash_union_buffer[2].uint16_type != MENU_FLASH_PARAM_VERSION) return 0;

    menu_params_apply_from_buffer();
    return 1;
}

static uint8 menu_params_save(void)
{

    if(flash_check(MENU_FLASH_SECTOR, MENU_FLASH_PAGE))
    {
        flash_erase_page(MENU_FLASH_SECTOR, MENU_FLASH_PAGE);
    }
    flash_buffer_clear();

    flash_union_buffer[0].uint32_type = MENU_FLASH_MAGIC;
    flash_union_buffer[1].uint16_type = MENU_FLASH_LAYOUT_VERSION;
    flash_union_buffer[2].uint16_type = MENU_FLASH_PARAM_VERSION;

    flash_union_buffer[3].float_type = RollPID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[4].float_type = RollPID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[5].float_type = RollPID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[6].float_type = PitchPID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[7].float_type = PitchPID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[8].float_type = PitchPID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[9].float_type = YawPID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[10].float_type = YawPID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[11].float_type = YawPID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[12].float_type = RollRatePID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[13].float_type = RollRatePID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[14].float_type = RollRatePID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[15].float_type = PitchRatePID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[16].float_type = PitchRatePID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[17].float_type = PitchRatePID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[18].float_type = YawRatePID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[19].float_type = YawRatePID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[20].float_type = YawRatePID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[21].float_type = HeightPID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[22].float_type = HeightPID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[23].float_type = HeightPID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[24].float_type = HeightSpeedPID.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[25].float_type = HeightSpeedPID.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[26].float_type = HeightSpeedPID.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[27].float_type = FlowSpeedPIDX.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[28].float_type = FlowSpeedPIDX.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[29].float_type = FlowSpeedPIDX.kd;
    
    flash_union_buffer[30].float_type = FlowSpeedPIDY.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[31].float_type = FlowSpeedPIDY.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[32].float_type = FlowSpeedPIDY.kd;

    flash_union_buffer[33].float_type = (float)g_trim_roll_deg;
    flash_union_buffer[34].float_type = (float)g_trim_pitch_deg;

    flash_union_buffer[35].float_type = (float)g_vision_en;
    flash_union_buffer[36].float_type = (float)g_vision_kp_x;
    flash_union_buffer[37].float_type = (float)g_vision_kp_y;
    flash_union_buffer[38].float_type = (float)g_vision_deadband_x;
    flash_union_buffer[39].float_type = (float)g_vision_deadband_y;
    flash_union_buffer[40].float_type = (float)g_vision_max_tilt;
    flash_union_buffer[41].float_type = (float)g_vision_lpf_alpha;
    flash_union_buffer[42].float_type = (float)g_vision_sign_x;
    flash_union_buffer[43].float_type = (float)g_vision_sign_y;
    flash_union_buffer[44].float_type = (float)g_vision_kd_x;
    flash_union_buffer[45].float_type = (float)g_vision_kd_y;

    flash_union_buffer[46].float_type = FlowPosPIDX.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[47].float_type = FlowPosPIDX.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[48].float_type = FlowPosPIDX.kd;
    flash_union_buffer[49].float_type = FlowPosPIDY.kp;
    flash_union_buffer[50].float_type = FlowPosPIDY.ki;
    flash_union_buffer[51].float_type = FlowPosPIDY.kd;

    flash_union_buffer[52].float_type = (float)g_flow_pos_kp;
    flash_union_buffer[53].float_type = (float)g_flow_pos_vmax;
    
    flash_union_buffer[54].float_type = (float)g_bb_en;
    flash_union_buffer[55].float_type = (float)g_bb_arm_s;
    flash_union_buffer[56].float_type = (float)g_bb_tz_s;
    flash_union_buffer[57].float_type = (float)g_land_rate;
    flash_union_buffer[58].float_type = (float)g_land_cut_h;
    flash_union_buffer[59].float_type = (float)g_land_still_ms;

    flash_union_buffer[60].float_type = (float)g_auto_takeoff_en;
    flash_union_buffer[61].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_target_h;
    flash_union_buffer[62].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_target_h_max;
    flash_union_buffer[63].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_throttle;  
    flash_union_buffer[64].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_max_time_s;
    flash_union_buffer[65].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_stable_time_ms;
    flash_union_buffer[66].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_beep_time_ms;
    flash_union_buffer[67].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_beep_delay_ms;
    flash_union_buffer[68].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_max_roll_deg;
    flash_union_buffer[69].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_max_pitch_deg;
    flash_union_buffer[70].float_type = (float)g_takeoff_abort_throttle;

    return (flash_write_page_from_buffer(MENU_FLASH_SECTOR, MENU_FLASH_PAGE, MENU_FLASH_WORDS) == 0) ? 1 : 0;
}

static uint8 menu_is_inline_param_page(const menu_item_t* menu_node)
{
    if(menu_node == NULL || menu_node->children == NULL ||
       menu_node->child_count == 0 || menu_node->child_count > INLINE_PARAM_MAX_COUNT)
    {
        return 0;
    }

    if(menu_node->children == menu_trim_items)
    {
        return 1;
    }

    for(uint8 i = 0; i < (uint8)(sizeof(menu_pid_items) / sizeof(menu_pid_items[0])); i++)
    {
        if(menu_node->children == menu_pid_items[i].children)
        {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

static void menu_inline_edit_begin(const menu_item_t* menu_node)
{
    if(menu_node == NULL || menu_node->children == NULL ||
       menu_node->child_count == 0 || menu_node->child_count > INLINE_PARAM_MAX_COUNT)
    {
        return;
    }

    for(uint8 i = 0; i < menu_node->child_count; i++)
    {
        g_inline_edit_values[i] = (menu_node->children[i].fval != NULL) ?
                                  (float)(*menu_node->children[i].fval) : 0.0f;
    }
    g_inline_edit_active = 1;
}

static void menu_inline_edit_cancel(void)
{
    g_inline_edit_active = 0;
}

static void menu_inline_edit_apply_and_save(const menu_item_t* menu_node)
{
    if(menu_node == NULL || menu_node->children == NULL || !g_inline_edit_active ||
       menu_node->child_count == 0 || menu_node->child_count > INLINE_PARAM_MAX_COUNT)
    {
        return;
    }

    for(uint8 i = 0; i < menu_node->child_count; i++)
    {
        if(menu_node->children[i].fval != NULL)
        {
            *menu_node->children[i].fval = clampf(g_inline_edit_values[i],
                                                   menu_node->children[i].minv,
                                                   menu_node->children[i].maxv);
        }
    }

    if(menu_save_params_to_flash())
    {
        menu_params_load();
    }
    g_inline_edit_active = 0;
}

static void ui_draw_local_camera_error(void)
{
    volatile drone_target_t *target = (volatile drone_target_t *)SHARED_DRONE_TARGET_ADDR;
    volatile uint32 *frame_seq = (volatile uint32 *)LOCAL_CAMERA_FRAME_SEQ_ADDR;

    SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)frame_seq, sizeof(*frame_seq));
    SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)target, sizeof(*target));

    tft180_set_font(TFT180_6X8_FONT);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_YELLOW, RGB565_BLACK);
    if((*frame_seq != 0U) && (g_vision_data.valid != 0U) && (target->valid != 0U))
    {
        tft180_show_string(0, 144, "X:");
        tft180_show_int(12, 144, (int32)g_vision_data.error_x_px, 4);
        tft180_show_string(48, 144, "Y:");
        tft180_show_int(60, 144, (int32)g_vision_data.error_y_px, 4);
    }
    else
    {
        tft180_show_string(0, 144, "X:--- Y:---");
    }
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
}

static void ui_draw_list(const menu_item_t* menu_node, uint8 highlight)
{
    if(menu_node == NULL)
    {
        return;
    }
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_full(RGB565_BLACK);

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_CYAN, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, menu_node->name);

    uint8 is_takeoff_menu = (menu_node->children == menu_takeoff_items);
    uint8 start_y = 16;  

    if(is_takeoff_menu)
    {
        
        auto_takeoff_state_t state = air_auto_takeoff_get_state();
        const char* state_str = "?";
        switch(state)
        {
            case AT_OFF:         state_str = "O"; break;
            case AT_RISING:      state_str = "R"; break;
            case AT_STABILIZING: state_str = "S"; break;
            case AT_HEIGHT_LOCK: state_str = "H"; break;
            case AT_BEEP_WAIT:   state_str = "B"; break;
            case AT_TRACKING:    state_str = "T"; break;
            case AT_ABORT:       state_str = "X"; break;
            default:             state_str = "?"; break;
        }

        float height = g_tof_state.height_m;
        uint32 now_ms = system_get_ms();
        uint32 elapsed_s = now_ms / 1000U;

        tft180_set_color(RGB565_YELLOW, RGB565_BLACK);
        char status_buf[32];
        snprintf(status_buf, sizeof(status_buf), "%s H%.2f %us", state_str, (double)height, elapsed_s);
        tft180_show_string(0, 16, status_buf);

        start_y = 32;  
    }

    const menu_item_t* items = menu_node->children;
    uint8 cnt = menu_node->child_count;

    #define MAX_VISIBLE_ITEMS 7
    uint8 max_visible_items = is_takeoff_menu ? 6U : ((menu_node == &menu_root) ? 7U : MAX_VISIBLE_ITEMS);
    uint8 scroll_offset = 0;
    if(cnt > max_visible_items)
    {
        if(highlight >= max_visible_items)
        {
            scroll_offset = highlight - max_visible_items + 1;
        }
    }

    uint8 visible_end = (cnt < scroll_offset + max_visible_items) ? cnt : (scroll_offset + max_visible_items);
    for(uint8 i = scroll_offset; i < visible_end; i++)
    {
        uint16 y = (uint16)(start_y + 16 * (i - scroll_offset));
        if(i == highlight) tft180_set_color(RGB565_RED, RGB565_BLACK);
        else tft180_set_color(RGB565_GREEN, RGB565_BLACK);
        tft180_show_string(0, y, items[i].name);

        if(items[i].type == MENU_ITEM_PARAM_FLOAT && items[i].flash_saved != NULL)
        {
            float display_value = (float)(*items[i].flash_saved);
            if(g_inline_edit_active && menu_is_inline_param_page(menu_node) &&
               i < menu_node->child_count)
            {
                display_value = g_inline_edit_values[i];
            }
            tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
            tft180_show_float(56, y, (double)display_value, 3, 3);
        }
        
        if(items[i].type == MENU_ITEM_DISPLAY_RC_CH && items[i].ch_index < RC_CHANNEL_NUM)
        {
            uint8 ch_idx = items[i].ch_index;
            tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
            
            tft180_show_int(44, y, (int)remote_data.ch[ch_idx], 4);
            tft180_show_int(96, y, (int)remote_data.ch_value[ch_idx], 3);
        }
    }
    
    if(menu_node == &menu_root)
    {
        ui_draw_local_camera_error();
    }

    if(cnt > max_visible_items)
    {
        tft180_set_color(RGB565_YELLOW, RGB565_BLACK);
        if(scroll_offset > 0)
        {
            tft180_show_string(120, 16, "^");
        }
        if(visible_end < cnt)
        {
            tft180_show_string(120, 144, "v");
        }
    }
}

static void ui_draw_edit_float(void)
{
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_full(RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_CYAN, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, "Edit");
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_YELLOW, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 32, "Value:");
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_float(24, 32, (double)g_edit_value, 3, 3);
}

static void ui_draw_monitor_flow(void)
{
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_full(RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_CYAN, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, "FLOW");

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_GREEN, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 16, "Vx  :");   
    tft180_show_string(0, 32, "Vy  :");
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, "Dbx :");   
    tft180_show_string(0, 64, "Dby :");
    tft180_show_string(0, 80, "Cal :");   
    tft180_show_string(0, 96, "Px  :");   
    tft180_show_string(0,112, "Py  :");   

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_float(48, 16, (double)g_flow_state.filtered_vx, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48, 32, (double)g_flow_state.filtered_vy, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48, 48, (double)g_flow_state.deb_vx, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48, 64, (double)g_flow_state.deb_vy, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_int(48, 80, (int)g_flow_state.bias_calibrated, 1);
    tft180_show_float(48, 96, (double)g_flow_state.pos_x, 3, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48,112, (double)g_flow_state.pos_y, 3, 2);
}

static void ui_update_monitor_flow(void)
{
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_float(48, 16, (double)g_flow_state.filtered_vx, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48, 32, (double)g_flow_state.filtered_vy, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48, 48, (double)g_flow_state.deb_vx, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48, 64, (double)g_flow_state.deb_vy, 4, 2);
    tft180_show_int(48, 80, (int)g_flow_state.bias_calibrated, 1);
    tft180_show_float(48, 96, (double)g_flow_state.pos_x, 3, 2);
    tft180_show_float(48,112, (double)g_flow_state.pos_y, 3, 2);
}

typedef struct
{
    uint8 m7_1_status;
    uint32 frame_seq;
    drone_target_t target;
} vision_diag_snapshot_t;

static void vision_diag_snapshot_read(vision_diag_snapshot_t *snapshot)
{
    volatile uint8 *m7_1_status = (volatile uint8 *)SHARED_M7_1_STATUS_ADDR;
    volatile uint32 *frame_seq = (volatile uint32 *)LOCAL_CAMERA_FRAME_SEQ_ADDR;
    volatile drone_target_t *target = (volatile drone_target_t *)SHARED_DRONE_TARGET_ADDR;
    uint32 seq_before;
    uint32 seq_after;
    uint8 retry;

    for(retry = 0U; retry < 2U; retry++)
    {
        SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)frame_seq, sizeof(*frame_seq));
        seq_before = *frame_seq;

        SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)target, sizeof(*target));
        snapshot->target.err_x = target->err_x;
        snapshot->target.err_y = target->err_y;
        snapshot->target.valid = target->valid;

        SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)frame_seq, sizeof(*frame_seq));
        seq_after = *frame_seq;
        if(seq_before == seq_after)
        {
            break;
        }
    }
    snapshot->frame_seq = seq_after;

    SCB_InvalidateDCache_by_Addr((uint32_t *)m7_1_status, sizeof(*m7_1_status));
    snapshot->m7_1_status = *m7_1_status;
}

static const char *vision_diag_state(const vision_diag_snapshot_t *snapshot)
{
    if(snapshot->m7_1_status == (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_ERROR) return "M1ERR ";
    if(snapshot->m7_1_status != (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_RUNNING) return "M1INIT";
    if(snapshot->frame_seq == 0U) return "NOSEQ ";
    if(g_vision_data.frame_count == 0U) return "NOCOPY";
    if(snapshot->target.valid == 0U || g_vision_data.valid == 0U) return "NOVAL ";
    return "OK    ";
}

static const char *vision_diag_m7_1_status(uint8 status)
{
    if(status == (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_RUNNING) return "RUN ";
    if(status == (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_ERROR) return "ERR ";
    if(status == (uint8)M7_1_STATUS_INIT) return "INIT";
    return "????";
}

static void ui_draw_monitor_vision_data(void)
{
    vision_diag_snapshot_t snapshot;

    vision_diag_snapshot_read(&snapshot);

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, "M1:");
    tft180_show_string(32, 0, vision_diag_m7_1_status(snapshot.m7_1_status));

    tft180_show_string(0, 16, "SEQ:");
    tft180_show_uint(32, 16, snapshot.frame_seq, 10);

    tft180_show_string(0, 32, "RX:");
    tft180_show_float(32, 32, (double)snapshot.target.err_x, 3, 1);

    tft180_show_string(0, 48, "RY:");
    tft180_show_float(32, 48, (double)snapshot.target.err_y, 3, 1);

    tft180_show_string(0, 64, "RVal:");
    tft180_show_uint(48, 64, snapshot.target.valid, 1);

    tft180_show_string(0, 80, "CFrm:");
    tft180_show_uint(40, 80, g_vision_data.frame_count, 10);

    tft180_show_string(0, 96, "CVal:");
    tft180_show_uint(48, 96, g_vision_data.valid, 1);

    tft180_show_string(0, 112, "STATE:");
    tft180_show_string(56, 112, vision_diag_state(&snapshot));
}

static void ui_draw_monitor_vision(void)
{
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_full(RGB565_BLACK);
    ui_draw_monitor_vision_data();
}

static void ui_update_takeoff_status(void)
{
    auto_takeoff_state_t state = air_auto_takeoff_get_state();
    const char* state_str = "?";
    switch(state)
    {
        case AT_OFF:         state_str = "O"; break;
        case AT_RISING:      state_str = "R"; break;
        case AT_STABILIZING: state_str = "S"; break;
        case AT_HEIGHT_LOCK: state_str = "H"; break;
        case AT_BEEP_WAIT:   state_str = "B"; break;
        case AT_TRACKING:    state_str = "T"; break;
        case AT_ABORT:       state_str = "X"; break;
        default:             state_str = "?"; break;
    }

    float height = g_tof_state.height_m;
    uint32 now_ms = system_get_ms();
    uint32 elapsed_s = now_ms / 1000U;

    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_YELLOW, RGB565_BLACK);
    char status_buf[32];
    snprintf(status_buf, sizeof(status_buf), "%s H%.2f %us", state_str, (double)height, elapsed_s);
    tft180_show_string(0, 16, status_buf);
}

static void ui_update_monitor_vision(void)
{
    ui_draw_monitor_vision_data();
}

static void ui_draw_monitor_remote(void)
{
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_full(RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_CYAN, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 0, "REMOTE");

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_GREEN, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(0, 16, "1:");
    tft180_show_string(0, 32, "2:");
    tft180_show_string(0, 48, "3:");
    tft180_show_string(0, 64, "4:");
    tft180_show_string(0, 80, "5:");
    tft180_show_string(0, 96, "6:");
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_YELLOW, RGB565_BLACK);
    tft180_show_string(16, 0, "raw");
    tft180_show_string(88, 0, "map");

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    for(uint8 i = 0; i < 6; i++)
    {
        uint8 y = (uint8)(16 + i * 16);
        tft180_show_uint(16, y, remote_data.ch[i], 4);        
        tft180_show_uint(88, y, remote_data.ch_value[i], 3);  
    }

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_CYAN, RGB565_BLACK);
    uint8 sig = remote_control_get_signal_valid();
    tft180_show_string(0, 112, sig ? "SIG:OK " : "SIG:LOST");
}

static void ui_update_monitor_remote(void)
{
    tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
    for(uint8 i = 0; i < 6; i++)
    {
        uint8 y = (uint8)(16 + i * 16);
        tft180_show_uint(16, y, remote_data.ch[i], 4);        
        tft180_show_uint(88, y, remote_data.ch_value[i], 3);  
    }

    tft180_set_color(RGB565_CYAN, RGB565_BLACK);
    uint8 signal_valid = remote_control_get_signal_valid();
    tft180_show_string(0, 112, signal_valid ? "SIG:OK " : "SIG:LOST");
}

static void ui_update_remote_list(const menu_item_t* menu_node, uint8 highlight)
{
    if(menu_node == NULL || menu_node->children == NULL || menu_node->child_count == 0)
    {
        return;
    }
    const menu_item_t* items = menu_node->children;
    uint8 cnt = menu_node->child_count;

    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    for(uint8 i = 0; i < cnt; i++)
    {
        if(items[i].type == MENU_ITEM_DISPLAY_RC_CH)
        {
            uint16 y = (uint16)(16 * (i + 1));
            uint8 ch_idx = items[i].ch_index;

            if(ch_idx < RC_CHANNEL_NUM)
            {

                tft180_set_color(RGB565_WHITE, RGB565_BLACK);
                tft180_show_int(44, y, (int)remote_data.ch[ch_idx], 4);
                tft180_show_int(96, y, (int)remote_data.ch_value[ch_idx], 3);
            }
            else if(ch_idx == 254)
            {
                if(g_rc_flags.unlocked)
                {
                    tft180_set_color(RGB565_GREEN, RGB565_BLACK);
                    tft180_show_string(72, y, "YES");
                }
                else
                {
                    tft180_set_color(RGB565_RED, RGB565_BLACK);
                    tft180_show_string(72, y, "NO ");
                }
            }
            else if(ch_idx == 255)
            {
                if(g_rc_cmd.height_hold)
                {
                    tft180_set_color(RGB565_GREEN, RGB565_BLACK);
                    tft180_show_string(72, y, "ON ");
                }
                else
                {
                    tft180_set_color(RGB565_RED, RGB565_BLACK);
                    tft180_show_string(72, y, "OFF");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

static void ui_draw(void)
{
    switch(g_mode)
    {
        case UI_LIST:
        {
            const menu_item_t* node = (g_menu_depth < MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH) ? g_menu_stack[g_menu_depth] : NULL;
            ui_draw_list(node, g_cur_index);
        } break;
        case UI_EDIT_FLOAT:      ui_draw_edit_float(); break;
        case UI_MONITOR_IMU:     ui_draw_monitor_imu(); break;
        case UI_MONITOR_REMOTE:  ui_draw_monitor_remote(); break;
        case UI_MONITOR_TOF:     ui_draw_monitor_tof(); break;
        case UI_MONITOR_FLOW:    ui_draw_monitor_flow(); break;
        case UI_MONITOR_VISION:  ui_draw_monitor_vision(); break;
        default: break;
    }
}

static const menu_item_t* cur_items(void)
{
    const menu_item_t* node = (g_menu_depth < MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH) ? g_menu_stack[g_menu_depth] : NULL;
    return (node != NULL) ? node->children : NULL;
}

static uint8 cur_count(void)
{
    const menu_item_t* node = (g_menu_depth < MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH) ? g_menu_stack[g_menu_depth] : NULL;
    return (node != NULL) ? node->child_count : 0;
}

static const menu_item_t* cur_item(void)
{
    const menu_item_t* items = cur_items();
    uint8 cnt = cur_count();
    if(items == NULL || cnt == 0) return NULL;
    if(g_cur_index >= cnt) g_cur_index = 0;
    return &items[g_cur_index];
}

static void menu_push_node(const menu_item_t* node)
{
    if(node == NULL) return;
    if(g_menu_depth + 1 >= MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH) return;
    g_menu_depth++;
    g_menu_stack[g_menu_depth] = node;
    g_cur_index = 0;
    g_mode = UI_LIST;
    ui_draw();
}

static const menu_item_t* cur_node(void)
{
    return (g_menu_depth < MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH) ? g_menu_stack[g_menu_depth] : NULL;
}

static void menu_back(void)
{
    const menu_item_t* node = cur_node();
    if(g_mode == UI_LIST && g_inline_edit_active && menu_is_inline_param_page(node))
    {
        menu_inline_edit_cancel();
        ui_draw();
        return;
    }
    if(g_mode == UI_EDIT_FLOAT)
    {
        g_mode = UI_LIST;
        g_edit_ptr = NULL;
        ui_draw();
        return;
    }
    if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_IMU || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_REMOTE ||
       g_mode == UI_MONITOR_TOF || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_FLOW ||
       g_mode == UI_MONITOR_VISION)
    {
        g_mode = UI_LIST;
        ui_draw();
        return;
    }
    if(g_menu_depth > 0)
    {
        g_menu_depth--;
        g_cur_index = 0;
        g_mode = UI_LIST;
        ui_draw();
    }
}

static void menu_open_item(const menu_item_t* it)
{
    if(it == NULL) return;
    switch(it->type)
    {
        case MENU_ITEM_SUBMENU:
            if(it->children != NULL && it->child_count > 0)
            {
                menu_push_node(it);
            }
            break;
        case MENU_ITEM_PARAM_FLOAT:
            if(it->fval != NULL)
            {
                g_mode = UI_EDIT_FLOAT;
                g_edit_ptr = it->fval;
                g_edit_value = (float)(*it->fval);
                g_edit_step = (it->step > 0.0f) ? it->step : 0.01f;
                g_edit_minv = it->minv;
                g_edit_maxv = it->maxv;
                g_edit_name = it->name;
                ui_draw();
            }
            break;
        case MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_IMU:
            g_mode = UI_MONITOR_IMU; ui_draw(); break;
        case MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_REMOTE:
            g_mode = UI_MONITOR_REMOTE; ui_draw(); break;
        case MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_TOF:
            g_mode = UI_MONITOR_TOF; ui_draw(); break;
        case MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_FLOW:
            g_mode = UI_MONITOR_FLOW; ui_draw(); break;
        case MENU_ITEM_MONITOR_VISION:
            g_mode = UI_MONITOR_VISION; ui_draw(); break;
        case MENU_ITEM_ACTION:
            if(it->action) it->action();
            break;
        case MENU_ITEM_DISPLAY_RC_CH:
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

static inline float clampf(float v, float lo, float hi)
{
    if(v < lo) return lo;
    if(v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static void menu_edit_apply_and_save(void)
{
    if(g_edit_ptr == NULL) return;
    float v = clampf(g_edit_value, g_edit_minv, g_edit_maxv);
    *g_edit_ptr = v;
    if(menu_save_params_to_flash())
    {
        menu_params_load();
    }
    g_mode = UI_LIST;
    g_edit_ptr = NULL;
    ui_draw();
}

static void menu_handle_key(key_event_t ev)
{
    const menu_item_t* node;

    if(ev == KEYEV_NONE) return;
    buzzer_beep(20);

    if(ev == KEYEV_BACK)
    {
        menu_back();
        return;
    }

    if(g_mode == UI_EDIT_FLOAT)
    {
        if(ev == KEYEV_INC)
        {
            g_edit_value += g_edit_step;
            if(g_edit_value > g_edit_maxv) g_edit_value = g_edit_maxv;
            ui_draw();
        }
        else if(ev == KEYEV_DEC)
        {
            g_edit_value -= g_edit_step;
            if(g_edit_value < g_edit_minv) g_edit_value = g_edit_minv;
            ui_draw();
        }
        else if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_SHORT)
        {
            menu_edit_apply_and_save();
        }
        else if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_LONG)
        {
            menu_back();
        }
        return;
    }

    if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_IMU || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_REMOTE ||
       g_mode == UI_MONITOR_TOF || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_FLOW ||
       g_mode == UI_MONITOR_VISION)
    {
        if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_LONG) menu_back();
        return;
    }

    node = cur_node();
    if(g_mode == UI_LIST && menu_is_inline_param_page(node))
    {
        if(ev == KEYEV_INC || ev == KEYEV_DEC)
        {
            const menu_item_t* it = cur_item();
            if(it != NULL && it->type == MENU_ITEM_PARAM_FLOAT &&
               g_cur_index < node->child_count)
            {
                if(!g_inline_edit_active)
                {
                    menu_inline_edit_begin(node);
                }
                if(ev == KEYEV_INC)
                {
                    g_inline_edit_values[g_cur_index] += it->step;
                    if(g_inline_edit_values[g_cur_index] > it->maxv) g_inline_edit_values[g_cur_index] = it->maxv;
                }
                else
                {
                    g_inline_edit_values[g_cur_index] -= it->step;
                    if(g_inline_edit_values[g_cur_index] < it->minv) g_inline_edit_values[g_cur_index] = it->minv;
                }
                ui_draw();
            }
        }
        else if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_SHORT)
        {
            if(g_inline_edit_active)
            {
                menu_inline_edit_apply_and_save(node);
                ui_draw();
            }
        }
        else if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_LONG)
        {
            if(g_inline_edit_active)
            {
                menu_inline_edit_cancel();
                ui_draw();
            }
            else
            {
                menu_back();
            }
        }
        else if(ev == KEYEV_NEXT)
        {
            uint8 cnt = cur_count();
            if(cnt)
            {
                g_cur_index++;
                if(g_cur_index >= cnt) g_cur_index = 0;
                ui_draw();
            }
        }
        return;
    }

    if(ev == KEYEV_NEXT)
    {
        uint8 cnt = cur_count();
        if(cnt)
        {
            g_cur_index++;
            if(g_cur_index >= cnt) g_cur_index = 0;
            ui_draw();
        }
    }
    else if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_SHORT)
    {
        menu_open_item(cur_item());
    }
    else if(ev == KEYEV_ENTER_LONG)
    {
        menu_back();
    }
}

void menu_init(void)
{
#if TFT180_DISPLAY_CORE != 0
    return;
#endif
    key_init_one(&key_next);
    key_init_one(&key_enter);
    key_init_one(&key_inc);
    key_init_one(&key_dec);
    key_init_one(&key_back);
    buzzer_init();
    flash_init();

    tft180_set_dir(TFT180_PORTAIT);
    tft180_init();
    tft180_set_font(TFT180_8X16_FONT);
    tft180_full(RGB565_BLACK);

    if(!menu_params_load())
    {
        menu_params_save();
    }

    g_menu_depth = 0;
    g_menu_stack[0] = &menu_root;
    g_cur_index = 0;
    g_mode = UI_LIST;
    g_last_draw_ms = 0;
    ui_draw();
}

uint8 menu_save_params_to_flash(void)
{
    return menu_params_save();
}

void menu_tick(void)
{
#if TFT180_DISPLAY_CORE != 0
    return;
#endif

    uint32 now = system_get_ms();
    if(now - g_last_draw_ms >= 100)   
    {
        g_last_draw_ms = now;
        if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_IMU || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_REMOTE ||
           g_mode == UI_MONITOR_TOF || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_FLOW ||
           g_mode == UI_MONITOR_VISION)
        {
            if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_IMU)      ui_update_monitor_imu();
            else if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_REMOTE)  ui_update_monitor_remote();
            else if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_TOF)     ui_update_monitor_tof();
            else if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_FLOW)    ui_update_monitor_flow();
            else if(g_mode == UI_MONITOR_VISION)  ui_update_monitor_vision();
        }
        else if(g_mode == UI_LIST)
        {
            const menu_item_t* node = (g_menu_depth < MENU_STACK_MAX_DEPTH) ? g_menu_stack[g_menu_depth] : NULL;
            if(node == &menu_root)
            {
                ui_draw_local_camera_error();
            }
            else if(node != NULL && node->children != NULL && node->child_count > 0 &&
                    node->children[0].type == MENU_ITEM_DISPLAY_RC_CH)
            {
                ui_update_remote_list(node, g_cur_index);
            }
            else if(node != NULL && node->children == menu_takeoff_items)
            {
                ui_update_takeoff_status();
            }
        }
    }

    static uint32 last_key_scan_ms = 0;
    uint32 key_scan_interval = (g_mode == UI_MONITOR_IMU || g_mode == UI_MONITOR_REMOTE ||
                                g_mode == UI_MONITOR_TOF) ? 100 : 0;

    if(now - last_key_scan_ms >= key_scan_interval)
    {
        last_key_scan_ms = now;
        key_event_t ev = keys_poll_once();
        if(ev != KEYEV_NONE)
        {
            menu_handle_key(ev);
        }
    }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
anonym_protocol.h - 匿名协议头文件

#ifndef __ANONYM_PROTOCOL_H
#define __ANONYM_PROTOCOL_H

#include "zf_common_headfile.h"

#ifndef ANONYM_UART_INDEX 
#define ANONYM_UART_INDEX      (UART_0)
#define ANONYM_UART_BAUD       (115200U)
#define ANONYM_UART_TX_PIN     (UART0_TX_P00_1)
#define ANONYM_UART_RX_PIN     (UART0_RX_P00_0)
#endif

void Anonym_ProtocolInit(void);
void Anonym_SendEuler(float roll,
                      float pitch,
                      float yaw,
                      uint8_t fusion_state);
void Anonym_DT_Data_Exchange(void);

#endif


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
anonym_protocol.c - 匿名协议实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "anonym_protocol.h"
#include "imu/IMU.h"

static uint8_t anonym_uart_inited = 0;
static uint8_t anonym_tx_buffer[50];

static inline int16_t anonym_angle_to_int(float value)   { return (int16_t)(value * 100.0f); }
static inline int16_t anonym_acc_to_int(float value)     { return (int16_t)(value * 1000.0f); }
static inline int16_t anonym_gyro_to_int(float value)    { return (int16_t)(value * 100.0f); }
static inline int16_t anonym_mag_to_int(float value)     { return (int16_t)(value * 1000.0f); }

static void anonym_uart_send(uint8_t length)
{
    anonym_tx_buffer[3] = length - 4;
    uint8_t sum = 0;
    for(uint8_t i = 0; i < length; i++)
    {
        sum += anonym_tx_buffer[i];
    }
    anonym_tx_buffer[length++] = sum;
    uart_write_buffer(ANONYM_UART_INDEX, anonym_tx_buffer, length);
}

static void anonym_send_status(float roll, float pitch, float yaw, uint8_t fly_model, uint8_t armed)
{
    uint8_t idx = 0;
    int32_t alt = 0;

    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0xAA;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0xAA;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0x01;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0;

    int16_t temp = anonym_angle_to_int(roll);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    temp = anonym_angle_to_int(pitch);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    temp = anonym_angle_to_int(yaw);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((alt >> 24) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((alt >> 16) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((alt >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(alt & 0xFF);

    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = fly_model;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = armed;

    anonym_uart_send(idx);
}

static void anonym_send_sensor(void)
{
    uint8_t idx = 0;

    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0xAA;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0xAA;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0x02;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = 0;

    int16_t temp = anonym_acc_to_int(IMU_Data.acc_x);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);
    temp = anonym_acc_to_int(IMU_Data.acc_y);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);
    temp = anonym_acc_to_int(IMU_Data.acc_z);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    temp = anonym_gyro_to_int(IMU_Data.gyro_x);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);
    temp = anonym_gyro_to_int(IMU_Data.gyro_y);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);
    temp = anonym_gyro_to_int(IMU_Data.gyro_z);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    temp = anonym_mag_to_int(IMU_Data.mag_x);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);
    temp = anonym_mag_to_int(IMU_Data.mag_y);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);
    temp = anonym_mag_to_int(IMU_Data.mag_z);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    temp = 0;
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)((temp >> 8) & 0xFF);
    anonym_tx_buffer[idx++] = (uint8_t)(temp & 0xFF);

    anonym_uart_send(idx);
}

void Anonym_ProtocolInit(void)
{
    if(!anonym_uart_inited)
    {
        uart_init(ANONYM_UART_INDEX, ANONYM_UART_BAUD, ANONYM_UART_TX_PIN, ANONYM_UART_RX_PIN);
        anonym_uart_inited = 1;
    }
}

void Anonym_SendEuler(float roll,
                      float pitch,
                      float yaw,
                      uint8_t fusion_state)
{
    (void)fusion_state;
    if(!anonym_uart_inited)
    {
        Anonym_ProtocolInit();
    }
    anonym_send_status(roll, pitch, yaw, 0, 0);
}

void Anonym_DT_Data_Exchange(void)
{
    if(!anonym_uart_inited)
    {
        Anonym_ProtocolInit();
    }
    
    static uint8_t cnt = 0;
    static uint8_t senser_cnt  = 5;    
    static uint8_t status_cnt  = 1;   

    if((cnt % senser_cnt) == (senser_cnt - 1))
    {
        anonym_send_sensor();
    }

    if((cnt % status_cnt) == (status_cnt - 1))
    {
        anonym_send_status(IMU_Euler.roll, IMU_Euler.pitch, IMU_Euler.yaw, 0, 0);
    }

    cnt++;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
vofa.c - VOFA协议实现
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "protocol/vofa.h"
#include "remote_control/remote_control.h"
#include "imu/ALL_DATA.h"
#include "imu/IMU.h"
#include "imu/attitude/Attitude_Est.h"
#include "control/pid.h"
#include "battery/battery.h"
#include "vision_uart/vision_uart.h"
#include "flow/flow.h"
#include "zf_device_uart_receiver.h"
#include "zf_common_debug.h"

static uint8 s_vofa_inited = 0U;

static void vofa_send_float_array(float* data, uint8 count)
{
    char buffer[512];  
    char* ptr = buffer;

    for(uint8 i = 0; i < count; i++)
    {
        
        int32_t integer = (int32_t)data[i];
        int32_t decimal = (int32_t)((data[i] - integer) * 1000);
        if(decimal < 0) decimal = -decimal;

        ptr += sprintf(ptr, "%ld.%03ld", integer, decimal);
        if(i < count - 1)
        {
            *ptr++ = ',';
        }
    }
    *ptr++ = '\r';
    *ptr++ = '\n';
    *ptr = '\0';

    debug_send_buffer((uint8_t*)buffer, ptr - buffer);
}

int motor_speed1 = 0;
int motor_speed2 = 0;
int motor_speed3 = 0;
int motor_speed4 = 0;

float g_vofa_roll_angle_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_roll_angle_current = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_roll_rate_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_roll_rate_current = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_pitch_angle_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_pitch_angle_current = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_pitch_rate_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_pitch_rate_current = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_yaw_angle_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_yaw_angle_current = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_yaw_rate_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_yaw_rate_current = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_thr_cmd = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_thr_total = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_h_outer_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_h_outer_current = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_h_inner_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_h_inner_current = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_z_velocity = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_z_acc_mps2 = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_tof_init_status = 1.0f;  
float g_vofa_tof_working = 0.0f;     

float g_vofa_smooth_acc_z = 0.0f;    

float g_vofa_height_hold_enabled = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_flow_speed_x_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_flow_speed_x_current = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_flow_speed_y_target = 0.0f;
float g_vofa_flow_speed_y_current = 0.0f;

float g_vofa_flow_hold_on = 0.0f;   
float g_vofa_tracking_on  = 0.0f;   

void vofa_init(void)
{
    s_vofa_inited = 1U;
}

void vofa_pid_tuning_stream_tick(uint32 now_ms)
{
    static uint32 s_last_stream_ms = 0U;

    if(!s_vofa_inited)
    {
        vofa_init();
    }

    if((now_ms - s_last_stream_ms) < 20U)  
    {
        return;
    }
    s_last_stream_ms = now_ms;

    float data[34] = {
        g_vofa_roll_angle_target,    
        g_vofa_roll_angle_current,   
        g_vofa_pitch_angle_target,   
        g_vofa_pitch_angle_current,  
        g_vofa_yaw_angle_target,     
        g_vofa_yaw_angle_current,    
        g_vofa_roll_rate_target,     
        g_vofa_roll_rate_current,    
        g_vofa_pitch_rate_target,    
        g_vofa_pitch_rate_current,   
        g_vofa_yaw_rate_target,      
        g_vofa_yaw_rate_current,     
        g_vofa_h_outer_target,       
        g_vofa_h_outer_current,      
        g_vofa_h_inner_target,       
        g_vofa_h_inner_current,      
        g_vofa_thr_total,            
        g_vofa_flow_speed_x_target,  
        g_vofa_flow_speed_x_current, 
        g_vofa_flow_speed_y_target,  
        g_vofa_flow_speed_y_current, 
        g_battery_voltage,           
        g_vision_data.error_x_px,    
        g_vision_data.error_y_px,    
        (float)g_vision_data.valid,  
        g_vofa_flow_hold_on,         
        g_vofa_tracking_on,          
        (float)g_flow_state.bias_calibrated, 
        g_rc_cmd.roll_cmd,                   
        g_rc_cmd.pitch_cmd,                  
        g_vofa_thr_cmd,                      
        (float)remote_data.ch_value[4],      
        (float)g_rc_cmd.height_hold,         
        (float)g_rc_cmd.pos_hold             
    };
    vofa_send_float_array(data, 34);
}

e | 控制误差（目标值减实测值） | —

 |
